<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-2002 lecture 1 -->

One thing is that sometimes there is a lesson of the second day... a lesson of the second day takes place, and sometimes a few days... a few different days... Ghalib also said this in his poetry... perhaps yesterday or the day before yesterday... it was a lesson of the third day. At once, two or three are being left out.

*A'udhu billahi min al-shaytan al-rajim, Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim.* (I seek refuge in God from the accursed Satan, In the name of God, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful.) [recites prayers]. *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*.

Last time, it was submitted to you that today, we will begin the discourse on doctrines (*mawdu'at*) with *Tawhid* (monotheism), which is the most exalted of sciences (*ajall-e-uloom*) and, among all discourses, is the one with the most greatness (*azmat*). The *Tawhid* of the Lord of the Worlds (*Rabb al-'Alamin*), which is... that is God, who, as in other chapters as well, everywhere, as I have told you, no book of ours is complete until the discourse on *Tawhid* has taken place. So the *Tawhid* of *Rabb al-'Alamin* is the most exalted of sciences (*ajall-e-uloom*). The discourse of the Prophet (*payghambar*), the discourse of Janab-e-Ali, the discourse of other scholars (*'ulama'*), all that comes later. First, it is the discourse of God.

For *Bari Ta'ala* (God, the Exalted), where we begin, that is... him... that is... yesterday also we understood what is meant by God and what is meant by *Mubdi' Subhanahu* (the Originator, Glorified is He). Now I will explain a little of the Arabic terminology we have, to remember it: that *Mubdi'*... with a *zer* (vowel mark) under the *dal*... *Mubdi' Subhanahu* (the Originator, Glorified is He), that is our *Parvardigar* (Cherisher/Lord) who created the spiritual world (*'alam-e-ruhani*). The spiritual world is a different matter, and the physical world (*'alam-e-jismani*) that we are seeing is a different matter. And I told you yesterday that God is just (*'adil*). Just as God has all His attributes, so too is God just. So God's justice (*'adl*) can be established at a time when all people here are together. Here we see that different people are in different forms, with different statuses, and in different forms. So, this... how is this God's justice? So one has to accept that this is not a matter of this world. God is just there in the spiritual world, where *Mubdi' Subhanahu* when He created the essences (*zawat*) in the world, He created the luminous essences (*nurani zawat*), the *khuksani* essences, the spiritual essences (*ruhani zawat*), He created them in an equal (*yaksah*) state. *Mubdi' Subhanahu* is that *Parvardigar* for whom we perform *namaz* (prayer). The prayers (*namaz*) we perform, many are attributed (*mansubat*). For example, a prayer of *Laylat al-Qadr* (Night of Power) is for Janab-e-Fatima, for the Prophet, and there are many other attributed prayers. In the month of Ramadan, every night, at the time... that is, before the *namaz*... I will show the attributed prayers, that the 17th is for whom, the 19th for whom, and the 21st and 23rd. But all these have the status of knowledge (*'ilm*). The *namaz* we perform is *Al-'Ibadatu lillah* (Worship is for God). Worship (*'ibadat*) is only for God.

And see that four things are only for God, not for any other. And in that, the first thing is that *Al-'Ibadatu lillah* (Worship is for God). Worship (*'ibadat*), that is, prostration (*sajdah*), is befitting only for God Almighty. Prostration is not for anyone else. It has only been said that if prostration were permissible for anyone, it would be the wife's prostration for her husband. No other prostration is for anyone. The prostration we perform for the Imam is a prostration of respect (*sajdah-e-ta'zim*). It means to bow, to stoop. But what is called prostration, and the specific condition for prostration is that our seven limbs (*'aza*) should touch the ground. There, once, a mention of an incident comes to mind. In Mecca, Alhamdulillah, Janab-e-Jalali Sahab was present. A gathering was held there in which the minister (*wazir*) and prime minister (*wazir-e-azam*) were also present, and very prominent people who were nobles there were also included. That is, there were several ambassadors present at that time. Next to Janab-e-Jalali Sahab, the ambassador from Libya was sitting. Now, all the people from the community who were there, they were all coming, and just as the sermon (*wa'z*) was finishing, they were shaking hands, doing this and that. He was watching. He was a staunch Sunni. Even there, the Qaddafi people were... those who followed Qaddafi. Now, he is watching, watching, watching, and he couldn't bear it. So finally, the host of the house, whose invitation it was, he also came and did a foot-kissing bow (*qadam-bosi*). At that moment, he couldn't bear it and said, "What is this? First it's the bow and then the prostration. This is not permissible in Islam and this is wrong." He spoke to Jalali Sahab in Arabic. At that time, Jalali Sahab said, "Sir, you do not know the definition (*ta'rif*) of prostration. What is prostration called? Prostration is that in which the limbs, our seven limbs, that is, both knees, both our hands, and the forehead here, and the two feet. These six and one seven, seven limbs, when they touch the ground, only then is it a prostration. This is not a prostration." At that time, he became quiet. The point is that prostration is only for God. *Al-'Ibadatu lillah*. Prostration is only for God.

The second thing, *Al-Mulku lillah* (Sovereignty is for God). Sovereignty (*mulk*) is only for God. It is not for anyone. What we see here, that this is our plot, this is our land, this is our house, this is our field, nothing belongs to anyone. The father earns, the son squanders. The father leaves an inheritance (*wirasat*), someone else takes possession of it. No one has a flat or anything else. It is all for a temporary life. So, only sovereignty, meaning the land, the whole world, whose is it? *Al-Mulku lillah*. On the Day of Judgment, this cry will be raised: *Al-Mulku limani 'l-mulk? Al-Mulku lillahi 'l-Wahidi 'l-Qahhar.* (To whom belongs the sovereignty? Sovereignty belongs to God, the One, the Subduer.) Whose is the sovereignty? Whose is all the land? The answer will come that today, it belongs to Him in whose grasp is everything. So, *Al-Mulku lillah*. Sovereignty is only for God, it belongs to no one else.

The third thing, *Al-Rizqu lillah* (Sustenance is for God). Sustenance (*rizq*) is only from God. To provide sustenance is no one's work. People become means; a factory owner looks after his workers, a trader looks after his shop. All these are just becoming means. The Giver is only God. The power is in His hand, how He provides sustenance. Sustenance is from no one else. *Rizq* is from God.

And the fourth thing, *Al-Amru lillah* (The Command is for God). The command (*amr*), the order, is only from God. There is no other command. How does this command run? The command is *amr-e-rabbi* (the command of my Lord). It has started from there, from the First Intellect (*'aql-e-awwal*), and has come down to here, where we are sitting. Until the command is given from above, the one below cannot do any work. This is the system of the *da'wah*, the *da'wah sharif* system. So, wherever we see, God entrusted the First Intellect, and from there, with support (*ta'id*), they entrusted the Second Intellect, and from there to the Tenth Intellect, and from the Tenth to the world of composition (*'alam-e-tarkib*), from the world of composition to the world of Jibra'il, from Jibra'il to the physical world. The greatest individual in the physical world is our Imam of the time. The Imam of the era (*Imam-e-'asr*). And the command is in their hands. *Ulil-Amr* (Those in Authority). *Allahumma thabbitna 'ala ta'atillah, ta'ati rasulihi, wa ta'ati Ulil-Amr.* (O God, make us firm in the obedience of God, the obedience of His Messenger, and the obedience of Those in Authority). This obedience to the command is only with the Imam. And when the Imam is in concealment, that same command is with the *da'i* (summoner), and from the *da'i*, it goes to the *ma'dhun* (licensed one) and from the *ma'dhun* to the *mukasir* (assistant) and continues in this way.

So these four are things, they are special characteristics that are only for God, not for anyone else. So, that God who is *Mubdi' Subhanahu*. He who created everyone from non-existence (*nist*) to existence (*hast*). From *nothingness*, He gave *existence*. He is our God. We do not believe in any other. *La sharika lahu* (He has no partner). Besides Him, there is no other creator, besides Him. And how did He create? Now see, among the doctrines (*mawdu'at*), from the monotheists (*muwahhid*), those who are, and in other books of doctrines, our scholars have stated that in the spiritual world... when and how, we don't know, because it was beyond time and space. There, in the spiritual world, countless essences (*zawat*) were... uncountable, countless... uncountable essences, countless people, were there in a form like the letter *alif* (*qamat-e-alifiyyah*), just as a person stands today, head up, feet down, this is called *qamat-e-alifiyyah*. Remember this. *Alifiyyah* means like an *alif*. In the shape of an *alif*, in a *qamat-e-alifiyyah*, in a luminous form, in a spiritual form, in a *khuksani* form, God Almighty created them there. And how did He create them? In a uniform (*yaksah*) state. On an equal basis. He created them in a uniform state. And how? In a single stroke (*ba-qat'in wahidah*). He created them in a single instant. If He had created anyone before or after, the one who came later would say, "I was created late." There was nothing like that. Everyone was created at a single moment. And this is God's characteristic, that this is the greatness of *Mubdi' Subhanahu*, that God created them in a uniform state, meaning He distributed uniform things to each one, and in them... in a single stroke, He created all the essences.

Now see... for God... a little more about this... that *Mubdi' Subhanahu*... now when we mention the *Khaliq* (Creator), the *Khaliq*, whom we call *Parvardigar*, *Khaliq* is a different matter, and *Mubdi' Subhanahu* is different. *Mubdi' Subhanahu* is the one whom we are saying created existence from non-existence. And the *Khaliq* is one who has a model, from which something is made. A sheet is there, from which... a thread is there, from which a sheet is made. Wood is there, from which a chair is made. The one who reshapes, that is the *Khaliq*. But the one who created existence from *nothingness*, He is our *Mubdi' Subhanahu*. He is called *Mubdi'*. Remember this. If it comes in your reading, in the prayers of Sayyidna, it is in the 22nd, and in the prayers of *Shab-e-Qadr* (Night of Power), there is *mubda'* and *mubdi'*. There, above the *dal* is a *zabar* (vowel mark), and under the *dal* is a *zer*. *Mubda'* means that which was created. *Mubdi'* is the one who creates.

So... this *Mubdi' Subhanahu*, *Jalla Jalaluhu* (Glorious is His Majesty), for whom we are saying *Mubdi' Subhanahu*, in the future for Him we will use just one small word: *Hu* (He). *He* and *waw*. Why? Because our scholars, our *'ulama'*, say that even to say *Mubdi' Subhanahu* is not permissible. How can we... we can neither understand Him nor think of Him. He can neither enter our imagination, nor can our hearts accept Him. So, we should just use a name in which no doubt of *shirk* (polytheism) arises. That is... last time... God willing... that is... the final thing is this, that we just call Him *Hu*. *He, waw*. *Illa Hu*. *Shahida Allahu annahu la ilaha illa huwa* (God bears witness that there is no god but He). So, God... now, I want to remind you of the verse *Shahida Allahu*, I will explain this too.

So this... in the definition of *Mubdi' Subhanahu*, it is stated that three things are special. And these three things are... this is all a discourse on *Tawhid*. *Tawhid*, which you understood, is the whole title, and its subtitles are three. The first is *Tawhid*, the second is *Tanzih* (transcendence), and the third is *Tajrid* (abstraction). So *Tawhid*, which is the first, the subtitle of *Tawhid* is *Tawhid*. And it is this: to believe that God is one. Now, what is the purpose of believing God is one? Well, everyone believes God is one. Christians believe it, Jews believe it, and Muslims believe it, the whole world, those who believe in God, they all believe God is one. Then we, who claim to be a special community, what is special about us? We who say that we Sulaymanis have a special philosophy of doctrines, and we are distinct, we differentiate ourselves from others, how do we do it? How do we do it? What is our distinct form? It has been said, Sayyidna Muayyad al-Din, *'alayhi 'l-salam*, says that to believe God is one is not permissible, but God should be considered *yakta* (unique). Unique, not one. If He is one, then this is two, this is three, this is four. The counting has begun. So one, two, three... after one comes two, after two comes three, after three comes four. He has entered into counting. That which has entered into counting, it has come into an enclosure, that it is placed here, it is placed here, it is placed here. It has been surrounded by that much space. That which became a surrounded thing, it has fallen into density (*kasafat*). And when it fell into density, it became a created being (*bandah*), not God. So what does this mean? That the *Tawhid* of *Rabb al-'Alamin* does not come in the count of one, two, but rather *Qul huwa Allahu ahad* (Say, He is God, the One). He is *Ahad* (One), not *Wahid* (one). The whole world that believes God is one, He is not one, but He is *Ahad*. He is absolutely unique, distinct from all.

Now, regarding this matter, what our narrators (*muhaddithin*) have stated is this: See, all communities believe God is one. Then what is the special thing about us? So Sayyidna Muayyad, whom we call... who is a great philosopher of ours and a great scholar, and of the era of Muhammad... he is at the peak of the... he is one of the pillars. He says, what is *Tawhid* the name of? He gives the definition that *Al-Tawhidu huwa ma'rifat al-hudud al-ruhaniyyah wa 'l-jismaniyyah, 'aliha wa daniha, wa i'tiraf inna kulla haddin minhum wahidan fi rutbatihi la yusharikuhu fiha siwah.* (Tawhid is the gnosis of the spiritual and physical ranks, the high and the low among them, and the acknowledgment that every single rank among them is unique in its station, with no other sharing it with it.) The *Tawhid* of *Rabb al-'Alamin* is not the name for just believing God is one, but He is unique. Rather, what is it? That while believing God is unique, every rank (*hadd*), its spiritual essences and its physical essences, whether high or low, spiritual or physical, high or low, and to acknowledge this—we should acknowledge in our hearts, *iqrar*, this is what it's called. Here, we outwardly profess it with the tongue—to profess that every single one, every rank among them, is unique in its own place, *wahidan fi rutbatihi*. Each one is unique in its own place, no one is its partner. You cannot make a *mu'min* (believer) a *ma'dhun*, nor can you make a *ma'dhun* a *mukasir*. You cannot make a *da'i*, nor can you make a *da'i* an Imam. Whatever ranks there are, wherever they are, they are fixed. Except when one is elevated from *nass-e-juz'i* (partial designation), that's it, it's done. Otherwise, no one has the authority to change it. This is a command (*amr*), coming from above. It comes from above, from the *nass-e-juz'i*. It is the *amr-e-rabbi*. So remember, that in this way, the meaning of *Tawhid* of *Rabb al-'Alamin* is that not only is God to be believed as one, but the spiritual ranks, from the First Intellect to the Tenth Intellect, and then here, the physical ranks, where there is the Imam, where there is the Prophet, *salawatullahi 'alayhim*, and from there the many ranks, to consider them all unique in their own places, this is the true meaning of *Tawhid* of *Rabb al-'Alamin*.

After this, the second thing is *tanzih* (transcendence). *Tanzih* means *munazzah 'ani 'l-sifat* (transcendent above attributes). To purify from attributes. What is God like? Oh, God is very great. God is there. He is above the heavens. He is below the earth. He is here, He is there. God is everywhere and nowhere. Where is He not? For us to specify that He is here and not there, here and not there, He is in the mosque and not in the house, this is no specification. God is *munazzah 'ani 'l-sifat*. Whatever attributes we are applying... which attributes are we applying? We apply the ninety-nine names. The whole ninety-nine are there. *Basit* (The Expander), *Ra'uf* (The Kind), *Qadir* (The Powerful), *Rahim* (The Merciful), *Wahhab al-'Iman* (Giver of Faith), and *Sattar* (The Concealer), *Ra'uf*... all these attributes that there are, He is the creator of these attributes. There are many among us who possess such attributes. Those who do charity, they will be called *Sattar* for concealing someone's fault. You, who conceal the faults of others. *Ghafur* (The Forgiving), the one who forgives, he will be called *Ghafur*. *Qadir* (powerful), who has power... "I have this much power, I can do this and that, if I want to flatten a huge thing, I can do it." So I have this much power. Not that much power. So it means I am a small *qadir*. If there is a small *qadir*, and a big *qadir*, there is no comparison between the small and the big. And between the servant (*bandah*) and God, there is no comparison. So what this means is that *Mubdi' Subhanahu* is the creator of such *qadirs*, the creator of such *ra'ufs*, the creator of such *basits*. These names *Basit*, *Ra'uf*, etc., they apply to others, not to God Almighty. God's ninety-nine names, for which we do *tasbih* (glorification)... the ninety-nine names are different servants of God. Some serve in the spiritual world, and some in the physical world. As the lessons proceed, you will come to know. Thirty-three, thirty-three are divided differently. So each one, the physical world, the spiritual world, etc... the names of angels and so on. But every person does the *tasbih* of the ninety-nine names, but they don't know their meaning. So the *du'at* say that when we do not understand something, then apply all these attributes to him who is worthy of them. And that is the being of the First Intellect. So the First Intellect, whose discourse will come later, we can apply these to him. The names of attributes (*asma' al-sifat*)... The name of the essence (*ism al-dhat*) is Rahman, it is *Mubdi' Subhanahu*, which is reserved for God Almighty. But all the names of attributes are for his special, most special servant, the one who came first, the First Intellect, the First Existent (*mawjud-e-awwal*), the First Being (*wujud-e-awwal*), the First Precedent (*sabiq-e-awwal*). Attribute them to him, it is permissible. So, *munazzah 'ani 'l-sifat*. To purify Him from attributes, this is *tanzih*. This is the second.

The third thing is *tajrid* (abstraction). *Jard* means to peel the bark of a tree, which in one year, the skin that comes on top, we peel it off to know that one year has passed. So that is to do *jard*. And in Arabic, where there are stores, where there are big warehouses or businesses, a *jard* is done there. *Jard* means that stock-taking is done. Of the factory or the shop, etc. So the stock-taking that is done is also called *jard*. So it means that in a year, you should prepare a balance sheet. So there, here, what does it mean in relation to God? *Tajrid* means *salb al-ilahiyyah* (stripping away of divinity). The power of divinity (*ilahiyyah*), the attribute of divinity, should be stripped away from everyone except God. That no one is a creator, meaning no one is a god (*ilah*). The attribute of divinity should be separated from everyone and you should... reserve it for God Almighty.

So *tajrid* means to reserve the attribute of divinity only for God Almighty and separate it from others. This is called *tajrid*. So what happened? There was *Tawhid*, there was *tanzih*, and there was *tajrid*. These three are special attributes reserved for God Almighty. And after that, as has been stated, God Almighty in the spiritual world, in the *khuksani* world, in the luminous world, there created countless essences in a single stroke, in a uniform state. When they were created in a uniform state, then the result of each one should have come out the same. But that did not happen. Just as everyone is taught throughout the year, all the children, but only one will come first. Not every child will come first. In the same way, the one who became the first person in the world, became the first, and that's it. *Fa 'l-awwalu awwalun*. What has happened, has happened. No one can equal him. How did this happen? That when God Almighty created these essences, there is only one question, see, with us. The meaning of which is not that why did God Almighty create the spiritual world at all. This physical world, there are stars, planets, humans, animals, plants, why were these created? Why was the mosquito created? The answer to every single thing is there. But only the answer to one question is not there, that Allah has kept it in His hand, that why did God create the essences in the spiritual world? This is not our domain. We, the physical nature, we can understand it to an extent. You cannot understand your own self (*nafs*), where your soul (*ruh*) is. When it leaves, you have no idea. So what will you... of God? *Man 'arafa nafsahu faqad 'arafa rabbahu.* (Whoever knows his self, has known his Lord.) The one who recognized his own self, that what is the essence I have, how can I progress further, if he understands that, then he attains gnosis of the ranks (*hudud*) and he progresses further. So... a person himself... *Man 'arafa nafsahu faqad 'arafa rabbahu.* (Whoever knows his self, has known his Lord.) The one who recognized his own self. Those who recognize the self, they are the ones who advance to higher ranks, they are the noble *du'at* (summoners), they are the ones who have attained complete gnosis, they have passed on.

So see... that we... the answer to this one question is with no one, that why did God create everyone in the spiritual world? God created them, it is His power. He is *Mubdi' Subhanahu*. And why did He create them? So that His obedience (*'itba'*) could be done, His worship (*bandagi*) could be done. Now, who did the worship? We were all among them. And all the creatures that we see between the heavens and the earth, they were all in it. But one person's mind... one person's... in their mind... I'm not saying mind, the mind was not there, I am saying this to explain, what will happen to the mind when there is no essence. There, one thing came to mind, that, now look, what did he do? These are the words of the prayer. What did he do? That he looked at his own kind (*abna'-e-jins*), all the people who were sitting, he looked at them and he looked at himself. He looked at his own existence and he looked at all his kind. And he acknowledged this, that neither can these people create me, nor can I create them. But there is a third being who has created all of us. That's it. The first time the First Intellect thought this, and he became the first. The first existent (*mawjud-e-awwal*). The first precedent (*sabiq-e-awwal*). About this, the *du'at* have written many long chapters. So he became the first existent. And after that, no one has the capacity to be included with him. The circle of the First Intellect is the circle of oneness (*da'irah al-wahdah*). There is only one person. No one is with him. And how did he do it? Without a teacher (*mu'allim*), and without any guide. No one inspired him, gave him an inspiration, or put anything in his mind, or taught him any knowledge. He did it himself. And what did he do? That "I was not created from my own essence, nor was the world created from my essence. But He is the one who created all of us." This is the greatest fact that occurred in the spiritual world, which, today, for centuries... not centuries, for crores of years, for billions of years, this thing is being repeated. Since the creation of the world. When was it created? That will come in a later discourse, in the discourse on the Day of Judgment. Since that time, we are sitting here, we have sat here thousands of times, and this same discourse will be repeated. We would have heard it. The believers, in the true sense, all have heard and have obeyed, that's why they are still sitting. They are sitting. So there, what happened? That everyone... saw that the First Intellect, he made an acknowledgment. With the acknowledgment, at once, spiritual substances (*mawad-e-ruhani*) began to flow upon him. From God Almighty, from *Mubdi' Subhanahu*, substances... substance means... a form of a glow. And the Word (*kalimah*) is the same thing. Support (*ta'idat*) is the same thing. The very source of support flowed, from *Mubdi' Subhanahu*, and it came to the First Intellect. Just as, now you... here with us, the one who comes first is given a report, and he is given a prize. This was the prize, from God Almighty. So, that thing was given to him. And as soon as he received it, all these people, the entire creation, we were all included in it. All of them... they were all luminous. There was no density in anyone. All were luminous. But in him, the luminosity increased even more. It started to increase. Where it happened, that... the light started to increase. It increased and increased to such a state that now people's eyes started to fall on him, that "Hey, what is this? He has... *sha'sha'ani* is what it's called. *Sha'sha'ani* is called... you see, one is *ziya'* (light) and one is called *sha'sha'ani*. *Ziya'* is that which emits its own light, like the light of the sun. And another is *sha'sha'ani*, which is reflected. That is, it came from God Almighty, struck your essence, and then it spread. So that is what is called *sha'sha'ani*. So his... the luminosity, from the support, started to increase. It increased so much, so much, that... you see, I'm giving an example. An examination of children happened. An exam was taken. The one who comes first, when the report is announced, as soon as his number is called, "Hey, you have come first," now look at his face. A special pleasure comes over him. A special happiness is born in him, a radiance. "Oh, in the whole class, I came first." The change that happens, the self-enjoyment that happens, that is what happened to him at that time. The whole world was behind. There was only one person, the First Intellect, who received this support. He received it. Now, this self-enjoyment, we call it... the *du'at* say it was *masarrat* (joy). A special enjoyment, joy was erupting from within. And veils of joy began to spread. I don't use the word *ghulaf-bazi* because there are *'ulama'* here. Veils began to spread over him. Now, when this state was seen, at that moment, it was necessary that all the people who were there, the countless essences, all of them should look towards him and do what he did. But that did not happen. What happened, that discourse, from the Imams... this has been stated. And what was it? Two individuals arose. They are called *munba'ithayn*. *Munba'ithayn* means two who arose. *Munba'ith* means to arise. So two *munba'ith* occurred, and they are called, like, rising stars. Two rising stars arose from them. And they saw him. And how did they see? See the difference between the two. He obeyed without a teacher. Here, they are being obeyed by seeing him. They saw him and thought, "Hey, what is this? He has... his form has changed, his luminosity has spread." When they saw, and they came to know that this is the situation, so at that moment, what did the first and second *munba'ith* do? Remember these names. There is the first *munba'ith* and the second *munba'ith*. The first *munba'ith* thought, "Why did his luminosity increase?" The increase in luminosity, why did it happen? This is called the inner reality (*batin*). To know the inner matter. And the other one, the second *munba'ith*, he saw, "Oh, what a grand being he is! What a great elder he is! What is he?" As you see, someone, you look at their outward appearance. This is called the outward (*mazhariyyat*). Look at the appearance. "Oh, what is he, what a grand man he is, what is this?" And another person is looking at what his intellectual capacity is, what is his inner reality, etc. So one saw the cause, and the other saw the effect. One saw the cause (*'illat*), one saw the effect (*ma'lul*). One investigated the reason, and one looked at the outward form. The one who was looking at the outward form, he, in terms of grade, fell behind. And the one who was looking at the inner reality, he moved forward. The first *munba'ith*, who saw the inner reality, "Why did this happen? Oh, this is the reason." What is it? Now both of them, who had risen by seven degrees, so at that moment they did, "Hey, we were created like this, and this is the reason he was created, but there is our third God." Both of them acknowledged this. Now see where the difference comes. Then both of them thought that the state he is in has changed, so his state is better than ours, and he obeyed first, the *Tawhid* of God, so he is greater than us. They accepted the First Intellect. Here, the matter of acceptance is that our elders have been appointed, we have to accept them. There, they accepted the First Intellect. Who? The first and second *munba'ith*. Now the two who had risen, one attained the inner reality, he got the inner matter. The other was looking at the outward. So the one who was looking at the outward, his duty was to obey the one who knew the inner reality. "Hey, you have attained the inner reality. Now you, we will follow you." That did not happen. This one thought, the second *munba'ith*, that you and I are equal. The illusion of equality (*wahm-e-masawat*). *Wahm* (illusion), you study *wahm* now, you teach it to children, that *wahm* is a bad thing. So what is *wahm*? This is the illusion of equality. When two people rise, and one is put ahead, they think this is what it means. So the two had risen, and the illusion of equality happened, the illusion happened, and this became wrong. And from there, what happened? That the thing which the First Intellect, in the spiritual world, the substance that was flowing from *Mubdi' Subhanahu*, it came only to the First Intellect. So what happened? That the First Intellect, who was the first being, who was the most virtuous, the greatest, the biggest, he became a veil (*hijab*) between *Mubdi' Subhanahu* and them, and this entire world. A veil, a curtain. See, the discourse on veils will come later. They became veils. Now, no one can see him, you can't see the *da'i* saheb until the *ma'dhun* allows. A veil has come. This is the veil. Who? The First Intellect. Now no one can attain *Mubdi' Subhanahu*, neither can our hearts attain Him, nor can He enter our minds. Now leave it, it's over. Just have to affirm that there is our Creator. And whom should we look at? So this is what... today, in the morning's lesson, it was said that "*tafakkaru fi 'l-makhluq, wa la tafakkaru fi 'l-khaliq*." (Reflect on the creation, and do not reflect on the Creator.) That reflect on the creation, about the Creator, that is, about *Mubdi' Subhanahu*, you do not need to think. This is the Prophet's saying. So what happened? That there, two kinds of *shirk* (polytheism) occurred. That the first *munba'ith*, they were virtuous, and the second *munba'ith*, they made two mistakes. They accepted God, that is, they established the *Tawhid* of *Rabb al-'Alamin*, and after that, they accepted the First Intellect, but they did not accept their companion. And by not accepting their companion, they became completely static. They remained there. Their rank remained there. The rank became static, rigidity (*jamud*) came, a halt came, they stopped. And this first *munba'ith*, they started receiving support (*ta'idat*). When they started receiving it, that was a world of freedom. The spiritual world, the *khuksani* world, there was neither time nor space. In such a world, whatever *hudud* (ranks) there were... now what should have happened? That all should have risen together. The two who had risen, and all should have risen. But the discourse that is given is this: that seven intellects advanced. Seven intellects, or seven Imams. Intellect means the one who is the leader, behind him the circle forms. And in the domain of each one, there were countless creatures, countless essences, who were behind him. So the third intellect occurred, the fourth intellect, the fifth, the sixth, the seventh. Seven intellects advanced. During which time they remained in thought. This second *munba'ith* is thinking, "Hey, what is this? I am just looking at him, amazed. What thing has... what is his... luminous state?" he is looking. At that time, these seven intellects advanced. Because it was a world of freedom. When seven intellects advanced and in the domain of each one... the discourse of each one is that in the domain of each one there were countless essences. Their number, no one knows except God, in which circle how many essences there were. As each one... the circle was moving, the seventh intellect, when it advanced, at that moment he realized, "Hey, where have I been left behind?" Who? The second *munba'e*. He was considering himself equal. The second *munba'e*. Who is also the second intellect. And he is the second *munba'e*. He realized at that moment, "I have made some mistake. I have made some mistake, for which I am being punished." Now what to do? The seven intellects have advanced. Now the first *munba'e* cannot be reached. The First Intellect cannot be reached. So one must go through the proper channel. So the nine intellects that were formed, that is, the First Intellect, and the first *munba'e*, so two, and then seven intellects advanced. This makes a total of nine. So the ninth intellect, you see, it's a circle. In the circle, one by one, points are placed, and there are thousands and lakhs of people in it, that is, the intellects are there, and souls (*nufus*) are there, and in it... the shape of the circle is for this reason, with us, the most perfect shape is that whatever is in the circle, it touches everyone. It's round everywhere, in a plate, this is... so the round shape is for this reason that it is the most perfect form in geometry. So that circle, when one circle was formed, two, three, seven, and nine circles were formed, then from the last individual of the ninth circle, he became higher than them. When he became higher, then he realized that "I need to... what's the matter? Why have I been left behind?" So from the last individual of the ninth intellect, he inquired, "Hey, what is my mistake, due to which I have been left behind?" It should have been that he would be the First Intellect, or the second intellect. Now here, the seven intellects have advanced. So his request was conveyed upwards. See, in the circle, it is free. It's not like what happens here, like in the military, your request goes and it takes years to go up. First, it has to go from the clerk's side. At the inward and outward, there are checkpoints, from there it goes up. But there, what happened? In the ninth circle, in the eighth, seventh, sixth, going through this, it reached the circle of the first *munba'e*. And there, after reaching, it was asked, "What is his mistake?" He said that he was afflicted with the illusion of equality. "I and he are equal." And this is his mistake. From there, the answer came down from above. When he was told this answer, he said, "I have understood my mistake. *Taba wa anaba*. I repent and I return." The moment he said it, he repented. Now see, here, a special thing happened in the spiritual world. And this is something to understand. They, for this reason, are called the Adam of the spiritual world. Just as Adam here made a mistake and he repented, so in the same way, the Adam of the physical world was... there, the Adam of the spiritual world was this being of yours, who... he at once obeyed and said, "I have made a mistake. *Taba wa anaba*." Now the question was that there, each one had their own circle. So you should also have a circle. His circle, the *du'at* say, was the largest of all. If you count by size, then the largest circle was his. But by chance, he was left behind. So the largest circle, meaning countless essences, were with him. Now with that, when he obeyed, when the leader accepted, then it was necessary for everyone to accept. But it happened that it did not. The fact that has happened is this, that each one started saying, "*La nasma'u wa la nuti'.*" (We will not listen and we will not obey.) This cry was raised in the heavens, that "*La nasma'u wa la nuti'.*" Neither do we listen to your word, nor do we obey your word. Why? Because the mistake was made by you. If you hadn't made the mistake, we would have been in the second circle. Now we have to accept that you are in the ninth circle. "*La nasma'u wa la nuti'.*" This is the discourse. They stopped. Now, when they stopped, it is always the case that in a gathering where a matter is presented, a movement arises, some people accept, some don't accept, and some are in a state of hesitation, and people are sitting. So those who accepted, they immediately accepted. "Alright, a hundred people are there, out of a hundred, fifty people said, we accept." So those who accepted, how were they? *Tabu bi-tawbatihi.* (They repented with his repentance.) They repented with his repentance. "You repented, we also repent, that's it." They repented at that very moment. So this first group that emerged there, in the spiritual world, and with him... with his companions... they were the first to become the Tenth Intellect. Understand? That after the ninth intellect, God Almighty placed them at the tenth number, because the seven circles were already formed. Nine circles were understood. They became the Tenth Intellect, and the people who were with them, which was only about 15 percent... it's just an estimation I'm giving... that 15 percent of the people, they were the ones who repented with his repentance, and they also repented. They are the most virtuous people. The ones who are in the heavens, they are the ones who got through. After that... they... as happens with us, there has been a custom that if something... a movement happens, some people accept immediately. Some need to be persuaded. The rest of the people, "Oh, he made a mistake." So "I have accepted, you also accept." When they were invited, when it was said like this, then in that, 30 percent more came out. "Alright, now you are saying it, we also accept." So those who accepted on being spoken to, they are the *labbu 'l-nida'* (those who answered the call). Their call was answered. Those who answered the call, they were 30 percent more who joined. "Alright, we also accept." They are of a lower rank than them. *Tabu bi-tawbatihi* is of a higher rank. *Labbu 'l-nida'* is because they needed to be spoken to, they needed to be invited. And all of them, in which there was *la nasma'u wa la nuti'*, we don't accept. In this, a group of another 10 to 15 percent emerged, and they are the group of *Kallamam*. *Kallamam*, remember these names. The first is *Tabu bi-tawbatihi*, the second is *Labbu 'l-nida'*, the third is *Kallamam*. *Kallamam* means *kalamhin min al-'ayn* (like the twinkling of an eye). In the time it takes for an eyelid to blink, that much delay there was. That is... the *du'at* say that we are saying this because we are in the world of duration (*zamani*), otherwise there was neither time nor a moment. A slight heedlessness occurred. "Oh, hey, we should also accept." So the 15 percent who were left, they said, "Oh, when they were persuading, at that very moment... just a little, they hesitated, and then they said, okay, we also accept." They are called *Kallamam*. *Kallamam* means... from *lamha*, the root is the same. *Kalamhin min al-'ayn*. Just a slight glance, and they said, "Okay, we also accept." They, the *du'at* say, this third group that was formed, they are our Imams of today. All the *Imam-e-mustaqarr* who are coming, from Mawlana Hasan to here... they are... these are the *Kallamam*. This discourse, when it comes... our discourse on Hajj will come, the discourse on *nikah* will come, and other things, at that time, remember this. The group of *Kallamam* was there. Just a slight... with a glance, they... there was a slight mistake. So they are our *Imam-e-mustaqarr* of today's era, in whose line there are... Mawlana Hasan, etc., who come, they are the *Kallamam*. They also rose there. Now after this, whatever was left, it was the stubborn group. All the people who were there were disobedient. And we are also included in them. And what were they saying? *La nasma'u wa la nuti'*. *La nasma'u wa la nuti'*. The invitation that is... is happening, the tenth invitation is happening, and we are not accepting. When they didn't accept, then... one period passed. Then God Almighty... now who is God Almighty? God Almighty has veiled Himself with the First Intellect. What did the First Intellect do? He made the first *munba'e* a veil. Now all the administration is run by the first *munba'e*. And his support is being given by the Tenth Intellect. So the Tenth Intellect gave the command, "Hey, what is this? The crowd that is... you know, it's like where there is a cloth, if a little bit of it gets soaked, it sinks. The rest of the cloth remains on the surface." So in the same way, "Hey, now they are not accepting. As much as we tried, it's done." Now, they are not listening. Now what to do? "Throw them out of here." So God's command from above, the message that came, was that "*Man kasara 'azmuhu fa-'alayhi jabruhu*." (He who breaks a bone, upon him is its mending.) "He who breaks the bone, the cast will be put on him, that is, he will have to get it fixed." You broke it, you have to fix it. Whose mistake was it? It was the Tenth Intellect's. "You did not accept at first, that is why they have been left behind. Now you have to reform them." As happens with us, "Hey, some students have become like this," then, "Alright, now you... this whole class, you have to reform them." In the same way, the command came that "*Man kasara 'azmuhu... fa-'alayhi jabruhu*." The one who breaks the bone, upon him is its mending. Now the administration of the Tenth Intellect started from here. That is why we say what? He is the administrator (*mudabbir*) of the whole and the great administrator. Now see, *mudabbir*... I'm using the term again. He is our *Khaliq* (Creator). The *Khaliq* is, there is a thing, there are luminous essences, from them now they have to be reshaped. *Mubdi' Subhanahu* was the one who brought existence from non-existence. Now it has been changed here. The God *Subhanahu* who was, our... to whom prostration is made, he is *Mubdi' Subhanahu*. This is the *Khaliq* who is... us... is making us. From the luminous world, he has brought us into the physical world so that we, here, by stooping, can go back from here. So in order to go... he is our *Khaliq*. Now his... the planning began. They are called *mudabbir*. The administration began. The Tenth Intellect... [unclear] I am sorry. The planning of the Tenth Intellect began.

[someone says something about a fan]

So now... he said, "Now, invite them." The invitation began to happen, but they said, "*La nasma'u wa la nuti'. La nasma'u wa la nuti'.*" So, they... to change them, there, what happened? By God's plan, all the essences that there were, they are called, in the prayer, the disobedient group (*nafarman tola*). This whole... the 50-65 percent that was left, they are all the disobedient group. This whole world that you see consists of... where you see the mountain, this, all these things are in it. Humans are there, animals are there, plants are there, all these things. This disobedient group, what was done to them? That there, in them, now they have to be sent to another place, so how to do it? They are luminous essences. How to reduce their light? Now, from within them, the luminosity started to decrease. Darkness (*zulmat*) began to be created. The absence of light (*'adam-e-nur*) is darkness. Where there is no light, there is darkness. So the light started to decrease. When the light started to decrease, a luminous essence... God Almighty created it, and in it the light is decreasing, so will it shake or not shake? Now each one is worried. "Hey, what is this?" Darkness is spreading over us. What is this darkness? It is the absence of light. When this happened, then, the *du'at* say, there are very long chapters on this, I'm telling it in brief, that first, inwardly, darkness was created in them, gloom was created. And after that, then... the density... outwardly also it started to come. When it started to come, and they started to become dense, or... the light started to decrease, then they became worried. And the *du'at* say that there, terror (*wahshat*) was created. Just as where there is darkness with us, terror is created. In terror, what did they do? "Hey, where should we go?" They made a leap (*jast*). The *du'at* say, *jast*. Sayyidna Muayyad says, *Lamza-e-jastiyyah*. There, they didn't understand. See, you are sitting here, right? The lights are all on. Suddenly the light goes off. And all the children are sitting. So what will they do? All the children will run towards the door. They will run. "Hey, there is light outside, let's go." In the same way, there, they got scared...

...the essences, there were no other creatures. So these were... these were thrown. When these three were formed inwardly and after that, in the world of density, when they came, then they were no longer fit to live there. Now... by God's plan, it is now over. Now these... the density has come into them, they don't need to be kept in the world. Throw them. So how to throw them? Where to throw them? If there is a place, then throw them. Where they are being thrown, how is... with what thing are they being thrown? Where are they being thrown? So everything, the pot's clay itself, the pot itself, everything is made from these very things. So when they were thrown into the world, that is, below the all-encompassing sphere (*falak-e-muhit*), they were cast down, and how were they thrown? That each one was given... that is, it was distributed, by the eternal divider (*khasim-e-azal*), each one was given what appeared to be its due. That each one, according to its capacity, was given... here, what happened? That now, throw them. Now throw them. The moment the command was given to throw them, at that moment, and it started to be executed, at that moment, one group hesitated. There were 65 percent. "Oh, now we are being thrown out. Now let's accept. Let's accept." At that moment, those who accepted, they are called *nadim*. One of the three from above has happened: *Tabu bi-tawbatihi*, *labbu 'l-nida'*, and *kallamam*. When... what was happening there? The command was given, it was not executed. Here, with the command, they are starting to be thrown. At that moment, a group got up and said, "We accept." They are regretful (*nadim*). Who is *nadim*? The one who makes a mistake is *nadim*. "We made a mistake, we accept." So regret (*nadamat*), is the best thing. If we have also committed a fault, then in the presence of God, we should become regretful. This is the best thing. So the regret that happened to us, that's why we have become Sulaymani. We became Sulaymani for this very reason. The group of the regretful was formed. So what was it? "We accept." Now, in the 65 percent, see, the same cry was being raised, *la nasma'u wa la nuti'*. So those who were accepting, they were called *nadimin*. The second group, what was it? Absolutely stubborn. They said, "We will not accept, whoever he may be." What has happened, has happened, we will not accept. *La nasma'u wa la nuti'*. That stubborn group, they are called *musirr*. Today, if we insist on something, we say, "You are very insistent on this matter." So *musirr*. *Musirr* and *mustakbir*. *Mustakbir* is one who shows arrogance. "Go away, we won't accept." He rejected the First Intellect. So there, this started to happen, that "we don't accept." So one group is showing regret, one group is completely... showing arrogance. They are called *mustakbir*. And another, what was it? *Nadim* and *mustaghfir*. One who seeks forgiveness. And the other is *musirr* and *mustakbir*. Now where these two groups happen, here, you see, a movement starts, or there is a party or some such thing, one group is those who accept, one who don't accept. Now a third group emerges in between. "What to do? Should we support them or support them?" They don't understand what we should do. So one, in between, there is a faction that emerges, which is hesitant, they are in a state of confusion, that they don't understand whether they are speaking the truth or they are. "We will remain silent." So those who were confused, they were bewildered (*mutahayyir*). They were in bewilderment. They didn't understand what to do. *Mutahayyir* and *shakk*. Those who were in doubt, *shakk* and *mutahayyir*. So this, the one in the middle... the middle faction. These three factions descended from there and they... by its decree, they were thrown into the lower world. So the one who... was distributed by the eternal divider, each one was given what appeared to be its due. Today, the world that you see, the high essences, the high stars you see, the shining world you see, that all is the faction of the *nadimin*. And here, those who are living in the world, besides us, that is, the earth, the plants, they are all... they are from the *musirr* and *mustakbir*. And for some, in such an era when we are in the era of concealment (*sitr*). And the other, the one in the middle, the *mutahayyirin*, they were placed in the air. Between... इसका बयान Inshallah tomorrow.

*Tadrij* (gradation) means the attribute of divinity, that to create from non-existence to existence, this power, besides God Almighty, is in no one. This is the thing, the *khaliq*'s thing is that there is some substance, from which... there is flour, so bread can be made. There is a thing... there is thread, so cloth can be made from it. This is all called creation (*khilqat*), it is a creator, but the attribute of divinity, which is to bring from non-existence to existence, this is with no one besides God Almighty. There, they separated the attribute of divinity, by separating, by doing *tajrid*, that besides God, that is, besides *Mubdi' Subhanahu*, no one has the power. Everyone is a *qadir*, a *ra'uf*, all are there, but the power to create is in no one besides *Mubdi' Subhanahu*. So to do *tajrid*, to separate everyone, every essence, all the creatures that were there, to separate them all from that and set them aside, besides God. *La sharika lahu*. That no one is a partner to that being except God. We, every... in the prayer, at many places we have to read *La sharika lahu*. There has been a lot of discussion about *shirk*, etc., and this discourse will come again, Inshallah.

...when in *namaz*, you stand for *namaz*, you say *Lillahi Ta'ala*. So *Bismillah*, I begin in the name of God... So in the name of God, for whom... whom are we calling God? In the name of God. If the name came, you said there is a dependency, if there is a dependency, then that is... you... it's *shirk*. But you... in your place, the knowledge that has been given, the understanding that has been given to a person, so in his mind, it is clear which thing is to be used at which time. You, when you are reading another subject, for the subject, whatever things there are, what is Mawlana Ali's rank, what is Janab-e-Husayn's, what is Janab-e-Hasan's, etc. But when you are standing for God, that is, for *namaz*, then it is only *Al-'Ibadatu lillah* (Worship is for God). The worship is for God. Now, in your mind... what happens? Somewhere, you are... when you are giving a mathematics paper, is geography not in your mind? It is there, isn't it? Everything is in your mind, but at that moment, you have to do what is to be done. So in the same way, the worship is for God alone. Now its meaning, to understand it, when you are sitting at leisure, is that "in the name of God." Why "in the name of God"? Why *Bismillah*? Why not start with "Allah"? You should ask that. *Bismillah*, why? *Bi-ism-illah*. "In the name of God," I begin. So see, "in the name of God" means that a name is your identity. Your name, until it is known, there is no identity of who it is. So to recognize God, one must recognize the First Intellect. So the greatest name of God is the being of the First Intellect. So the *ism* (name) is the being of the First Intellect. "In the name of God," I begin. And we are prostrating to *Mubdi' Subhanahu*. All the prayers, the attributed ones, the postures of *ruku'* are different, the postures of *sajdah* are different, the two *rak'at* prayers are different, the four *rak'at* prayers are different, all these things, you have to understand the exegesis (*ta'wil*). When you sit down... and a person becomes learned in it, and then the realities (*haqa'iq*). *Ta'wil* is a different matter, then the realities, when a person becomes that learned, learns it, then the realities come to him. What thing is what. There is a difference between *ta'wil* and *haqa'iq*. *Ta'wil* is that this microphone is like that. You compared the two, that this is like that. What is this microphone? It is a piece of iron that has become this. What is this? This is the reality (*haqiqat*). Reality. And here, what is it? The very realities are the ones given to us. The books of realities are taught later, after the *ta'wil* is done. Now this subject of ours that is going on, where we are, it is a subject of *ta'wil*, mostly. The realities, some things are that... they are very... Mawlana Ali is... Janab-e-Payghambar is... what are their ranks? What are their titles? What are these things, you will come to know. That he is this, Ali is this, the Prophet is this, their veils are this. Many things are left to be known. Up to the time of *da'im-e-sitr* (the concealed Imam). So, this, Inshallah, will be revealed. Now we are in the discourse of *Tawhid*, and Inshallah, in the future, the discourse of the ranks, the discourse of the caliphate (*khilafat*), the discourse of the *du'at*, the discourse of the Imams, the discourse of the *hujjat*s, there are many such discourses, that one by one, these things will be revealed. First, let the alphabet be done, then we will come to the word. So, Inshallah, it will happen, but it is being said that the attributed prayers, if you all remember them, then we... see, in the time of the previous *da'i*, it used to be like this. The attributed prayers... now people are sitting with the prayer book. When? On the night of *Qadr*. "Oh, this is wrong. Oh, this is a mistake, this is a mistake." What is this? The attributed prayer is not to be read like this. The attributed prayer is for your understanding. Mawlana is there, the meaning is understood, it's over. You have to pray your *namaz* for God. The timings will also be told, Inshallah, of *Laylat al-Qadr*. From this, it is told that for whom each one is. But it is not known. One, where the *hudud* sit, and the *muhaddithin* and *ma'dhun* sit, I will also tell you that the 17th night's prayer, which we read, of *Tawhid*, for whom it is read. And each one's... in the same way, *Laylat al-Qadr* and the *tasbih-e-lazim* of so-and-so, for whom it is, the *du'at* have specified this. But that is for awareness, it is not that the *namaz* is to be offered for them. We are not offering *namaz* for Janab-e-Ali. It's not like that. We are doing it for *Mubdi' Subhanahu*. It is only for God that the prostration is done. Prostration is not permissible for anyone. There are many such... Sayyidna Malik, Sayyidna Muqaddas Ali bin Husayn, may God sanctify their spirits, when I, 28 years ago... when I went for Hajj, I was 26 years old at that time. At that time, I asked many questions to Sayyidna Malik. I used to ask at such a time when people... he used to say before, "Hey, the visa... you will not get a visa for Najaf." And this, so and so, and these are Gujarati and they are going back, the Hajj is over. "You go there, you know Arabic," "Get a visa from Sayyidna Sahab." Various people, the Pakistanis were there, and the other one, and different people, I took all of them... I went there. Now I used to consider it a great opportunity that if Sayyidna Sahab is free, I should ask something else. I used to go and ask at the very first opportunity. Sayyidna Sahab never... he used to give the answer. He was a great personality, a high personality. When to ask the question, even that we did not know. And when to give the answer, he knew. He was a man of high station. Now, such an occasion happened that after that... there was an occasion of a conference. From there... I had gone from Karachi, but there... the situation of the passports was that the Hajjis could not go to Najaf. Mecca, Medina, and Jeddah, they can go. No one can go, except for the Hajjis. There was a lot of restriction. Sayyidna Muqaddas tried so hard, I was there, the late... Sahab's son was there, and we three or four were going. But the visa was only mine because I was new to learning. He tried very hard, daily going to the Ministry of Interior and coming from the Ministry of Foreign Affairs, but the visa was not granted. Finally, when he got tired... what to do with the servant of God? He said, "Let's go, Nasrullah, we'll go and stay there," in Mecca. When he got tired, two months had passed. I had... I had put my job on the line. I had taken leave for one month from Kuwait, and two and a half months had passed. I stayed there. I said, "Now what to do?" And after that, Sayyidna... I got the news of Sayyidna Sahab's death. I was there, and Sayyidna Sahab was there, and in it, I got the permission to go to Yemen. When I was sitting there, Sayyidna Sahab's... was free from politics. He was sitting. The book that was open, I did not see it. We were young, we used to sit at the back. We were just reading the lesson. I went and sat next to Sayyidna. First, I didn't sit. Sayyidna Sahab asked, "Did the boy from Kuwait not come?" First, they had given permission, and Sayyidna Jalali Sahab had also said. But on the occasion, they had to ask again. He asked, "Yes." I said, "Yes." I at once went inside and went and sat next to Sayyidna Sahab. Now, the discourse that was happening, it was about the attributed prayers, and I used to ask the same question, that if the prayer is for so-and-so, then the *namaz* is for God alone, what is this? He said, "Hey, wait, the answer to your question will come." He didn't give it at that time. Now Hazrat... Sayyidna Jalali Sahab is wondering, "Hey, when will this boy ask the question?" When to ask the question? So we used to ask the questions beforehand, but the answer would be given on the occasion, by Sayyidna Sahab. This is... the ways of the elders are... the ways.

...that a white board or something... If there is a white board, then... ...There is a place for sitting in the mosque below. ...There is no light there... ...The arrangement is to change it. ...If you sit here, it's a bit better. ...If you want to move the fan, move it. ...Let's keep the fan on, a bit. [Background chatter about seating and fan arrangements]

Yesterday, there was a mention of the world of intellects (*'alam-e-'aql*) and the world of the soul (*'alam-e-ruh*, spiritual). That the expanse of the spiritual world is from the First Intellect (*'aql-e-awwal*) to the Tenth Intellect (*'aql-e-'ashir*). This is called the spiritual world. And if we go below that, that is, after *Tabu bi-tawbatihi* and *Labbu 'l-nida'*, then the world of life (*'alam-e-hayat*) begins. As I told you yesterday, the spiritual world is a separate thing, then there is the world of life, then the world of the body (*'alam-e-jibra'il*), and then the world of the physical body (*'alam-e-jism*) in which we are sitting. So it's not that we think there's a spiritual world and a physical world. That's not it, there are three or four levels, and different things are contained in them.

Now, have a little conception, you see, of the spiritual world, which is the Tenth Intellect. The moment he repented, when he was told that his mistake was this, he at once repented. *Taba wa anaba*. His mistake, it was pointed out to him that your illusion (*wahm*) and supposition (*guman*) was that I and you, that is, my companion, are equal, that is, the first and second *munba'ith* are equal, and for this reason, you... and you, were considered at fault and you stopped. When he accepted this, he repented at that very moment, *taba wa anaba*, and reached the station of the Tenth Intellect. These are the ten intellects. And with every intellect, with every intellect, there are countless essences (*zawat*). And the number of their army, no one knows except God. How many are with the seventh intellect, how many with the third, how many with the fourth. Only one sketch is given to us by the First Intellect, that is the circle of oneness (*da'irah wahdah*). He is a single individual. There, the circle is such that there are no individuals. The first *munba'ith*, there too the circle is contracted. He is alone, at the end of the end. And after that, the seven intellects that have formed, there are countless essences in them, and no one knows their number.

But it is stated that the largest of them, the group, the disobedient one, was with the Tenth Intellect. And in it, I had divided it, that *Tabu bi-tawbatihi*, the one who at that very moment, with his repentance, they also repented. *Tabu bi-tawbatihi*, that is, with the repentance of the Tenth Intellect, a group, meaning the entire army of 100%, out of that, 15% came out and after that, the *labbu 'l-nida'* came out. *Labbu 'l-nida'* and this have a difference, you see, that *tabu bi-tawbatihi* are those who saw him and did it. And *labbu 'l-nida'* are those who were invited and then they separated. And the third are the *kallamam*, who had a delay of the twinkling of an eye. A *twinkling of the eye*, there was a delay, and for that reason they were called *kallamam*. And in the future, when the chapters of doctrines open, in the very end, there are the stations of the *Imam-e-mustauda'* and the *Imam-e-mustaqarr*. What are the stations of the *Imam-e-mustaqarr*? There, all the *Imam-e-mustaqarr* are from the group of *kallamam*. There are *mustauda'* also, but the *Imam-e-mustaqarr* are, and I am not going into its details.

Now, the Tenth Intellect is the Adam of the spiritual world. And with them, at the time when *la nasma'u wa la nuti'* (we will not listen and we will not obey) was being cried out, Hazrat... Muqaddas says that there, one... the one who initiated it. The one who incited. When there is a commotion, there is one inciter for all the people. And he said, "Hey, don't accept." He who incited is Iblis, the spiritual Iblis. And the one who gave him the satanic quality (*shaytaniyyat*), the one who did the work of inciting, is Shaytan. So Shaytan and Iblis are separate. In this regard, Iblis is the one who, out of ignorance, said the word. And Shaytan is the one who is a rebellious instigator. And in that regard, in the future, when we go, with us, in the era of Muhammad, the first caliph is in the rank of Iblis, and the second caliph is the inciter, he is the real one, and he is Shaytan. The translation in books comes that Iblis is in the rank of the first, and the second is Shaytan.

Anyway, now, there, what happened? That Adam of the spiritual world, they repented, and with them, three groups came out. Now it happened that on God's part, as I told you, in them, density (*kathafat*) and darkness (*zulmat*) began to spread. So, with the first leap (*jast*), length (*lamba'i*) came into them. When length came, they became more terrified, and in the opposite direction, they ran. When they ran in the opposite direction, width (*'ard*) was created in them, breadth was created. And when they made the third leap, depth (*'umq*) came into them. When these three dimensions came, at that moment, they were not fit to live there, in the spiritual world. The command was given to them, "*Ahbitu*." (Descend!) That is, "fall down, go down." Just like the word from the Quran, "*Ahbitu*." When they were thrown, at that moment, one individual in it at once... agreed to accept. And with him, twelve separated and twenty-seven separated. Twelve and twenty-seven. He who is, he is Adam the Lesser (*Adam-e-Bida'*). And with him, twelve are the *naqib*s and twenty-seven are the *hujjat*s. These individuals also remained there, in the world of life, or rather, they went higher. So now, you see, if there was a report of the world of life, then I would have told you, that the map of the world of life is... where is this world of life? It is below the world of intellects. Below the Tenth Intellect. Now, there was no mention of "below" and "above" there, but to explain, one has to say this, that the world of life is below it. So in the world of life, what is there? There is the Spiritual Pillar (*Ta'ud al-Ruhani*), that is, the Spiritual Kaaba (*Ka'batullah al-Ruhani*), which we call the center (*markaz*), which we call the heart (*qalb*), that is the world of life. Center. And near it, there is the Pillar of Light (*'Umud al-Nur*). *'Umud* is what they call a pillar. Pillar. Now, all these essences... you see, the degree is decreasing. Now, at the time when this *la nasma'u wa la nuti'* was being cried out, and a disturbance was being created in the intentions of the people, *labbu 'l-nida'* and *kallamam* etc. had all gone up, after that, they were ordered to be thrown. You see, with the command of *ahbitu*, those who went up, they were the twenty-seven, twelve, and one. And after that, the one who... the whole rest of the group, at that time, with the execution of the command of *ahbitu*... when it was being executed upon them, at that time, a group agreed to accept. And they started to show regret, "Oh, we should have accepted." We accept. Our mistake. That regret, but what happened? The *du'at* say that they said this at a time when the execution had begun. They were being thrown out. They were not fit to live there. They are called the regretful and the forgiveness-seekers (*nadim wa mustaghfir*). And after that, the second, as was told, is the group of the insistent and the arrogant (*musirr wa mustakbir*). They were absolutely stubborn, "We will not accept. You go, do whatever you want." And the third, a group that was in the middle, it was the group of the doubtful and the bewildered (*shakk wa mutahayyir*). They were in doubt, "Hey, this group is saying this, the regretful ones, are they right or are they right? Whom should we support?" So now, the whole world, all the creation that we see, if you... as you look, there is a separate world of plants, a separate world of animals, which they call the animal world, or there is the world of minerals, and of humans. But if an 'alim, an 'alim looks, he will divide it this way, that the doubtful and bewildered are from the *musirr* and *mustakbir*, and these are the *nadimin*. So now, those from above, who are being thrown, the division was made by the eternal divider. On that day, the division was made on that day by the eternal divider. Each one was given what appeared to be its due. Each one was given according to its intention. And the *nadim*, they were of a high rank. They had accepted. Meaning, we Sulaymanis are from them. But in which group we are, that will come later. So we, who are the *nadimin*, they were given a place in the stars. In the world of *jirmani*. See, now two levels are left, two stages were mentioned. The world of life has happened, the world of the spiritual, that is, the higher intellect has happened. The world of *jirmani*, that will be called the area from the all-encompassing sphere to the surface of the moon. The moon is the final limit. *Qamar*. And above it are the seven planets. Above the planets is the sphere of constellations (*falak-e-buruj*). Above the sphere of constellations is the all-encompassing sphere (*falak-e-muhit*). And the all-encompassing sphere is the final limit of the physical. And above it is the world of life. So now, should this be called physical or *jirmani*? Its sections have been made again. That from the surface of the moon to the all-encompassing sphere, this is the world of *jirmani*. *Jirmani* because *jirm* is what they call... gems shine. Just as these are shining ones, meaning, the stars that are placed in the sky. So the *nadimin*, all of them... now see, the *nadimin*, all of them, from them this world of stars was made. What is this... when the creation was beginning. It has begun. These *nadimin*, from them the stars... above, the entire sky, the space, was made. And this lower part (*safrah*), that is, where we... what we call the earth, if we go to the very core of the earth, where its center is, there, up to there, the solids (*salabat*), that is, the rocks and whatever... diamond is there, these are all the things, this *safrah* is made from the *musirr* and *mustakbir*. All... the lower earth. The first earth and below it. This is from the *musirr* and *mustakbir*. Now the *shakk wa mutahayyirin*, from the sphere of the moon, here, up to the surface of the earth, they were placed. And how were they placed? Above, there is the sphere of fire, the sphere of air, the sphere of water, and the sphere of earth. In these, all the *shakk wa mutahayyirin* were shut in. They were placed. Now see that the *nadimin*, their intention, according to that, they were made shining. And the *musirr* and *mustakbir*, you see them, they are rocks and... solid, dark things. And the *shakk wa mutahayyirin*, who were in doubt, "Should we go here or there?" they were placed in the four spheres. Now you see, fire... the Messenger of God said, fire burns upwards. That is the nature of doubt. A person in doubt is consumed. He's always in turmoil. "Should I go there or here?" A state of anxiety, a state of doubt. So it is in fire. It is in air, with the cyclones, etc. It is in water, with the waves that rise. And here, with the dust storms that rise. These four spheres, in them, the *shakk wa mutahayyirin* consist. Now, from this, a change has come. As the world progresses further, a slight composition has taken place. As the beginning took place, I'm just giving a brief sketch. Now from there, when they were thrown... so what happened? The sun has its own station, the moon has its own station, Venus has its, Mercury has its, all these... the Great Administrator... now see, the Great Administrator is, as I'm telling you, it's like a master of a factory. The master has not even seen the factory. He is sitting in his own place. He is sitting. Who is running it? The General Manager is running it. So... the General Manager has also become so high that he is always in meetings, and he has appointed a manager. The manager sahib is also sitting in his office, and he has appointed someone as the in-charge. In the same way, here, the matter of the Tenth Intellect happened. The Tenth, whom we are calling, he has become elevated, and below him, his subordinate, the last individual of the *labbu 'l-nida'*, he is now the administrator of the world. And after that, there are other... they have gone further. Now the discourse is that the Adam of creation, who is in fact the Tenth Intellect. Now his plan is working. How to... "*Man kasara 'azmuhu fa-'alayhi jabruhu*." (He who breaks a bone, upon him is its mending.) The one who breaks the bone, he has to fix the bone. God Almighty's command was that you have broken everyone. Now you have to reform everyone. Now, to reform, the whole world has been created. For us... for our sake, crores of years have passed. But there, with God, there is no one day of the year. Not even a moment. And for years, the work is going on. Why? Because we, there, were luminous and *khuksani* essences. There, we made a mistake, that we also supported him. When the Tenth Intellect was not being accepted, we also did this. Now the *nadimin* are there, the *mutahayyirin* are there, or the *musirrin* are there, that is... a person himself should assess from which group he is, with that glasses, and then look at the whole world from that perspective, then you will understand what a Christian is, what a Parsi is, what a Hindu is, what we are, and those who are in the heavens, and on earth, the other things, the world of stars, from which group? Now see, this *nadimin*, we think this is one group. That's not it, it has fifteen groups. It has further sections. The *du'at* have explained this with great detail. In the book *Jam'u 'l-Muntazam*, when it is asked, it is explained. So there, one by one, the groups have been taken out. That the first group is this, the second is this, the third is this, the fourth is this, the whole fifteen are listed. And they... in the morning lesson, you would have read... the first *nadim* who is... In the morning's... the lesson we are reading, it has come that who is the first *nadim*? It's the all-encompassing sphere (*falak-e-muhit*).

The all-encompassing sphere is the most virtuous thing in the entire world. All the lower planets and also, below it, we who are there, that is, our... the believers are there, the infidels are there, the *ma'dhun* is there, the *da'i* is there, the *mukasir* is there, the *hujjat* is there, these are all the ranks. But the ranks that have been given in it, the first, second, third, all fifteen are explained in the lesson. Our work is not for them, it's a matter of the future. And after that, the *du'at* say that the *shakk wa mutahayyirin* that there are, their groups have been taken out. How many groups are there? Twenty groups. One, two, three, four, the numbers are written with the names. And the *musirr* and *mustakbir* that there are, they are divided into thirty-five groups. So 35, 20, and 15. This is, there are 70 groups. They are 70 groups. And Muqaddas has said that these 70... now you see, a *shakk wa mutahayyir*, if he is there, then in it, there will be one part which is the highest of all, and that will be the one who has the capacity to become *nadim*. The middle one. Or, say, the highest of the *musirr* and *mustakbir*, he will be similar to the lowest of the *mutahayyirin*. It will be like that, won't it? The three levels are there. So their highest will be that. So in this way, you take out a cream from within those 35, that which one is the highest of all, and is moving towards progress. And another, take out a cream from the *shakk wa mutahayyirin*. And another, take out a cream from the *nadimin*. So these three, the cream that will come out, so 70 and these three, 70... Now you see, the Prophet, the hadith he has given, and in the books of hadith it comes, and it is read out, that the Prophet's hadith, which is also... after the Holy Quran, is the third book, and it is not easily understood. Whoever reads the translation and thinks they have understood, that doesn't mean anything, its meaning is not there. The Prophet says, "After me, the community (*ummah*) will be divided into 73 sects." My community will be divided into 73 sects, and only one sect will be the one that attains salvation (*najat*). Now you tell me, today, have 73 sects been formed? You take it out and see, how many have been formed. Shia, Sunni, so-and-so, this... there are more than a hundred. So the Prophet's hadith, did you falsify it? What will you say? The Prophet's indication was not towards this, but these sects that are in the world, the 70 into which they are divided, their one-one... take out one-one... and they become three more, and 70 and three become 73. And in that, the correct, upper part of the *nadimin*, that is the one that will leave this world. It will have real salvation. What salvation we will get is that we will go one rank higher. One step higher. But the real one who will attain salvation, that is a matter of the future, and that is the most virtuous part of the *nadimin*. And what is that most virtuous part? It is the all-encompassing sphere. So the all-encompassing sphere... now you have understood that all the stars, the planets, it is below the seventh *qamar*, above the *qamar* is 'Utarid, above 'Utarid is Zuhra, above Zuhra is Shams, above Shams is Mirrikh, above Mirrikh is Mushtari, above Mushtari is Zuhal. These seven are called planets. And its... this is the solar system, and it is related to us. Today, science is discovering eight, nine stars, it has no relation with us. For us, what is related to the system of life, the system of *da'wah*, is these seven planets. These seven planets are effective. Now for 90 years... in the lessons of the *du'at*, it is explained... seven *da'im* prophets came, seven *mutaqq* prophets came, seven seas, seven this, seven that. In various places, the mention of seven has come, that is why it has been kept. So these are the seven planets. And above the seventh, Zuhal, there is a sphere of constellations (*falak-e-buruj*). The sphere of constellations is that which has been divided into 12 parts. And this is what we call the Zodiac. Those who read palms or their birth charts, etc., which month it is, when is your birth, based on that, the zodiac sign is taken out, all that is within the Zodiac. And that is... so its every section is a constellation (*burj*), and the whole sphere is called the sphere of constellations. This is the eighth from the bottom, the second from the top. And above this, at the very top, is the all-encompassing sphere which has covered everything. Now at the time when that all-encompassing sphere... by God's will, when the creation of the world was to take place, that is, the beginning, at that time, the all-encompassing sphere moved. It made one revolution. One revolution of it makes several revolutions of the inner ones happen. And the sphere of *qamar* revolves... and the *qamar* is *qamar*, what is the sphere? So... you see, *qamar* is a planet. The place where it revolves, the orbit, is called the sphere. The place from which it emerges and it revolves, that is the sphere. So we are talking in terms of the spheres (*aflak*). That from the all-encompassing sphere, down to here, there are nine spheres. And these are the nine months in which the creation of a child takes place. Seven are of the planets, and two are above. In nine months, the child is fully formed. This whole system is all God's. Everything is prescribed, a whole... in a sense, it's computerized. What will happen in the first month, what in the second, what in the third. Yesterday, I told you, and its exegesis, Inshallah, will come later and there will be more discourses on it. So anyway, this... the group of the *nadimin* is the one that was placed in the heavens.
