After that, in the spheres, the *mutahayyirin* were placed. And below it, this lower earth, or the solids that are there, in that, all the *musirrin* were placed. When this happened, then neither was there Adam, nor the progeny of Adam. No human had this form. All this was, in the form of a ball, it was thrown down, *ahbitu*. In the form of a ball, it came down. Now after coming here, then the forms of minerals, plants, animals, etc., were given to them. How were they given? That now, as I told in the morning's lesson, and in it, Inshallah, I will explain it further. Now just two or four things are about the world of life, that in the world of life, the Kaaba that is there, the Spiritual Kaaba, in it, these... when we were falling, that is, the *nadimin*, at that time, we showed regret, "Hey, we accept." So this regret was liked by God. It was pleasing. "They are regretful." You see, regret. The one who sins, he is regretful. So that regret, our... our leaven of regret. Leaven is what they call, that a small piece of it, you put it in the milk, and the whole thing becomes yogurt. The leaven, a small piece that was there, our leaven of regret, that leaven was deposited there, in the Spiritual Kaaba. Which is called *ta'ud*. *Ta'ud*, or the Spiritual Kaaba, or the center, or the heart, or the world of life, it was placed there. And next to it, there is another thing in the form of a pillar, where our rational souls (*nufus-e-natiqah*) are. All of us believers who are coming, our rational souls are attached there. To the Pillar of Light. Like a pillar... around it. Why? Because we had shown regret. That is why we have come as Sulaymani. We have come as children of Sulaymanis, as grandchildren, and this chain is continuing. And since when is it continuing? You have no idea, the moment your eyes close, then again, when they open, you will find yourself in the same place. But God Almighty knows that this chain is continuing. When will it end? When will the door close? When will the next door open? The whole account is clearly laid out. So see, our regret is there...

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-2002 lecture 2 -->

So we are better than others, and better than many. What does “many” mean? The *nāzimīn* are in a rank that is better than us. We are one of them. And which one are we? That is a separate matter, as to where our seat is, which of the fifteen we are, what our number is. So remember this, that all the groups in the world that have been formed – the Parsis and Hindus and all these that are mixed in it, the *mushrikīn* who are there, and the groups of *kuffār*, and the *ahl al-taslīs* – some are *mutāhirīn*, some belong to this, so wherever there are *mutāhirīn*, it is much better to take from them, because you can eat *ḥalāl* food from their hand. You can eat meat from their hand. This is what it is. If they are neighbors, then take care of them. This and that. All these various obligations come from this connection. In any case, it is that the *musirrīn*, who were the insistent ones, where the group was falling from above, at that time we came as per the will of God, this very thing happened, and from there, when we were thrown down, we were a *nūrānī* and *rūḥānī* *jamā'at*. We were completely pure, luminous, *ṣāfī*, and spiritual. From there we were thrown down, and just as a cannonball is launched with great force, and there are known things in artillery, that one has to shoot it… it was thrown. It fell from above, and after coming down here, we were scattered into different things. We were divided into different things. So now, you should look at the whole world from that perspective, that today this whole world is seeing that what is the matter that a tree grows, one tree of a certain type gives its fruit, and the other tree dies. Or there are animals, one animal dies, another starts giving milk. Everywhere, something or the other is happening. What is this thing after all? What system is at work? So if you reflect with a cool mind, you will realize that there is a superpower. There is a single being who is running this system. And that same one is the *Khāliq-i 'Ālam* (Creator of the Universe), the same one is the *Mudabbir-i 'Ālam* (Governor of the Universe), who is running all this. And a talk has already been held about this, that *'Aql-i 'Āshir* is our town planner, he is the one who created the entire world. And how did he do it? Where they were falling, there was an intention from them. When you make a building, any thing, first there is sketching, first its map is made. In their thought, first it came into thought and then it was drawn on paper. From there, where this matter came into the mind of the great one, that now… `man kasara 'aẓman fa 'alayhi jabruhu` (he who breaks a bone, it is upon him to mend it). That due to their mistake, all the people have to suffer, so a command came from God Almighty that you are the one who has spoiled them, and now you must… `man kasara 'aẓman fa 'alayhi jabruhu` (he who breaks a bone, it is upon him to mend it). The one who breaks the bone, it is incumbent upon him to join it. He thought, what planning should I do, in what way should I establish a system so that from here, the world turns back and then returns to the spiritual realm. So for this, in this, the intentions that were made, one of them was the first intention that the celestial spheres (*aflāk*) should be made to revolve. This revolution of the celestial sphere started the cycle. From that, the cycle of day and night began, and from that same, months and days and years were formed. The second thing is that this thought came to their mind, that *Lāhūt* and *Nāsūt* are two, two methods through which I will make man progress from here. The third thing is that man, who stands on two feet, as we are standing, this man will progress in the form of a human only, not as an animal or an angel. He is *Ashraf al-Makhlūqāt* (the noblest of creations), and in this very way we will move forward. This is the highest weapon. Among minerals, plants, and animals, he is called *ḥaywān-i nāṭiq* (the speaking animal). It is a branch of animal, but it is a speaking animal. In this way, the speaking animals that are seen all over the world, in comparison to them, we are the original *nāṭiq*. If they have received *nuṭq* (speech), then we have received *nāṭiqah* (the faculty of reason). Remember this, that however many are living as humanity, meaning they have been made human, they have… in comparison to animals, you see, the animal and the human, what is the difference? Nose, eye, ear, hand, foot, all these things, the parts of the body, are almost similar. There is a lack of one intellect. They have *quwwat-i tamīz* (the power of distinction), you have an excess of intellect. Now this, which is the thing that has been given to them, is that man has been given *nuṭq*. In comparison to an animal, a human has been given *nuṭq*. And *nuṭq* is now, you see, great scientists, great professors, they are... all people have been given *nuṭq*. And their intelligence, which is there, has been given based on intentions. They are very intelligent, they have done very great works, everyone in the world is doing them. But *nuṭq* has been given. No one has *nāṭiqah*, which is our thing. *Nāṭiqah* cannot be obtained by anyone until he comes into the *dā'irah* (circle) of *walāyah* (guardianship). This is that he should not become *halqa ba-gosh* (obedient) and accept the Imam of the time. So this, in whose destiny it is, from where it has come, he will come from there. If he comes from a different religion, he will leave that and come here. Many people have come to India, have come to you. And there are many who have left. They have turned back and left. Destiny, everyone has their own destiny. He was born in a Sulaymani home and left, went outside, into the home of a stranger. Girls have been brought, boys have also gone, everything has happened in every way. So what is this? The intentions we made in the world of spirits, the deeds that were done, the intentions that were made, there we have been appointed here. Anyway, it is that here now, from that perspective, you should see the world. This is a plant, this is an animal, this is all this. But this is the exoteric. In esoteric terms, according to rank, this is the plant section, this is the animal, this is what they see. But an *'ārif* sees it and says this is *shākir-i mutlaqīn*, this is *mustaqirrīn*, and this is *nāzimīn*. Whatever is higher, the stars that have been placed, the planets that have been placed, the suns and stars that have been made, the gems, that is what is called *nāzimīn* in esoteric terms. And all the spheres, the sphere of fire, one of water, of air, and of earth, that is, of soil, these four spheres, all the science of geology tells, the science of spheres tells, and this is what? It has been placed among the *shākir-i mutlaqīn*. And below it, the crust, which is the hard part of the earth, and below it, up to the Earth's core, there, all the *musirrīn* have been filled. Now when do they come out, when are they locked back in, this is like a prison, it is of this kind, and as it will be explained to you, which durations they have to serve, what kind of release they get, what their relationships are, where they have to go. In any case, the gaze of an *'ārif* sees this, that in these three parts, which things are divided. And *halāl* and *harām* is that very thing, that which is a nearby thing, that has come within human reach, it is valued, and the thing that has gone far away, its… just as we have, for *istinjā'*, use this thing, don't use that thing. So why? What calculation has been made for this? That all the Prophets who have brought a *dīn*, they have a system, they have a vision, they see which thing is suitable to clean this filth. It has come to be a human after millions of years. But some things are such that they are very close, so it has been stopped. No, this should not be used. A bone, a fruit pit, and these things that have been forbidden, they should not be used. In any case, these are small matters. The point I was making is that the *mu'min-i mustajīb* is the first level, when one accepts, he enters *īmān*. And after that, he keeps progressing, then he becomes a *mu'min*. Then there are ten levels. After that, he becomes a *kāshif*, from *kāshif* he becomes a *ma'dhūn*. There are two levels in *ma'dhūn*: *ma'dhūn-i maḥdūd* and *ma'dhūn-i muṭlaq*. *Ma'dhūn* means permitted. That is, one who has permission, who can conduct the *da'wah* in the islands, who are our representatives, delegates, that is a rank. *Ma'dhūn*. Higher than that is the rank of *dā'ī*. And the status of a *dā'ī* is so great that he has governance over three islands. Everything. And what kind of governance is this? This governance, I will tell you, that this governance is such that it is not political, like it is in the world today. Rather, they have *milkīyat* (ownership). I will tell you next time. They have *milkīyat*, by which they are made masters. And it goes further, that there are *tālī-i bāb*, then after *tālī-i bāb* is *ḥujjat*, after *ḥujjat* is *Bāb*. Then there are the four ranks of *Bābīyat*, then one reaches the rank of *Imāmat*. And above the Imam, then up to *Jussīyat*, there are many stages. There is *Imāmat-i Rūḥānī*, *Nuṭq-i Ilāhī*, and after that is *Hujjīyat*, *Hijābīyat*, *Kashfīyat*, *'Ādīyat*, *Samadīyat*, and after that is *Khiyālīyat*, *Fatrīyat*, *Jaddīyat*. Above *Jaddīyat* is *Qā'imīyat*. Above *Qā'imīyat* is *Jussīyat*. *Jussīyat* is the last point. This is the last link, after which the soul will leave the celestial sphere. Until then, you will keep revolving. All the great humans, great *dā'īs*, *ma'dhūns*, *kāshifs*, all of them are revolving, making their own progress. They are moving forward in their ranks. God knows how much has happened. So now you see that this is… we have to see in this light that we who have come as Sulaymanis, who have a special status. And you should always see that the entire population of the world, which is crores, our population of *da'wah*, if we include Najran and its surroundings, is less than four hundred thousand. It is not more than four hundred or five hundred thousand. Najran's population is about two to three hundred thousand people. The rest of the world, you can see. We are three to four hundred thousand in total. How many are we? We are not even equal to the salt in flour. But that same salt gives flavor to the flour. So you, in that sense, our community, who we are, the Sulaymanis, this is the real essence of the world. And why is it the essence? Because the Prophet's *dīn*, the divine constitution that you have brought, the constitution of life that you have brought, the *Da'aim al-Islam* that you have brought, we are the only true followers of it. Now you ask that this is… apart from this, they also have the *fiqh*, so-and-so also has the *fiqh*, the Sunnis also have the *fiqh*, the Shias also have it. There are many great *mujtahids*, great scholars, professors, and such. Everything is there, but if you look, just like our body, the body is great and performs great functions, it sees, hears, eats, performs actions and so on. As soon as the soul leaves, it is of no use. They take it and immediately, the father buries his son, the son buries his father, that it shouldn't be kept even for a day, bury it quickly. Why? Because the essential thing has left. So the soul is the essential thing. In the same way, among all the religions, the entire soul is the love of *Amīr al-Mu'minīn*. If someone does not have the love of Mawlana Ali in his heart, he is neither a Sulaymani nor is he on the path of truth. The first and most obligatory thing is the *walāyah* of *Amīr al-Mu'minīn*. All the *namāz*, *roza*, *ḥajj*, *zakāt*, *ṭahārat*, and *jihād*, all these pillars of religion that are there, the pillars of Islam, in them, the *walāyah* of *Amīr al-Mu'minīn* is both the first and the last. If that is not there, then nothing is anything. So just remember that the station of Hazrat Ali, I will slowly tell you, as many people already know, but many who are new, I have to tell them too, what is the position of Hazrat Ali? You, regarding the caliphate, you have heard only a little bit, many things. I will explain the fundamental matter so that you can compare, that today when you... for example, great people, if ten liars speak against one truthful person, the ten liars cannot defeat the truth. Their words have no truth in them, and the truthful one is truthful. Even if there is only one. So it is the same example, that today, the world of Islam, the Sunni sect, the Shia sect, those people who hold their beliefs, even if they are very large in quantity, they are small in quality. They do not have the system of life, the system of death and life, and besides that, the foundation of their *dīn* is wrong. The foundation of *dīn*. And you all know what the foundation of *dīn* is, that the Prophet established Islam on a great platform, for such people who were ignorant, *jāhil-i muṭlaq*. And the history of the Arabs is there, that they used to bury their daughters alive, and they used to drink wine in this skullcap. In such a bad environment, if you read the history of Islam before Islam, the era of ignorance was such that these kinds of customs were there. He gradually, gradually, gradually brought them onto a great platform, that you bring Islam, and believe in God. At your hands were Abu Jahl, Abu Lahab, and Abu Sufyan and other such harsh people. So you said that I cannot leave this. All the people came to you and said, "We will give you so much wealth, we will make you the king here, we will give you so many women, we will give you all things. You just leave this *kalima*." `Lā ilāha illallāh` (There is no god but Allah). We worship other gods, you can also worship your god. This is it. You said, "No." `Lā ilāha illallāh`. Tell them there is no god. There is no god until... there is a negation, then there will be an affirmation. And you said that if they put the sun in one of my hands and the moon in the other, even then I will not leave this path. With great difficulty, with great effort, you established this mission. And on a great platform, he brought all such people. And after that, the world continued. But the real station begins at Ghadir Khumm, when you made *Haydar-i Karrār*, *Mawla 'Ali Murtaḍā*, your successor. From there, the circle of faith begins. There is a big difference between Islam and *Īmān*. Every Muslim is not a *mu'min*, and every *mu'min* is a Muslim. You should know this. So we, Alhamdulillah, we are also *mu'min*, and because we are in the circle of *Īmān*, and we also accept the Prophet's Islam. `Ashhadu an lā ilāha illallāh` and `wa Muhammadur Rasūlullāh`, we recite both. And this recitation is a proof that we, in this way, are truthful. Our Prophet is the verifier of all the prophets. Isa, Musa, and whatever, Daniyal, Yahya, Musa, Samud, Lut, however many are named in the Bible, we say *'alayhi al-salām* (peace be upon him) to all of them. We say *'alayhi al-salām*. But we do not follow anyone except the Prophet. We follow, that is, we are in the *ummah* of Janab Muhammad Mustafa. We consider him our Prophet. Now see, when you talk to Christians, they do not accept our Prophet. We accept their Prophet. But we do not follow them in this way, that their time is over. God gave them a period of 600 years, there the era of Janab Isa ended, and the era of the Prophet began. In this way, Isa, if you say that why don't you follow our Isa, then you, why do you follow Musa? Musa also brought a great shari'ah, he brought the Torah. When the era of Musa ended, Janab Isa, superseding it, brought his own shari'ah. In the same way, the Prophet, superseding all the shari'ahs, brought a new shari'ah, and such a comprehensive shari'ah that until the Day of Judgment, it will not be changed, nor will any part of it be subject to change. Today, scholars, great people, ulama, fuḍalā, whoever they are, they bring up issues in this. This is a flaw, this is a small thing that if it changes, Islam will be ruined. In the matter of divorce, in the matter of shari'ah, in the matter of 'iddah, in the matter of inheritance, they find a fault in everything. This is only a flaw of the mind. It is a flaw of our mind. That we have not been given that much training, but we are looking at the higher thing. The Prophet, `i'tirāḍ barā-yi i'tirāḍ`. I had mentioned in the sermon before this that *Da'aim al-Islam* is our constitution book. In reading *Da'aim al-Islam*, which thing should we focus on correctly? That we have to learn this, not to do `i'tirāḍ barā-yi i'tirāḍ` (criticism for the sake of criticism), that who wrote *Da'aim al-Islam*, to examine it, or say that we are reading this to see what he is saying. They are saying, on this basis, we will see, in Christianity it is like this, what do you have? Compare and see. This comparison, this is all `i'tirāḍ barā-yi i'trāḍ`, this is all, all the angles are wrong angles. The correct angle should be that you read *Da'aim al-Islam* with sincerity that I will follow this. If you are appointed, then you should accept it in the same way. And after that, your progress will happen, and you will leave the circle of Islam and enter the circle of *Īmān*. When we become Sulaymanis and come into Sulaymaniyyat, then after that, what are the steps of progress? The progress is that your life here, how long is your life? Those who are 30-40 years old have passed, and there are more. 50, 60 years, 70 years, 80 years. After 80 years, you yourself wish to die, rather than getting caught up in the entanglements of old age. It is better to go than to live. The world that I am seeing, there are many old people, you ask them, they say, what has the world become, our childhood was like this. That's how it is. So the standard of life is 70-80 years, which is nothing. The eternal life which is ours after death, now you have to strive for that, to know that. You go to any other religion and ask, to Musa's shari'ah, that is, ask the Jews. There are great bishops sitting. Or ask the Christians, or ask the Pandits, or the priests who are there, ask them, that after all, what happens after death? Why does death come? How does the body go and how does the soul go? Where do we go? Why did we come here? We came here, and why did we live? And why were we brought at the time of going? Why were we brought to be given a send-off? At the time of coming, we came crying. And the world, everyone was laughing. We were crying, the world was laughing, happy that you have been born. And now when you are going, the world is crying, you are going laughing. So we, this is the theory of the world, that to settle in the world, to live, everything, every single minute detail is already prescribed, it is prescribed for your body. Only the rope of choice is in our hands, that how we become. Do we accept or not? Those who do not, they have become very big caliphs, big muftis, big rulers, big ministers. But we have no respect for them until they come into Sulaymaniyyat. And this is what I have told you, that the Prophet said that… `hattā yalija al-jamalu fī samm al-khiyāṭ` (until the camel passes through the eye of the needle). This is when it is said that it is impossible. Until a camel passes through the eye of a needle. A camel is, that it passes through the eye of a needle, that is a special thing, and what was that? I have mentioned this in the sermon before this, that a camel, it is not possible that it can pass through the eye of a needle. But it will have to pass. That is, all of humanity that we are seeing from heaven to earth, they will have to come to this very place and make progress. First, they will become human, all the minerals, plants, animals, and after becoming human, after coming to this form, this invitation will be presented to them. Here, after coming here only, from here… this is the eye of the needle, the `'Urwat al-Wuthqā` (the firmest handhold), this is the needle’s eye, one must pass through it to reach there. In any case, it is that we, as you see the ranks, from *mustajīb* to *Imam*, there are ten ranks. And in one sense, there are seven ranks. We complete the seven ranks and what happens? Our soul also progresses, our body also progresses. We take both the exoteric and the esoteric along. There are many groups in the world that are only exoteric. Some are such that they are esotericists. We take both the esoteric and the exoteric. Because we have both a body and a soul. The soul is that which is esoteric, the body is that which is exoteric. So how can we do it? If your soul leaves, you become a corpse. All these that you are seeing today, they have the soul of *īmān* gone, they are just walking with their physical bodies. Those who say "Islam, Islam," they do not have the real soul. They do not have the *walāyah* of *Amīr al-Mu'minīn*. Therefore, all of them have their souls gone from inside. They just have the *kalima* `Lā ilāha illallāh` on their lips and they have become Muslims. So to be a Muslim is one thing, to be a *mu'min* of *īmān* is another. This is the difference between a *mu'min* and a Muslim that I am telling you. Now see, this is the progress. Today, as we have seen, that this is our life which is passing. But a time comes when our end comes. Death comes. Now death, this is no small thing. You see, people about death… they do, that death, oh my, what a disaster death is. This is that people fear death, they are terrified of it. Hazrat Da'i Sahib once, some ladies from Hyderabad came and said, "Hazrat Sahib, tell us something about death." He said, "I am ill, I cannot speak much." They insisted, "No, just say a little something about death." He said, "I will tell you one sentence. People fear death. Everyone fears it." And it is a reality. `al-mawtu ḥaqqun` (death is a reality). So now you see that if any person is told that you are going to be finished tomorrow, now see… let alone tomorrow, I say you are going to be finished today. If he is told that he will be finished tomorrow, that his death will come tomorrow, then half of his life will leave him today. Half of his life will leave him. But this is a strange blessing of God. If, during that time, the person in front of him says, "You are not the only one dying, we are all going to be finished in this group," then the half-life that had gone, it will come back. "Oh, I am not alone, everyone is going." So this is the contentment of this life of ours, that we see death as a `rayḥān al-mawt`, not `bayān al-mawt`. It is a fragrance. For us, it is a sweet thing. For a *mu'min*, it is a very sweet thing. For an unbeliever, it is bad. And why is it bad? Because their whole life is bad, and after death is also bad. So the badness after death, I will explain it in four or eight lessons, but for you, I will briefly explain… I will begin the topic of the *jism-i mu'min*. *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*. See, our… our body, you yourself can understand that it is made of *nāmiyāt*, genetics, and in this body, there is bone, there is blood, all things are there, and from this, a whole structure is made, and it is called a human. But this is that just as a machine, running and running, gets worn out, in the same way, a human, by God's decree, it is decided how long his period is. There is a specific period. The period of everyone is appointed. Although I have told you about the lifespan, that there is a specific part of life in which death occurs. But that it goes back and forth, someone dies at 25, someone at 75, someone at 100, these different ones that are there, what do they mean? Its meaning will also come gradually. Now see that we, who are born with a human body, you are not just the body. You have a heart that is ordering you to lift a thing, to move it, to act. So what is the heart? The heart is also a lump of flesh. Today science says that it is a pumping thing. So you, there is some other thing as well that is animating it, and what is that? It is our soul. Now the soul, you go and ask, ask the Sunnis, ask the Shias, whoever you ask, they will not be able to tell you where it resides, what it is, and how much it is, and whether it is one or two or four, what is it? So the one telling will tell you, or not, that is not known. You go and ask. We have been told that the soul is divided into three parts. Three types of souls we have that are given to a *mu'min*. And what are they? There is *nafs-i nāmiyah*, *ḥissīyah*, and *nāṭiqah*. This is a special kind of power. *Quwwat-i nāmiyah*, *quwwat-i ḥissīyah*, and *quwwat-i nāṭiqah*, these three things. I have already mentioned *nāṭiqah*, that this is what one gets at the time of the *'ahd* from the *ahl-i 'ahd*. There is an attachment that happens when we take the covenant. In any case, I am telling you that besides this, we also have a body. And the body is subservient to our soul. As the soul desires, we do that. To pray, not to pray, to fast, not to do this, we do it. So the body also endures hardship. When the soul orders it, its knees ache, this happens, that happens, that one should pray like this, fast like this, feel hungry like this, all these things. So the body should also get some reward. And along with that, the souls, their reward should also be given. So see, what are we all wishing for? That after death, we will go to paradise. So what is paradise? A garden, some orchard, where fruits are hanging, rivers of honey are flowing, this and that, or some mansions are built? It is not like that, which is written in the exoteric. All of it has an interpretation. And the explanation of the interpretation has already been given, that whatever has come in the Quran, it is in symbolic form, and its matter has to be understood. So the body also has a part of ours that we want to go to paradise. And along with that, the souls too. It is paradise for everyone. So what kind of paradise will it be? I have told you in the previous sermons that we humans, wherever we live, after finishing a specific period, we will die, and then after our parts are dispersed, they will go to different places, its detail will come. And then, in the world, after 1000 years, we will come here again. 1000 years will seem like a moment to that person. Except for someone whose sins are many or so, then they are made to wait, in God's system, there are different things, everyone has their own account. But the standard, the general thing is this, that after 1000 years, our manifestation in the world will happen again. How will it happen? Our… see, I will tell you briefly that one died. Just died, and it was known that his breath had stopped. So what happened when the breath stopped? When the breath stopped, it means that in the sphere of air, a special… zamharīr, in the sphere of zamharīr, there is a section of *nasīm*. And what is *nasīm*? It is oxygen. That oxygen from where it was being received, whether you are sitting in a cave, wherever you are sitting, you are getting your quota. You are getting your quota. It will keep coming, a time will come, a moment will come, and your life will continue in this way. Where your quota is finished, that for this much duration it has to be finished. Where it is finished, you are finished while sitting. Whether you are sick and finished, or finished while sitting, or finished in some accident. From there, your quota of breath is finished, and here your eyes are closed. This is it. And after that, the heart slowly finishes, the brain has some life in it, these are all scientific things, which gradually, as the body decays, in the same way. But his life is over. After this is over, you intend to bury him. You bury him. See, wrapping in a shroud, shrouding, all have meanings. What cloth is coming on his body, what shroud they are giving him, why is the shroud important? That from this valley, from that valley, it is not like that. Many old-timers used to dip their shrouds in the water of the Kaaba, that is, in Zamzam water, so that that same shroud would become theirs. These are all traditional things, but this is the method. And the shroud's cloth has a special dignity. When the topic of *fiqh* begins, I will also mention that. In any case, this is our end, and we are placed in the earth. That person whom people loved, after his face is also gone, there is nothing left. Although the grave has a status. Why does the grave have a status? I will tell you when tomorrow's topic comes. But this is the rule, that he is taken in the morning and buried. And when he goes, the earth is put on him, and after that, his chemical changes begin. The chemical changes that have to happen, you all know how decaying, rotting, whatever substance is put in the earth, it finishes. And this is God's power, that the same thing, then becoming earth, will become mineral, from mineral it will become plant, from plant it will become animal. This is a process of ascending. This is a natural mill. What kind of mill is it? That a thing from a mineral progresses to become a plant. A plant progresses to become an animal. And an animal progresses to become a human. And if it regresses, then from human to animal, from animal to plant, from plant to mineral. This is regression, or its decay. This is the method, these are the things. But one is an ascending order, one is a descending order. So we, when we are placed in the earth, there are things, chemical changes happen. Now after happening, its residue, just as a… there is a big refinery. As we cook something on the stove, there are chemical changes, its color changes, this happens, that happens. In the same way, the residue progresses and in the form of vapor, as its final system is, that it will go into a liquid form, then it will become vapor, it will come out in the form of vapors, so from there they will come out. So there is a special center for that. A special center is there. Only there will we go. And what is that? The center of the island, the heart of the island. We are under Hind and Sindh. I mean, two islands. And the masters of the two islands are there. And Bombay is our… earlier it was Baroda, before Baroda it was Ahmedabad. But today, Bombay is our center. Spiritually. And physically too, you see it is the same center. But spiritually, where our prayer hall is, our big prayer hall, the *miḥrāb* of that is a center point. Wherever you are, in America, in Argentina, in London, wherever you die, but your residue, through which channel, which wave you come down from, you will come straight to the *qalb-i jazīrah*. If a *mu'min* is a true *mu'min*, and has taken the covenant, they come straight to the *qalb-i jazīrah* and reside in the *miḥrāb* for a period. Now how long is the period? That is according to their own accounts. And one, the *da'wah* of the Imam that is going on, it resides in that. Now, in the same way it happens, as we make a curry, or make something, first its color changes, we see it, if the water is less, water is added, then this happens, then tempering happens, this… just as we have here in the world. And I have told you that the image of the esoteric is our exoteric. The image of the *dīn* is our world. So in the same way that our *dīn* is based on, it goes to the *qalb-i jazīrah*. From the *qalb-i jazīrah*, then, from there, they do not stick. Where the period is appointed, they stay there. From there, they go straight to where? The *mīqāt*. *mīqāt*, where we don the *iḥrām*. Between Madinah Sharif and Makkah, and there are five other *mīqāts* as well. Where, at the time of Hajj, the Prophet has appointed, that you should don the *iḥrām*. And make the intention. Where we pray two *rak'at*s. So the *mīqāt*, at that time, in the time of the Prophet, it was a small prayer hall. Today, if you see the mosque, it is so big that about three to four thousand people pray there at one time. The Saudis have built it. All this is happening by the power of God. It was a… under a *shajarah* (tree), a small mosque under a tree. It is called *Zū l-Shajarah*. That is the biggest *mīqāt*. The second is at Ghadir Khumm. One is for those coming from Damascus. One is for those coming from Iraq. One is for those coming from Yemen. For everyone, there are separate *mīqāts*. So what is a *mīqāt*? A center. See, this is the Kaaba. This is the Kaaba. Now... These are the twelve islands of ours, where the believers live. This is, for example, Bombay, Hind, then Sindh, then Yemen. In this way, there should be twelve in our world where the Imam's *da'wah* is running. In these twelve islands, the believers live. And from here what is happening? The same residue is collected and goes to their respective *qalb-i burj*, the center, it reaches there. After staying there for a specific period, then from here, it is close to this, so here... one, two, three, four, and five. These are the five *mīqāts*. There are five *mīqāts*, where this, whatever *mīqāt* is related to us, and we often, what do we do? We go from Madinah Sharif and from there we don the *mīqāt*. Whatever is close, from there at the *mīqāt*, this thing comes, our residue. And the circle is shrinking. Where there were twelve, from twelve, five places have been made. After being made five places, from here, it comes straight here. This, see, these are the *butayn*. These are called the *butayn*. Just as we have, the medical science people know, around the heart, there are special sections, there is a *tadābīr* which is like this, like a triangle is formed. It is called *tadābīr*. Which is inside. This is more important than this. Those who see the heart, they can tell. In any case, that from these twelve places, after collecting, to these five *mīqāts*, this thing of ours, the residue, comes. And after the residue, then this is the *parnāla* (waterspout), about which it is said that it is made of gold. After becoming, it comes here. This, see, the people who see the picture of the Kaaba, there is a *parnāla* on it, for the water to flow, a golden one has been made. There is a *parnāla*. Our residue must come to this *parnāla*. And from here, then... After this, this, above, in the sky, you see, this is the *falak-i muḥīṭ*, which is the last sphere. After that, this is the *falak-i burūj*. *Burūj*. *falak-i burūj*, which is the eighth sphere, here these... I have already told you about the spheres. The seven planets. Which ones? Mars, Saturn, etc., all seven. Above that, this is the eighth sphere, which is called *falak-i burūj*. In this, all the zodiacs are ours. So this is such that, and it is drawn like this. This, twelve sections are there. This is one section. This is called a *burj*. And this is the *falak-i burūj*. The whole thing is called *falak-i burūj*, and each section is a *burj*. In this *burj*, after coming, these our residue, from here to here, from here to here, from here to here, and from here to here, through the *parnāla*, they go up. And when they reach the *parnāla*, their name changes. See, as our... a person... someone is Tafazzul, someone is Falana, someone is, after that they become so-and-so, then they become Barawala, then Falana. As they are, they keep changing. Where the thing that comes from here, it becomes even softer. From here, then it comes here, it keeps getting softer, here it reaches in the form of vapor, as is God's will, it reaches here. From here, straight it goes into the *burūj*. And in the *falak-i burūj*, after going, in one part, it takes 60 years. 60 years, it stays in that one part. Then, just as the 60 years here in our world are, then in the second, in the third, in the fourth, in this way, in the twelve sections, in the twelve *burj*, that residue, of whom? Of all the believers who are passing away from the world, it goes and resides there. And it is filtered, tempered, just as something needs to be cleaned, there is chiseling, polishing, its cutting is happening. Just as the *īmān* here is, that many things are not followed, many things, no fasting, no this, no that. So all those things are polished there and become soft and they are deposited there. So 70 into 12, that is 720 years, our period is there. After passing, then from there, it comes down as vapor and here, in the mineral world, our things come. In the mineral world, they come into the earth, whether in gold, however God wills. After being, that which, as I have told you, that the earth where we grow a plant, any plant. So what happens? The earth around it is very soft. It becomes soft in this way, that until it becomes viscous, thick, it is not ready to become food for the plants. It is necessary for it to become fine. Then it becomes food for the plants. Now that plant has taken it. Just as we put fertilizer, the fertilizer it pulls, and what happens? Now when it has become a tree, a *shajar*, a tree has grown, now that earth is not there. Now what will its name be called? It has become a plant. The plant, as I have told you, that if a green leaf is plucked and given to a goat or a cow, it is permissible. So it is a permissible animal. A cow is permissible. When it eats it, now it has become a part of the cow. That plant is not there. You cannot take it out and say this is a leaf, this is so-and-so. No. It has become a part of its body. And then that, which is a camel, a cow, a goat, which are permissible animals, or a chicken, whatever such things are, the knife is passed over them, there is a *tabī'at*. What is *tabī'at*? It is to kill oneself. In obedience to God, we are saying `Allāhu Akbar`. All the things are being transformed. It is not just that this action happened, that action happened, grab this, take that, bring the pot, cook this. It is not like that. But the thing that is to be eaten, that is what is to be eaten. `Allāhu Akbar` must be said. God's name must be taken. Then the thing will be permissible. And see, some things are such that if a *mu'min* is true and is in great trouble, and such a thing happens, that there he has to starve, or say he has to kill his own child, or death comes to him, and he does not get any food, what does he do? At that time, if he says *Bismillah* at the last moment, it is possible that its chemical changes can happen. And they have happened. As it has been told in the stories of miracles, I will tell you. In any case, that we eat, so what does it become? That cow, etc., the things, the meat, it comes into the form of a human. And what thing is coming? Your building. One substance is revolving. One and the same thing, coming from here and there, has come. So this, the process that is, of mineral, plant, and animal, this is completed in 280 years. 280 years. These 720 years there, 280 years here, this makes 1000 years. In 1000 years, that substance, that substance, will come into your father's seed. And it is called *sulālah*. In some stages it is mentioned in the Quran. That *sulālah* will be, and then when it is mixed with the mother, there it goes, the thing that reaches the mother's womb, at that time its name will be *nuṭfah*. This is the second stage. Then the *nuṭfah*, when it progresses further, it will become *'alaqah*. After *'alaqah* it will become *muḍghah*. After *muḍghah* will be *'iẓām*. After *'iẓām* will be *laḥm*. Meaning, one lump, one lump, as in the mother's womb, as it grows, the fourth month comes, the baby's movement starts. The fifth month comes, its bones are formed. The sixth month comes, its flesh is formed. These are the stages, and the whole Quran has explained it. So there are six, so what happens? One child, a whole structure is formed. He is complete. Only the soul has to be put into it. So see, I am just giving a hint, that this, when it came in our father's seed, and from there it went into the mother's womb, the *quwwat-i nāmiyah* (power of growth) is what has been transferred from there. It is the power of growth. And that goes and the child slowly, slowly expands, grows, in the mother's womb. This is what? It is one soul. That is, it is the *quwwat-i nāmiyah*. As I told you, there are three souls, this is the manifestation of the *quwwat-i nāmiyah*. So at that time, he comes out into the world. And at that time, you are told that you are nineteen. [unclear] this is that you are going to have a child. This is that the first stage has begun. You have received the *quwwat-i nāmiyah*. And after receiving the *quwwat-i nāmiyah*, as it progresses, when it completes nine months, seven months, the seven planets have an effect. From Saturn to the Moon, these seven stars, then the second cycle, the effect of Saturn comes in the eighth month. And for this reason, if a child is born in the eighth month, he is stillborn. Because I have told you in the first sermons, that the effect Saturn had on the world, the whole world was covered with ice. So there too, the child's birth was destined, its date is there, so there it is born frozen and stillborn. If it is in the eighth month. Then the ninth month, which is Jupiter, which is a lucky star, its effect falls, and the child is born alive. And when he comes out of the mother's womb, he gets the second power. The second power is given, and what is that? It is the *quwwat-i ḥissīyah* (sensory power). And its center is what? The sun. And what is the center of the first one? The moon. The center of the first one, the *quwwat-i nāmiyah*, is the moon, and the second one's is the sun. It comes from there and it gets the *ḥissīyah*, and it is born a living child. And after that, one year passes, two years, three years, four years, five years, here until he reaches puberty, becomes an adult, then the parents give him the covenant. And the covenant was given, so see, the third birth has happened, the spiritual birth has happened, and the third power, which we left in the world of spirits, which has been deposited, is given to him, and he becomes a *mu'min*. So what I mean is that this one thing that I have told you today, that the residue, the parts of our body, when we, after making full progress, have come back here, we have come back. If we have come back completely, say that your body has 100 parts, for example, 100, and we have come back with 100, then what is the benefit? What is the progress? Take 100 and come back with 100. It's not progress. So progress is this, that one part of yours is subtracted. You become 99. One part, one part of your body has progressed. And that progress is... [unclear] One part, in this way, is subtracted. If one part of ours, when the second birth happens, if it is minus, then for us, in paradise, in terms of the body, we have been reduced. That is, this body of flesh, in this state, we cannot enter the spiritual realm until the whole soul is formed. So one by one, our particles will be formed. We will not be formed all at once. This is our prison, such is our condition that we have come into the prison, that this will happen slowly. It does not happen all at once. If it happened all at once... [Lady's voice: But according to today's scientific view, it happens quickly.] How does it happen? [Lady's voice: The progress...] [Lady's voice: The way you have explained progress.] So explain it. [Lady's voice: The nine-month thing.] The nine-month thing? [Lady's voice: The mother's womb... that thing.] Science says? [Lady's voice: It tells.] One thing it tells and another thing it doesn't tell, that is science, not religion. And what is right, that is what is told. So for this reason, the scholars, the great astronauts, philosophers, scholars, whoever they are, the philosophies they have stated, these people, what they say, they do not do. And the prophets, what they say, they do. This is the difference between prophets and scholars. They say it, they also do it. In any case, that this system is of the prophets and the Imams, on this, until there is certainty, we... our piety is not... spots appear on our faces. If there is a wrong belief about this. And... [repeat of a different section starts]...they used to mock. The Prophet… `istahzūna` (they mock). They used to mock the religion. This mockery of theirs will become serious when death comes. The mockery will become serious. So to mock the religion, this is a very wrong thing. And no 'alim will tolerate it. Remember this. That those who mock the religion, they themselves will be mocked. Just as if you do not pray correctly, prayers will be said over you. It is for everyone. No one who has come will not die, everyone has to die, and death is a hunting bird that is chasing from behind. And we are running away from it. From here, from there, dodging. And somehow or the other, we will come under the trap, that where death will seize us. So remember this, that *dīn* is a very noble system of ours. This noble system is not something we have created, it is not borrowed from science, we have not taken a loan from philosophers, or say we have not taken it from mathematicians. We have given to mathematics, to philosophers, and to scientists. We have given. All the science today, that is on the way, it is not at the destination. We are at the destination. We are seeing which paths they are coming on. They are on the way, so we give examples, that look, it is like this, it is like this. They are coming, they are behind. Science has not reached the destination yet. If it had, then all the truths... it is not that today they discover this and tomorrow they discover that. Rather, they are discovering one thing at a time, and that is why what do the *du'āt* say? That *'Ilm-i Ladunnī*, this knowledge, after this, if there is any correct knowledge, it is medical science. *'Ilm al-Abdān*. And a lot of work has been done on *'Ilm al-Abdān*, a lot of work has been done on its physiology and its... so *'Ilm al-Abdān* is correct because it is based on experiment and observation. For this reason, it is correct. And after this, the *du'āt* have explained in detail, after this, if there is a second science, it is economics, the science of livelihood. But all are behind. The leader is our religion. And the theory of religion, this... we have had great thinkers. Those who have compiled it, are Sayyidna Mu'ayyad al-Shirazi. And Sayyidna Mu'ayyad al-Shirazi is such a personality that we are proud of him. Our Tayyibi *da'wah* and our Sulaymani community. This Sayyidna Mu'ayyad, you have heard the sermon, of Mawlana Salman Farsi. You have heard about Salman Farsi. What his station was and what it was. `Salmānu minnā ahl al-bayt` (Salman is from us, the people of the house). That we are from Ahl al-Bayt, Salman has become from Ahl al-Bayt. But Sayyidna Mu'ayyad was born 300-400 years after him. He said with a challenge, that if I were in the time of the Prophet, I would have been like Salman. He did not have the power to say this, but he had it in him. He was a *gohar*. You have seen that they do this, and they used to tell the rank, that this is your rank. So that Sayyidna Mu'ayyad is the one in whose supplications there are realities. His supplications, which we have, or if you see in the *sahifah*, there are supplications and prayers, in which the realities have been laid out. Many things. That is what Sayyidna Mu'ayyad has compiled, which is our doctrine. What is *mabda*, what is *ma'ād*. Otherwise, many... see, in Bombay, about 30, 30-35 years before this I am talking about, I was quite young. At that time... see, there is a... about religion... What to say about religion... If there is a talk about someone, that he is like this, it is possible that there is some flaw in his character, or there is some flaw, it is a different matter. About religion, there is nothing to be said. Our religion is completely pure, complete, and with absolute steadfastness. Now, one who does not understand it, God knows. So one time, we had a great thinker who used to come, a very high one, who? Asaf Fyzee Sahib. Very high. And he did a lot of work, for the world he translated *Da'aim al-Islam*, he did this, all. But what is it? That he too, in the truth of *mabda* and *ma'ād*, there was a little doubt for him. He had not understood it completely. He was thinking that this whole theory of *mabda* and *ma'ād* is borrowed from Greek philosophy. Borrowed from Greek philosophy. Today, about 30... while Baba Sahib Mukaddas Hazrat Da'i Sahib's tomb is there, and in his final years, perhaps you may also know, or not, that in his final years he admitted that I know nothing, I want to study with you. He came to Hazrat Da'i Sahib's place for three or four days, after that his health was not good or what, it is not known, so he could not come after that. So he said, "You start from the beginning from here." That you don't know the higher things. You start from the beginning. Such a great thinker, who has a great name in the world of Islam, he one day was defeated by me. And I was 26 years old at that time. And he said that... just after hearing the name, that he is the younger brother of Da'i Sahib. He said, "The theory you people state, and these things about *mabda* and *ma'ād*, this is all borrowed from Greek philosophy." Greek philosophy. Now, I, his age was 70 years, I was not even 30. Out of respect for him, I said, "Hazrat Sahib, I am younger, but you are making a mistake in understanding. This which you are thinking, that it has been taken from Greek philosophy, you are putting the cart, the Victoria that runs in Bombay, the Victoria runs, the horse is in front and the carriage is behind. You are putting the horse behind the carriage." The carriage is behind, you are putting it behind the Victoria. We are from the time of Adam, this theory has been going on. You have taken Greek philosophy as the final thing, and are saying we have taken it from there. Because Aristotle, Plato, and Galen, etc., all the great philosophers, they have explained the matters of *tawḥīd*. And why have they explained? Because Aristotle was also in our religion. He was a believer in this very philosophy. But the time was of Musa, and he was higher in level than the shari'ah of Musa. Therefore, he did not follow the shari'ah of Musa. He was a very great sage. And he knew all the truths. Now who will explain to them that the time was of Musa and he was so-and-so and what was it. You read the books and you will find out what the rank of Aristotle, whom we call Arisṭūṭālīs in Arabic, is. Then you will know what Aristotle is, what the world is saying, and what we are understanding. The greatest scientist, who first started science, was Aristotle. And why did he do it? Why did he do it? You, the afternoon sermon that is there, the sermon that is in the evening, I have mentioned in it that wherever the great philosophers, wherever the great personalities who have presented special things in the world, the effulgence of *Amīr al-Nahl* was upon them. Otherwise, not a single sentence could come out of their mouths. *Amīr al-Nahl*, that is, *'Ayn 'Aẓīmah*, *'Ali Murtaḍā*. The dominance of *'Ayn* was there, his effulgence was happening, so all these... `āṣim`... what's his name... no, he was from Iran... Dhul-Qarnayn, who was, and all the kings of Iran... Nowsherwan. The justice that Nowsherwan did, what was that justice? It was the effulgence of *Amīr al-Nahl*. You will read it in the book of the *da'wah*. The effulgence was upon him, then he did justice. From where does one learn justice? So the stamp of Janab 'Ali Murtaḍā has been there since the time of Adam, from the time of Adam *al-nīl* until here. And when you read the Du'a Ashura, the great one we read, in it, it is all described, that you were the one who saved Yusuf from the well, you were the one who saved Ayyub from the worms, and Janab Musa, you saved him, Janab Nuh, you gave the command to build the ark and you saved him from the flood. All the prophets, 124,000, all are indebted to Mawlana 'Ali. So before we listen to our lesson, before we understand the lesson, first of all, we have to make our faith strong. And if we have to do it, then we have to sit here. This is the most important thing. I don't like at all to mock religion. Someone does it or not... [Lady's voice: Forgive me, I said it because...] No, I am not telling you. Your... [Lady's voice: Because of science... anything...] No, it's not for your sake, but our youth, this has become a fashion, and it has become their habit, that they think that we have become very educated, we know science, we know this, we know nothing. We know what is visible. We don't know what is behind the curtain. The things behind the curtain, we bring them out and we tell them. And whose charity is this? Our Mawlana Salman Sahib's. This is the present era. And the effulgence upon him is of his Imam of the time. So you understand that we have to explain this. We are mature, who are of understanding, we have to explain to our children, teenagers, the generation that is to come, we have to persuade them, we ourselves have to acquire the ability. Not just that they should be left alone, so that they do not stray. How many have strayed? A young man comes into the *dīn*, then he leaves. Whose negligence is it? It is of our parents. It is the negligence of the parents. This is what? `min al-nūr ilā al-ẓulumāt` (from light into darknesses). They are going from light to darkness. Our religion is that we should pull from darkness to light. And many such instances, you will know, in the time of Hazrat Zikr Allah Sahib, in the time of Hazrat Zayn al-Din Sahib, there were many such people who have come back into the *dīn*. Hindus have also come and have come and accepted. And there are many such who have become Hindus after being Sulaymani. So what is this? Now this you, in the light of *mabda* and *ma'ād*, now that the introduction has begun, you will know that what is the world we live in. We see our paradise, our hell, our proximity, what is this thing? This whole world will have to be seen with a different perspective. A special kind of glasses will have to be worn. And then see the world from that perspective. It is not that we are flowing in the currents of the world, that we have to earn money, build big factories, do this, do that. All these will remain here when the call from the grave comes. We also have to die. `al-mawtu ḥaqqun` (death is a reality). The creed that we read in *'aqā'id*, all things are true. The scale is also true, the bridge is also true, and death is also true, hell is also true, paradise is also true. To acknowledge all these things, this is the name of *īmān*. You are in the levels of *īmān*, so Inshallah, I will explain a little more when the next sermons are there. Inshallah, tomorrow there will be a sermon. *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*... ...Lesson seven. ...[prayer recited] The previous lesson's topic was the *jism-i mu'min* (body of a believer). As a recap, we are not one, but three: *nāmiyah*, *ḥissīyah*, and *nāṭiqah*. *Naḥnu* (We) refers to these three. *Nāṭiqah* is our deposited essence in the spiritual world, which we receive upon our spiritual birth. Besides this, there is *ḥissīyah* (which we call our "self") and *nāmiyah*. The body also exists. The body is rewarded after death, not punished. We are confident of our reward because we are attached to the *Ḥabl Allāh al-Matīn* (the strong rope of God). He explains the continuous chain of Imams, one after another, father to son, a *sunnah* (practice) of God that will never change (`lan tajida li sunnatillāhi tabdīlā`) or be altered (`lan tajida li sunnatillāhi taḥwīlā`). He gives a detailed exegesis of the verse about the two seas meeting (`maraj al-baḥrayni yaltaqiyān`), identifying them as the chains of Prophethood and Imamat. They met at the marriage of Ali and Fatimah. From them came forth the pearl and coral (`yakhruju minhumā al-lu'lu' wa al-marjān`), who are Mawlana Hasan and Mawlana Husayn. The Prophet's hadith: "Hasan and Husayn are two rightful Imams, whether they rise or sit, and their father is better than both of them." He explains how Hasan "sat" (made a truce) and Husayn "rose" (in Karbala). Their father, Ali, is superior to both. He asks what makes Ali superior. It is his status as *Wasī* (successor) to the Prophet. The Imamat started with Hasan. He returns to the main topic of the lesson. The *fuḍlāt*. He draws a diagram of the body of a believer. The body will progress, but it is a solid object that cannot enter the spiritual world. It must change its form. He uses the analogy of ice melting into water, then into steam (gas). Similarly, our body, through acts of worship (*riyāḍat*), is heated by the fire of *īmān*. The heart is the leader of the body's organs. He describes the heart's chambers (*tajāwīf*) and the scarlet blood (*dam-i sarwīzī*) surrounding it, which is our life force. This is a liquid form. When this blood circulates rapidly, it produces vapors (*bukharāt*), which is the *ḥarārat-i gharīzī* (innate heat). This is our *jān* (life). When this heat leaves the palms, the person is dead. So, the *ḥarārat-i gharīzī* is in a gaseous form and is more capable of becoming spiritual than the solid body. It is the next stage of progress.

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-2002 lecture 3 -->

A spark that falls on a coal… you see how it falls. And that spark, if you make it... if you blow on it... it will become an ember and a flame. This is our system. And from this, it... the light is born.

Anyway, this... this is our system of da'wat (mission). The next topic will also come up in my speech.

Now, there, when Allah's plan was that the world should be adorned so that the purpose of creation, which is mankind, who should... so that he should be created. Then `'Aql-e-'Ashir` (the Tenth Intellect)... as soon as it came to `'Aql-e-'Ashir`, you will have understood that just as you have understood about Allah that what is `Tawhid-e-Rabb al-'Izzat` (the Oneness of the Lord of Honor), in the same way, you have also understood about `'Aql-e-'Ashir`, that our final boss will be our `'Aql-e-'Ashir`. Now, the plan of `'Aql-e-'Ashir` was to adorn the world in such a way, to do this, that then mankind would come into being and... this all, these are all its effects.

Here is our first... as many as... these seven planets, they have special `mudabbirin` (governors/planners). By `mudabbirin`, it is meant that, look... we, whom we consider a `sayyara` (planet)... that there is the moon (`qamar`). The moon's surface, the outer surface that we are seeing, this we call `sadaf`. We call it `sadaf`. Mother of pearl. What is visible. Inside it, and further inside, are its `lata'if` (subtleties). `Lata'if`. Today, science is saying that inside it there is helium gas, and such-and-such gas is present, this is there... all these are the `lata'if` that are in between. And in between that is the center point, which is... that center (`markaz`) which is... that is `ruhaniyyat` (spirituality). Its switchboard is that. From there, the `tadbir` (planning/providence) begins.

So, those `mudabbirin` are... there is one `mudabbir` for each planet. Now, Allah's plan was that these very ones... just as it happens in a class... the class teacher goes away, another teacher comes. The teacher goes out, he says, "Who... who..." two children who are... the whole class knows that they will take charge. So just as... in the same way, here, the first `sardari` (leadership) was given... to `falak-e-Zuhal` (the sphere of Saturn). Zuhal (Saturn) was told to cast its `ta'thir` (influence) upon the world. It is Allah's will, but the `tadbir` is being done by `'Aql-e-'Ashir`.

A `murabbi` (guardian/guide) was made, first `falak-e-Zuhal`. You know about Zuhal's `tadbir`, that Zuhal is a harsh star, and it is number one among the `nahsayn` (the two inauspicious ones). What are the `nahsayn` called? Among the stars, there are types. Two are `sa'dayn` (the two auspicious ones), two are `nayyirayn` (the two luminaries), and two are `nahsayn`. Mirrikh (Mars) and Mars... Zuhal. These two are counted as bad stars, their influence is bad, so they are called `nahsayn`. And the two `sa'dayn` are `Zahra` (Venus) and `Mushtari` (Jupiter). And the two `nayyirayn` are the moon and the sun. And one, `'Utarid` (Mercury), is left, the seventh, which is called `munshi-e-falak` (scribe of the sphere), it has its own separate system.

Each one has a different `ta'thir`. So the first `ta'thir` was that of Zuhal, and Zuhal began to cast its things from above. So how? Through that `ta'thir`, what happened was that the entire world was covered in ice. The whole world was covered with ice. He says that there was so much cold and so much harshness that all was covered with ice... the whole world. Now, how was this `sardari` given? Look, Zuhal was given `sardari` for 1000 years. In many books it is said that 1000 years were given to Zuhal individually. After that, in the second thousand years, Zuhal was joined with Mushtari. In the third thousand years, Zuhal was joined with Mirrikh. In the fourth thousand years, Zuhal was joined with the sun, with Aftab. In this way, 7000 years passed. This is called the `dawr` (era) of Zuhal, `dawr-e-Zuhal`. So Zuhal is bad anyway. Where Mirrikh was joined with it, it became even worse. And where the sun or Zahra is, there is a combination of good and bad, and things become separate. So in the first thousand years, the entire world was completely covered with ice. Then the `dawr` of Mushtari came, and in that `dawr`, in that whole thousand years, the ice began to melt. And the whole thing melted, then it became level. In the third thousand years, Mirrikh came again. Mirrikh and Zuhal, both are `nahs` (inauspicious) stars, and a great mist descended upon the whole world, and the whole thing was covered. Then when it was the turn of Shams (the sun)... Shams... its time... Shams is a good `sayyara`, and in its time, various things began to appear. Now look, this is a very detailed... I am not going into the details, I am just giving a hint. That... the matter of humans has not come up yet. First, `jamadat` (minerals), `nabatat` (plants), and `haywanat` (animals) have to be formed. First the world has to be adorned, then the resident will come. First the house (`makan`) is adorned, then Hazrat Insan comes. So... there... the things that are... this surface that is, the surface of the earth, in it, in the time of Zuhal and in the time of Mirrikh, potash was formed. And all these things... from which bombs are made... sodium, and this is... its mines were prepared. In their time. And in the time of Mushtari, good things were prepared from them. This is a very long detail, in which time gold was made, in which time silver was made, in which time this... all the mines. And just as it happened in the `ma'dan` (mineral kingdom), in the same way it happened in the `nabat` (plant kingdom) too. In the `nabat`, first greenery appeared in it. During Zuhal, etc., thorny bushes, thorny plants, their thorns, they were created. And then after that, in the time of Mushtari, greenery and small vegetables etc. were created. And after that, growing and growing, in the time of the sun, good things were created. So... there is a whole book on this, `Tuhfat al-Qulub`, if you read it, you will know the full details, that in which era what things were created. So it happened in minerals, in animals, and this... in animals, which, which animals were created? Fruit-bearing trees, which were... in this era, these were created. Bushes are there, plants are there, animals are there, all the birds and beasts, when were they created? All these were being done. And for Hazrat Insan, the `khamire` (leavens/ferments) were being prepared. For mankind. In this way, 7000 years of Zuhal's era passed, 7000 of Mushtari, 7000 of Mirrikh with it, 7000 of this... 42,000 years passed. And this is the detail, this is the breakdown. After 42,000... now the final, the turn of the moon (`qamar`) came. When the moon's time of control came, then with the moon, this came, this came, this came... in the final years, in the thousand years... now, what was this? That in each of the moon's 7000-year periods, in every thousand years, the `khamire` started to be prepared. `Khamire` were being prepared in the world, that is, for your existence, those necessary things, the `sulale` (progenies/lineages) were being prepared. The `sulale` from which man is made, that was being prepared everywhere. It came into minerals and plants, and from there it changed rapidly and began its preparation to reach us. And when the final time of the moon came, at that time, the creation of man was decreed. Now, about the creation of man... it is said that, look... after all, a man is born from a man. Without that, how will it happen? So this matter that is being discussed, it is of the very beginning of creation. Before that, the world did not even exist. And it is in another book that `Kayumarth-e-A'zam`, the very first human who graced the world, his name was `Janus`. `Jussa-e-Ibda'iyya`. You will not understand `Jussa-e-Ibda'iyya`... `Kayumarth-e-A'zam`. So look, there are two ways of creation. Everywhere you look, the `kulliyat` (universals/principles) are different. This has a different `kulliya`, that has a different `kulliya`. Its application is on that... Malthus's theory is different, this theory is different. Similarly, it has been told to us that what we have been seeing... what is our memory? 100 years at most. What do you remember about the previous life? You have no memory. So now, the method is this: one is to be born from a mother's womb, which we are familiar with. The second method is that `khudda` (pits/cavities) were created in the earth, and humans were made from them. They were created from them. This is called `khudda`. `Khudda` means pits. And the shape of the pit is exactly like the `rahm` (womb) in a mother's belly. Exactly that shape. The shape that the `rahm` has, that shape was created. Now, in various places... the description that has to be given, it is of the Ka'ba. The most noble of the `khudda` were at the Ka'ba. Then at Madina-e-Munawwara. At Masjid al-Nabawi. In Jannat al-Baqi'. At Masjid al-Aqsa. In Jerusalem. All these venerable places that are counted today, they all have signs of the `khudda`. And that is why our head bows there, we go there... We do `tawaf`. So these, like a mother's womb, `khudda` were created there. Now how did the `madda` (matter) come into it? So look, there, all the... when the `ma'dan` (mineral) phase ended and it was transformed into the form of `nabat` (plant). How was it transformed? The `ma'dan`, until the earth is ground up and does not bow its head and come near the plant, it cannot become the plant's food. To become the food of the plant, the soil around it has to be completely soft. So that it can become its food. So until it is ground up... 'being ground up', remember this. 'Being ground up' means it bows its head. It then goes into the plant, that is, it moves forward on the path of progress. And the plant, until it becomes a tender shoot and bows down, the goat or halal (permissible) animal does not go near it. And that goat or the animal that we... through `zabiha`... that, until it bows and becomes ready for the knife at our feet, it does not become the food of man. Now that same thing, the animal, then becomes man's food. Now what happened here? That plant... came into the animal, and the `haywan`... `mahmuda` (praiseworthy) animals were created. Various kinds of animals. Now those animals... the `hudud` have described this in `Ganj al-Walad` etc. in high places. Those animals were `mahmuda` animals. The best... I mean, today's time is not like that, where one is eating this, one is eating that, now we see that the food is... the most noble were the `mahmuda` animals. And their death... there was no hunter. No one to slaughter them, no one to beat them. They died a natural death. After dying a natural death, `bukharat` (vapors/exhalations) rose from them. Those `bukharat` were the most noble `bukharat`. And they were the `tamhid` (prelude/preparation) for man to be created in the future. So how did they come? Those `bukharat`... these `khudda` that were there, they came and settled near the `khudda`. They came to the bottom (`penda`) of it. And after coming, the heat from the earth reached them. Just like if you put water in a vessel and heat it from below, steam starts to form and it starts to boil. That heat from the earth below reached it, and it went straight up and hit the roof of the pit. Then it became water. Now this water went up, it came down, it went up, it came down... He says that this happened many times. And this water was our body (`tan`). Just as a man's `mani` (semen) is, in that way this water became oily. How? It had the form of water, and an oily quality came into it. Stickiness came into it. And it got heat from below, and coolness from above. Where it got coolness from above, water would fall down from above. Then it got heat from below, it went up. This up and down motion, this fluctuation started, and becoming thicker and thicker, a stickiness came into it, and from it, the `surat-e-insani` (human form) was created. And when did this happen? When nine months, as we have here, when our nine months were complete, there the human `takhliq` (creation) happened inside the pit. It had no mother, no father. The pits themselves were its parents. There, the creation of man took place. And it has been said that... all the... I am telling about the creation at the Ka'ba. `Khudda` happened everywhere, but at the Ka'ba, the most noble `ruhani`... was given... there, 28 pits were made. And the 28 pits are... say that... how did they happen? ...the number 28 was everywhere, but the one we are describing, in those 28, one individual (`fard`) who was the very first, he was the `afzal-e-bashar` (most excellent of mankind). He is called `afzal-e-bashar`, he is `Jussa-e-Ibda'iyya`, he is `Kayumarth-e-A'zam`. The first individual of the whole world is called the most excellent person (`shakhs-e-fazil`). He was one. And with him were 27 others. So the count of 28 was complete. So how did it happen? 12, 12, and 3. And this count, remember, is with every Imam. Wherever the Imam's cabinet is, this is the count. So how? 12 will be one kind of `du'at`, 12 will be another kind of `da'i`, one will be a `mukasir`, one will be a `madh'un`, one will be a `Bab al-Abwab`. 24 and 3 is 27, and the Imam himself is 28. This structure has existed in the world from eternity (`azal`). And there's more above this, but let's leave that. That this structure was here, that one was there. So this count of 28 was completed. Now, it has been told here that this creation that happened... the form that was created, the man that was created, it was the entire male species (`mard-zat`). All 28... in the count... all of them were `mudhakkar` (masculine), men were created, women were not created. So in one pit, when one year passed over them... so how did the year pass? After all, they needed sustenance. Where would the sustenance come from? There was no one to take care of them, no one to look after them. That child, the naturally born child... I mean, at the beginning of the `dawr` (era), this kind of creation happens. I'll tell you this in the future, if Allah wills. So what happened to that child? He began to roll around in that same pit. The water that was left over became his food. Just as here, when a child is born, we first massage it with oil, and from its pores we provide it nourishment. Before that, we give these things. Just like that, he started rolling, and his nourishment happened there. When he became one year old... one year old, he was able to stand. And `du'at` say that that child... the naturally born child... it was not the child of weak parents. That naturally born child, because of that, his height was the same as our four-year-old child. 1 is to 4 ratio. Just as a one-year-old child here is not much, but a four-year-old child, whatever height he has, whatever strength he has, that is what happened to the first creation. And after that, what did he do? He came out of the pit. Now, hunger. There, it's not like with us where first milk is given, but when the age of milk is over, he will eat food. He came out. The whole world is adorned. There are figs, pomegranates, and all these things. So the fresh fruits, he started plucking and eating those fruits, and his nourishment was from that. Now from here... the creation of men happened. And after that, the water that was left in the pits remained there. Even after this happened, it remained. Now it is said that then again, the `khudda`... this is the second round (`nishist`). The second batch (`khep`) happened. And in it again, the same boiling (`ubal`) was created from below, and the water went up, down, up, down... and then the second form was created, and all of them were women. So in the first batch were men, and in the second were women. That is why it is... why are women considered weaker (`kamzor`)? Because their creation happened from the leftover water. `al-rijalu qawwamuna 'ala al-nisa'` (Men are the protectors and maintainers of women). The Qur'an says that `rijal` (men) are stronger (`qawi-tar`) than women. Why are they stronger than women? Because they were created from fresh water. And since women were created from the leftover water, they have a weakness and are called `nazuk` (delicate). After both were done, from these pits, 28 men were created, and then 28 women were created. 28 men were created there, 28 women here. They were created there, there... like this. This is the beginning of creation (`shuru'-e-afrinish`). Now after that, the men who were created from the pits, they went forward and married the women from the other pits. From here, the world's population began. Because these who were there, one who I am calling Imam, `Jussa-e-Ibda'iyya`, he was the Imam of the time. And his was the `tadbir` (plan). What kind of `tadbir`? The `dawr-e-kashf` (era of unveiling) began. Whenever the world has begun, it has begun with `kashf`. `Dawr, kawr, mawr`... this count will come up later. Insha'Allah, I will tell you, what the `dawr-e-sitr` is, what `kawr` is, what `mawr` is, what `nawr` is, and then जाकर... This happened at the beginning of creation. These women and men were created, and through the act of marriage, the population began to grow. From there, the system of `da'wat` started. Now, towards the system of `da'wat`, we will spend a couple of days... our time will be spent. And after that, there are many other things. All this that is happening is the description (`bayan`) of the `mabda'` (origin). This will go on for eight more days, and then after that, the description of the `ma'ad` (return/eschatology) will begin. Because it's possible that... our... 17th, 18th... when the Laylat al-Mubarak comes. Until then, perhaps I will finish this. Because then it will be very late. After the prayer... it will be done. So I want this to be finished by the 17th or 19th. The `bayan` of Hajj is longer. If you all want to sit, want to hear... the `bayan` of Hajj will take three or four days... a majlis is held in Madina Sharif so that those who have to go for Hajj in the future can get information. Anyway, this, the woman and man were created. So Allah's proposal (`tajwiz`) was this... that now this `Kayumarth-e-A'zam`, who is the Imam of the time, he was ordered to go and spread the `din` (religion). To their respective places, he sent a Nabi (prophet) and an Imam. This `Kayumarth-e-A'zam` was from the lineage of the `Mustaqarr` Imams. From here, the Imamate became widespread. It is said that on the first day, we created the Ka'ba... this was that Ka'ba. This `shakhs-e-fazil` (excellent person), he was the very first man. Through him, the `nasl-e-insani` (human lineage) continued, and after that, no other human lineage remained except for this one. `Wahidan ba'da wahid, waladan min walad ila yawm al-qiyamah` (One after another, son from son, until the Day of Resurrection). Every lineage will be destroyed, will end. But this lineage, which is of the Prophet Muhammad (S), it has been continuing from him. Whether it is a `dawr` or `kawr`, it is continuing. Now a `kawr`'s duration is so long that you will find it difficult to imagine. One `kawr` is of 16,000 crore years. 16,000 crore years is one `Kawr-e-A'zam`. Now how will you remember this? And in that, this lineage continues. For us, this much is enough... this is the duration. Then another `kawr`, a third, a fourth. Now, `dawr, kawr`, their stages (`manazil`) have also been fixed. What the first `kawr` is like, the tenth, the twentieth, the 400th, the 100th... their capabilities, their ranks, those are also in higher books... that also continues. Anyway, that this creation that I am telling you about, it happens at the beginning of a `kawr`. Now, we, in whichever `dawr` we are in, this is in the belly of the `kawr`. And here, the creation of man does not happen like this, but as it happens from the mother's womb. Now, look, yesterday I had said, this is the `ibtida'-e-da'wat` (beginning of the da'wat). That Allah's system (`nizam`) is that just as `ma-ba-al-aql al-awwal`... the first of creation... this `amr-e-kull`... is ours. This very teaching, to call people towards Allah, this is the institution (`idara`) of `da'wat-e-Hadi`. Just as `'Aql-e-'Ashir` or `Mumba'ith al-Awwal` had made a `da'wat`, here too we have a special institution which we call `da'wat-e-sharif`. That `da'wat-e-sharif` is what? To call towards Allah.
