The one who is its head, he is the one who calls towards Allah. The institution of `da'wat-e-sharif` is based on this very foundation. And this, the system of `da'wat`... its head, the highest personality, he is the Imam of every era. Now, look... the system of `da'wat` will come. That Nabi, Rasul, Imam, Wasi, Awliya, Qaim, Mahdi... what are these different posts? Their responsibilities. And whom should we consider great, whom small, what is `mustawda'`, what is `mustaqarr`, what is `nazr-e-ashraf`, what is `nazr-e-adwan`? All these stages, when we were in the `alam-e-hayat` (world of life), and our intentions (`niyyat`) were being divided... what were we doing? A `niyyat`... we were knitting. Various `niyyat` formed. We were split. Like pieces, we were scattered to many places. So at that time, the `niyyat` we were making... what happened? Today, what have come as our `jazbat` (emotions/sentiments), they were our `niyyat` there, that with whom we fixed our gaze (`nazar milaye hain`). Hazrat Muqaddas used to explain this very well, that with whom we have fixed our gaze, from what angle did we fix it? With love (`muhabbat`), or with hatred (`nafrat`), with revenge (`intiqam`), or with what? The `jazbat` that have come in us... that we, whom we saw only with the emotion of love... whether she was a woman or he was a man... only love. He has come here as a brother. Brother or sister. The ones who are our... from the same mother's womb, the children who are born. The relationship of a sister and brother is of absolute, unbreakable love. Now, if we fight amongst ourselves here, that's a different matter. But there, they are of one and the same. Brother means a companion. Why? Because from one single pit... where man and woman were created, there, from one mother, the brother and sister are. So this emotion of love, which is purely love, is only between brother and sister. In contrast, the mother has a different group, the father has a different one, all are different. I'll explain two or three things. There is one emotion which is hatred and love. Both are anti... The gaze of hatred and love that fell upon both, that is the husband and wife. The husband and wife have come in this. Hatred and love, not just love. And here the same thing happens, that every wife sometimes has hatred, sometimes love. They meet, then they separate. This is all the eternal system from there that is descending from above. And similarly, we see our Imam of the time, our Mansub of the time, our Da'i of the time... when we... we weren't in small rooms, we... the two Imams of whose time we fixed our gaze with... here they have become our Imams of the time. We are their `ummat` (community), we are born in the time of the Imam of the time. Now this is a different matter, that two, three Imams of the time... our gaze reached... that's how it is. Now look, from here... this is a slightly difficult `bayan` (discourse) coming up... that we, who profess the `dawr-e-Muhammadi`... for us, 'Isa 'alayh al-salam and Musa 'alayh al-salam... their `bayan` will come when it comes. But we have to take care of our own `dawr`. That Janab-e-Paygambar (S), Janab-e-Qaim, Janab-e-Mahdi, Janab-e-'Ali... what kind of system should we see for them? There, the gazes (`nazrein`), he used to say, the gazes are... when we are looking at the Murshid-e-Haqiqi, there, everyone was making a `da'wat`. And I have told you that the Ka'ba... this four-sided structure, where it was in the `abstract form`... I mean, here we say Ka'ba... the spiritual Ka'ba has a four-sided shape, but the `mawqif` (station) inside it... are those who are `wahidan ba'da wahid, waladan min walad`. And those around it, the `hawali` (environs), are all the `mustawda'`. `Du'at`, `anbiya'`, these... `hudud-e-din`, who are `mustawda'`. This is a subtle point, remember it. The `hawali`... the `Ka'ba-e-ruhani` is separate, and its 32... those around which we do `tawaf`... they are `mustawda'`. There, what happened? This `mustawda'` and `mustaqarr`... there, different gazes fell. These gazes... where we were at the beginning, with `'Aql al-Awwal` or `Mumba'ith al-Awwal`, there was a difference of gazes. How? That `nazr-e-ashraf` (most noble gaze) belonged to `Mumba'ith al-Awwal`, who was looking at the `cause`. And one was looking at the `sabab` (reason), and one was looking at the `anjam` (result). `Mumba'ith al-Awwal` and `Mumba'ith al-Thani`. The one who tried to grasp the `kull` (whole), who tried to grasp the `reason`, he advanced, and he became (`nazr-e-ashraf` prevailed). And the one whose gaze was lower (`adwan`), he will be called (`nazr-e-adwan`). Now here, the people of `nazr-e-ashraf` are those who have become our Imams... `Imam, Du'at-e-Balaagh, Madh'unin, Mukasirin`. And the people of `nazr-e-adwan` are those who are `da'i`, who are `mansub`, or `hujjat`, and `abwab`, and `mansub`. They are also masters, but they are their slaves. They are our masters, but their slaves. In this regard, there was a distinction between `hawali` and it. Here, after coming, the beginning happened with `dawr-e-kashf`. What will we call `dawr-e-kashf`? I will go into the `ta'wilat` (esoteric interpretations) for a bit. Look, here... `dawr-e-kashf` is that which is called when the Imam is present (`hazir`). And the time of light, i.e., daytime, is when the sun has risen. The `similitude` we are giving is that we have given the similitude of `dawr-e-kashf` and `dawr-e-sitr` as day and night. In terms of `din`, we are in the night, as the Imam is not present. And when the Imam is present, we will call it `dawr-e-kashf`. So, since when has `dawr-e-sitr` been going on? It started from Adam 'alayh al-salam and continues till now. In between... as a glimpse, the Imams like Imam Hasan, Imam Husayn, etc., were manifest, or even after them they were manifest... it is like the stars and the moon in a dark night. Otherwise, the whole night is a dark night. So since when is it going on? For 7000 years, the time of night has been going on. Now, look, we will call this night, when the sun has not risen. And when the sun has risen, we will call it day. Similarly, the `aftab` (sun) of the `din`... whose being is it? We will first say it is Janab-e-Paygambar (S). He is the `Natiq` of the time. He is, in the true sense, the `aftab` of the `dawr`. And his `wasi` (successor) will be the `mahtab` (moon). The `mahtab` of the `da'wat`. The `aftab` and `mahtab` are the Nabi and Wasi. In every era. Musa and Harun... these are prophet and `wasi`. So Janab-e-Natiq is the `aftab`. Now what is the proof (`dalil`) for this? You will see, it has come in the Qur'an that regarding the sun, many verses have come, that We have placed two lamps (`qindil`) in the sky. One we have made `mubsira` (perceptible) and one we have made `mah` (effaced). One is a thing that we cannot look at. We cannot look at the sun for five minutes. And we look at the moon and enjoy it. So looking at the sun... what is this? For the sun, the verse that has come is `sirajan wahhajan` (a blazing lamp). `wa ja'alna sirajan wahhajan` (And We have made [therein] a blazing lamp). We have made the sun a blazing flame, a ball of fire. `Wahhaj` means burning like a flame. Just as the word `siraj` has been used, `siraj` means a lamp, a blazing lamp. Similarly, for the Prophet, it has been said, `sirajan munira` (an illuminating lamp). So what does it mean? He has been called a `siraj` and that has been called a `siraj`. We can prove that... Janab-e-Paygambar (S), just as the `aftab` gives warmth to the world, gives life to the world, a cause of life, similarly our Nabi of the time was one because of whom we found guidance, because of whom we received his light. I mean, this is a physical `aftab`, that is a spiritual `aftab`. And similarly, the `mahtab` which has been given the similitude from Janab-e-'Ali ('alayh al-salam). Why? Because Janab-e-'Ali said that the Prophet opened for me the doors of knowledge, and from every door, a thousand other doors were opened for me. Similarly, whatever light the sun has, it is transferred to the moon. The moon has no independent light of its own. So it means Janab-e-'Ali acquired it from the `Bab` (door). Now, Janab-e-'Ali... the Nabi and Wasi, both have passed away from the world. Now the world that remains, how will it find guidance? In the time of the Nabi, there was guidance from the Nabi. If not the Nabi, then 'Ali. Now how? So, a lineage, a spring of guidance, has remained, which is the Imam of the time. In every era, there must be one `aftab`. Just as we cannot imagine the world without the `aftab`. If the `aftab`'s existence vanishes even for a moment, the whole world will fall into chaos. When you believe this, similarly in our `din`, in our `ruhaniyyat`, the `aftab`... if the existence of the Imam of the time is finished, the whole world will fall into chaos. So one after another (`wahidan ba'da wahid, waladan min walad`). After the father, the son. To the extent that, in the future you will hear, that one father performs `nass` (appointment) on his son, and then he... there is a gap of one, one and a half minutes, and at that very moment, his passing (`intiqal`) has to happen. Now, if it's visible in front, that's another thing. There are veils (`hijabat`), many... that's another matter. But in one and a half minutes, the `intiqal` of the Imam, the `walid` (father), has to happen. After performing `nass` and `tawfiq`. So this is a lineage that is continuing, of the `mustaqarr` and `mustawda'`. The `mustawda'` are the `walida` (mothers), they are their slaves, and the `mustaqarr` are the masters. Now what are the masters doing? They are sitting in the `hazarat` (presences). Now Nabi is coming, Da'i is coming, Mansub is coming, and they are running the `nizam-e-da'wat`, and the master is sitting there. Because now the world is so corrupt, the position is such that this luminous thing cannot come here. The level of the world is so bad, in the `dawr-e-sitr`, that the Imam cannot appear manifest (`zahir`). When the time comes, he will come in the `kashf`, and the world... all these vile people will be wiped out from the world. And at that time, there will be the rule of the believers (`mu'minin`), that will be called `dawr-e-kashf`. Just as in the light of day, everything is visible, similarly in the `dawr-e-kashf`, everything will be visible. And today the thing that is not visible, that we are facing hardship with good and bad, difficulties are there, it is like searching for something in the night. So the time of night is passing. For 7000 years, the night has been going on. So how many such nights have passed? You have no idea. How many nights have we finished. Just as... `As-sath-il-yahi wa'l-saba'at min misal al-falak` (?). That Allah has placed the foundation of `din` on the example of the sphere. The cycle of night and day has been set, that after a day, night must come, and after a night, day must come. Similarly, in `din`, after `kashf` comes `sitr`, after `sitr` comes `kashf`... it must come. A cycle is continuously going on. Now, is it that it will start with `kashf` and end with `sitr`? No. First `kashf` will come, then `sitr`, then `kashf`, then `sitr`. So how long will it go on? There should be a limit. Just as here there is a limit... that a day happened, then night, a day, then night. When seven days and seven nights pass, you will say a week (`hafta`) is over. A `week` is over. Similarly, a `week` also happens there, that when seven `kashf` and seven `sitr` pass, one `dawr` is completed. It is called a `dawr`. One period, a `period` is completed. Such `dawr`s... how much was it? 7000 years of `sitr`, and 50,000 years of `dawr-e-kashf`. 50,000 years, the `ma'ad` happens. I mean, you see Allah's system, that we are getting scared in these 7000 years, that what kind of world have we gotten into. But when we get the chance, the time of `kashf` is 50,000 years that we will enjoy. So compared to this world, to this `dawr-e-sitr`, that is paradise (`jannat`) for us. And a sample of that has been established in this very world, and that is the `hazarat` of our Imam (S). If you want to see the world of `kashf`, what `kashf` will be like... then the events of the `hazarat`... I can't narrate them now, but a sample of `kashf`, if you want to see, it is the `hazarat`, which we call earthly paradise (`jannat-e-ardi`), where Imam (S) is present. All good things are there. In this very world. And this is Allah's special `tadbir` (plan). Anyway, that... the period of `kashf` is 50,000 years, `sitr` is 7,000 years. So `kashf` and `sitr`, `kashf` and `sitr`. 57,000 years is one `unit`. Which we call a day, that the 17th has come, 18th, 19th. When this happens, when seven days pass on the world, then one `dawr` is complete. This `dawr`... how many years was it? It was of 4 lakh years. 3,99,000... I mean, 4 lakh years is one `dawr`. These 4 lakh `dawr`s, you keep placing them one after another. 4 lakh's first `dawr`, second, third, fourth, fifth... like this, place 4 lakh `dawr`s. Now see how much it is. 4 lakh years of the `dawr`... when you multiply this... the duration of one `kawr` comes out. Multiply 4 lakh by 4 lakh, then the duration of one `Kawr-e-A'zam` comes. One `Kawr-e-A'zam`. It is called `A'zam` because it is a very long period. This one `Kawr-e-A'zam` is 16,000 crore years. I have calculated it, you can calculate it on a computer. Now after this, if you take this `kawr`, and multiply the duration of the `kawr` by 4 lakh, then what duration will come? 18 years ago, in Bombay, at the university there, Hazrat Qibla were there... new IBM computers had come out... There, my nephew was the head. He said, 'Come see, a new thing has arrived.' 18 years ago. So he calculated 4 lakh by 4 lakh... the duration that came... on the screen it showed... it came that we cannot describe this, you just add zeros. 25 zeros were added. That is the duration of `mawr`. After `kawr` comes `mawr`. The duration of `mawr` is such that you cannot even express it. Arab, kharab, what will you say? This 16,000 crore years is just the duration of a `kawr`. And on every `kawr`, this will happen, that creation will happen naturally like this. Otherwise, the durations of the `dawr`s, one `dawr`, second `dawr`, third `dawr`... 4 lakh `dawr`s will pass, we will be born from the mother's womb in this way. Now, in one `kawr`'s duration, there are also divisions in it. I am leaving that out, when we move forward, perhaps Insha'Allah I will explain it. Anyway, that... look, it's not expected that this `bayan` will come in another majlis, but I am telling you with some detail... that Mawlana Zayn al-'Abidin (`salawatullah 'alayh`)... very high-ranking Imams. You cannot even conceive of their spiritual rank (`ma'rifat`). In Mawlana Zayn al-'Abidin's time, there was a `Bibi` who was Umm al-Da'i. She was at the rank of `hujjat`, a very high rank. She said, 'Mawla, now she is asking this 1000 years ago. Today we are in the 1400s. At that time, she said, 'Mawla, tell me, what is the duration of the world?' How long is the duration of the world? He said, 'O Umm al-Da'i, whatever duration has passed, I will tell you. What is left, I will not tell.' So what does this mean? That the Imam knows both. `Ma kana wa sa-yakun` (What was and what will be), he knows. So the Imam is counting the duration. Now you see, the duration of a `kawr`... when 1000 years... if you multiply it by that, what duration will a `kawr` be? So now we have to calculate... you are all educated and read magazines... In every era, this search is on, how old is the world. Every person, someone says billion years, someone million... 10 million years old... artifacts are being found, fossils are being found... You know... in the Islamic world, the Muslims... they believe Adam 'alayh al-salam was the first. And not just the Islamic world, but Christians too. And if you add Christians and Muslims, it's more than half the population. They believe that Adam 'alayh al-salam was the first. If Adam 'alayh al-salam was the first... then how are human fossils being found from 10,000 years ago? How are human artifacts found? Or Heidelberg, Java, and Sumatra man... skulls have been found. Which, through anthropology, have been given flesh, and they look completely human. In Heidelberg, in Java, in Sumatra, they have been found. 1 lakh years old. 10 lakh years old. And it's a human form. Then how can Adam 'alayh al-salam be the first? That is why when someone asked Mawlana 'Ali, 'Who was before Adam?', he said, 'Adam'. 'Before that Adam?' 'Adam'. He kept asking, and he kept saying 'Adam'. He said, 'If you ask me all night, I will say Adam'. The world is so old that you cannot count it. And as I said yesterday, the one who starts every period is named Adam, and the one who ends every period is named Qaim. So this, which Mawlana Zayn al-'Abidin, Umm al-Da'i asked, 'How much duration has passed?', he said... now look, how precise this is. Here they are just guessing, looking at rocks, searching... looking at butterflies, insects, and from that they are calculating how much duration of the world has passed. Some say billion, some trillion. But the correct... after every ten years, a new doctor, a new scientist comes and presents his new theory. But Mawlana sat there and said that one `Mawr-e-A'zam` has passed over the world. Now, see `Mawr-e-A'zam`. It will go over your head. One `Mawr-e-A'zam` has completely passed. The second `Mawr-e-A'zam`... A `mawr` is 4 lakh `kawr`. One `Mawr-e-A'zam` has passed. Of the second `mawr`, 399 `kawr` have passed. Of the 400th `kawr`, we are in its first `dawr`, of which this is the last `sitr`. Now tell me, is this knowledge better, or what science is telling you? One `Mawr-e-A'zam` has passed. 399 `kawr` have passed. Of the 400th `kawr`, when it began, we are in its first `dawr`, and in the end of that `dawr`. 'End of the `dawr`' means the `dawr` is finishing. So when the `dawr` finishes, what happens? When a week finishes... you say today is Thursday. The week is finishing. So Thursday day has passed, and Thursday night has passed. And before that, the days have passed. So seven `sitr` have passed, seven `kashf` have passed. Seven `kashf` have already passed in our `dawr`. Now the seventh `sitr` is ending, which started with Adam 'alayh al-salam. So this is the exact thing, that in the last era... In this regard, our `qiyamat` (resurrection)... I am taking the `bayan` forward... Our `qiyamat`... which is called `qiyamat-e-saghira`, `qiyamat-e-wusta`, `qiyamat-e-kubra`, `qiyamat-e-uzma`. All have different meanings. Just as a day has a calculation, a week has a different one, that this is the plan for the week, this has to be done. Then when a month is over, the month's calculation is different. Similarly, we... should we call this the `qiyamat` of the `sitr`? Or of the `dawr-e-kashf` and `sitr`? Or of the `dawr`? Which `qiyamat` is this? This is the `qiyamat` of the `dawr`. The `qiyamat` of the `dawr` means the 4 lakh years that have passed from Adam 'alayh al-salam till here... its reckoning is not happening, but of 4 lakh years. 4 lakh years' reckoning... that `qaim` is coming who will take the reckoning of 4 lakh years. So he is not `Qaim`, but `Qaim-e-Muntazar`. `Qaim-e-Muntazar`, not `Muntazir`. `Muntazar`, whose wait has been going on for years. 4 lakh years people have been waiting for him, that a `qaim` will come, a `qaim` will come. He came at the `qiyamat` of the `sitr`, he will come at the `qiyamat` of the `dawr-e-kashf` and `sitr` too. But this is the one of the `dawr`. He is a very high-ranking being. Now, this future system that will be there... after the passing of `Imam-e-Zaman`, there will be many stages to understand. That our Imam... first week, second week, third week... these Imamates are `nashista` (seated). If Janab-e-Qaim is sitting on a high throne, then where is Mawlana Zayn al-'Abidin? Where is Mawlana 'Ali? And where is the Prophet? Where is Janab-e-Mahdi? And where is Janab-e-Isma'il, Janab-e-Ibrahim, Janab-e-Hunayn? To understand all these things is to understand the structure of `din`. This is according to Imamate. According to Nubuwwat, it's different. According to `Wahdat` it's different. `Mustawda'` is separate, `mustaqarr` is separate. `Mustawda'` are those who are lower. Now the `bayan` will go further... that the `majaziyyat` will go further, and when it is final, how it will be. So that the world can see with its own eyes how things are done. Yes, there's a question. You said that from the pits, 28 were created. After that, women were created. So the `ashraf` (most noble) pits of Khana-e-Ka'ba, from them men were created. Then from those same pits again, the creation of women... so will all those women also be `afzal` (most excellent) in the same way? Of course. The Imam's wife is also an `Imam`. The Imam's wife is... one Imam's wife is another Imam's mother. `Wahidan ba'da wahid`. So they are very lofty. If you read the book, you will find out what the rank of an Imam's wife is. There is no question for which there is no answer. Hazrat Zaynab (`'alayha al-salam`) used to say that questions have not yet been created for which our answers have not already come. You cannot ask questions. We have so many answers with us. There is only one thing, as I told you the day before yesterday, why did Allah create the `alam-e-ruhani` (spiritual world)? We have many who think and ponder, who are very philosophical. They go and say, 'What is this, this `alam-e-ruhani`, why was it created?' Then finally they get stuck. No one has the answer to this. When we cannot understand our own `nafs` (self), how can we understand Allah's `tadbir`, that why Allah created the `alam-e-ruhani`? We cannot. The 28 who were created from the pits... this `tamam`... will be known in the `ma'ad` that `hamur`... `dajis`. `Jist`. From it this... That is there, but in it, what is it that the Imam of the time is superior. `Natiq`... this is a matter of `ba'd` (eschatology). More noble. They were more noble. But one was the most noble. And after that, there were 12 `da'i`s. Just like that, there are 12 `da'i`s in the world. Now, there aren't 12 `da'i`s because the world is in a very bad state now. Only three جزیره are active. Yemen, Hindustan, and Sindh. That's it. So there are 12 `da'i`s. These 12 and these 12... they went and became 24. And after 24, there is one `mukasir` and one `madh'un`. `Madh'un` is below the `mansub`. To whom permission is given. And one is `Bab al-Abwab`, who controls the 12 and 12. `Bab` is... `Bab al-Abwab` is the door of doors. The one who is the way to reach the Imam. One `mukasir` and one `madh'un` are also there. With us, who is `mukasir`, `madh'un`, the rank of `mukasir` is not told. The `bab` of the `bab` is told. But the `mukasir` is not. He is kept secret. You can understand who the `mukasir` can be. He is below the `mansub`. Now, this, `mukasir`, `madh'un`, and `Bab al-Abwab`, these are three, and these 12 and 12 are 24. These are 27, and one Imam makes 28. The count of 28 is complete. Now these are the most noble (`ashraf-tarin`), and from them the lineage of Imamate is running. The lineage of Imamate. But it will run from the Imam, not from the others. Not from the `lazimin` (those who adhere). They are from the `lazimin`. But they are `ashraf-tarin` in the sense that they came in the first batch. They were given a special opportunity. And in it too, there are many subtleties that I am leaving out... that as soon as they came, the first batch... is so excellent that as soon as they came, they became `jawharani`. They went up. He could not wait even for a moment... that is our standard is low. There are many things... then where is its place, its succession... many such matters. Anyway, that they are from the `lazimin`. We are among them... `Sulalat al-Nuraniyyah` is one. At what number are we? We cannot locate it. In 15 groups, we are also one. From `falak-e-muhit` down to here. I mean, whoever is from the `Ahl-e-Haqq` (people of truth), they are in the 15. If we are truly `lazimin`. Now where are we, what is it, we shouldn't get into that. The stars... `lazimin` means only this much: we have been brought into the human form (`khawat-e-insani`). If we were not in this form, we would be somewhere else. What do you know, which place we would be, what is it. We have been brought into this form, so we have intellect (`'aql`), we can speak... So what is it? We are in the `khawat-e-'arifah`. For that, we should be thankful. We could have been in the stars, where, where... In the `alam-e-'alam`, many things have been created. Taking from there, the `dayan`s will bring you... where, from where... these are very subtle things. And then the `mu'min` progresses like this, that in the `ma'ad`, he gets such a rank that the `ma'ad`'s section is for us to understand. The first thing is that we should see our own progress. `Ma'ad al-Mahmud`. Our progress is... after this, it will start tomorrow or the day after. Our system of `da'wat`, then the `ma'ad` will come, and then `ma'ad al-mahmud`, `ma'ad al-madhmum`... `abrak`, `sakra`... there are ten days more in it. [unclear] Not only Sulaymani. There are others too. But in the `khawat-e-'arifah`, in the human form, we are. All others are in other places. Now why they have been kept there, that is Allah's `tadbir`. When their turn comes, they will come into the `khawat-e-'arifah`. We cannot say what Allah's purpose is, that they are kept there. And all the `du'at` are in higher books... their names have been given. They tell this much, that 15 are these, 20 are these, and 35 are these. And in this regard... the Prophet said that `sataftariqu ba'di ummati thalathan wa sab'ina firqah` (My community after me will split into seventy-three sects). After me, my `ummat` will be divided into 73 sects. And this division of the `ummat` is not that Sunni, Shia, this and that, but these are the sects which are 35, 20, and 15. And its total is 70. And a conclusion (`khulasa`) is drawn from each one. That conclusion is three. And in 73, there is one which is the conclusion of the `lazimin`. He will rise from here. And at the very end, I will mention this. That from here, leaving this, the one who... now you are asking how we will go to the sky. Here you are sitting, there you are sitting. There is progress. From one place to another. But this is partial (`juz'i`) progress. Not total (`kulli`). It's not like you left from here. So when will total progress happen? In which period will it happen? That is at the end, which is `falak-e-muhit`. You said that the Imam, after performing `nass`... passes away in about one and a half to two minutes. So Hazrat Ja'far al-Sadiq ('alayh al-salam) performed `nass` on Hazrat Isma'il ('alayh al-salam). Then after that, his life... there's a lot of debate on it. It has been told that in the apparent (`zahiri`), it does not happen. The `hijab` (veil) of `kitabat`... the `hijab`s were there. And there was a great purpose (`hikmah`) behind it. If you read the books of `hijabat`, you will understand. Everything that is there, it is not hidden in the apparent. It's such that there are many things that are apparent, which we cannot understand, until we understand the whole affair of `hijabat` and `munjibat`. `Hijab` and `munjib` are very lofty topics, there are many things. Now we are in an era, a time, when in the Bombay majlis, we should understand `hijab` and `munjib` so that it has an effect in our prayers too. That when we recite `subhana` etc., what is `muhtajib`, what is `muhtajab`, what is `satween`... What is `satween`? `Satween` is also a `munjib`. In their respective circles. The Da'i's circle is different, the Hujjat's circle is different, the Bab al-Abwab's circle is different, the Imam's circle is different, the `hawalayn`'s circle is different. For each one, the name... the other day I... you, as much as you remember, if it gets fixed in your mind, that's enough. To do `sajda` (prostration), to pray, is only for Allah (SWT). But this awareness, for the sake of information, you should have it. So that our prayer is the prayer of the knowledgeable (`'alimin`), not the prayer of the ignorant (`jahilin`). That whatever we are doing, we should do it with understanding. They are also observing Laylat al-Qadr, we are also observing. What's the difference? They are also awake all night, we are also awake. If we understand what `Tasbih-e-Fatimah` is, and what `Tasbih al-A'zam` is, and what they have. About the `hijab` and `munjib` you mentioned... So if we assume that Hazrat Isma'il ('alayh al-salam)... his `hijab`... There is a question, but... let it be. Yesterday in the `Jannat` newspaper, there was a topic... Two or three days ago, in the `Shabab` magazine, there was a talk of `taslim-e-tin`. Surah `al-Tin`. The verse `wa'l-tin wa'l-zaytun`. That Allah has sworn by the fig (`anjir`), by the olive (`zaytun`), and by `Tur-e-Sinin`. I swear by this, for example. So here, Allah is swearing by these things. This whole `bayan` has not opened up. In that, you gave the similitude of the Prophet ('alayh al-salam), but still, that is lesser than Allah. And when we swear, we swear by Allah, someone greater than us. Why is Allah swearing by things lesser than himself? The matter is not like that. Rather... in another book which is very beneficial for us, `Manazil`... these are matters of the `batin` (esoteric). It is not known that who is Allah there. Who is swearing by what, is not known. 'O Prophet, swear by this, the `tin`.' The Prophet is not less than the `tin`. Swear by `tin`, i.e., Janab-e-Adam (`salawatullah 'alayh`). Janab-e-Adam. You, if I tell you... you say we swear by someone greater. If you come to a situation where you have given a loan and you want to convince someone that you will return the money... You say, you put your hand on your child's head and say, 'I swear I will.' The child is smaller than you, or bigger? So, one also swears by the dearest thing. Here, the `tadbir` that is there... they are all different similes. The `bayan` of `majlis-e-ta'wil` is different. `Tin` is Janab-e-Adam (`S`), `zaytun` is Janab-e-Nuh, `Tur-e-Sinin` is Janab-e-Musa... And its conclusion... it's possible that in the `majalis` of Mawlana 'Ali and others, in the `majalis` of ta'wil, the `tasbih` of `tin` is explained in more detail. The whole... their words, I will present them according to that, so that the complete matter is understood. A summary is not like that. The `mahmuda` animals that were created after the pit... were they the first ones? The details are not available. We are in the `dawr-e-Muhammadi`, so you will give importance to Muhammad (S). But they are talking about the `mutahharin` and all that. The Prophet's words, his gaze is on the whole universe. What we are seeing, we are seeing from the Islamic perspective. That the 73 sects happened, did not happen... We are in difficulty. The Prophet told about 73, this is 70. For us, it makes no difference. That meaning is also wrong. Look, I am telling you the Prophet's hadith. How far will you take the hadith? I'll tell you a short hadith. According to the translation of the hadith, if you take the meaning... ...what meaning will you understand? The Prophet (S) says in a hadith: 'Give the laborer his wage before his sweat dries.' Now tell me, this, which is written in `Aqwal-e-Zarrin`... what good words have been said. Big operators, industrialists, they have hung up that the Prophet said... 'give the laborer his wage before his sweat dries.' Now, you can say this, and I can say this. A personality like the Prophet, has he come to say this nice thing? There is depth in it. There are many things behind it. There are treasures. So what will you understand? The laborer's sweat, what will you call it? This has become a moral lesson. Every good-mannered person will say, 'Yes, it should be given early.' Or, give an advance before the work is done. This happened. Now, if a personality like the Prophet says this, then what are these worldly things... 'weigh correctly', what does this mean? 'Don't give short measure'. It has come in the Qur'an... 'what is it?'. Has the Prophet come to tell these things? All the verses of the Qur'an and the hadith have meanings behind them. What are they? The laborer's sweat... the laborer, in the true sense, is the `mu'min`. The real one is the `mu'min`. That the `mu'min`, when he passes away, when the time of `sakarat` (death throes) comes, the state of `niza'`... life is over. At that time, his `surat-e-ruhani` (spiritual form), which is his wage, what he has earned, or done in his life, that `surat-e-ruhani` is shown to him. And seeing that, his face beams with joy. Then what happens? He sees his `murshid-e-haqiqi`... the form is shown. Mawlana 'Ali is shown. `Ya Harith Hamdan, man yamut yarani` (O Harith Hamdan, whoever dies sees me). 'O Harith Hamdan, whether he is a `kafir` (unbeliever) or a `mu'min`, everyone sees me.' A `kafir` sees it in a frightening way, a `mu'min` sees it with joy. Janab-e-'Ali is seen. The Imam of the time is seen. And his own `murshid`, with whom he has taken the `'ahd` (covenant), he comes near him. And then, the laborer's... your `surat-e-ruhani`. What you have done as provision for the hereafter (`zad-e-akhirat`). If you see your report before admission to college, you will be happy. That is the wage. 'Before the laborer's sweat dries' means before his breath leaves him, it is shown to him. Now, see this meaning, and see what is written in the books of hadith. There's a world of difference between this meaning and that. All hadiths, their meanings, Sayyidna Hasan ibn Hibat-Allah (`'alayhi al-salam`), a very high-ranking `da'i`. He has written the `haqa'iq` (realities/esoteric meanings) of them. Of the hadith. The `haqa'iq` of every single hadith. That work, Hazrat Janab Sahib Muqaddas is doing now in the Munajat. Because we have the book of hadith... we read the translation in the hadith book and we become happy that we have understood. The `Abu Dinar`... that is from `falak-e-mirrikh`... it came down from above. It is not below. I will explain it when the opportunity comes. It is our `mustaqarr`. But why did it come? Where is the place of `al-Ma'mur`? Above `falak-e-muhit`, or... above. It is in the `alam-e-hayat`. It is in the `alam-e-hayat`. `Falak-e-muhit` is the final limit of the physical world. The `bayan` of that will come a little later. The physical world begins from `falak-e-muhit`. And all these things of the `alam-e-hayat` are above it. And its connection is made with us. And when? When there is `ma'ad`. `Ma'mur` is also there. And `Hudud` are also there. Yes, they are there. They are in the `alam-e-hayat`. [Prayer] [Prayer] [Prayer] Sabak Number 4. This is the outline of what was said before. It's written lightly. I'll just explain it. You have heard that the `alam-e-ruhani` is the upper part. After that is the `alam-e-hayat`. And this is the firmament. Below it are the spheres (`kurrat`). Here, the `alam-e-jismani` (physical world)... below this moon (`qamar`) is the `alam-e-jismani`. And its explanation has been done. So if you haven't understood it, you can ask those people. [unclear] Here, below `falak-e-qamar` (sphere of the moon), these are the `ummat`s. These are two stars, which are called `madhnus` stars. They are also called `Rahu` and `Ketu`. They are `ra's` and `dhanab`. They are `'uqdatayn` (two nodes). `'Uqdatayn` means two knots. When we tie a knot, it is called `'uqdah`. It gets closed. So there are two `'uqdah`s which have to be below `falak-e-qamar`. They have been taken out from the group of spheres (`mutaf-e-falak`), from the `aflak` (spheres). They have been thrown down. They have been removed from the `aflak`. Now its `bayan` will come later. I am just explaining the top and bottom. And this is your `kurra` (sphere) of air, sphere of water, and sphere of earth. These four spheres are where... ...the `tahayyul` is kept. `Shah`... this is the part of the `mutahharin`. This is the part of the `lazimin`. And above this is the `alam-e-hayat`. Above the `alam-e-hayat` is the `alam-e-ruhani`. The `bayan` has gone up to `'Aql-e-'Ashir`. And after that, this also happened yesterday... the `'Amud al-Nur` and the `Ka'ba-e-Ruhani` are in the part of the `alam-e-hayat`. And where, at what time the act of `hubut` (descent) happened... the order for `hubut` happened. You can see in the `munajat`, that `ihbitu`... ...'throw them down'. This rebellious group... who is saying, 'I will not obey', throw them down. At the moment that it was said, with the command, one person obeyed. And he was from the group of `lazimin`. And with him, 27 and 12... they stayed right there. And they were very noble people. The one who is, he is `Adam-e-Bidayah`. `Adam-e-Bidayah`, the head of all. He is there. And with him are 27 and 12. The 12 are called `mudabbirin`. Now these `mudabbirin`, this is a bit complicated, where the `mudabbirin` went and what they became. For now, understand this much, that after that the `aflak`... which, the `planets` as many as there are, the seven `sayyara`s, they were entrusted with the `tadbir`. And as I told you yesterday, the `tadbir` was given to `Zuhal`. After `Zuhal`, `Mushtari` was joined with `Zuhal`. In the third thousand years, `Mirrikh` was joined. In the fourth thousand years, with the sun. In the fifth thousand years, with `Zahra`. Then with `'Utarid`, then with `Qamar`. These were completed, 7000 years, and they are called the `dawr` of `Zuhal`. After that, the `dawr` of `Mushtari` came, then another 7000 years passed in the world. And in it, different... different `adwar` (eras) passed, and here different things appeared. In some, small insects were created. In another place, greenery came. In plants, just as... the combination of two stars, according to that, the `takhliq` (creation) started happening below. The good ones... in the time of the sun, if in minerals, gold and silver were created, then in animals, the `haywan-e-mahmud` (praiseworthy animal), the horse, and all the noble... ...their creation happened. The same in animals, same in minerals, same in plants. In plants, the `musammar`, i.e., fruit-bearing trees, were created. This went on and on, and when the turn of the moon came, in the 49th thousand year, the `dawr` came. After 42. In that 7000 years, `khamire` (leavens) started to be made. `Khamire` started to be made. For whom? For humans. Now, look, this `khamir` is being made. And being made, being made, it went, and in the last 49th thousand year, at that time, the manifestation of man happened. And as we heard yesterday, how `khudda`... people came out of them. And this has been explained, that the most noble of the `khudda` were at Khana-e-Ka'ba. After that, in Madina-e-Tayyiba, in Masjid al-Nabawi, in Masjid al-Aqsa, and in... here, in Jannat al-Baqi', in Masjid Quba. All the sacred places we have today, all are signs of the `khudda`. Sayyid 'Abdullah Hamid, who came with Sayyidna Muqaddas... here... 20, 25 years ago, our first Hajj was with them. We went to Masjid Quba. There, for the `ziyarat`, Sayyidna Sahib said, 'Hamid, you take them.' All of us who were with him, we all went for the `ziyarat`. There, as we were getting out of the car, he said, 'Ibrahim, look here. What is this?' So I, according to the books, told him, and he said, 'Yes, this is the `khudda`.' That was, at that time, our knowledge of 17, 18 years ago. He was a very lofty personality. And he said that here the `khudda` are kept. Now after that, there is a lot of water... when the `bayan` of Hajj comes, and the story of Masjid Quba, and Masjid al-Nabawi, what treasures are kept there. Why, after Masjid al-Nabawi, is Masjid Quba given rank in Madina-e-Munawwara? First. To the extent that the Prophet (S) first came to Masjid Quba. Masjid Quba, Quba is a suburb (`zayli`) of Madina Sharif, a lower part. You first dismounted there, at the house of Abu Ayyub al-Ansari, and from there you came to Madina-e-Munawwara inside. So that was the place where the Prophet (S) dismounted. Masjid Quba. Today a very big mosque has been built. At that time it was very small. But the artifacts that were there, they are from that time when they were kept. Anyway, that this is the state of the planets. And after that, when the creation was done, humans were created, both men and women. As I told you, the `dawr` always starts with `kashf`. `Dawr-e-kashf` was going on for 50,000 years. At that time, all the Imams were present, the Imamate was running, and... the whole `da'wat-e-sharif` was working in its entirety. The `dawr`... in the `dawr-e-kashf`, the whole `da'wat` is active. After this, it happened that as soon as the `dawr-e-sitr` came... I am talking about the first `sitr`. Now on our own `sitr` we will apply it, because `dawr-e-kashf` and `dawr-e-sitr` have been happening one after another. Where `sitr` comes... now this should be understood, I have repeated this several times. The `dawr-e-sitr` is of 7,000 years. `Dawr-e-kashf` is always 50,000 years. First `kashf` comes, and then `sitr` comes. Similarly, as day comes first, and then night comes. When this day passes, any thing, as it continues, continues, continues, in the final days, a sluggishness comes into it. Any thing. You also, working from the morning, by evening you get tired, and you feel that you are tired. That fatigue, I mean, when 50,000 years pass, after 47,000 years, sluggishness starts to come in the `din`. Those who were obeying the Imam, all those... they were obeying perfectly. Now what happened? Leaving the venerable elders... in that `dawr-e-fitrat`... they started going to the `'amil`s (practitioners of occult arts). They adopted `ruhaniyyat`. Palmistry, geomancy (`raml`), `jafr`, all these sciences are the product of that era, which is called `dawr-e-fitrat`. In the `dawr-e-fitrat`, they started going to the `'amil`s, leaving the venerable elders. Now what happened here? This period is specified, that it will be of 3,000 years. And in it, great `awliya-e-kiram` passed. `Qawaim` passed. Not ordinary people. But they passed away in hiding. Because now it is the time of evening. As darkness falls in the evening, just as night is about to come, evening comes. Similarly, before `dawr-e-sitr` comes, `dawr-e-fitrat` comes. And it is of 3,000 years. When 3,000 years were completed... at that time, the `sardar` (leader) of that era... the end of `dawr-e-kashf` and the beginning of `dawr-e-sitr`... the Imam who was there, was Mawlana Hunayn (`salawatullah 'alayh`). This is for all our `sitr`s. Take any `sitr`, it's the same thing. So he, when he came, he said that now the `dawr-e-kashf` is over, and the `dawr-e-sitr` is starting. At this time, what happens? In this situation, the Imamate is hidden. Just as when night comes, the sun is hidden. Similarly, the Imamate, the Imams are hidden. And in their place, their agents... just as the sun is hidden, night comes, stars come out. Similarly, in the `din`, stars, `manasib`, `du'at`, `hujjat`, these people start working for the `din`. And the Imamate is sitting in the `hazarat`. The Imam is sitting in the `hazarat`. No one can deny his existence. He is sitting in his own place. But he is hidden from sight. Similarly, as the `aftab` is hidden. Today we think that the `aftab`... at this time we are sitting, for us, we feel that the `aftab` is not visible. Can you deny its existence? Never. It is hidden from sight. It is hidden. You cannot deny it. That since it is not visible now... you call America, it is out there. Call any country, it is out there. Similarly, as our Imam is hidden from our sight... but where the `hazarat` are, there they are working, all things are happening. It happens on this very earth. This is a strange power of God, that the `hazarat-e-Imamiyyah`... and I will explain further... ...that in the `hazarat-e-Imamiyyah`, there is a problem, a term. That the `hazarat-e-Imamiyyah` can be anywhere. In today's world, the people who live, such new adventures have happened, such new communications have come... that man can go anywhere. Why can't we reach the `hazarat`? I will explain this through science, in another two or four `majalis`. Anyway, that `kashf` and `sitr`, day and night, have been alternating, and the era has been moving forward. Now look, the duration I told you yesterday, that the world... Umm al-Da'i asked, 'O Mawla,' to Mawlana Zayn al-'Abidin, 'how much age has passed?' This, I am repeating this because many new listeners did not understand some things. They said, 'Repeat it, so that we understand.' So, this happened, that Mawlana Zayn al-'Abidin (`salawatullah 'alayh`) said, 'O Umm al-Da'i, whatever has passed, `ma mada`, the thing that has happened, has passed, I will tell you that. What is left, I will not tell.' This means that the Imam knew that as well. `Ma kana wa sa-yakun`. Then Janab-e-Imam said that one `Mawr-e-A'zam` has passed on the world. Of the second `mawr`, 399 `kawr` have passed. Of the 400th `kawr`, we are in the last `sitr` of the first `dawr`. Now `sitr` is going on. And `sitr` continuing means that 7000 years have started. So seven `kashf` have passed. Six `sitr` have passed. This is the last `sitr`. Seven and seven is fourteen. Seven `sitr`, seven `kashf`. This is the last `sitr`, because night comes at the end. So the night is ending, so our week is ending, just as we are sitting on Thursday night. A new week has been holidayed from tomorrow. Similarly, this is the last part. The seventh `sitr` is ending, so not only will the `qiyamat` of the `sitr` come... as I told you, that every `sitr` has a `qaim`. A `qaim` comes at the end of every `sitr`. So that `Qaim-e-sitr`'s status is different. Then... when a day passes, i.e., 17th day passed, so the night of the 17th and the day of the 17th, both together make one date. So 57,000 years passed. I mean 50,000 years earlier, and 7,000 years later. This has a `qaim`, `Qaim-e-dawr-e-kashf wa sitr`. His status is different. And then after that, seven `sitr` and seven `kashf` that passed, a very high-status `qaim` will come. Whose name is `Qaim-e-Muntazar`. `Qaim-e-Muntazar`, whose wait is going on for years. I mean, not 7000 years, but 4 lakh years. That `qaim`, his question will be that. He will take the reckoning of 4 lakh years. That when he comes, all this population that has grown, which cannot be contained, all of it will be finished. What will be punished will be punished, what will be transformed into minerals, plants, and animals will be transformed. And then the `dawr-e-kashf` will come, in which only `mu'minin` will be there. There will be no one else. Some `du'at` say that perhaps some such `Ahl-e-kashi`, `Ahl-e-shia`, who sincerely believe in Mawlana 'Ali but are not `walawi` and `ulwi`, they might also remain in the `dawr-e-kashf`. But `mu'minin` will remain. Now you see, in this big world, if we are only this many `mu'minin`... Hazrat Janab Sahib used to say that there could be lakhs. So all these, if they are spread across the world, then everyone will be a multi-millionaire. They can live comfortably, eating and drinking. All these troubles will go away. The `dawr-e-kashf` is the name of this, that is `jannat`. For the future `ummat`, the `dawr-e-kashf` is `jannat` compared to our `dawr-e-sitr`. Today, the trouble we are enduring in the world, the `hazarat` is more blissful than that. In the `hazarat`, who are the `mu'minin`? Here, who are the `mu'minin`? There is a difference. See, in the `hazarat`, there are `mu'minin`, there are `hudud` too, `siddiqin`, `kiran`, `manasib`, `du'at`, great venerable elders. So why, these venerable elders, our `du'at` have passed away, very high-ranking `da'i`s, they explain everything, so why didn't they go to the `hazarat`? Why are they not in the `hazarat`? The matter of the `hazarat` is... only he goes to the `hazarat` whose `qisas` (retribution/reckoning) is finished. When `qisas` is finished, he will not come here again. And no one finishes it. It keeps going on. Hatred is going on, enmity is going on, revenge is going on, all things are being added to the account by one's own hand. You don't know, he is forgiving, but one account is closing, another is opening. So this cycle of `qisas` will not stop. He will not get release from here. `Qisas` is such a matter that it goes all the way up to the `hijabat`. They are stuck in the `chut` (?). Why was Janab-e-Husayn martyred? Why was this `da'i` oppressed? Why was this one... this happened... all these things are that the `hudud al-din`... though they are very lofty, the `mu'minin` and `hudud` in the `hazarat`, they might even be greater in rank, in status. But they are stuck here because of `qisas`. There are illnesses, there are thoughts... that is why they are brought here to this prison. Otherwise, he could have been... Hazrat Qibla used to say that there are many who might be higher than those there. I mean, according to their hadith, their conduct. But they are trapped in `qisas` here. The one who has crossed `qisas`, he has gone to the `hazarat`. Now what is this `qisas`? Many here don't understand `qisas`. It's a story (`qissa`). What is `qisas`, how... look, it has stages (`madarij`). `Qisas` doesn't just happen. First, in it, there is `istihsan`. Man falls in `istihsan` first. The first link is of `istihsan`. `Istihsan` means... that we appreciate a thing from another religion (`ghayr-madhhab`). We look at it with a gaze of praise. Without taking Allah's name, we just keep praising it. That affirmation (`taslim`), that `istihsan`, is a cause of trouble for us. Two things. And the second thing, to get trapped... it's a swamp. Where you put one foot, the second foot also gets stuck, and you go deeper inside. The second thing is `maylan` (inclination). `Maylan` is... which is more than `istihsan`. And the third number is `qisas`. So `istihsan`, `maylan`, and `qisas`. So what is `istihsan`? Appreciation. That... I am giving examples. That we... a thing from another religion... what is it? The English say that we wear a tie because it's a sign of culture. It's our symbol. I am just giving an example to understand. And we, if we... to protect our throat from cold, wear it, or say to fulfill our office duty, wear it, then it's not a bad thing. But no, the boss said it. The English have... that we make it our attire. This attire is the best... it looks good, and all things... its qualities, it's very easy to explain its qualities. If you keep explaining, then what is it? It's appreciation. This is `tahsin`, this is `istihsan`. You are looking at that thing, which is of another religion, with a gaze of `istihsan`. This is `istihsan`. What is the second thing? `Maylan`. What is `maylan`? You are looking at that thing. Now you have worn it. 'Oh, you look very good wearing that thing.' 'I should also wear it, it looks good.' Now what is this? This is `willingness`. There it was appreciation, now it's `willingness`. 'I also want to... if not today, then tomorrow I will wear it.' This is what I use. Like in women. What are women doing? They see the customs of others. In India, with Hindus... society... What is it? 'If you just put on a little bindi, your beauty will increase four-fold.' Now, if it might happen in appearance, then what is that? It's appreciation. Then a friend wears it. 'Oh, how good you look wearing it, look at her in society.' Now it went from appreciation to `willingness`. And when you yourself wear it, you are trapped in `qisas`. Why are you trapped in `qisas`? Today you have worn a bindi, tomorrow you will eat `prasad`. The `bhog` that comes from the temples. You will eat that too. The `phagun` festival... you will eat that too. The things you are getting into... that thing will then... all these things, which are getting ingrained... now that thing will do what? I am telling you that in the whole world, whatever thing is visible, whether it is a watch, or this... iron thing, or walls... all these things are divided into only three parts, from our perspective. Either they are from `shah-e-mutahharin`, or from the `lazimin`, or from `musur-e-mustaqbil`. And as is its rank. The gaze of the knowledgeable (`'alim`) works on this. 'This is this.' Why in the `da'wat-e-'alam` is it said that from rat droppings and mango pits, do not do `abdest` (ablution). For `abdest`, if you don't have water, then do not use this thing, which is in the hadith. Why? The thing that is forbidden is in an ascending state. In the future, very soon, it is going to come into the `khawat-e-'arifah`. And you are getting it dirty by using it. So use a thing like a clod of earth. There are still long years for the clod of earth. Lakhs of years later it will come into the `khawat-e-'arifah`. So use that, not this. Every thing has a meaning here. So look, you got trapped in `istihsan`. From `istihsan` you came to `maylan`. From `maylan` to `qisas`. When `qisas` happened, you are in `daulat-e-alam`. Now that `qisas` is running, running. It happened in one lifetime, then you came here, you did that here too, then in the future... you are stuck in the cycle of `tanzil` and `tadbir`. And this `qisas`, until it is completed, you will not get release from `daulat-e-alam`. You see, there are many who know very little. They did the best deeds, and their `qisas`... as it is said about our Mulla Farhat 'Ali Sahib Muqaddas... ...that you never spoke ill of anyone. A great elder, though he had to deal with everyone. But you were so... if you just bumped your hand, you would apologize. I mean, whatever is there, if it gets washed off right then, then the cloth becomes clean. It happened to such an extent that the venerable elders after him... it is possible that he will not have to come here again. That he will go to the `hazarat`. It is said about many other people too, that such and such... our `amal` is such that he will not have to come here again. Qasim Sahib, who was with us in the time of Hazrat Sayyidna... Sayyidna `Diya` had brought him, from Nadiad. A 12-year-old boy. And he came and became so absorbed, served so well... that he took the `'ahd` and lived like a slave here. His whole life. I saw him in my childhood too. And he didn't ask anything from anyone, just kept benefiting people. Sometimes making tooth powder, sometimes this, sometimes oil, sometimes something else... just giving gifts to people for free. And prayer. He wasn't very educated, his name was Yasin. And he always used to recite Yasin. If he sat down, he would be reciting Yasin. It was said about him by the `du'at` that it is possible that he is such a soul that he will not have to come here again. So what does this mean? `Najat`... `amal` is there, but knowledge is not. He is not a `hujjat`, not a `da'i`. But he has reached the `hazarat`. And here very great knowledgeable people are trapped. So this is the cycle of `qisas` which is running in our `daulat-e-alam`. Now, apart from this, today's topic of ours is, for two or three days, this: the system of `da'wat`, what it is like. What are the `maratib` (ranks)? Regarding the `maratib`, what should be our conception? Who is great, who is small, how the ranks from `mu'min` onwards progress. Look, this, the `dawr-e-kashf` finished. `Dawr-e-sitr` began. When `dawr-e-sitr` began, the scales of `nubuwwat` (prophethood) became heavy, and the Imamate was hidden. The Imamate, after Mawlana Hunayn, Janab-e-Adam came. After Janab-e-Adam, Habil, then Shish, then Idris came. All were sent into the world in the form of `nubuwwat`. The Imams were sending them. They were in the `hazarat`, and these people were doing the work of `da'wat`. And in every era, as it happens in every `dawr`, there are 12 `jazira`s (islands). Where the Imam's `da'wat` is spread, we call that a `jazira`. Wherever the Imam's `da'wat` is. Where there are `mu'minin`, there will be `da'wat`. Where `da'wat` is, there will be `mu'minin`. And they are the Imam's 12 always. At that time, when the Imam is fully present and manifest. But today's era, you know, the Imam has been hidden for years. So it means... only three `jazira`s of ours are currently active. Yemen, Hindustan, and Sindh. These three `jazira`s have always been fertile. And they have remained so because the `jazira` of Yemen is the highest `jazira`. Why is it the highest? Because Makkah and Madina are in it. Makkah Sharif and Madina Sharif are both in it, so the `jazira` of Yemen... I mean, we are taking it like this... that is an island. In terms of `jazira`... Mawlana Idris has made it like this. Otherwise, in terms of area, that part is included in Yemen. So, because of the greatness of Makkah, it has been given that rank, that the `jazira` of Yemen is the most superior. And that is why, in today's time, whom we call `da'i`, in the time of Bani Isra'il, they were called `nabi`. As many prophets (`nabi`) as passed, 1,24,000 prophets passed, until today. Now you will say, where will we get the count? We can't even count 1220. Where will we get 1,24,000 from? So it means... from Adam 'alayh al-salam till here, all the venerable `hudud` who were the `du'at` of that time, they had the rank of `nabi`. Whom we call `da'i`, today our `du'at` have that same form. And in other books that you are reading, the names are given, the cabinet that is with the Imam, the `du'at`... their names are given. And the names that have come in the Qur'an, among the 1,24,000, are the most excellent names, the highest prophets. And among them too, there has been a division of `Natiq` prophets, and a division of `Ulu al-'Azm` prophets. Among the `Ulu al-'Azm` prophets, two prophets are the highest: Janab-e-Ibrahim and Janab-e-Paygambar. And in that, the most noble, `Sayyid al-Mursalin`, Janab-e-Paygambar (S), is called the highest prophet in the whole world. Now, what is `nubuwwat`, what is `risalat`, what is `wasiyyat`, what is `imamat`, what is `qa'imiyyat`... we have to understand this. The topic for two or three days is this: we, who are called `mu'minin`... after this, I will directly start the `ma'ad` of the `mu'min`. I mean, we are going into the `mabda'`. So... we, who are called `mu'minin`, a `mu'min` is one who is observant of prayers, and follows all of Shari'a. When a person takes the `'ahd`, he doesn't become a `mu'min`, but becomes a `mustajib`. A `mustajib` is a rank of the `mu'min`. It is the first rank. That you have taken the `'ahd`. You haven't done any `amal` yet. It is the first rank. Then as the days pass, his progress is known to him and to Allah. How will it be? When the ten ranks of the `mu'min` are completed, he is brought to the rank of the `hudud`. Now how will he be brought? The Mawla's gaze will fall on him. That is not our work. The Mawla's gaze will fall, and he will come to the rank of the `hudud`. When the ranks of the `hudud` are completed, he will go to the rank of `mukasir`. From `mukasir`, there is `madh'un`. `Madh'un`, then `da'i`, then `hujjat`. And in these, there are ten ranks each. Until the ten ranks are passed, a `madh'un-e-mahdud` cannot become a `madh'un-e-mutlaq`. Then the ten ranks of `madh'un-e-mutlaq`, i.e., of the `mansub`. `Madh'un-e-mutlaq` means the master of two `jazira`s. `Mahdud` is lower than that. So the `madh'un-e-mutlaq`, until then he cannot reach the rank of `da'i`, until he passes those ten ranks. That is why with us, there is a very subtle point, but it is that `du'at` can tell that when a `mansub` passes away, to which rank he has been promoted. After becoming a `madh'un`, there is also progress. After becoming a `madh'un`, according to his deeds, two ranks, four ranks, six ranks... he passes to the rank of... To the extent that where his `inzimam` (joining) has happened, that is also all mentioned in our books. It's not a small matter, but it is higher than our standard. Anyway, that he... the ten ranks of the `mu'min`, then the `hudud`, `mukasir`, then `madh'un-e-mahdud`, `madh'un-e-mutlaq`... in between there are others, `da'i-e-ihlal`, `da'i-e-ihram`... there are many such things. And going, the `da'i-e-mutlaq`, he is the one who is the master of our three `jazira`s. Now, here a subtle point has come, because this is a matter in front of us... our Sayyidna is present, so we must understand this, our duties. Sayyidna 'Aliqadar, whether he is the previous one, or the current one, or the future one... so this...

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-2002 lecture 4 -->

There is no difficulty, that is why I am teaching. And then Allah Ta'ala makes them say that... makes one say, that *dhalik al-kitab la rayba fih* (that is the Book, in it is no doubt). So what will be its meaning? So why is Allah Ta'ala making me... forcing me to read more? When it has already been taught that this is the thing, that there is no doubt in it, so then you should read it. So there is no doubt in the promise of Allah Ta'ala. We are Muslims, we are *mu'mins* (believers), we don't have any doubt at all, so why is this written? Anyway, leave that. *Dhalik al-kitab la rayba fih*. There, the word that has come is *dhalika*. *Dhalika* means 'that'. Those who know a little bit of basic Arabic, a little, they know *hadha* means 'this' and *dhalika* means 'that'. This and that. So 'this' is used for *hadha*. When the book is right in front of you, which you can see, which is on the desk, then will this be said, will 'this' be used or 'that' be used? You tell me. The near pointer which is there, in Urdu or in English... 'this' will be said or 'that' will be said? 'This' will be said. What should be said for 'this'? *Hadha kitabun*. And there, it is not said that, it is *dhalika*. No one can tell its reason. For 1400 years it has been written as *dhalika*, so what is its meaning? Anyway. So, what do the commentators do? I didn't understand it, but in a book... Abul Kalam Azad and great... are sitting. And what did they do? They translated it as: 'This is that book.' This is that book. This is that book, in which there is no doubt. Like, there is no... any doubt. So, what will be its meaning? That *dhalik al-kitab*, we believe that what is written is correct, and its meanings need to be understood. If it is not understood, it means our minds have a flaw, Allah Ta'ala's... there is no... one cannot have any doubt. In the word of Allah Ta'ala, there is not even a mistake of a single *zer*, *zabar* (vowel marking). A word is a different matter. And if a word with a *zer*, *zabar* is wrong, then one cannot even be called a *mu'min* (believer). There are some instances where if you put a *zer* instead of a *pesh* (vowel markings), then it becomes *kufr* (disbelief). I will tell you. Anyway. This, see, what is meant by *dhalik al-kitab*? The commentators did not understand it. But we have... in the first *sabaq* (lesson) of *Kitab al-Mabda'* it is written that 'this' should be there, 'that' should not be. Now, listen to what Mawla 'Ali said. [unclear] Go back. When, during the time of 'Uthman, the Quran was being compiled, a person from his clan... was sitting. He had a Quran compiled five times. And he said, "*Hadha kitabullah*" (This is the Book of Allah). This is the Book of Allah. He had it said. Then, during the time of 'Uthman, this is the book of the Prophet. So someone there said, "What about that book?" The father's book. *Untuq*, he said, 'you speak'. Meaning, O Messenger of Allah, O Prophet, what about my father's book? Now you tell me, what book is in the *satr* (concealment)? Which Quran Shareef is written on paper? The Quran Shareef is the word of Allah. He recited it three times before the people, "Speak, O book. Speak, O book." The words were not spoken. After that, he showed the book, the Quran Majid, and silenced everyone. He said that, "*Hadha kitabullah al-samit, wa ana kitabullah al-natiq*" (This is the silent Book of Allah, and I am the speaking Book of Allah). This is the speaking book of Allah and that is the silent book. So now, Janab-e-'Ali, that is the *Kitab-e-Natiq* (Speaking Book). And where is he written? Far, far away. The *Kitab-e-Natiq*, the person of Janab-e-'Ali *salawatullah 'alayh*, was born 1400 years ago. And he was also the *Qur'an-e-Natiq*. Now you tell me, *dhalik al-kitab la rayba fih*, so whose proof will this be? That book... this book is not... in the Quran... there is no doubt in it, everyone does it. The doubt is in whom? In 'Ali. The doubt is in the station of Janab-e-'Ali. For this reason, *dhalik al-kitab*, there should be no doubt in him. And he who is far, 1400 years ago, we cannot say 'this' to him, we have to say 'that'. That's it. Anyway, these are... there are many things where we... from two things... he can learn his lesson. This which is in the world of Islam... is so much that it is about Hadiths, and it is within the narrations, there are many things. I will, *insha'Allah*, slowly explain it. Today, I have to explain a very important thing about our prayers (*namaz*). Our prayer which we perform, the Fajar prayer which has two *rak'at*s of *sunnah* prayer... two *rak'at*s are for our Imam, and after that, two *rak'at*s are prayed, so four *rak'at*s are completed. The rest of the thing that... he has also done. The prayers which are imposed, from the side of Allah Ta'ala, those are the *fara'id* (obligatory) prayers, which are 17 *rak'at*s. 17 *rak'at*s. Fajr has two, Zohr has four, Asr has four, Maghrib has three, and Isha has four. These are the *fara'id* (obligatory prayers). These we... it is a must for us. It is absolutely necessary. Every day, these 17 *rak'at*s should be performed. After that, *sunnah*s are there, which are just below the *fardh* (obligatory). Like, it has its place. Its rank is after that. The rank of *sunnah*. So *sunnah* has this rank, below the *fardh*, and *nafil* and *tatawwu'* are of a lower rank than that. And what is its reality? That these are kept as *mansulat* (attributions). The meanings are behind it. Usually, in Zohr, we pray four *rak'at*s. In Zuhr, before the *fardh*, we pray six *rak'at*s. All of these have their *manzilat* (stations). And according to their station... which is... which *manzilah* is higher, which one is lower. Which... Anyway, the prayer that we are commanded to do is of five times. These cannot be missed. This is... say, a syllabus that this many *rak'at*s in a day must be completed. As long as your life is, if this decreases, then in the Imam's presence, the *mu'min* is considered a sinner. Now this which we... we pray... prayers. These are prayed at *Zohr* (noon)... in the afternoon. *Asr* is the time after that. And *Maghrib*, which is the evening... when the darkness begins to set. And *Isha*, which is in the middle of the night, at that time. *Fajr*, which is in the morning. So, just as there are many *mansulat*, as you have read, that Mawlana Dr. Saheb has said that the *tahajjud* (late-night prayer) is not just one, there are many *tahajjuds*. In the same way, there are many *mansulat*. As you know, our five times are attributed to the five prophets. Nuh, Ibrahim, Musa, 'Isa and the Prophet. They are attributed to the five. I am telling you this so that you remember and always keep it in mind. And it is in this way. You see, this is the period of *fikr* (contemplation), which we call the time of night. And this is the day... after the evening, night comes, and after that, the dawn breaks... that when the sun is setting, we will call it night, and when the sun is rising, we will call it day. This is the word. Now, we will apply the sun to this: when the Imam is manifest (*zahir*), it will be the time of *dhikr* (remembrance). And when the Imam is not manifest, we will call it *fikr* (contemplation). This... darkness and light... it is like that. Now, the first prayer which was made obligatory on us, in the world of Islam, the first time the Prophet prayed was the *Zohr* prayer. He prayed the *Zohr* prayer for the first time... So *Zohr* has four *rak'at*s. There are four *rak'at*s of *Zohr*, and on the Prophet... Muhammad... Muhammad has four letters. On the four letters, Mim, Ha, Mim, Dal, on that the four *rak'at*s are a proof. It is a proof. I am telling you the *ta'wil* (esoteric interpretation). The reality is much deeper. It cannot be understood. But this *ta'wil* is a middle, a bridge, which we... to reach the reality, we use this. This is... those four letters of Muhammad, it is a proof on that. And what did the Prophet do? The first prayer he prayed was the *Zohr* prayer in the world of Islam. And the sun of the Prophet, when it rose, it shone upon the entire world. In the whole world, it was proclaimed that such a person has been born in Arabia, whose glory is very great, and everywhere... even the emperor of Rome was trembling. Iran, and other places, even in Andalusia... the embassies and all the emperors of that time, they were all surprised that who has been born in Arabia. So when the sun of prophethood shone... in the same way, it was shining at that time, as the sun shines at the time of noon. At noon, when it moves a little from the meridian, then our time for *Zohr* begins. This is the time when the sun is shining brightly, so that is the time of light, and that is our *Zohr* prayer. Now, what is the real meaning of prayer, I will tell you after this. See, prayer is... first I will... prayer that we perform, that is... as I told you, that we... on the command of Allah... we perform two, four, four *rak'at*s. But we... what reward do we get? What is the thing that is received as a reward? The reward is this, that at those times... at those times, because it is written everywhere, it probably comes at the time of *Maghrib* prayer... *kitaban mawquta* (prayer at fixed times). That this is... the prayer is a *salat al-mawqudah*, it comes that it is a fixed prayer. It should be prayed at this very time. If you pray in the evening, it becomes invalid. So, at the times they pray, in the world, at the time we are bowing and prostrating, the surface of the world, *falak-e-muhit*... on the *falak-e-muhit*, there are such issuing gates. There are... openings, windows. From the spiritual world, from where... There, from there, *suwarat* (spiritual forms) from the spiritual world descend at that time when our prayers are. So remember that the times are always greater than the prayer. The times of prayer are greater than the prayer itself. Remember this. Because prayer comes from our side, and the times come from Allah's side. So from above, at the time when these issuing gates, meaning the streams of reward, or the blessings that are in them, these things descend, meaning *suwarat* descend. When *suwarat* descend, at that time in the world, it is its time. Today, our watches have been made, everything is fixed. In those days, what was it? They would put a stick and see if the sun had passed the zenith or not, all these things had to be seen. Now everyone knows when the time of *Zohr* is and how much it is changing. So whenever from above, the time of its descent happened, that time became our *Zohr* time. Our *Zohr* time is not connected with it, rather we are subject to it, from above. So this is... they open at the time when our *Zohr* time is. And... this is also a matter, that ordinary Muslims pray five times, and we pray three times. What is the reason for this? That we combine *Zohr* and *Asr*, and we combine *Maghrib* and *Isha*. Why is it like this? They should be prayed separately. So we pray this for the sake of necessity, and the second thing is that the gate that opens for *Zohr*, it remains open for two and a half hours. The *suwarat* descend. Meaning, if at 12 o'clock, for example, it is the time of *zawal*, then until 2:30, the time for *Zohr* is there. And the time for *Asr* begins from 1 o'clock. So in its shadow, the gate for *Asr* opens. And it remains until when? Until 5 o'clock. Meaning, five o'clock means one and a half hours before *Maghrib*, or one hour before. When the sun, its entire disc is finished, it turns yellow. When it turns yellow, then the time for *Asr* is finished. You should pray before that. So we can pray like that too. There is no restriction for us that you pray *Zohr* and then leave, and at 2:30 or 3 o'clock you pray, there is no difference. It is such that when one comes to the mosque, at the time of congregational prayer, we pray them combined. So, what it means is that at the last time of *Zohr*, the time for *Asr* begins, so we pray them combined. And the same is for *Maghrib*. But what is with *Maghrib*? Among all the prayers, it has a very short time. It should be finished in 15 to 20 minutes. That is why we get up after praying the *sunnah*, because the *tatawwu'* (voluntary prayer) is not there for *Maghrib*. It is not written anywhere in the *sahifah* that there is a *tatawwu'* for *Maghrib*. We can pray it later. The *sunnah* should be prayed with the *fardh* (obligatory prayer). Otherwise, if you go to drink tea, it will be late. So the time for *Maghrib* is 15 to 20 minutes. And *Isha*... that is *Isha-e-Akhirah* (the final night prayer). When the night becomes dark, then the time for *Isha* begins, and it lasts until 12 o'clock. Now look at its *mansulat* (attributions). And when is *Fajr*? At *sahari* (pre-dawn meal time). After *sahari*, its time begins, and it ends before the sun rises. So, this is... in the *mansulat*, I told you for *Zohr* that it is *mansul* (attributed) to the Prophet, the *Zohr* prayer. Because the sun of the world... the sun of the world, as it shines, in the same way, the sun of prophethood was also shining at that time when this prayer was made obligatory. Similarly, there is the *Asr* prayer. The *Asr* prayer comes after *Zohr*. And in the same way, after the Prophet, Janab-e-Qaim's arrival is there. Janab-e-Qaim, who is to come, he is the *mansul* of our *Asr* prayer. And the *Asr* prayer is what? *Asr* means... *'asir* means juice. The essence of something, a sherbet. So the essence of something is called *ras* (juice), or if you say sherbet, and in Arabic, it is called *'asir*. From *'asir*, *asr* is derived. *Asr* is called the era, and it is also called the essence. So the essence of the world, the essence of the entire world is the person of Janab-e-Qaim, so *asr* is *mansul* to him. And *Asr* has four letters, in the same way as *Qaim* has four letters. And just as after Janab-e-Qaim, no new *shari'ah* will come, when Janab-e-Qaim *'alayhi al-salam* comes, then prayer and all these things will be finished. And after that, the *dawr al-kashf* (period of unveiling) will begin. Just as the prayer is lifted, in the same way, after the *Asr* prayer, there is no command to pray. You put your hand on your head and recite the *du'a*, after that, there is no *du'a* to recite, and the prayer is finished. Then at the time of *Maghrib*, you have to pray. This is Janab... this is the *Zohr* prayer and the *Asr* prayer. Now after this, see that after the *Asr* prayer... meaning after the light, suddenly the darkness of the night does not come. Rather, the evening comes first. Which we call twilight, or when it gets dim, we call it evening. That the sunlight has finished and now the sunset is happening. At that time, we are commanded to pray the *Maghrib* prayer. So this is the time when Janab-e-Paygambar's eye was closing, Mawla 'Ali's time was coming. So at that time, this Janab-e-'Ali *salawatullah 'alayh* is in the place of *Maghrib*. And *Maghrib* has three *rak'at*s, just as 'Ali has three letters. And now, let me also add this, if you remember, that the four *rak'at*s of *Zohr*, what are its *mansulat*? The entire four *rak'at*s are on the Prophet. And the *Zohr* prayer is also *mansul* to the Prophet. But if we individually dissect it, then the first *rak'ah*, second *rak'ah*, third *rak'ah*, fourth *rak'ah*, what are these? So the first *rak'ah* and the second *rak'ah* in which we recite two *surah*s, that is on Janab-e-Paygambar and his successor. The first *rak'ah* is on him, the second is on the successor. And the third and fourth are on both of their *Bab*s. This is the custom. And similarly, *Asr* is on Janab-e-Qaim and his successor, and both of their *Bab*s are in the third and fourth *rak'at*s. Now, Janab-e-'Ali *salawatullah 'alayh*'s... the *Maghrib* prayer is *mansul* to him. So Janab-e-'Ali and his successor are the first and second *rak'at*s. And the third *rak'ah*, a single *rak'ah* is for both of their *Bab*s. It has been kept. This is the special glory of *Maghrib*. And its gates, which are there, open for a short time, so 15 to 20 minutes are kept for it. It is called *manfas*. These are issuing gates, from above, from *'alam al-arwah* they come out. And when they emanate *suwarat* (spiritual forms) upon us, our prayer becomes a prayer. If someone sees, then our prayer is considered a prayer. Meaning, the *suwarat* from *'alam al-arwah*, which are in an abstract form, they cast their gaze upon us. That is why we... as it is written in *da'a'im*, and in the *sahifah*, that even if you are in the jungle, you should call the *adhan*. It is written that the angels are all present. So what does it mean? It doesn't mean the angels have wings. Those *suwarat* descend at this time. And from this, our prayer becomes a prayer. This is all a matter of secrets, but it is also a matter to be understood, that how this emanation of *suwarat* happens. And especially, whose name we take, meaning that Mawlana Fatimah is *mansul* to *Shab-e-Qadr*. Now, who is the 21st for? Who is the 23rd for? And who is the 17th for? Who is the 19th for? Who is *Laylat al-Milad* (the night of the birth) for? Every single one has a *mansul*. I, as the night comes, on that day, I will narrate it. Now after this, this *Isha-e-Akhirah*. After *Maghrib*, when the period of Janab-e-'Ali passed, and how much did it pass? A very short period. Three caliphates passed for 30 years. And after that, Janab-e-'Ali had to become the caliph in the end, so his caliphate was very short. It is just like the period of *Maghrib* is short. And after that, it went on, and evening came. Meaning, in Islam, an evening and a darkness started to come. As it went on, it happened that now the time for *Isha* began. Now, just as *Isha* is a deep night, in the same way, when in the world, the sun has completely set. Because at the time of *Maghrib*, even if the sun has set, there is still a little bit of light. Even though the sun has set, there is still light. But at the time of *Isha*, that twilight also ends, the redness ends. After that, there is the command to pray. And this is what happens, that we pray *Isha-e-Akhirah* in the complete darkness of the night. This darkness is what? That after Mawlana Tayyib *salawatullah 'alayh*, all the Imams went into complete concealment. Not a single one was known, where they are, what they are, except for the *du'at*. So that era, which is the period of *satr*, from Mawlana Tayyib until Janab-e-Hujjat, the whole night that we are passing through, how many? Where are we passing through? This is the 21st, and today is the 48th. This long period that has passed... this is not one *mansul*, there are many *mansulat*. So the Imam of the time of every era, on him, the *Isha-e-Akhirah* is *mansul*. If you were born 100 years ago, the Imam of that time was the *mansul*. Today, in whichever era we are, our Imam is the *mansul*. This is what? The *Isha-e-Akhirah*. And this period is long for this reason, that from Janab-e-Tayyib until here, it is a complete dark night.
