Now when this *Isha-e-Akhirah* ends, as it goes on, at 2 o'clock, 3 o'clock, 4 o'clock, now the time of twilight came and the *Fajr* prayer is near. So this time of twilight is... Hazrat Dai Saheb and the *mansus* before him have stated that the time of twilight has also ended. Meaning, we have passed *Isha-e-Akhirah*, rather, according to the demands of the time, the arrival of the *Qaim* is so near, the period of *qiyamah* is so near, that we have also finished the stage of twilight. When did this happen? That Sayyidna Wafi Ahmad, and Mawlana Zuhayr ibn Musa, and Ibrahim Hamidi, these who passed, they are the *mansul* of that period. And after that, as time went on, when the time for the *Fajr* *adhan* will come, that will be the time when Janab-e-Mahdi will arrive. Now this is the time of light. Just as it was the Prophet's time, this is M-H-D, this is also M-H-D. That is Muhammad, this is Mahdi. So when the period of Muhammad *sallallahu 'alayh* ended, now Janab-e-Mahdi's period will be such that in the world, there will be such a proclamation that no one in the world can equal him. And it will be such that, like suddenly, the dawn spreads. It has been said, I, in the last *sabaq*s, about Janab-e-Mahdi and Janab-e-Qaim, and how the field of *qiyamah* will be, what it is, all the details will come in the last *sabaq*s. Janab-e-Mahdi's period will be such a commotion in the whole world, and what will happen? That the light will be at random. It will be at once. It will be sudden, it will be unexpected. So for that, in the Quran Shareef, a verse has come: *baghtatan* (suddenly). For the *qiyamah* (resurrection), two signs have been told. One is that it will be *baghtatan*, and one will be *yawm-e-ma'lum* (a known day). The one that will be *baghtatan* is the arrival of Janab-e-Mahdi. And *yawm-e-ma'lum* is the *qiyamah*, meaning the arrival of Janab-e-Qaim *salawatullah 'alayh*. So the *qiyamah* when Janab-e-Qaim will come, all its things are written. They exist. But when Janab-e-Mahdi will come, no one knows. His arrival is *baghtatan*, it will be completely at random. So how will he come at random? For that, there must be a resemblance. And I am telling you from the beginning that *as-salatu mi'raj al-mu'min, 'ala mithal al-khilqah* (Prayer is the ascension of the believer, in the likeness of creation). The creation of religion is on the likeness of creation. And what is its likeness? That it is... there, when in *Fajr*, you stand on the terrace, when at the time of *Fajr*, there is talk of the sun rising, suddenly there will be light. As you watch, the whole thing will spread. You can observe it. In the same way, Janab-e-Mahdi *salawatullah 'alayh*... in the whole world, through television, everyone will know that someone has been born. And he has come like this. So today, for us, television etc. are providing this facility, that everything will be known. And I will draw a map for you of how... when Janab-e-Qaim *salawatullah 'alayh* will come, then how the *barkat* (blessing) is to come. From tomorrow or the day after, it will start. So how this is... how a *mu'min* (believer) will have his *raj'at* (return), his progress. The *mu'min* progresses on the path of progress, and in the same way, the *kafir* (disbeliever), all the non-Haqq... *fa madha ba'd al-haqq illa al-dalal* (And what is there after truth except error?). After the truth, if there is anything, it is misguidance. There is no middle path. That if one did not become a Sulaymani, he is considered a non-believer, it's over. There is no middle ground that, "No, he was my relative, he was this, he was that." There is no such thing. Just *ba'd al-haqq illa al-dalal*. Besides misguidance, there is no other thing. Meaning, it is destruction and darkness. So you will say that, "We, from a *shari'ah* perspective, say that the Majusis, Parsis, or Hindus, compared to them, the people of the Trinity, Christians, are better. At least they believe in Allah." But when the time of punishment comes, when Janab-e-Qaim's time comes, everyone will get the punishment. Except for those who believe in 'Ali. So the only path is the person of Mawla 'Ali. And that started from Ghadir Khumm. Remember that what started from Ghadir Khumm was the start of *iman-e-ghayb* (faith in the unseen). Until then, it was Islam. Islam was a big platform. And after that, the small platform that was made, that was that only those who believe in 'Ali are on the right path. Those who do not believe in 'Ali are misguided. And they are misguided until today. You see that they do not understand the meaning of anything. Now I will conclude on a small example. I will conclude on one example. That we have... I have told you before that some things that we have, the principles of *da'wat* (mission) or say of *shari'ah*, that some things are *ahkamat* (rulings), that this should be done, this should not be done, this should be done. So for that, to the common child, today's teenagers, the boys or girls, to explain to them becomes difficult for us. Because we use Arabic sentences and tell them the *ahkamat*, quote the *Da'a'im al-Islam*, it is not understood. But if you parallel it with science, then today's era is of science, with that it is very easy for a person to understand. So I have been stating for the last 17-18 years that many things have come to light, and I have had them confirmed. Our *Hudud al-Tullah* who are there, and Hazrat Muqaddas who was there, Mawlana Abbas Saheb and all the people who are there, I have told them all and have had it done from them. And Hazrat Muqaddas gave permission that you can state this in the *sabaq* (lesson) or in your *majalis* (gatherings). So one thing came to my mind, that just for understanding, this is not making a rule, but just for understanding, that we have... you see, today in our... among others, among non-Sulaymanis, in our midst too, such boys, girls, or whoever it is, in them a custom has come that, pop singers or so-and-so, who are such misguided people, they wear bangles there, or have big hairstyles, or wear earrings in their ears, many such things have come which, according to our principles, are not correct. They are made of iron, they are made of brass, there are many materials. And at the time of prayer, it is not permissible. But they wear it. Today, even if you say it throughout the day, from an ethical point of view it is not right, but wearing it at the time of prayer is a completely wrong thing. Now this thing, if you explain it to a child of yours, that "take these things off, so that we... at the time of prayer, we take off the watch, we put down the pen, the keys, these are things of iron, things of brass, we put them on the prayer mat in front, we don't keep them attached to our body." Now why not keep them? Now if the child asks, the same child asks who is sharp, he said that "I have memorized the entire *sahifah* prayers." It's possible you haven't memorized them, I have. You give the answer. I have memorized it. My clothes are pure, the place is pure, my mind is pure, I am facing the Qibla, and I know the intention. Now I am praying. Now this thing that you... this bracelet I have worn, or this so-and-so I have worn, or any such thing for beauty that is worn, or the different... whatever they wear. This... what is the point of taking it off? If he asks. How will you explain it? To explain it, in simple language... that by wearing this, our prayer becomes deficient. He will say, "What is deficient?" You are also praying the same prayer. It's possible that you are old, you have memorized the *surah*s, I have also memorized the *surah*s. So-and-so, I know more than you. So how will you convince the child? So I, the scientific thing that I have found, I have made a theorem of it, a problem, and then solved it. So this is... that we explain to him that it becomes deficient. The *suwarat* that I have told you about, that from *'alam al-arwah*, from above, from the heavens, they come. Those *suwarat* see this. These things are in a falling state. As it will be known in a day or two that which things are falling in the world and which things are progressing. So these falling things, you... we are in a rising state. We are in the state of a *mu'min*, so rising. With that, we attach this. So it means that we are inviting our own ruin. So it is explained that "this thing is made of metal, from this, the prayer becomes deficient." They don't understand. Now how to explain it? That it becomes deficient. Now, to explain it, I will explain it from a bit far, that when we pray, we pray for Allah. For Allah, with humility and concentration, we are praying. So its meaning is that we are seeing Allah. We are standing in front of the *mihrab*, so our humility and concentration... as Mawlana Zayn al-'Abidin *salawatullah 'alayh* used to pray, his face would turn pale. And people would ask, "Why does this happen to you?" He said, "I am going before the King of kings. If this doesn't happen, what will happen to my state?" So when it is the state of *iman* (faith), our concentration, what state will it be in? Now at such a time, when we are standing, we should imagine that we are seeing in front of us... Allah Subhanahu. Now this is not possible, that Allah is a thing to be seen. How to see? This is no matter that we cannot see Allah. He is not a thing to be seen. So we can know this much, that Allah is seeing us. This is understood, that we are not seeing, but Allah is seeing us. He is... *innallaha 'ala kulli shay'in qadir* (Indeed, Allah is over all things competent). He is powerful over everything. So when Allah is watching, it means that our prayer should be completely straight and correct so that the reward is received. Now at such a time, when we are receiving the emanations, the blessings are raining down. Meaning when we understand that this is the prayer of *Shab-e-Qadr*, or other prayers, that at the time when we are sitting in the mosque, and we are praying behind a great one, a *mansub* or someone else, so from above, the emanations are happening at the time of prayer. Now when it is happening at the time of prayer, then we are in front of Allah. Allah... you, I am standing, my brothers are standing next to me. I don't know if their prayer is correct or not. See, I am talking personally. That is he praying the *surah* or not? I don't know. And they don't know if I am praying correctly or not. So no one's state is known to anyone except Allah. Allah knows everything. So Allah knows means that we are being X-rayed in the true sense. We are being X-rayed. That he knows the state of the heart, whether one is praying or not. He knows about every person, whether they are praying correctly or not. When we are being X-rayed, then the issue is this... in the Mukhtasar, in the Da'a'im, in the Sahifah, it is written that when you stand on the prayer mat, fold the prayer mat properly so that it faces the Qibla, and then stand correctly and pray with humility and concentration, make the intention properly, face the Qibla, and these things of iron, gold... iron and other things, put them in front, do not keep them on your body. So that the prayer is completed. Now at such a time, when we are being X-rayed... the child says that "I will not do it, I have to wear it." So I ask him, that at the time when, say, you were flying a kite on your terrace and fell from top to bottom, your hand bone broke... and you had to be taken immediately that the bone is broken or not, go and get an X-ray. Now, while getting the X-ray, you... you people will have an idea, when we get an X-ray, the hand bone... from here, the X-ray is being done, so first take off your shirt, then if you are wearing a watch, take off the watch. Whatever thing is there, a ring, this, that, remove everything, then the X-ray will be clear. So when medical science says this and you accept it, then why don't you accept divine science? And what do they say? That if the watch is on, this part will not be X-rayed. So here, in the spiritual world, how will the X-ray be done when you are wearing a falling thing on your body? So this is a comparison, for understanding, that the things which are deficient, meaning the rulings which are told, that this should be done at this time, this should be left at this time, then that should be obeyed. Because behind it, there is a lot of wisdom. There are many things. It is just that we are late in understanding. May Allah give us all the strength to understand our religion properly and to understand its minute things as well as its major things. *Wa akhiru da'wana an al-hamdu lillahi rabb al-'alamin, wa al-salatu wa al-salam 'ala rasulih al-karim*. The fourth Imam... what? The fourth Imam of the period... Yes. The fourth Imam... The fourth Imam... In the book of Sayyidna Hasan... The fourth Imam corresponds to whom? See, the question is good. The fourth Imam is... Mawlana Baqir. Baqir *salawatullah 'alayh*. In every week, the fourth Imam will be this. Every... the first, second, third, fourth Imam happens. The seventh Imam is very special. Along with that, the fourth Imam also has a special importance. Now what is this? That every Imam receives a special light from above, from the solar system, from our planets. From that, a special light is received by the Imam. So the seventh Imam gets it from the moon, and the moon and the sun are special factories for us. For the spiritual world, they are very special things, where Allah Ta'ala has placed his things. I will slowly explain it. That what things are in the sun, which are beneficial for us. So just as the moon is on the seventh degree, in the same way, the fourth sphere is what? It is the sun. And the power of the sun is special, the power of the sun is special. And it is called the power of *tarbiyah* (nurturing). Power of *tarbiyah*. The fourth power. And with the fourth power, every fourth Imam has received that thing. A special power was given to them. And for this very reason... the Prophet *sallallahu 'alayh* said that to Janab-e-'Ali, "O 'Ali, after you, Hasan will be the Imam, after Hasan will be Janab-e-Husayn, and after that, Mawlana Ja'far al-Sadiq... Mawlana Zayn al-'Abidin was sitting, who was very young, six or seven years old. He... 'Ali will be the Imam. And 'Ali's son will be one, Muhammad al-Baqir. His name will be Muhammad." Muhammad Baqir we say, but his name is Muhammad. "One Muhammad will be there," he was not yet born, the Prophet's Hadith has come. "One Baqir will be there, and you should convey my salam to him." A great... he said. "And you should convey my salam to him and say, '*Ya Baqir, ifra' al-'ilm*' (O Baqir, split open knowledge)." O Baqir, split open knowledge. Spread knowledge. And what was conveying the salam? From the Prophet's side, special *suwarat* were there which were received by Mawlana Ja'far... Baqir *salawatullah 'alayh*. And Janab-e-Baqir *salawatullah 'alayh* and Mawlana Ja'far al-Sadiq, the fourth and fifth Imams. All the narrations and rulings in the entire Da'a'im al-Islam are from these two Imams. Isn't it? *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Ila hadrati sayyidina wa mawlana... sayyidina 'Ali... mishkat al-iman... sayyidna al-sadiq... sayyidna 'Abd al-Malik... sayyidna Sulayman... Allahumma... Allahumma... al-Fatihah*. *Sabaq* 11. Until now, the discussion that has happened of ours, it has all been about *mabda*. Now the issues of *ma'ad* are remaining. And we have time until *Shab-e-Qadr*. And my intention is that the two days when we have a big night, 17th, 19th, and 21st, on that day, we should not keep the *sabaq*. Because it gets quite late and... to end... it takes time. [unclear] for eating. How many? There will be a *majlis* for two days, so after the *majlis* and... other food... There will be a *majlis*, and there will be food, so for a couple of days, it will be closed there. And until *Shab-e-Qadr*, I want to finish it in some way. And today's discussion is... in a couple of days, I will... whatever the remaining discussion of *ma'ad* is, as much as possible, it will be completed. And... many of our companions have said that we don't get time for questions. That it goes on until the last minute, and after that, we recite the *Fatihah*. So there is no time for questions. So I am thinking that if you have any questions in mind, then today we should finish that, so that... whatever discussion has happened, there should be no doubt about it. Or doubts. About Hadrat 'Ali, there is a question. Yes, say. You said that every *mu'min* has a *hadd-e-'ali* above him. So now we see that we are in a world of choice, in this world. So if our *hadd-e-'ali* is already defined, then when we progress, we cannot progress beyond that. We can go above it too. So our *hadd-e-'ali* is not confirmed, that this is the *hadd-e-'ali*. No, as much as your *hadd-e-'ali* is, if your rank is increasing, then your *hadd-e-'ali* will also change. It can change. It can change. It happens. The ranks are raised, so the *hadd-e-'ali* changes. But the thing is that the *hadd-e-'ali* is not known to anyone. The ranks start from the *Hudud*. There, up to *mutlaq*, everyone is considered of one rank. So the *hadd-e-'ali* of a *mu'min* is not known to anyone. That is why it is said that it can be anything. And whether the ranks are increasing or not, that depends on the command. That if you are made a *ma'dhun*, then your *hadd* has started from there. You have reached here. Otherwise, the *hadd-e-'ali* remains the same. The rank of a *mu'min*. And this is only being discussed for a *mu'min*. For the *Hudud*, it is as their... their ranks increase, their *hadd-e-'ali* also increases. A person who is low becomes a *mansub*, so his *hadd-e-'ali* has been raised. It has changed. And he himself becomes the *hadd-e-'ali* of the entire *jazirah*. What about the people who die and are brought back to life in *dawr al-kashf*? What happens to them? The *akhira*... *dafn* (burial)... In the grave. No, no. For thousands of years, they will be... their age is written. It is 51 years or 50 years and some... months also no, some hours and some minutes and some seconds. It is written. Sayyidna Ghani Saheb has detailed it. So it is counted as 51, but it is 50 and something. And its... A soul that dies in *dawr al-satr* has to come back. Yes. It is all a complete routine. And if it has to come quickly, then Allah Ta'ala brings it quickly. Now today, see how quickly things are changing. That 100 years ago from today, there was no plastic. So the things that are being changed through chemical changes and industry, they are changing very quickly. Its form is changing. And the discussion of *khalq* will come, the things that are falling or rising, its calculation is separate. According to that, those that are falling are also bad, and those that are in a rising state are good. So, just as the Imam of the time has a thing, it is acted upon according to that. I wanted to ask, you were just telling us about the *mansulat* of the prayers. So in the *Asr* prayer, why is there no *sajdah*? Because the period of Janab-e-Qaim comes... Maulana, the question... let him repeat it clearly. In the *Asr* prayer, after that, why is there no *sajdah*? So Maulana Qaim *salawatullah 'alayh*'s prayer is *Asr*, and after that, there is no... that is, no prayer happens. So just as the *sajdah* we do in terms of completion, there, the *Asr* prayer, along with the *fardh* prayer, your period is such that there is no period. It is the period of *qiyamah*. The last... last part. After that, even the *du'a*s are prayed in a short form. If one has to pray, it is a very short *du'a* of a few lines. *Du'a* is not there, nothing is there. Just *Rabbana innaka* is prayed and it's over. Some people are so... they say that even that should not be prayed. When the *fardh* is prayed, it's over. But the *du'a* has been kept after *Asr*. To place the hand on the head and do *masah*, and... that is only done in *Maghrib*. In *Maghrib*, it is done because... Maulana 'Ali *salawatullah 'alayh*'s followers are us, and all the sects are coming from the same way, but the only difference is with us and the Dawoodis. The entire prayer is exactly the same. Because for 400 years we came together. So there is not a slight difference in the prayer. Whereas with the Sunnis, there is a little difference in the *sunnah*s and other things, but with the Dawoodis, there is no difference from the point of view of prayer. Every *du'a*, the entire *sahifah* that we have, they also have the same. So just to create a difference, this *du'a* was made. And why was it made? That the three things there, *marifin*, *naqisin*, etc. from whom we want *bara'at* (dissociation), the *marifin* of that time were... the Nusayris and those who were Mu'awiyah's people, after the battle of Jamal, this is all from the time of Jamal. So now, these people are those people. Now we have to have *bara'at* from them. This is the... of the time... these are the *marifin*. Those who are denying the Imam of the time, the Dawoodis. So the difference is only this much that our... And they also do *taqiyyah* (dissimulation) that if they have to go to a Sunni mosque, or if they have to pray with a Dawoodi, then they will hide their hands and pray the *du'a*. Otherwise, this *du'a* has been kept just to create a difference. The Dawoodis don't keep their hands? No. They don't have this one thing. Then the *'aql-e-dhalat* of *ta'awwudh*... Maulana, there is one more question. For example, there are some people who, after the age of 50, and... they take the oath... and they attend a lot of gatherings, etc., and they die. So when they die, what will be their *'aql*, that of the *mu'min* or the *dafn*? The *dafn* is such that... the graveyard in which... his will is to that. The graveyard he goes to and he wishes to, he will be buried there. But their matter of the hereafter... as the intention is, according to that they will receive it. That they are showing that they are Sulaymani... No, the creed that they have held, the Sulaymani creed, so their... has been cut off from that. That the creed that... whatever happened. So they are counted in your group... no, they have been cut off from the group. That the creed... what happened. They are counted in your group. But that creed... it is according to the intentions that the actions are judged. The intention you have today, if it gets corrupted, then you are finished. So the actions are judged according to the intentions. There, for them, it is with those who... look, the group which... will follow the truth, he is a *mu'min*. The Hadith Shareef... The part has been cut off. The Hadith Shareef has come to us from the Hadith Shareef. The Hadith Shareef is not followed by them. So they are not... So they are absolutely... But what is it that, for example... if he took the oath for a while, and the statement of the *du'at* is this, that for example, we have many people who have been outside for years or have gone... for the sake of livelihood... Now he has taken the oath once. And for the renewal of the oath, we take it for this reason every seven years, that in many things we are lacking, we are forgetting the rulings, we are not doing them. And a person who takes the *da'im al-islam* does not read it. So to renew it again, the oath is taken. So that you are confirmed. Every time the conditions are told. Now the one who is far away has taken the oath once. So he is called a Sulaymani. According to the creed of *khalid* and *dhamam*, there in *'alam al-dhurr*, he is made. But he got astray. He came here and his environment got corrupted, or his friendship and that... other factors worked. Such as for the sake of earning a livelihood, he went to another country, got astray and finished. A small thing went wrong. But if he is connected in terms of faith, then he is a Sulaymani, if not, then he is not. There is no middle ground in this. Maulana, you said that in the prayer of *nafil*, it is a compensation for the mistakes we make in *sunnah* and *fardh*. It is offered for that. It is offered for that, but it is also one thing. It also has its *mansulat*. So if that is the case, then I am praying बिल्कुल correctly, so what is the need for *nafil*? It is not like that. See, in that also... as I have told you, the *nafil* of *Zohr* are four for the pillars and two for the prophet. And six *rak'at*s which are prayed for *Zohr*, they are for the *mutlaqin* of the *da'wat* of 'Isa. Now look, the *sunnah* of *Maghrib*, we pray after *Maghrib*. Because after *Maghrib*, Janab-e-'Ali's... those who are his followers, their *du'at* are the ones on whom it comes. But here, before *Zohr*, *Zohr*, as I told you in *rahmatullah*, is a proof on Janab-e-Paygambar. That *Zohr* has four *rak'at*s. And just as Mim, Ha, Mim, Dal are the letters of the Prophet, in the same way, *Zohr* has four *rak'at*s. And for that, the explanation that I have given is that the first *rak'ah* and the second *rak'ah* are on Janab-e-Paygambar and his successor, they are prayed. And the two are on their two *Bab*s, the other two *rak'at*s. But before that too, the *da'wat* is coming. The *da'wat* has not ended. So the last, last *du'at* of the period of 'Isa, they are the *nafil*. And *madhar-e-dhahir* is there, *sar-das* is there, many... there are four or five names to which the Prophet also showed reverence and they are also called *mutlaqin*. *Mutlaqin* means those who came at the very end of the period of 'Isa. So the six are on them. So on the six, these six *rak'at*s are performed. And since they came before the Prophet, they are prayed before the *fardh* of *Zohr*. You were saying that we have been told that *kashf* has started. But then in *Maghrib*, the period of *satr* starts. In *Maghrib*, it is not... after *Maghrib*... when *kashf* starts, then prayer, fasting, all this will be lifted. There will be no prayer to pray, no... So the prayer that will be there is only the prayer of gratitude. We say that, "How will we pray then?" It doesn't mean anything. Now that we have all the rewards, it is a paradise. So *dawr al-kashf* is a paradise. There is no *mabda*, no *qisas*, no... no punishment is there. All these things are for the period of *satr*. The child is, then there is *qisas*. So then you will be rewarded, and the reward is... But a reward will be given, and that is slavery. That you are now a slave of the *Imam*. So your... has become that. Just. That much. So you have to pray the prayer in that, not... all the acts of worship that are seven pillars, all are gone. But the people... will pray more than this prayer. Today, people are being called for prayer... the *adhan* is being called and people are not coming. At that time, there will be nothing, and people will be praying the prayer of gratitude. That "O Allah, you have done us a favor." And many things are there which are specific to the *dawr al-kashf*. See, the Da'a'im al-Islam that we have today is in two volumes. So the first volume... the first volume is our religion for today. But the second volume is also there. At that time, in the period of *kashf*, only the second volume will be in effect. The first volume will be finished. *Salat*, *zakat*, all these issues will be finished. And the second volume, of *'iddah*, of divorce, of so-and-so, of marriage, of inheritance, these will all be in effect. But the acts of worship will be finished. Because the Imam will be present himself, so at that time he will be... will be taking care of all the *shari'ah* and... The series of *kisass* will also continue in the *dawr al-kashf*. No. You just said that... No, that *kisass* and all the issues will be accepted by everyone. A person will accept it with happiness. And in that too, I have not mentioned, that there, people will not even marry. To have sex will be considered a bad thing, and people will... what is it? That to increase the progeny, once in a year they will meet their wives. It will be like this. This has been mentioned... in the *kashf*... the conditions of the *kashf*. And today... the population is working, that is one reason. That the earners are less and the eaters are more. That is the calculation there. Is this the case? Yes, absolutely. And for this reason, it has been said that the time which is there... at that time, there will be no desire. You said that the soul that dies, it is brought back in *dawr al-kashf*, so... it is... And it is punished. What is its punishment? A thousand years it will be... it will be wandering. Its age will be written. Science has progressed in this period of *satr*. Will there be similar progress in *dawr al-kashf*? No, in *kashf*, it will be... more than this progress will happen. *Kashf* is what? The progress will be even more. Hazrat Dai Saheb used to say that it may be that this also happens, that we, as we are experiencing today, just think and you will reach there. Think and you will reach. Now you have seen that 50 years ago, there was no radio control, no remote control. You had to get up and turn the button. Now radio control has also come, remote control has come. So all these things, the possibilities are opening up. And these things, the inventions are coming and making it easy for us to explain. I will tell you, when Janab-e-Qaim will come, what will his court be like, or how will he declare the truth, and after that, how will the people be punished. All the people like us, the *mu'minin*, how will they progress, and how will they be thrown into decline. All the verses of the Quran Shareef are there. In that, it is recited, "*khudhuhum fa-ghulluhum, thumma al-jahima salluhum*" (Seize him and shackle him, then into Hellfire cast him). "O angels, grab them by the neck and you... throw them into a valley of 70 cubits." Allah Ta'ala has said this in the Quran Majid. Our faith is in the Quran Shareef, and the verses in it have such a meaning that until now, we... if we just read the translation, what is 70 cubits? It means nothing. When you hear the explanation that what are *barkat*s, how many are there, what are they... I will give a hint that there are seven types of *barkat*s. They are called the gates of Hell. The gates of Hell are seven. And these are the lesser punishment. The least punishment. There are punishments above this too. So the first punishment is that from the human form until the end of the mineral kingdom it will go. All the evil souls which today are in dominance in the period of *satr*, the Imam is in concealment, who is the real owner, and all the thugs and dacoits are in power. They are all out. As it happens at night. And this... when it ends, the *qiyamah* of Janab-e-Qaim will happen. All the things will be shut down in such a way that you will not even know where it is. Even today we are seeing. You look at the wood, you look at this, what is this? That Allah Ta'ala has brought it out from *'alam al-arwah* and shown it. In whatever form they have come, they have come in that form. So at some point, they were in human form. Otherwise, it would not be that you are walking and you get hurt by a stone and you start bleeding. Why is it happening? Some people are walking, it doesn't happen to them, but it happens to you. So there is some account between you and it. So in the physical world, when it was in the period of *khilqat-e-awwaliyah*, at that time, the account remained. That is being fulfilled. So there are many such accounts, some are ending, and some are... a new file is opening. That something has happened, now its settlement will happen, okay, the file is closed. A new file is opening, a new one is closing. So we are doing this. We are creating relationships, we are making them less, we are increasing them. So increasing means that you close ten files, so you open twenty new files. It is going on. This is going on in the world of rebirth. We are coming as humans, we are coming as *mu'mins*. We are taking the oath. We are becoming *mansub*, *dai*, *hujjat*, up to the *bab*. But with that, the accounts are also opening. And if we don't remain with them, then we are not even born here. We are born in the heavens. And in the heavens, there are *du'at*. In the heavens also there are *du'at*. But such *du'at* who do not have *kisass*. And they are called *hujjat-e-layl*. And they are of a high rank. But here, they have come. So it is not that the *du'at* here, the *ma'adhin* here are of a low rank. Their rank is higher than there. Some are also such that our... the *hudud* in our group, they are of a higher rank. But they have *kisass*. They have *kisass*. What can be done? They are human. And this cycle is going on, it's going on. Whenever the *qiyamah* comes, the small *qiyamah* is separate, the big *qiyamah* is separate. I have told you that the *qiyamah* of *satr* is separate, of *kashf* is separate, the *qiyamah* of the period of *kashf* and *satr* combined is separate, then the *qiyamah* of the period is separate, then after that, the *qiyamah* of death. And with the *qiyamah* of death, the entire account will be settled. That how many are in the *barzakh*, as they are progressing, the small sphere we say that a *mu'min* progresses, where does he go? The sphere of *jadd* became his, it's over. That's one life. Now he will progress from there, he will go to another sphere, one plus one will happen. There, up to the *Bab*, everyone will gather. And one will become a *barzakh-e-'ali*, for example, the *Bab* of the time of the Imam. From there, to the Imam's presence. Now such Imams, how many have there been in 4 lakh years? The points have changed. So each one has such a large store. So after every seven, then after seven more, then the 7000-year period of *satr*. Then the 57000-year period. In this way, gathering, it becomes one *kawn-e-a'zam*, whose duration is what? It is 16000 crore years. 4 lakh *dawr*s... 4 lakh multiplied by 4 lakh. One... one *dawr* is of 4 lakh years, so 4 lakh such *dawr*s are placed, so that becomes one *kawn-e-a'zam*. One *kawn-e-a'zam*'s duration is 16000 crore. Such big, big... huge structures exist. They are structures. Where are they? Allah and His Prophet know. These nebulae that are there, this entire space that is so vast, this universe we are seeing, and Allah is what? *Rabb al-'alamin* (Lord of the worlds). It is said *al-hamdu lillahi rabb al-'alamin*. This universe is not just one, there are hundreds of such universes roaming around. The universe is separate, we are talking about our world. The universe is very big. There are waiting rooms. Which in Arabic we call *barazikh*, or *barzakh*. They go and stay in one place. Such are *sawa'id* that come and fill it. Then there are *qawa'im*. A *qaim* is, from the point of view of the Imams, he has a partial authority. The Imam is a complete authority. From our point of view, the Imam is a complete authority. But from the point of view of the *qaim*, he is a partial authority. Such are *qawa'im* that when they come into being, they become a partial authority for them. And in the end, when death comes to everyone, then the biggest of them all comes. And that discussion will come, that how in the end, it will be, how the end of death will be, there is a whole... a book has been filled, that how... He is the master of the time. It is a very long duration. We cannot even calculate it. Neither the calculator can do it, nor... on the computer, on the IBM, when they did it on the computer, then the calculation came. That one death's duration... I mean, put 25 zeros after it. That is what we have on the television screen... it doesn't come. On the television screen, it is... and that you... I have calculated it 14-15 years ago. When the new... the IBM had come. This earth which... in which everything is progressing, in the same way, the moon, sun and other planets, is there some similar concept of progress there too? No. Only on Earth. So just as some people went to the moon and from there that soil and that... some things were also brought here. So they have brought them, but the things for which the account is there, from above, something has to come down, it will come, it is by the power of Allah. But it is not that any human lives there or anything. It is only on Earth that it has been kept. And from here itself, they will sprout, from here itself they will grow. So the progress is on the moon too. So the progress is on the moon too. The moon is what? The moon is a big store of the *nafsiyyin*. It is of the *nafsiyyin*. And those things are coming down and... as I have told you that the moon and the sun are two big factories. And they are not doing just one job, like giving light, it's not like that. Rather, they are doing many other things. The moon and the sun, or say every planet has three parts. Three. On a chart you have seen, in science books you will read, that there are three parts. One is the outer surface, which we see. And inside it, there are its subtleties. And inside the subtleties, there is one... the center point, that is the actual seed-world. From where the work happens. And here, as science also says, that there is a center core, and around it, there is helium gas, or say the... the gases that fill it. And the thing around it, the surface, that is called *satah* (surface), or *madar-bandh* (orbit-bound). And that is the crust, which we see. And how was it made? I have told you before this, that in the beginning, a cycle of ice happened from above. When the *falak al-muhit* spun, and inside it, all the small ones started spinning, so the first light that came from above, of every sphere... because they were also *mawjud*. They were *mawjud*. So these spheres are very special parts of the *nafsiyyin*. They were so good, not like ours, that they could not come into the world of density. They were not visible, they were like a complete fog. So that light, throwing it from above, after throwing it, it struck here... the surface of the earth. After striking, it rebounded. It bounced from here. It went up so fast that what it had to take from the earth, the dense things, taking them from here, it went straight up. And going there, a circle was formed of it. And that became the *satah* (surface). It became that. If that was not formed, neither the moon would be visible, nor the sun would be visible. Absolutely... then a second time, the *du'at* say that then another time the light was thrown. And the light was thrown again, so its power was less. And it went and... these spheres which are there, of fire, water, air and earth, it reached here and the spheres were formed. It could not reach above. The Bermuda Triangle... The soil that he brought... what are its... So those are... The soil he brought, that... he said this much that he brought it from the moon. Now he has brought it or not, it is his secret. But if he has brought it, then on the moon... it is...

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-2002 lecture 5 -->

The knowledge of who the *Hajj ‘Ali* is, is not known to anyone. This is a special arrangement from Allah, so that it is not known to anyone who his *Hajj ‘Ali* is. Otherwise, what would happen? One would sit at their feet, saying, "You are everything," "I will not leave you." And such incidents have also occurred in the past, among the *salaf*, that, well, regarding the *Hajj ‘Ali*, no one has any knowledge. They are not told, nor is it revealed. And what happens? We, the *jazira* that we belong to in our *‘aqidah*, the *jazira* we are in, or the three *jaziras* whose *maliks* they are, all those many, many *jaziras*, their *Hajj ‘Ali* is the same one. For this reason, their *ta‘zim* (respect) is necessary upon us. Otherwise, we are not bound to show *ta‘zim* to anyone, because the *Hajj ‘Ali* is not known. Now, look, in this… [Audience member asks a question, inaudible] ...then we don't know who the *Hajj ‘Ali* is… So, it's not like that. We have been told, the *Wali Amr* has told us that these are the senior ones, after them are these senior ones, and after them are these. We are to follow (*ittiba'*) that order, that's it. The *Hajj ‘Ali* is not known. It's possible that the *Hajj ‘Ali* is sitting in Najran. It's possible that the *Hajj ‘Ali* is sitting in Sindh. They can be anywhere. Only the Imam has this *khabar* (knowledge). And if he wishes to inform, then the *da‘i* can have this *khabar*. And for this reason, the *rutbas* (ranks) are changed, they are replaced. Anyway, the point is that regarding this concept of *Hajj ‘Ali*, I want to narrate an incident. The three *da‘is* that we are studying in history, one of whom is Sayyidna Qadi al-Nu'man, who, along with Maulana Mu‘izz, three or four, you served during the time of the Imams. You were the *Qadi al-Qudat* (Chief of Judges), the Chief Justice of your time, the greatest *qadi* you are considered to be. And in your *nasl* (lineage) too, there have been three or four *qadis*, who have been *Qadi al-Qudat*. Your status is very high. Our, that is, the special books of our *deen* (religion), *Da‘a’im al-Islam*, *Ta’wil al-Da‘a’im*, *Mukhtasar al-Athar*, and this *Asas al-Ta’wil*, all the *fundamentals* books of the *shari‘ah* were written by Sayyidna Qadi al-Nu'man. And his status is so high that during the time of Maulana Mu‘izz *salawatullahi ‘alayh*, when you saw, Maulana Mu‘izz, that the influence of the Maliki, Shafi‘i, Hanbali, and Hanafi schools of thought was growing a lot, at that time he said, "O Nu'man, you too should codify our *shari‘ah*."

Now, that's a special rank. Maulana Mu‘izz's rank was special, he was the seventh *da‘i*, and he held a *natiq*-like status, which is why he said this. Well, that's a separate matter, that he gave the order, and Sayyidna Qadi al-Nu'man began to write. After writing it, when he brought it, at that time he was told, "I have brought this book, please look at it." And he saw the book, and on it, he gave a certificate that this achievement of the *qadi*, this achievement of the *qadi*, this *Da‘a’im al-Islam* that he has written, is greater than all the deeds of the *ummah* (community) until the Day of Judgment. My *ummah*, that is, the *mu’minin* (believers), all the achievements of the *mu’minin*, all their deeds, as many as there will be until the Day of Judgment, this one achievement of the *qadi* is greater than all of them. In terms of greatness. What does this mean? That is, by reading *Da‘a’im al-Islam*, we are performing deeds. Whatever deeds we are doing, we are reading *Da‘a’im al-Islam*. Anyway, the point is that a certificate was given to you, that the deeds that will be done until the Day of Judgment, this is greater than them. Now, this Qadi al-Nu'man, whom we can call *Rasulullah* (Messenger of Allah) — in what way *Rasulullah*? That he is the messenger of Allah's proof, Maulana Mu‘izz *salawatullahi ‘alayh*. You are studying not just the *shari‘ah* but other books as well, from which it is known where the word *Rasulullah* is used. So, he was a very great personality, that Qadi al-Nu'man *salawatullahi ‘alayh*. What happened? At one time, you used to live in Qahira (Cairo). In Cairo, you fell ill. Sayyidna Qadi al-Nu'man became sick. And he was the *Qadi al-Qudat*, the Chief Justice. Now, at that time, the government was of Maulana Mu‘izz. So all the prominent people, ministers, and others, all the people came to inquire about the health of Sayyidna Qadi al-Nu'man, that the Qadi has fallen ill. All the great, prominent figures came. Now, when he got well, the period of illness was over, he came to the Imam. The Imam asked, "How is your health? What's the matter?" He inquired about his well-being. And after that, he said, "O Qadi, who all came to your side to inquire about your health?" "Who all came, the prominent ones?" So he said that so-and-so came, so-and-so minister came, so-and-so great person came. He named everyone. He said, "Everyone came, *illa Ja‘far*" (except for Ja‘far). "Except for Ja‘far, everyone came." And the Imam is raising his inner state through his speech. The Imam is the one who can access it, to see what is in their minds. Just as Maulana 'Ali has said, that he sees up to seven generations if there is a *mu’min*, so was the power of Maulana Mu‘izz. When he asked who all came, he said, "Except for Ja‘far, everyone came." He said, "Alright." And after that, he sat for a while, took out a book, and gave it to Sayyidna Qadi al-Nu'man. Now, Qadi al-Nu'man began to look. He was a scholar. He looked and started praising it, "Maulana, this is *Kalam al-muluk, muluk al-kalam* (The speech of kings is the king of speech). What can I say about your words? It contains the best of the best things." And he began to praise it greatly. When he praised it too much, he took it from his hand, and he said, "*Haza min maulak Ja‘far*" (This is from your master, Ja‘far). "This is written by your master, written by Ja‘far."

At that moment, he was shocked. "*Haza min maulak Ja‘far*" (This is from your master, Ja‘far). At that very moment, he fell at his feet, Sayyidna Qadi al-Nu'man. He understood. He was made to have the *ma‘rifat* (gnosis) of the *Hajj ‘Ali*. That if someone is greater than me, it is him. He had the rank of a *Bab al-Abwab*, Ja‘far. Ja‘far bin Mansur al-Yaman, who, from Qahira, turned the *Dawat* and turned it in Yemen. So he, Ja‘far bin Mansur al-Yaman, was a very high-ranking person. Though in the government, he was made a *qadi*, and these were senior, and they did great work in the apparent *Dawat*. But in the esoteric *Dawat*, the one who was the chief was Sayyidna Ja‘far, Ja‘far bin Mansur. He said, "*Haza min maulak Ja‘far*." At that very moment, he fell at his feet, asked for forgiveness, that he had made a mistake. And the *qadi* got up right away and kept searching in the streets of Qahira. And he found out where Ja‘far bin Mansur al-Yaman lives. He found out in the streets that he lives in such-and-such a house. He went at night and knocked. As it happened, he was there. He knocked. From inside, an old man came out, Ja‘far bin Mansur al-Yaman. He was old. He asked, "Who has come?" He said, "The *qadi* has come." And as soon as he met him, he fell at his feet and asked for forgiveness for the mistake he had made. So this, I have to tell you, is how the mistake against a *Hajj ‘Ali* is forgiven. So for us, we haven't been told who the *Hajj ‘Ali* is, but a hierarchy of ranks has been established, where the Imam makes the *da‘i*, the *da‘i* makes the *ma’dhun*, the *ma’dhun* makes the one below him. So our job is that as far as we have to follow, we should follow. *Wa akhiru da'wana an al-hamdu lillahi rabb il-'alamin, wa salatu was-salamu 'ala rasulih il-karim. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim...* [Arabic prayer]... *Al-Fatihah*.

[Sound of people talking, a question is asked]

Lesson 10. *A'udhu billahi min al-shaytan al-rajim, Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*. Our subject is *madda'*. As far as the discussion has gone, it was about three things, that our main subject is *mabda'-ma'ad*. The chapter on *mabda'* is almost over, but I will take another day or two because many things are still ambiguous, like the *maratib* (ranks) and the status of our *du'at* and their divisions. All these many *'ulama*, *anbiya'* and others who come, they bring the means of *hidayat* (guidance) for us. And their actions, their words, their miracles, their achievements, their attributes, whatever they are, they are a spring of guidance for us. As many as there are. From the Prophet to here, the Imam, the *awliya Allah*. And this is their way, in whatever *mazhab*, in whichever community you look, this is how they follow their *awliya Allah* and take lessons from them, take admonition. So we should also respect our *awliya Allah*, those who are alive and those who have passed away. We should respect them for how they protected this *deen* and its message. And today I have explained a little about Sayyidna Hasan bin Nuh al-Zaman, who was the *Sitt al-Tahirah*, *Sayyid al-Yaman*. The summary is, what is the summary of the three *jaziras*? And he had a very, very great status. He came at a time when one era was ending, and another era was beginning. You were ending a *Muhammadi dawr* (era). You are told that there is a *mustaqarr* era of Muhammad and a *mustawda'* era of Qaim. The era of Qaim *salawatullahi 'alayh* which has begun, it began 400 years ago. So we are not in the *dawr-i-Muhammadi* at this time, but in the *dawr-i-qaimi*. Of Qaim *salawatullahi 'alayh*. But we are called the *Ummat-i-Muhammadi* because we are the community of the Prophet, and this *shari'ah* that the Prophet brought, it is such that it has been going on for 1400 years, and not a single iota of change will come to it. These many scholars, *fuqaha'* and *mufassirin*, and as many great scholars of the Islamic world as we see, they put their own stamp on it and bring a new thought, that in this matter it should have been like this, it is like this, it is about the veil, it is about the matter of interest. In every place, discussions and debates continue. It is not within anyone's power to change the Prophet's *shari'ah* even a little. The Prophet's *shari'ah* is what was initiated 1400 years ago and will continue until the Day of Judgment. To alter it is a very wrong thing and is to tamper with the *shari'ah*.

And see, as many... As many sects of the Islamic world as there are, and we see this, that with them, the different things that have come, the matters of *shari'ah* that have come, you will see that in many things, *bid'ah* (innovation) is visible. In many things, *bid'ah* is visible. Many things they have started in a new way, which were not there in the Prophet's time, not in 'Ali's time, not after that, but have been started now. And you see, the biggest issue of *bid'ah* that came after the Prophet was the matter of the *taraweeh* prayer. This was started in the time of 'Umar, the *taraweeh* prayer, and has been going on since then. And the *taraweeh* prayer has no importance, nor have its meanings been explained. This was started from the time of 'Umar. Such *bid'ah* leads towards *gumrahi* (misguidance). It is to divert people. It leads towards *gumrahi*, and *gumrahi* is the precursor to *kufr* (disbelief), and *kufr* leads towards hell. So the Prophet's *shari'ah* is not such that someone can create a new *bid'ah* in it, introduce a new thing. We are grateful to Allah, and it is His grace, that we are connected to the Dawat of Sayyidna Humam, and Sayyidna Humam is the one who has جاریed the true *deen* (religion) and its *ta'wil*. He was a great scholar of his time. I have already mentioned his age, that his age was 44 years. But even in that short age, he accomplished great achievements in the religious sciences. The things that are from the Imams, he accomplished those very things as their *hujjat* and distinguished between truth and falsehood. The Dawat of Sayyidna Humam is called by this very name. And then, we see, it is in our nature, that, well, this person is a bit lazy, or a bit of a *jahil* (ignorant). It's not that, but spiritually, many things... many things have been changed, many things have been trimmed.

Now, see, the sects that we see, the Judaism, Christianity, and all the other sects, Hinduism. In this, which ones are very bad and which ones are close to us? We should make some distinction in this as well. The things about which, the sects about which, that is, the religions about which we have been told that you can eat what they have slaughtered, are the Jews and Christians. We, because they say "Allahu Akbar". That's why. They believe in the Trinity, but they believe in one Allah. So those who believe in Allah, the sects, you can eat their slaughtered meat. Besides them, their things are not permissible. And as many Hindus as there are, their things... now, you see, their things are becoming a fashion among us these days. It's less here, but in India it is, and here there are other ways, like going to the dargahs of peers and sheikhs, and many things that are distributed at the dargahs, to eat them with relish or great respect. Until the heart believes it, a person does not put anything in his mouth. And there, see, we are forced to do some things out of compulsion. But compulsion is one thing, and choice is another. So, in a state of choice, we take something, go to the Hindus and eat their *prasad*, or put on their *bindi*, or instead of reciting the *ashrah*, go and sit there. There are many such things, and because of this, our... I have detailed this before, that we fall into *istihsan* with them. The door to *istihsan* opens. *Istihsan* is what you call appreciation. That is, if we consider the religious values, the religious good things of another community to be good... that, well, putting on a *bindi* is good for them, so we also like to put on a *bindi*, it looks very beautiful. This is the first appreciation, the first step of falling. And, we fall. And the second is, after that, there is *maylan* (inclination). There is an inclination, that, well, our sister, our brother has done this thing, as I mentioned in another instance, that the sign of the cross, they believe in it, and many other things. About which, if we say that, well, out of fear, we have been saved, that's a different matter. But we are doing it knowingly, their community is doing it, it looks very good, so we are doing it. So what is that? It is appreciation. *man tashabbaha bi-qawmin fa-huwa minhum* (Whoever imitates a people is one of them). Whoever follows another community, he will be counted among them, and his resurrection will be with them. So this is the second thing, that you are doing it, it looks very good. This is the second step, and that is *maylan*. Then, after that, if you yourself start doing that same thing, then it becomes *qisas* (retribution). And *qisas* is such an issue that it is universal. The history of the world is filled with *qisas*. All the affairs that are going on with each other today, among us *mu'minin* and outside of *mu'minin*, the trade we are doing, the employment we have, working in banks, getting injections from doctors, everything is filled with *qisas*. And these *qisasat* (retributions), until they are finished, we have to stay here. We will be born in the world and keep coming here. If we want to end this from the world, that is, if we want to ascend from here, this will only happen when we are completely pure from *qisas*. So *qisas* is such an issue that it always continues. From a small *mu'min* to the great *du'at*, they are connected to it. No *hujjat* is completely free from it. Except for the Imam of the time's being, who is completely *ma'sum-i-mutlaq* (absolutely infallible), for them these *qisas* etc. are not applicable. Now, the question is, this is a very high matter, that if the issue of *qisas* is not for them, then why was Imam Husayn martyred? Or why was Maulana 'Ali martyred? Or why was the Dawat's 'Isa crucified? All of these have meanings. It is not the topic of our lesson now, this is the beginning of the discussion on *mabda'*. This is not something that everyone can understand, so I am leaving it.

In our discussions so far, the discussion of *mabda'* that has happened is about birth. Spiritual birth, and then physical birth, how we humans came into the world. We were inhabitants of the *'alam-i-arwah* (world of spirits). From there, we were thrown. The action was of the *hujjat*. It was from Allah Ta'ala. And here we became physical. These were all our own sins, because of which these chains were formed, this whole world and universe was created. This whole universe that was created, what we are seeing, we should not see it in relation to trees and mountains, but in relation to *shaki*, *husayni*, *musirr*, *mustajib*, and *nadim*. The whole world is divided into these three sects. And in this regard, we are a link in the chain of the *nadim*. Now, which part are we in? I had given the count from 1 to 15, that the first... from 1 to 15 is its count, of the *nadimin*, and in that, one sect is the Sulaymani. One group is the Sulaymani. And where it is, leave that for now, but we are from the *nadimin*. If, in the true sense, we are *mustaqim al-siyrat mu'minin* (believers of upright conduct), who are acting on the right deeds, as the *shari'ah* of the Prophet *salawatullahi 'alayh* shows, we are going on that path, then it is that we are in a sect. Just reciting *la ilaha illallah* is not enough to be a Muslim. Similarly, just taking the oath of allegiance from us is not a sign of being a *mu'min*. Rather, the oath that is taken, the conditions on which it is taken, until that is fulfilled, one is not a true *mu'min*. A *mu'min* doesn't just become a *mu'min* by taking the oath. Rather, the conditions for a *mu'min*, which are the observance of prayer... see, one who doesn't pray 40 prayers is considered a Muslim. How can we be *mu'minin*? And there is, with prayer, there is fasting, Hajj, all the requirements. These six or seven pillars, they are our syllabus, they are the structure of our life, which we have to complete. If this is not done, then we will keep wandering in the cycle of the world, in this physical world. Ascension from here is not possible. Anyway, this... I am saying that it is a great blessing from Allah that we are connected to the Dawat of Sayyidna Humam. He is Sayyidna Humam, who was a *hujjat* of Sayyidna 'Ali. The *shari'ah* of the Prophet is essentially the *shari'ah* of Sayyidna Husayn. So, in essence, if you do research, you will find out that the Prophet's *shari'ah*, in the true sense, we are the ones who follow it. And the *sunnah* of the Prophet, whatever the Prophet has said, we are the ones who truly follow it. And in this regard, in terms of meaning, *ma'nan*, we are the Sunnis. We are the ones who act on the Prophet's *sunnah*. These people, they have left the Prophet's words and all the things he taught. You see, the Prophet said to pray like this. No one, when Islam came from Jahiliyyah, no one knew how to pray. The Prophet taught by praying, that this is how prayer is performed. He taught by fasting, that this is how to fast. And he gave the command for zakat, that this is how zakat is given. He taught every pillar. So how was it possible that such a great duty, the duty of successorship, the Prophet would leave? That he would leave small things... you see, how to apply *surma* in the eyes. At bedtime, the Prophet used to say that at bedtime, don't leave the light on. The lights, that is, the brightness, put curtains and make it dark so that you can sleep properly. These small sayings, the Prophet... in time, in some gathering, I will explain, that when he had concern for such small things, our Prophet *salawatullahi 'alayh*, how was it possible that he was departing from the world and not appointing anyone in his place? Without telling? This is not possible. And he, in a gathering of 70,000, in the sermon of Ghadir Khumm, made Maulana 'Ali his successor and performed the *nass* (designation). In front of a crowd of 70,000, you were standing there, and you are saying, *man kuntu mawlahu fa-haza 'Aliyyun mawlah* (Of whomsoever I am the master, this 'Ali is his master). I, until today, whose master I was, from today, 'Ali is his master. And not just 'Ali, but *haza 'Aliyyun mawlah* (this 'Ali is the master). This 'Ali. And you, Maulana 'Ali, at that time, lifted him up and showed him to the 70,000-strong crowd. And all the creation that was there at that time, who had performed the final Hajj... the Prophet performed three Hajjs. In the year 8, 9, and 10. So the Hajj performed in the year 10, on the way back from there, at the place of Ghadir Khumm, you stopped. And you had to stop. And how? The revelation came upon the Prophet, *balligh ma unzila ilayk* (proclaim that which has been revealed to you). You had been stopping until then, you had kept Maulana 'Ali hidden, you had not performed his *nass*. And you were afraid of the three individuals. That what would happen, it wasn't known. But when the command of Allah Ta'ala came, that now you must deliver it, because the time of your passing is near. After that, you went to Medina and in a few months, you passed away. So before that, it was necessary for you to do it. When the verse *balligh ma unzila ilayk* came, you, in the gathering of 70,000, made Maulana 'Ali the successor. And in a few days after that, the community forgot. All the Islamic sects, you see, two billion are the inhabitants of the Islamic world, and what is our count? We are not even equal to a pinch of salt. But the taste is from it. All the taste is from it. You see, among these two billion, all are lost. They think that after the Prophet, who is the successor? They are the ones who were made caliphs: the first caliph, the second caliph, the third caliph. These three caliphs ruled for a period of 30 years. 30 years. And these 30 years, the silence that was maintained, the silence that was maintained... that is, Maulana 'Ali remained silent, even though the *nass* had been done. He remained silent, that is the *ta'wil* of your 30 days of fasting. The 30 days we fast, in *ta'wil*, its symbol is that, the symbol of the fasts. And God willing, in the final sermon, I will explain its realities, what its meanings are in essence, the true realities of the fasts. Anyway, this...

Our subject that is continuing, I was wanting to say that during the blessed month of Ramadan, we are only doing the lesson on *ma'lumat*, but along with that, the sayings of various *du'at*, their miracles, the miracles of Sayyidna Humam, all these things we must listen to, so that our faith is renewed. Only these difficult discussions, not only do we not remember them, but many things are forgotten. The memory becomes weak. In three or four years, we forget. But if there are entertaining dramas, we remember them by heart. But these discussions are not remembered. Why? We don't understand that they don't sit in the mind. We hear them again and again, but still forget. For this reason, these discussions, which are the word of Allah, are *'ilm-i-ladunni* (divinely inspired knowledge), they need a base. For this, the most important thing is the observance of prayer. As the Hazrat has commanded, the observance of prayer is the most necessary thing. In the entire *deen*, if there is a stamp on anything, it is on prayer. Nothing else. May Allah give us the ability, and I am seeing that the prayer here is very regular, and there is great enthusiasm for it, that there is observance of prayer and the other things, as many as our pillars of Islam are, in that, they are ahead, and very much ahead.

In the discussion of *mabda'*, we had reached the point of *hadd-i 'ali* to *hadd-i 'ali*, which is our *surat-i-ruhani*. Now this *surat-i-ruhani* is a special thing, which is very important to understand well. What is this *surat-i-ruhani* of ours? It is called *surat-i-sharifah* (the noble form). This is the very thing that we want to create throughout our entire life. We have come into the *mazhab*, the Sulaymani *mazhab*, and the prayers and fasts that we are establishing, we are doing it for this reason, that our *surat-i-ruhani* may become good. If the form is good, we will call it good; if it is bad, we will call it bad. If it is bad, it will be the *surat-i-khabisah* (the vile form). Its discussion will come after a few days. But for now, the *surat-i-nurani* (luminous form) or *surat-i-sharifah*, which is of a *mu'min*, its discussion I have done, that everything... today science tells us that for every action, there is a reaction. Every thing. If a sound is given to someone, it circles the world seven times, as has been found out. So how can it be possible that the prayer we are offering, its impression just goes empty? With Allah, all these things are being noted. On our two shoulders, two angels are sitting, who are keeping our account. And when the field of resurrection will be, at that time, the reward and punishment will be given. A small thing comes to mind regarding the prayer of *saba'-i-zujaj*... From today, a few days ago, I was in Kuwait, and at that time, there was a lot of interest in cricket there. We also have a team that used to play cricket. And there, matches etc. used to happen. Often, the special tournaments that used to happen at that time, I was called to watch them. One day, it was a Friday, and everyone gathered. They had a very good party, and they had kept many prizes, for who is the man of the match today and other things. So there was a distribution of many great prizes. Now, when the prizes were being distributed, at that time, the captain who was there, he said that you, while giving a prize, should say a little something about the *deen* (religion) as well. I was called. So, although it was not the time to hold a *majlis* (gathering) on a Friday, that is a somewhat improper thing. But what happens is that in a *majlis*, if a *majlis* is held at the place of a *majlis*, then sleep comes. And the time to be awake is on the playing field. So I said, at this very time, the time of being awake, if something is said at this very time, it would be good. So I thought that this thing should be explained. That to whom we are giving the prize, the cameras, the transistors, and he was the *Sitt al-Tahirah* man of the match... So what happens is that the method of taking a prize, you see, in all of... in the whole world, this is one way that has been established, that from the President etc., from the great ones, when we take a prize, we take the prize with the right hand, and shake hands with the right hand. Now this, you see, the prize is being taken with the left hand. And I have told you that among the limbs of our body, the left, right, etc., all have a special reason. The right eye is superior to the left eye. The right hand is superior to the left hand. The right foot... the left foot... the right foot is superior to the left foot. These are all things that will come when we get further into it. But we also know this, that the left hand is kept for just one task, while everything else is done with the right hand. Now there, they are taking a prize, and they are taking the prize with the left hand and shaking hands with the right hand. So I said that this is a very wrong thing. That Allah Ta'ala, when He will cause a *mu'min* to pass away, a human being to pass away... when a *mu'min* passes away, and he will be presented before Allah Ta'ala, his book of deeds will be narrated to him. His report will be given. When his report is received, he will take it in his right hand, the *mu'min*. And in whose hand it is given in the left hand, he will be sent to hell. Now you tell me, is it better to take it in the right hand or in the left hand? For this, there are verses in *Surah al-Haqqah*: *fa-amma man utiya kitabahu bi-yaminihi* (As for he who is given his record in his right hand...). *fa-amma man utiya kitabahu bi-yaminihi* (As for he who is given his record in his right hand...), *fa-yaqulu ha'umu qra'u kitabiyah* (he will say, 'Here, read my record!'). *inni zanantu anni mulaqin hisabiyah* (Indeed, I was certain that I would meet my account). *fa-huwa fi 'ishatin radiyah* (So he will be in a pleasant life). *kulu washrabu hani'an bima aslaftum fi al-ayyam al-khaliyah* (Eat and drink in satisfaction for what you put forth in the days past). Allah Ta'ala says that when he is given the book in his right hand... *fa-amma man utiya kitabahu bi-yaminihi* (As for he who is given his record in his right hand...), when his book is given in his right hand, of the *mu'min*, he will say, "Here, read my report." "Come on, I was waiting for this. I was wanting to meet this." And *kulu washrabu*... and then the command of Allah Ta'ala will be that you eat and drink from the bounties of Paradise, for the hardships you endured in the world, now this is its reward, its prize you should receive. And this prize they will take in their right hand, they will go to Paradise, they will eat and drink. If we are *mu'minin*, then it will be given in our right hand. Now, instead of this, *wa amma man utiya kitabahu bi-shimalihi* (But as for he who is given his record in his left hand...), and in whose left hand the book is given, he will say, "I wish I had not been given my record, that this report was not given at all. It would have been better if this report was not given. What is this thing that has been given to me? I don't even recognize it." *halaka 'anni sultaniyah* (My authority has perished from me). I, who was a minister in the world, a ruler, a big director, this that I did, what is that? *halaka 'anni sultaniyah* (My authority has perished from me). My authority has destroyed me, that I have been given the book in my left hand. I did not fully fulfill the deeds of the *shari'ah*. At that time, what will happen? The command of Allah Ta'ala will be... this is all for the non-believers, not for the *mu'minin*. What will be said for them? *khudhuhu fa-ghulluhu* (Seize him and shackle him!), *thumma al-jahima salluhu* (then drive him into Hellfire!), *thumma fi silsilatin zar'uha sab'una zira'an faslukuh* (Then into a chain whose length is seventy cubits, insert him). *khudhuhu fa-ghulluhu* (Seize him and shackle him!). O angels, grab him by the neck. Pull him out and take him. *khudhuhu fa-ghulluhu* (Seize him and shackle him!), grab him by the neck and drag him and put him in the seventy valleys. The valleys that there are, in their seventy valleys... *fi silsilatin zar'uha sab'una zira'an* (into a chain whose length is seventy cubits), the chain which is seventy hands long, in that he will be inserted. So the point is, I explained this there, and after that, what happened? Alhamdulillah, as many as were coming for prizes, they all took their prizes in their right hands, and after that, they... with the left hand, shook hands with the right hand. So, this is not that it's just for sitting in a *majlis* and listening, but many such occasions come where we are active, and we are awake. At that very time, the message of the *deen* should be conveyed. And that is the occasion when the impact is made, at that very time one understands what is to be done.

The concept of *hadd-i 'ali* should be completely clear to you, that from here, our *surat-i-ruhani*, which is so noble, so luminous, so radiant, that the *du'at* say that if someone were to be shown a glimpse of it in life, it has such brightness and radiance that no human could bear to look at it. Just as we cannot look at the sun, we cannot look at it. And what happens? It goes straight, from *hadd-i 'ali* to *hadd-i 'ali*, to *hadd-i 'ali*, and the final level... there are many stations. Our *ma'dhun*, *ma'dhun-i-mutlaq*, our *da'is*, all of them... with them, the *surat-i-ruhani* is deposited. And for this very reason, they are called *malik* (owners). When we say *malik*, it's because they have ownership of our *surat-i-ruhani*. Whoever is the *ma'dhun* of his time. And after that, from there, it goes to the *malik* of the three *jaziras*, and the *malik* then sends it to the *Bab al-Abwab*, who are present at that time. But the entire connection is with the *da'i-i-mutlaq*, that the deposit of the *surat-i-ruhani* of the twelve *jaziras* is with them. It is a large deposit that the *Bab al-Abwab* possesses. *Bab al-Abwab* who? The one who is called the *bab* of four *babs*. The final door, *bab al-abwab*. It reaches the *Bab al-Abwab*, and there the *surat-i-ruhani* is refined. Many things, the shortcomings in our prayer, zakat, hajj, many things are there, its refinement happens there with the *Bab al-Abwab*. And then, because the *Bab al-Abwab* wants that whatever "group" (*jaththa*) I give of these many congregations to the Imam, it should be the best of the best group. For this reason, it all, until it is with him, it is being refined, and after that, when the Imam desires to perform *nass* (designation), and that desire is his own, he himself brings about his own passing. He does not pass away; he himself does it. And after that, his *hujjat*... who will we call *hujjat*? This is the second *hujjat*. We call the Imam's son the *hujjat*, and there is one *hujjat* who is higher than our *da'i*, who is the *hujjat* of the *da'i-i-mutlaq*, he is the *hujjat*. There the thing reaches, the *Bab al-Abwab*, he wants to give the best of the best group to the Imam. So he prepares it all, and at the time the Imam wants to perform *nass* on his son, that son who has been prepared from the world of *ulwiyyat* (the high realms), the *nasit-i-mubarak* has been prepared, when the time for his *nass* comes at 10, 12 years of age, at that time the Imam desires, what does he do? He takes that whole 'essence', the deposit of the *surat-i-ruhani*, from the *Bab al-Abwab* and bestows it upon his son. When he bestows it upon the son, the *Bab al-Abwab*'s work is finished at that moment, and his passing becomes necessary. Because it is a *haykal-i-imami*. Now what is this? It is *imamah*. The Imam is a different thing, and *imamah* is a different thing. The Imam is the one who is the speaking proof. But they do not get the *imamah* until then, he is not the Imam. Rather, he is a son of the Imam. On whom the *nass* is being performed, he becomes the Imam. So when the *nass* is done, and after that the ability is given, all the people are made to take the oath of allegiance, that this is my son, he is the Imam. Then what happens? That thing is bestowed upon him. And what is that bestowing? That is what is called *haykal*. That is what is called *laghut*. And that is what is called *imamah*. So *imamah*, *laghut*, and *haykal-i-imami*, these three are one and the same thing. And this *haykal-i-imami*, where is its residence, where does it live? In the *qarin-i-qarin*. But it is connected. Its connection, its acquisition is always from the Imam. And this *haykal-i-imami* is where our *surat-i-ruhani*, wherever we may have reached, wherever our footing is, the station we have reached, that will be our station in the *nasit-i-mubarak*. If we are in the earth here, then we are in the earth there; if we are in the water, then in the water. That is the same thing. This is an abstract form, which is difficult to understand. And that is the *nasit-i-mubarak* which appears to us as Imam Husayn, Imam Hasan, all the Imams who have passed.

So anyway, this is the map. And after this, the duties of *imamah* are what the Imam, who is the establisher of the *dawat*, what happens? After passing away... he himself brings about his passing. The *Bab al-Abwab* passes away, and in a minute or two, he brings about his own passing, and after that, a new *dawat* begins, a new account begins. And then, that is, just as a father gives all his earnings to his son as inheritance, so the Imam, all the deposit of the *dawat* that he has gathered, he doesn't leave it for his son. And for this, there are its own requirements, how many things, where in the world they are settled. It is a big chapter after this. Anyway, this... I was wanting to say that many things are now... we are passing through Ramadan, so in this, not only the lesson of *ma'lumat* are we taking, but along with that, the sayings of various *du'at*, their miracles, the miracles of Sayyidna Humam, all these things we should also listen to, so that our faith is renewed. Only these difficult discussions, one does not remember them, and many things are forgotten. The memory becomes weak. For this reason, it is better if some light things are also in our minds. Now you see, there are many things. I will tell you two... Sayyidna Humam's miracles are many. Sayyidna 'Ali's, I have told you some miracles. Sayyidna Humam has almost 1,000 miracles. Sayyidna 'Ali wrote them. There are so many miracles that one gets tired of listening.

One time, a gentleman came and... to Sayyidna 'Ali. And he said, from Ahmedabad, "Mawla, I have invited my son-in-law for a feast. And he is coming from Shiyapur. He is coming from Shiyapur, and he is coming with 200 people. And I have prepared food for 200 people. But I have just received news that... from Shiyapur to Ahmedabad, it takes two days to reach. This journey of two days was very long, and I have just received the news that here they have to... bring 500 people with them." So they came in a panic, that now I will be shamed, because I don't understand how I will manage, and the food will not be enough. He came to Sayyidna 'Ali. Sayyidna 'Ali said, "Don't worry. Go, on my behalf, just go and wherever the pot is, where it is being cooked, there, lift the lid and just say 'Ya Sayyid-i-Suleiman' and cover it." And as he did, all 500 people, 550 people came and they all ate their fill and left. So, there are many such miraculous stories about Sayyidna Humam, that you... he was in Zamanbar. He was imprisoned in a fort. At that time, for some reason, a glass tumbler of yours, that glass tumbler of yours was... below there was water and above there was something like a latrine. From there, it came down, but the glass did not break. Many stories are told about Sayyidna Humam's time, as you have read, that there was a person named Iblis, who was your opponent, in Yemen. There were three opponents in Yemen: your own uncle, who was Isma'il, and one was Iblis, and the third was 'Abdullah al-Nakhili. So this Iblis, the ruler, who was from the Turks... in Sana'a... so he imprisoned you. You were imprisoned in Zamanbar. At that time, the days of Hajj came. So you wanted to go for Hajj. But he did not give permission. He said you cannot go. So you were very sad, as a saint, that you did not get the opportunity to go for Hajj. Now see, at that time, at night, that unfortunate man had a dream. The dream was this, that Sayyidna Humam has come, and he is in front, and he is asking, "What is the matter with you?" What had happened? That when he woke up in the morning, his servant who used to massage him, he looked at his face and said, "Sir, what has happened to your head?" So he went and looked in the mirror, and here there was a whole wound. And there were all red marks. When he saw in the mirror, at that time he understood. So he said, "Be quiet, don't say anything. This is what happened." What he revealed was that at night Sayyidna Humam came, and he was in shackles, and his feet were shackled. So he came and you said, "O enemy, you prevent the Da'i from the pilgrimage of Hajj?" And at that time, he picked up the shackle and hit you on the head with it. In a dream. So this was the dream he had, and here it became a reality. So he saw that his whole wound was filled. There were red marks. He said, "Alright, it is a true matter that this is what has happened." As the elders have explained, the purpose of telling is to take admonition. Today the whole world knows that there is no such person whom we can bury after two days, four days, six days. If we don't, the corpse will decompose. It is not possible to keep it for one or two days, in today's age. Sayyidna Humam's body was buried after 11 months. And this was a miracle of Allah Ta'ala. There are many references in books about it. Many questions have been raised about what the reason was for this. You see, one way it is written in the duas, on the *dabihat* of the *hujjat*. That the *da'i-i-haqq* is like this. He was brought from Lahore, from Lahore to Ahmedabad, and for 11 months, the body was brought. In various ways. Because in every place, there were enemies present, and they wanted to desecrate the body.
