…that it should be ruined. It is called *madat-panah*. That in terms of the body too, its preservation was done. And in terms of the soul, arrangements were made so that our entry into the *nasit-i-mubarak* can happen. We, if we enter the… for this reason, they are included in the *jumra-i-aal-i-Muhammad*. O Allah, this *mu’min* who has come into the *deen*, include him in the *jumra-i-aal-i-Muhammad*. What is the *jumra-i-aal-i-Muhammad*? It is the *haykal* of Imam *salawatullahi 'alayh*. In the *haykal* is the *surat-i-ruhani*, and in the *nasit-i-sharif* is our soul. These two are the goals. If they are reached, then the *nasit-i-sharif* is our paradise. And in the *haykal-i-imami* is our *surat-i-ruhani*, which is the most noble thing. It is our capital, our wealth, the provision for our life in the hereafter. Whatever we are doing, we are doing it for this reason, that our provision for the hereafter… that it should reach there.

Look, I may have already narrated this in a *majlis*, but the sweat of a laborer… I think I have narrated it. But still, let me say it once more. Maulana the Prophet said that you should give the laborer his wages before his sweat dries. Before his sweat dries. So today, the world, where the hadith sharif has been translated a lot and its meanings are being derived, they say, well, what morals the Prophet has taught, that whoever you employ as a laborer, before his sweat dries, you should give him his wages. The Prophet, although he is apparent, he is also esoteric. Apparently too, he was at the peak of morals. But it was not just this much. If it were so, then all wise people would be prophets. So the point was that, as Maulana the Prophet has commanded, behind it there are symbols, behind it there are signs, that which laborer is being talked about? Who is that laborer? The laborer is that *mu’min* and *mu’minah* who, after passing away, having spent their whole life in worship, in the *shari'ah* and love for the family of Muhammad, when they pass away, their *surat-i-ruhani* is shown to them at the time of passing. At the final moment, the state of *naza‘* (agony of death), when the prayer for *sakarat* is recited, at that time, a glimpse of their *surat-i-ruhani* is shown to them. And this is what we add after the *adhan*, "*wa 'ayn-i-nuraniyyin zawwijna*" (and marry us to the luminous eye), it is for this very reason. That, O Allah, we are deserving of paradise, we want to go to paradise, and we take refuge from hell. And give us in marriage to the luminous eye (*'ayn-i-nuraniyyin*). What is marriage? *Hur* is not there. What is *hur*? It is not paradise, nor *hur*, nor *ghilman*. Whom will we call *hur*, whom will we call *ghilman*? These are all things that are famous in the common parlance, that there are *hur-i-'in* in paradise, and they will be married. There is no marriage with *hur* or *ghilman*. Rather, what is *hur-i-'in*? It is feminine, *mu'annath*. The final letter, *ta*, is for *mu'annath*. Our *surat-i-ruhani*, its glimpse is shown to us. And with that, the *mu’min* is absorbed in the final moments. "Yes, this is my report. Oh, I was going to meet this. Bring it to me." So that is received at the end, and the person finds peace. And along with that, as many *murshidin-i-haqiqin* as there are, the Imam of the time, Maulana 'Ali *salawatullahi 'alayh*, and on whose hand he has given the oath of allegiance, and whoever are the guides of that time, his form is also shown. And many such people have been there in the community who have passed away. In Hyderabad, there was a gentleman who was a very pious person. He was steadfast in the best of deeds. Along with prayer and fasting, his knowledge was also such, all things. He passed away in the time of my respected father. His son was an engineer, had a B.E., had a tremendous grasp on all sciences and a very good personality. So at the time of his passing, he... my respected grandfather reached there. So he kept his head in his lap. At that time, he said, "My Mawla, look, my Hazrat Tufail Sahab is coming." "And this is the Imam of the time. This is Qaim. These are so-and-so." He is calling each one by name. "And just this, that sever the bond of my life. Sever the bond of my life." All the time he was making this plea. I was also present. That sever the bond of my life. Just. And in the end, he did such a violent act that he broke a glass container in front of him. And after that, he passed away. So it was explained by the elders that his ranks were very high, he was a very sincere *mu’min*, very good. For this reason, a glimpse of his *surat-i-ruhani* is shown at a time when a person's speech has stopped. We cannot speak. But there are some such people who are saying that, "Look, this is what is being shown to me." So there are such people in the world too, *mu’minin* who are. So a sincere *mu’min* can be such that it is not that he is among the *hudud*, or is a *nasit*, or is a *da'i*. Rather, a *mu’min* who is a true *mu’min*, there too are ranks. He too can be very sincere. Look, in the graveyard of Baroda, during the time of Hazrat Sheikh Adam Sahab, about 100 years ago, you, with a few companions, had gone there for a visit, and you went for the *ziyarat*. So there was a very heavy rain. Such a heavy rain that you could not return from the Mazar. So in the old building of Fatehpura, you stayed there, that, "We will spend the night here and then leave." They spent the night, and at that time, since they are people of *kashf* (spiritual unveiling), they saw that on one grave, a lamp is burning. A lamp is burning. And he was worried. So he noted the sign, these two or three companions who were there, and in the morning, he inquired that what is this, I saw this last night. In the morning, when they went and saw, his name was Muhammad 'Arif. The one who had passed away. And it is famous about him that he was a man of great austerities. And in the context of the history of the Baroda community, he has some inclusion, he... during his time, and many other good things he has done. For this reason, these miracles are manifested by Allah Ta'ala. So anyway, this is what I have to say, that a human being, whatever he is, in the capacity of a *mu’min*, it is not for us that we should be told the ranks. Ranks are obtained from the three *jaziras*. From deeds, one does not get ranks. One has to learn knowledge and also act. And if one has learned knowledge and has high ranks, but there is no action, then he will be beaten even more for that. Remember, one who acts, according to how much he acts, he gets that much reward. But if one is a scholar and does not act, then that is even worse for him. May Allah give us the ability to be both knowledgeable and active. As much knowledge as we get... what is knowledge? This is the end of the *dawr-i-satr* (era of concealment). In the *dawr-i-satr*, there is a hadith of the Prophet that at night, do not sleep on an empty stomach. Eat a little something and then sleep. What does this mean? You tell me. It is not good to eat at night, it doesn't digest, and this and that. All the doctors' and health advice is there. Should it be done or not? But the Prophet's hadith is that eat a little and then sleep. Don't sleep on an empty stomach. Meaning, that the time of night is the *dawr-i-satr*. In the *dawr-i-satr*, acquire some knowledge. Spiritual sustenance, acquire something. It's not that we should just leave this world ignorant. As we are, so we should go. Rather, if we are *mu’minin*, true Sulaymanis, then we should acquire something and then leave from here.

Anyway... I, before this too, once this has been mentioned, that about the world, I will mention it again, because many new faces have joined, so for them too, the matter will be clear, that we have come into this world. So since we have come, there is a purpose behind it. Life is not without a purpose. Just as we see that even an insect has a purpose. Allah has not created anything without a purpose. So an *insan* (human), who walks with his head held high, animals walk with their heads bowed. So he has a purpose. And we are *ashraf al-makhluqat* (the noblest of creatures), the adornment of the whole world. So we have been created with a purpose. And we have been given intellect. Animals have the faculty of instinct, not intellect. And we have been given intellect. But if from intellect we search for that thing, that is, we read *Da'a'im al-Islam* and see how the *shari'ah* tells us, and we do that. So anyway, this... our purpose is to look at this world only from this perspective, that what will be for our survival in the hereafter. Not just for the life of this world, but how we prepare our provisions for the hereafter. Today, the world we are seeing, its purpose is not that we live for 60 years and are buried, and the matter is over. That whatever we did, oh, we did good, we did bad, it's over here. This person keeps coming back. It is being recorded. After every 1000 years, we have to come and then give our account. And we have to progress step by step. And the *du'at* say that one day, it will take time, but someday or the other, salvation will happen, and we will progress slowly. So this world, where science says it is two million years old, four million years old, one million years old. But no one has the correct information, except the *du'at-i-mutlaqin*, who have received it from the Imams. And that is Maulana Jalaluddin *salawatullahi 'alayh*'s being, that Maulana Jalaluddin *salawatullahi 'alayh* said, from the *Umm al-Hada'iq*, that how old the world is, I will tell. What is remaining, I will not tell. And he has said... pay close attention, I am doing it for the youth, so that they remember this, so that we have a sense of in which era we are sitting. Today, in what position are we? That we keep going back and back, then it will be known how many pages are behind. Look, one *mawr-i-a'zam*... you know the duration of a *mawr-i-a'zam*. 7,000 years is the duration of a *satr*, and 50,000 is a *kashf*. Combining both, it is 57,000 years. 57,000 years, if it is multiplied seven times, then it becomes the duration of one *dawr*, which is called 400,000 years. 400,000 years that have passed on the world is one *dawr*. And such 400,000-year *dawrs*, if they are lined up one after another, then it becomes one *mawr-i-a'zam*. And the duration of a *mawr-i-a'zam* is 16,000 crore years. In 16,000 crore years, one *mawr-i-a'zam* is completed, and after that, the whole world is destroyed. The stars, the planets, the sun, the moon, whatever solar system is visible, what things fall in a *mawr*? What things fall in a *dawr*? What things fall in a *qarn*? All these things are detailed in the books. Nothing is hidden. And after that, what is there? That such a *dawr*, whose duration is 16,000 crore years, if it is repeated 400,000 times... then what will be its duration, you think about it. That duration is called one *mawr-i-a'zam*. And in the Quran-i-Sharif, its mention has come sometimes. *Idha sh-shamsu kuwwirat*. The mention of *qarn* has come, the mention of *mawr* has come. *Takwir al-arz*. All this, in the Quran-i-Sharif, in a compact and expanded way, all things have come. And there it has been told that one *mawr-i-a'zam* has passed on the world. This is the first thing to understand. One *mawr-i-a'zam* has passed. In the second *mawr-i-a'zam*, 399 *qarns* have passed. In the 400th *qarn*, in the first *dawr*, at the end of it, we are sitting. This is our past history. And how much more will come, Allah has its account. Imam-i-Zaman has its account. So remember this, that we are in the final *dawr-i-satr*. Seven *kashfs* have passed, six *satrs* have passed. This is the seventh *dawr-i-satr*, at the end of which we are sitting. So this is not the doomsday of the *dawr-i-satr*, not the doomsday of the *dawr-i-kashf-i-satr*. Rather, it is the doomsday of one *dawr*. And the doomsday of a *dawr* is called *qiyamat-i-wusta*. The doomsday of *satr* and *kashf* is called *qiyamat-i-sughra* (the lesser resurrection). And the doomsday of *qarn* and *mawr* is *qiyamat-i-wusta* and *qiyamat-i-kubra* (the greater resurrection). So 400,000 years of account, the world will take. The *qaim al-qiyamah* who are awaited, who will be the awaited *qaim*, after many eras from today, they will be the ones who will take this account. They are the Imam of the time. They are the *da'i* of the *dawr-i-kashf*. They are the *sahib al-qum*. When they come, it will be such that no one's will will prevail. The jihad of the lesser and greater will be over, and when the Qaim comes, at that time, intentions will be fixed. People's hearts, wherever they are, will be fixed there. There will be no effort to become good, nor can one become bad. And at that time, the world will see how the master of reward and punishment is. I will discuss this in the final two days of the lesson, that is, before the night of Qadr, I will conclude it, and slowly the discussion will move forward.

The lecture for tomorrow will also be like this, and it is possible that tomorrow we will start a little on *ma'ad* (the return). *Ma'ad* will be for five or six days, and after that, the... as you have heard the *tarjihat* (preferences), I was thinking that until the prayers, we should have a little discussion on Hajj, so that the board can be used, and those who want to go for Hajj and are desirous, for them... now the *maratib* are mostly known, who is called *mustawda'*, who is *mustaqarr Imam*, what was the position of Maulana 'Ali... आगे खुलेगी, but as much as we need to understand, has come, and for Hajj, I will take three or four days, and after that, the final... *ma'ad-i-mazmum* (the blameworthy return). That is, just as the progression of the *mu’min* has been explained, so too the regression of the infidel. As many as are the people of falsehood, whom we see, how will their regression be? After all, we call them hell-bound. What does hell-bound mean? Where will they go? Where will they stay? What are their punishments? For how long? And what are the punishments? For what misdeed are they being put there? All these things, God willing, will come, and that will be in the final discussions, that is, up to the night of Qadr, God willing, it will be done. And after that, in two or three days, it will be finished. *Wa akhiru da'wana an al-hamdu lillahi rabb il-'alamin. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim...* [Arabic prayer]... *Al-Fatihah*.

Lesson 10. *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*. O *mu'minin*, the discussions of our *ma'lumat*, in that, half the part which is of *mabda'*, has been completed. Except that the *maratib* (ranks) are still not clear to many people. What is the rank of an Imam? What is the rank of *nubuwwah*? And what is the difference? How do Imams become Imams? And what is meant by *nubuwwah*? And besides that, the Imams of our chain, that is, after the Prophet, from where the chain of *imamah* started. And such a chain in which the Islamic world has a difference of opinion. They believe in the Caliphs, while we believe in *imamah*. And from Maulana Hasan, the *imamah* has continued, and the Imams are coming until today. So this thread of *imamah*, this is what we call the rope of Allah. This is the firm rope which we have been commanded to hold fast to. And in which, as many Imams as there are, you have read the names of the 21 Imams. So with the names, their eligibility, that who is greater than whom, or what are their ranks, what are their principles, we have to understand that a little.

As I have mentioned before, the foundational ranks are four: *nubuwwah* (prophethood), *risalah* (messengership), *imamah* (imamate), and *wasayah* (executorship). In this, two ranks are of a lower degree, and two are of a higher degree. That is, *nubuwwah* and *risalah* are of a lower degree. According to us, *Rasul-i-A'zam* (the Greatest Messenger) is the greatest. Indeed, he is the greatest. But that is a specialization, a specialty of *Rasulullah salawatullahi 'alayh*. Otherwise, the prophets, as many prophets of the Banu Isra'il as passed, whose names are in the Bible, they are lower than our Imams. As many Imams as there were at that time, they remained hidden. They were the *hujjat*, and in the apparent, these people used to come, as many prophets as there are. So the rank of a prophet is such, just as with us the *da'is* or *ma'dhunin* etc., from small to big, slowly they progress, they acquire knowledge, and progressing, their rank becomes worthy of *nubuwwah*. So this is acquired. Acquired means that anyone can acquire it, but reaching that rank... So the one who acquires it, that is, living in the world, he acquires it. Every prophet gets the rank when Allah Ta'ala gives him the stamp that he is worthy of being a prophet and meets the standard. But the Imam is completely different from this. The Imam is such a being who has been coming since eternity, from Adam, that is, the universal Adam. From where the *qarn* starts, where the *hudud* are born, this… its birth did not happen… the birth of the spheres happened, from there the first *hujjat* is born. From the *hudud*, who is our Imam of the time of that era. And his name is *jussa-i-ibda'iyyah*. He is an Imam, but such an Imam who is the very first, who is born from the *hudud*. His name is *jussa-i-ibda'iyyah*. And I have mentioned in the *munajat* that after *imamah*, the final rank is the *jussa-i-ibda'iyyah*. Meaning, there is no rank greater than this in the world. And after that is the realm of *'alam-i-jid*. Which I will explain in the final lessons, how the ranks are transcended and what Allah's will is. Allah who? The *'Aql-i-'Ashir* (Tenth Intellect), who has created this world. The *Mubdi'* has no need. For whom we say our prayers, he is our *Mubdi'-i-subhani*. Who has created the *'alam-i-'aql* from nothing. But now, who is our manifestation, that is, the *'Aql-i-'Ashir*, who has created this entire world. The entire universe, the world and what is in it, are all under his command, and his plan was this, at the time he made this plan, that if I... because *man kasara 'azman fa-'alayhi jabruh* (whoever breaks a bone, its setting is upon him). Upon him is the duty to set the bone, the bone he has broken. Due to their mistake, all these people came into existence. For this reason, it became incumbent upon them that now you guide them and provide the means for their salvation. So all the administration is theirs, and their intention was this, that in what way I should give salvation to all these humans, that is, all the seed that was scattered... I have explained this, that we have come from the loins of the *hujjat* and with them all the world, whatever we are seeing, trees, mountains, fields, everything is included in it. We just need to see it with the eye of *ta'wil*, that which are the *taharin* and which are the *hakim*, and who are the *nadimin* and who are the *musirrin*. So the plan of the *'Aql-i-'Ashir* was that if I am to give salvation, or salvation will be attained from here, then it will only be in the form of a human. The form of jinn, angels, or animals, or plants, all these creations of Allah, in these forms, salvation will not be given. And for this reason, our form has been made upright. This is the upright form of an *alif*. For this reason, in the *'alam-i-a'zam*, we were also in a luminous form. So, salvation will be through this very form. The second intention he had, the intention, that is, the determination he made, the plan he made, was that he created two principles: one of *laghut* and *nasut*. And you have understood what *nasut* is. What is *nasut*? It is the Imam's body. The body of the Imam, which we call Imam, the Imam's body is called *nasut* in the language of philosophy. And *imamah* is what we call *laghut* in the language of philosophy. So *imamah* say or *laghut* say. Or *haykal*. That in which all the souls, that is, our *surat-i-ruhani*, are deposited, and it takes a form, its name is *haykal*. *Haykal* means a large structure. And this is also called *laghut*, it is also called *imamah*, and it is also called *haykal*. It is one and the same thing. So the Imam is not superior, but *imamah* is superior. If he receives the *nass*, at that time he becomes worthy of being an Imam, otherwise he is counted among the sons of the Imam, he is a son. So now, the plan of the *'Aql-i-'Ashir* was, how should I bring them out? So the first principle was that salvation would only be through the human form. The second thing was through *laghut* and *nasut*. These two poles, as they are, these two methods, it is through this. That is, the souls of all people will be deposited in the *nasut*, and their spirits will be deposited in the *laghut*, and through this, they will progress. Then the third thing that happened was that disobedience was happening, there were those who were disobedient, those who did not believe in Allah were many. The biggest group was that. And in that, those people, when punishments are given... so they should be punished in such a way... so the punishment, he at that very time devised, when we fell. And he devised that I will make such cages, such enclosures. And what are those enclosures? These are the animal forms, of different kinds, which are their enclosures. That if I give punishment to a human, and he goes into a state of descent, then these are the forms he will take. And those forms, he had all of them in his mind. And these three are there. And after that, a plan was made that I will make the spheres... this celestial sphere, I will make it revolve in such a way... so the celestial sphere is the highest sphere. As it revolved, all the smaller spheres under it started revolving. From this, day and night were formed, and from this, months and years were formed. And in this way, the world moved forward.

So I was saying that the *'Aql-i-'Ashir*, when he determined... *nasut* does not mean... it means through *imamah*. It will be through *imamah*. That is, we, progressing, *mu'min* is there, *mustajib* is there... *mustajib* is first, then *mu'min*, after *mu’min* is the rank of *hujjat*, then *da'i-i-mutasir*, then *ma'dhun*, *ma'dhun-i-mutlaq*. After *ma'dhun-i-mutlaq* is *da'i-i-mutlaq*, after *da'i-i-mutlaq* is *da'i-i-balagh*. After *da'i-i-balagh* is *hujjat*, after *hujjat* is *bab*, *bab-i-zahir*, *bab-i-batin*, *bab-i-silsilah*, *bab-i-inqita'*. After *bab-i-inqita'*, then the ranks of *imamah* begin. So whoever went, went. Their salvation, is on a different subject, God willing, it will be mentioned, which individuals of the *Muhammadi dawr* are such who have crossed over. Who have left the rank of the *babiyyah*. At this time. 1400 years ago. At the time of the Prophet, their spirituality was so high that they left the world of humanity and entered the realm of *imamah*. There are many such people. Now, what we have to see is that the *sahifas* we read about prayers, the belief we hold is that from prayers we get *sawab* (reward). So what is this *sawab* after all? The prayer we offer... I have already told you the importance of prayer, that nothing in the *deen* is as accepted, as important, as great as prayer. Not fasting, not zakat, not hajj. All are after it. The first, most important thing is *wa-aqimu s-salah* (and establish prayer). So establishing prayer is the most necessary thing. And with Allah Ta'ala, when every person, *mu’min* or *mu’minah*, passes away, and they will surely pass away, there, with Allah Ta'ala, when they go, the first question will be about prayer, not about anything else. Remember, what we are being taught is that the most necessary thing is prayer, after that are all other things. Because everything has a face by which it is recognized. And the face of the *deen* is prayer. So the face... it will be asked, did you pray or not? All other things are secondary. "I gave a lot of charity, built many buildings, planted many fields, did this." But how many prayers did you offer? If there is no prayer, then from the other side, Allah Ta'ala and the angels will turn their faces away. It has come in the books. Anyway, when we go there, our progression happens in this very way, through *laghut* and *nasut*. And through *imamah*. It is not that we individually, as I am doing a lesson today, "I" am doing it, it is not like that. Rather, it is in the form of a group. This is our misfortune, our sin, that we have been sent from above, we have become separate. And now, small, small... our one *surat-i-ruhani* cannot progress alone. Rather, in the form of a group, in the group of the Imam. With whom we have established a connection (*nazar milayi hai*), he is the Imam of our time. Today's Imam. The era in which we are born, our Imam of the time is... all the Imams from Maulana Hasan to here, we have a connection with all of them. And in the books of *haqa'iq*, I have mentioned it, but I will mention it again, that the realm of gazes that was happening was the *'alam-i-hayat* (world of life). It was below the *'alam-i-khudrat* (world of power). Go up from the jinni world, the world of stars, the spheres, above that is the *'alam-i-hayat*. Which is a special part of this world, above it. *Falak-i-muhit* (the encompassing sphere), above that is the *'alam-i-hayat*, where there is all light, and it is the *tamur-i-ruhani* (spiritual oven). *Tamur-i-ruhani* means the *da'watullah-i-ruhani*. This Kaaba, in opposition to it, in parallel, is a Kaaba. We call it *bayt al-ma'mur*, it is that very thing. There, it is also called the heart (*qalb*), it is also called the center (*markaz*), it is also called the *tamur*, it is also called the *ka'batullah-i-ruhani*. There, in the *muhit-i-nur* (ocean of light), the *nafs* (souls) of all of us have been deposited. The *nafs* of everyone who is Sulaymani here, who belongs to the people of truth, their *nafs* have been deposited. Now, when we take the oath of allegiance, at that time, we get a spark in the form of a ray. And our job is that through our ascetic practices, our prayers, our zakat, our fasting, we should ignite it and make it... like the seeds of a date, which are pounded, and from that, a shoot grows, and from that, a tree is formed, or a plant is made. So this is because of the heat of our worship that it happens. So from there, they have been sent for this reason, that we should all return to that same world. So the whole struggle is for this very reason. Now, we who have come down, have come down into different levels. As I have mentioned, there are *nadimin*, *shakkin*, and there are... and we have a connection with each one. But we, in our individual capacity, are *nazim*. From the *nazimin*, who are the Sulaymani branch. Now, if you ask, questions, that how do you prove that we are Sulaymani? So you, the most important thing is that the Quran-i-Majid is a constitution, it is the title of guidance, it is the spring of guidance. Its verses, its verses, you see and ask some other, from some sect, from some... who is a mufti, who is a learned person, ask them, what does this mean? And if you understand that meaning, then you ask our elders, and see if the explanation is done or not. So there is no book greater than the Quran. All the books that have come to the *anbiya'*, the Zabur, the Tawrat, the Injil, after that, the Quran-i-Azim that has come, it has abrogated all the books. This is the greatest book that has come from Allah Ta'ala. So its source, its spring, has come from a very high being, from the *'Aql-i-Awwal* (First Intellect). Remember all these things, that all the other books, the Bible, the other books, have come from the *'Aql-i-'Ashir* (Tenth Intellect). They have come from the tenth intellect, and this has come to us from the first intellect. And then there have been various intermediaries, and the final intermediary who descended upon the heart of the Prophet was Maulana Abu Talib *salawatullahi 'alayh*. Who was the Imam of that time. The Prophet was the *natiq*, and the one who made him a *natiq* was Maulana Abu Talib. And the world is ignorant of this. The Islamic world is so ignorant that they only consider him a *muwahhid* (monotheist), they don't call him a Muslim. Now you think about this matter.

That as many of you as are sitting, if you want to get your marriage performed, by whom will you get it performed? You would want a great personality to perform our marriage. And if not, at least a Muslim, a *mu’min*, if not some great elder. We want our marriage to be performed by such a *mu’min* or such a *mukin*, who knows the subtleties of the *shari'ah*. Now you see, we, whose follower we are, whose *kalimah* we recite, after believing in one God, we say *ashhadu anna Muhammadan Rasulullah*, the Prophet *salawatullahi 'alayh*, who is the *Nabi-i-Barhaqq* (True Prophet), the final prophet, the *Mahi-i-Rusul* (Eraser of Messengers), the *Sayyid al-Mursalin* (Leader of the Messengers), who performed his marriage? Could an infidel perform it? And Maulana Abu Talib performed his marriage. The whole world knows this, history is a witness, all things are witnesses. But the world did not want to accept it, so they did not accept it. They say, "The Prophet tried very hard for his uncle to accept Islam. He did not." "Until the end, he kept trying, that 'O uncle, you should accept Islam. You should accept it. I have brought such a good religion.'" He did not accept. You could give, but how could you take back from him? The Prophet was given prophethood by Maulana Abu Talib, who was the Imam of the time. So how could he take it? And *muwahhid*... believing in one Allah, this has been going on for ages, since Maulana Isma'il, the son of Maulana Ibrahim, from there, as many Imams as there are, they were all hidden and all were steadfast in their belief in one God. They were great *muwahhids*. In no... in the books of the *du'at*, it is written that none of them, since Maulana Ibrahim, have worshipped idols. You cannot prove in any place that they were involved in it. Rather, all were holders of high ranks. As many Imams as there were, they were all hidden. And they had... Zakariyya are coming, Yahya are coming, Shu'ayb are coming, 'Isa are coming, Sham'un are coming, all are their servants. As many prophets as there are, they are all servants, and the masters are sitting in the *hujjat*. So for this reason, they are sitting, that this time is a time of hardship for the *mu’minin*. This is a night-like era for us, and for them, it is better. Just as a *mu’min*, as pious as he may be, he is in a state of veiling at this time. For this reason, we *mu’minin* are in a state of *taqiyyah* (precautionary dissimulation). This is the *dawr-i-satr*. We are present in *satr*. As you see, *satr* is likened to the night. And at night, as many pious people as there are, they stay at home. Thieves and robbers come out, as many ruffians as there are, they stay out. Snakes and scorpions come out, harmful animals come out. In the same way, today, in the world, as much turmoil as you are seeing, reading in the newspapers, all are thieves and robbers who are out, and we are sitting quietly, in our own place. So in the same way, according to the era, Maulana the Imam *salawatullahi 'alayh* is present in the *hujjat* at this time. I have mentioned this before, in *tazkirah* and *ta'limat*, that the Imams, whoever they are of their time, are the spiritual sun. And I have proven this with various verses from the Kalam Allah, that where for the Imam, for the Prophet *salawatullahi 'alayh*, the word *sirajan munira* has come, there for the sun, the word *sirajan wahhaja* has come. What is that? A lamp. What kind? A blazing lamp. And the sun. And similarly, *sirajan munira*, a shining sun, that is, the Prophet's being is likened to the sun. So the Prophet and 'Ali were like the sun and the moon. The sun and moon of the *Dawat*. And after that, when both departed from the world, at that time, their vicegerent, the Imam of every era, takes the rank of the spiritual sun. And the *hujjat* of our *Sayyidna Sahib* of that time, they are in the rank of the moon. Now you see, the parallel should be complete, and I have explained this many times. It is a fundamental point, so you understand it. That always, as science also says, the sun has its own light, the moon has no light of its own. Just as the moon takes its light from the sun, in the same way, our *hujjat* acquire knowledge from the Imam *salawatullahi 'alayh*. The Imam has the original source, the original knowledge. So they take from him, then it comes down from there, and as the knowledge increases, it happens. So this is our system, and it is the favor of Allah that he wants that from here, the souls, all of them, should go to the *dar al-'ulum* (abode of knowledge). I will read a couplet from an upcoming *qasida*, so that you will understand. Sayyidna Shakir Sahab's poem is this, that *paron mein itni taqat de ke hum pahunche nasheman tak* (Give so much strength to my wings that I may reach my nest). *paron mein itni taqat de ke hum pahunche nasheman tak, bahut muddat hui chhuta hua hai aashiyan hum se* (Give so much strength to my wings that I may reach my nest, it has been a long time since our home was left by us). That *'alam-i-ruhani* (spiritual world), we have been gone for years. That is, you cannot count it, crores of years, even that is nothing compared to it. Crores of years have passed since we came from there. You have no idea. You don't even remember your life before this one. And if a person thinks about this, he will go mad. He will die thinking. So this is a system of Allah, that you don't know about your previous life. And we have passed through many lives, and we have to pass through many more. So this system, the system of the *shari'ah*, the system of the *deen*, this is what has always been, that in the *satr*, the spiritual sun is hidden, and the spiritual moon works. And it is from this that we get light. And look, with us, Maulana Hasan *salawatullahi 'alayh*... I am mentioning the *maratib*... after that, I will come to some *mansubs* of the prayers, because with us, the days of prayer are coming, and those are the *fara'iz* (obligatory duties) I will explain. And the day of prayer, which is the 17th, 19th, 21st, and 23rd, these are our big, special prayers. And in them, who are the *mansub*? I will just take their names so that you understand what their rank is and what it is. So with us, the first Imam... look, with us, *imamah* does not start from Maulana 'Ali. Maulana 'Ali is... *abu l-a'immah wa khayru l-ulama min shia-tina* (the father of the Imams and the best of the scholars from among our Shia). That you are above *imamah*. You are in the rank of *wasayat*. So the first Imam is Maulana Hasan, after him is Maulana Husayn, and Zayn al-'Abidin, then Maulana Baqir, then Maulana Ja'far al-Sadiq, then Maulana Isma'il, then Maulana Muhammad bin Isma'il. This is the first week, which is of seven. These seven, when they pass... and you see, with every Imam, as you have heard in the lives of the *mu’minin*, how the souls are made, the bodies are made, and with every Imam, those things come, that he bestows upon his son. Like inheritance, as inheritance is given, so he gives. That deposit, the *surat-i-ruhani*, which has been collected from all the twelve *jaziras*, every Imam has it with him. Until the seventh comes. Every seventh Imam is superior to the six. As many as you name, there you... it is written in the *sahifa*, that the six Imams, after them the seventh who comes, is the *sabi'* (the seventh). And *sabi'* means what? With us, it is *usbu'*. In Arabic, it is called *usbu'*, the week. So when one week is over, what comes with us is *jum'ah* (Friday). *Jum'ah* means what? To gather. The six that have passed, it gathers them. So *jum'ah* means to gather. In this way, the seventh person who comes, he is a very great Imam. And as many *haykals* of the seven, of the six, as are gathered, he absorbs them into himself. He is their Imam. And his degree becomes even higher. So in this way, seven such weeks have passed. Until today, how many have passed? Six weeks have passed. The first week is this. The second week is of Maulana Mu‘izz *salawatullahi 'alayh*, who established the government in Cairo, and in the Fatimid dynasty. And the third is, then, Maulana Tayyib. Tayyib. He is the 21st Imam. Then the 28th Imam came. Then the 35th Imam came. Then the 42nd Imam came. Now the 49th Imam is the era in which we are sitting. So now, in this calculation, the first week is over. The second week is of Maulana Mu‘izz. He is also the master of his week. This is a special thing to remember. He is also the master of his week. But the third week that passed, of Maulana Tayyib *salawatullahi 'alayh*, whose name we take, we put our hands on our chest. For this reason, that your kunya and the Prophet's kunya are one, Abu l-Qasim. The second thing is that many things have come to you by way of legacy from the Prophet. The third thing is that you were not the master of one week, but when you came, you absorbed your previous week into yourself. In your own being. That is, you are the master of two weeks. Who? Maulana Tayyib *salawatullahi 'alayh*, the 21st Imam. Similarly, then the fourth week started. And Maulana Ma'ad, who... the 28th Imam passed. He was the master of his week, of one week only. After that, the 35th Imam came. And his name is? Maulana Ahmad *salawatullahi 'alayh*. Maulana Ahmad is the one who came as the Imam during the time of Sayyidna Humam. Maulana Humam... Sayyidna Suleiman's time, the Imam who was there, was the 35th Imam, and he is a very great being. He is called *Qaim-i-'Al*. There are many degrees. One is this, that he is the master of two weeks. He and the master of two weeks became two. Maulana Tayyib and the 35th Imam, who was at the time of Sayyidna Suleiman. So remember this, one who remembers this knows more, the others do not know this. That who is the master of two weeks, and who is the master of one week. So the 35th Imam, at the time of Sayyidna Humam, and he was so great that at his time, 1000 years of the Prophet were completed. So on the completion of 1000 years, you came. He is called *Qaim-i-'Al*, and he has a special rank, and along with that, he is the master of two weeks. After this, the 42nd Imam. The 42nd Imam, from today... about 100 years ago, Hazrat Kiyam Sahab and Ruzali Sahab, Sheikh Adam Sahab were just before them. So those, the 42nd Imam came, he was the master of his week. After that, the week that will come now is the 49th. We are sitting in the era of the 48th. Our *Sahib al-'Asr* (Master of the Age), his number is 48. Now when the 49th comes, what will happen? He will absorb the six. According to the rule, he should absorb them. But here the rule broke. There is an exception here, that today's Imam is not included in it. And this is the real *ma'rifat* (gnosis) of today's Imam. If you do not have this *ma'rifat* of the Imam, then your death is the death of *jahiliyyah* (ignorance). *Man mata wa lam ya'rif imama zamanihi, mata mitatan jahiliyyah* (Whoever dies and does not know the Imam of his time, he dies the death of ignorance). Whoever does not have the *ma'rifat* of the Imam of his time, and passes away, his death is the death of *jahiliyyah*. He has learned nothing. He came into the world and just learned worldly things. He has learned nothing of the *deen*. So this is, his death will be the death of *jahiliyyah*. So you understand that the 48th Imam, who is the *hujjat-i-qaim*, the representative of the Qaim *salawatullahi 'alayh* who is to come, he is in their group. And he is a very great being, who in the future will have a very high, luminous rank. Some individuals will be superior, who will be the 49th Imam to come. This is the detail of our Imams. Now, I want to go to another side, that when I said that we pray. So for that, there are some conditions too. There are many such conditions. The first is that, in the *Nisab al-Ta'lim*, it is written that there are *fara'iz* (obligatory duties), *wajib* (necessary duties), and *sunan* (prophetic traditions). The *fara'iz* are that you should face the *qiblah* and pray. If you do not face the *qiblah* and pray, your prayer is invalid. This is the highest degree. The second thing is that your intention should be correct. If the intention is not right, then there is no point. And in the common people, no one has the intention. Just think in your mind that I am praying the *fardh* prayer, that's it, the intention is done. With us, the intention that has been taught, from child to old, we take that same Arabic intention. So this is the second thing, intention. The third thing is *khushu' wa khudu'* (concentration and humility). That you should pray with concentration. And one thing is our *attahiyat*. This *attahiyat*, without which I have said that there is the *tashahhud* (testimony), without which the prayer is not valid. So many things are in the *fara'iz*, many things are in our... and the most necessary thing is *takbir-i-ihram* (the opening takbir). Remember this, many people don't know that when we, after making the intention, say *Allahu Akbar*, it is necessary to say *Allahu Akbar*. And with that, to raise the hands up to the level of the ears. Some do it like this, some do it higher. Many people are like this, you see in the common people. They don't know what the ear is, what the nose is. Everything has a meaning. Everything has a meaning. No word is hidden that doesn't have a meaning behind it. If you think about it, that if the nose smells, and the eye does not see, then why did Allah make the nose like this? It's like a lump has been put on the face. So everything has a meaning behind it. When there is a word, there is a meaning to it. And whatever scripture there is, there is no translation of it, but there are its meanings. The translation is not a correct thing. Remember this. The translation is just a translation. Its meanings are a different thing. As a philosopher from Karachi said to me, he said that the translation is the original thing. A very learned man. I told him that the translation is not a correct thing, it's not an original thing. The meaning is a completely different thing. Different than translation. So I explained to him through some verses, in what ways, which you have heard from me before, from the life of Maulana 'Ali, how he proved it. Look, let me repeat it once more so that it comes to your mind. That when Allah Ta'ala starts the Quran-i-Majid, the first surah is *Umm al-Surah*. After *Umm al-Surah*, he starts with *Alif Lam Mim*. *Alif Lam Mim, dhalika al-kitabu la rayba fih* (Alif, Lam, Mim. This is the Book about which there is no doubt). Now, look at the translation under *Alif Lam Mim*. In the Bihari version, you see, in the one printed in Syria, you see, in the one from Iran, you see, in the one from Turkey, you see. Under *Alif Lam Mim* is *Alif Lam Mim*. There is no translation. There is no translation. In any language, in any sect. The reason is that these are *muqatta'at* (disjointed letters). They are locked. So with us, the books that have come, they have their meanings. I will tell you. But there, in any book, or with any translator, it is not there. And we rightly believe that under *Alif Lam Mim* is *Alif Lam Mim*. After this, *mansub* you were saying... I am explaining *mansub*. That concentration, even otherwise, is generally not possible in prayer. When praying, *mansub* is supposed to be... No, I'll tell you. *Mansub* is when we are listening to the lesson, at that time we should be aware. A human, even while praying, many things are in his thoughts. That is, even while reciting the *surah*, many things are there. A human's mind is made like that. So when you read the *mansub*, you should remember. It should be in your memory that this is their *mansub*. This is its importance. It's not that while praying, you should first take out the book of *mansub*, sit down, and then go and say the prayer. No. The prayer is only for Allah Ta'ala. The *Mubdi'-i-subhani*. For him only. No one else. But it should be in your knowledge that tonight, whose *mansub* is it? The *mansub* is *ishtifaq*. That's what Maulana Fatima is for, on the night of *saba'-i-satar*, her *ishtifaq* is there. And if it's not, then the reward has no meaning. Then we are deceiving ourselves. We are doing useless things. There is no point. Unless there is some reward for it. And whatever we are doing, we are doing it uselessly. We are doing it for no reason. There is no point. For this reason, for every *rak'ah*, there is a meaning. For every two *rak'ahs*... when we come for the two *rak'ahs* of *tahiyyat al-masjid*, what does it mean? If here, on the *hadd-i 'ali*, on the *hadd-i 'ali* and its *hadd-i 'ali*. So unless there is a meaning, it doesn't make any sense. So I was saying that the prayer we offer, we do it for Allah. Look... I was explaining the *surah* of *Alif Lam Mim*. That *dhalika al-kitabu la rayba fih*...

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-2002 lecture 6 -->

[Opening prayers in Arabic] Bismillah... al-Fatiha... [unclear recitations]. Wa 'ala alihi wa sahbihi wa sallam. Wa 'l-qur'an al-'azim... [unclear recitations]. Al-Fatiha. [Sound of setting up, a cup is placed down] Thank you.

[Prayers in Arabic] Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Allahumma salli 'ala Sayyidna Muhammadin wa 'ala ali Sayyidna Muhammadin wa barik wa sallim. [unclear recitations]...

[Prayers in Arabic] ...wa aj'alna min anil muminin al-sadiqin al-muhsinin al-mukhlasin... [unclear]... Al-Fatiha.

Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Yesterday's sermon (*bayan*) that happened, a small review of it is necessary, as is the daily practice, so that it becomes a routine, because there are many young men who are such that they want the difficult things, the words, to be repeated, so that the Arabic terms (*istilahat*) which many people don't understand, are repeated. And becoming accustomed to them is a good thing. In a way, it is like what happens in *jugali* (chewing the cud). You all must know what *jugali* is. *Jugali* is what a cow does. The food that a cow has eaten at some point, it later does *jugali* with it so that it is fully digested in its stomach. So we also need to repeat that thing from yesterday's lesson so that it is fully digested.

Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Yesterday, I was explaining that which are our religious seminaries (*dini madaris*), what is in them, and what is the religious system (*dini nizam*). This will continue for two or three days. And so that all the young people and everyone comes to know how our religious system works. And the meanings of Prophet (*Payghambar*), Messengership (*Risalat*), Imamate (*Imamat*), *Hujjat*, *Da'i*, *Ma'dhun*, *Mukasir*—what are the meanings of all these? So, many things have already been said to you, but many things have also been left out. That many very old terms (*istilahat*), every field has its own specific terms. Science has its own terms, philosophy has its own terms. Similarly, we also have them. And it should be that those specific terms are remembered. But not everyone knows Arabic, so for that reason, we are simplifying it, explaining it in Urdu, so that it is fully understood.

So, look, *Hujjat*. *Hujjat* means to make an argument for something. But *Hujjat* in our tradition is also a rank (*martaba*). And *Hujjat* is of two kinds. You can tell, anyone can say, that the ranks of *Hujjat* are of two types. Two kinds of *Hujjat*. [unclear] We use the word *Hujjat*... No, no, I explained yesterday that the meaning of *Hujjat* is to give an argument. In terms of ranks (*maratib*), what is it? The Imam's *wasi* (successor) and a... *Da'i*... No, the Imam's *wasi* is one, he is also called a *Hujjat* or a *wasi*. But the ranks of *Hujjat* are of two kinds. One is that below the Imam are the ranks, which include *Bab al-Abwab*, which I mentioned yesterday, and below that is the rank of *Hujjat*, which is the rank of our *Ma'dhun* today. This is one *Hujjat*. And the second *Hujjat* is the one which, in a special meaning, is what we call the son of the Imam, who will be the next Imam. And this is the meaning, that the Imam and his son, this is our... Mawlatuna Fatimah... yes, she is called *Umm al-Du'at*. There is one wife and one mother. She is a *Hujjat* in many senses. Mawlatuna Fatimah had a special rank, and in terms of ranks, she became the *Hujjat* of Mawlana Ali. What rank Mawlatuna Fatimah attained is a very deep matter. But the meaning that is being conveyed here is about the two kinds that I mentioned. One is on the Imam and the other is his *Hujjat*... and *ruku'* (bowing) is one. So in our tradition, *ruku'* has ranks (*darjat*), everything has a meaning. I was explaining this yesterday, that *ruku'* is the evidence (*dalil*) upon the *Hujjat* or the *Da'i*. Meaning, in our actions, we do... And what is it? That in their obedience (*ita'at*), one must be completely straight. In their obedience. And here, and when you go into *sajdah* (prostration), you don't look at your knees. You don't look at your knees, but rather you look at the place where the *tasbih* (rosary/praise) is placed. The place where the *sajdahgah* is, you fix your gaze there. In fixing your gaze, you bow and slightly tilt your neck. So its meaning is that I am bowed in the obedience of the *Da'i* and *Hujjat*, and I would rather my neck be cut. It is such an obedience. For this, the head is kept slightly raised, and we look ahead. And the *Hujjat*, which are the two prostrations, they also have interpretations (*ta'wil*). You all know, one *ta'wil* is that the two prostrations are evidence of the Imam and his *Hujjat*. And the second thing is what? *Minha khalaqnakum, wa fiha nu'idukum, wa minha nukhrijukum taratan ukhra* (From it [the earth] We created you, and into it We will return you, and from it We will extract you another time). When we place the deceased in the grave, we recite this, that *Minha khalaqnakum*, from this you were created, into this we are placing you, and from this you will be raised. So that prostration, then rising, then prostrating, its evidence is towards those two prostrations. That is its evidence, *Minha khalaqnakum*. So, anyway, the point is that our *Hujjat*, which I want to clarify to you, is that our *da'is* today are in the ranks of *Hujjat*. And their explanation will come later when the sermons (*bayanat*) proceed.

Anyway, the point is that the *Mumin* (believer), the ranks of the *Mustajib* (one who accepts) begin. *Mustajib* is the one whose pledge (*'ahd*) has been taken. When a *Mumin* or *Muminah* gives the pledge, he is not a *Mumin* yet, but a *Mustajib* who is accepting. He who has accepted. The first pledge that is taken is a special pledge. In our tradition, there is a custom of celebrating it with great joy, that the boy or girl has given the pledge, because his spiritual birth (*ruhani paida'ish*) occurs on that day. A later sermon will come on spiritual birth, when it happens, when the shortcomings are removed. This is an attachment (*ittisal*) of ours to the *Natiqah* (Speaking Soul). That *Natiqah* which we had left behind in the *'alam al-dhamud*. In the *'alam al-dhamud* and in that light (*nur*), all souls (*nufus*) were called *nufus-i natiqah* (speaking souls). And when that *nafs-i natiqah* takes the pledge here for the first time and says the name, at that time the connection (*ittisal*) happens. And through whom does it happen? Through one's *Hadd-i 'Ali*. Now what happens is that whoever's hand you give the pledge to, only they are told that you are their superior, whether they are *mullahs* or whoever, that the pledge... at that time, your *Hadd-i 'Ali* is that person. The *Hadd-i 'Ali* is not told to anyone, who is whose *Hadd-i 'Ali*. Right up to when they become *Mufid* and *Muzid*, and after that the *Hudud*, *Bab al-Abwab*, *Bab al-Zahir*, *Bab al-Batin*, until the chain reaches the Imam. So it is such a chain of the *Da'i* that gives us certainty. Where does it emerge from? Its ultimate origin is the *'Aql-i Awwal* (First Intellect). From the *'Aql-i Awwal*, the flowing substances (*mawad-i jariyah wa sariyah*) flow. From there, whenever... as I mentioned yesterday about spiritual birth, in the spiritual world, when *'Aql-i Awwal* achieved *Kamal-i Thani* (the Second Perfection). What is *Kamal-i Thani*? In the state of *Kamal-i Awwal* (First Perfection), everyone was created equally. With *'ilm* (knowledge), *hayat* (life), and *qudrah* (power), they were created. As the *bayan* has already mentioned, no? *'Ilm*, *hayat*, and *qudrah*. Three attributes were given. And the *'ilm* was that of *ma kana wa sa-yakun* (what was and what will be), they had knowledge of everything. And the second thing that came was *hayat*. *Hayat* means the most excellent state. The prime state that was there, that was what was... And the third thing is *qudrah*. *Qudrah* does not mean the power to lift a small thing or a big thing, but *qudrat-i tammah* (complete power), full power was given. So with these three things, we were created there.

Now, all these things were with us in a potential state (*bi 'l-quwwah*). To bring them into an actual state (*bi 'l-fi'l*) was our task. And the first one who brought it into an actual state was the being of *'Aql-i Awwal*. That is why he was the first to achieve *Kamal-i Thani*. Second perfection. In the state of first perfection, everyone was created equally. Just like a seed. A knowledgeable person, looking at a seed, will say that this is a mango tree. No? But it is in a potential state. Until water is given to it, it is planted in the ground, and it gets sunlight and water, only then will it become a tree. That is when it comes into its active form. But in its passive state, a knowledgeable person will say that I see the whole tree in it. Or if the seed is bad, they will say that this tree will not be good, it is spoiled. Anyway, the point is that this second perfection, the entire world has been created for this second perfection. That the sins and shortcomings through which we have passed and come here, after we achieve *Kamal-i Thani* here, we will depart from here, and our progress (*taraqqi*) will occur. And that will happen when this heaven and earth, which are like a sheet, are folded. Progress is relative (*izafi*). To progress from one place to another, from one rank to another, to reach your *Hadd-i 'Ali*, he reaches his *Hadd-i 'Ali*, he reaches his... until you reach the Imam, this is also progress, but this progress is relative. When will complete progress happen? In the final days, when you fully understand what true progress is.

Anyway, the point is that the sphere of the *Mustajib* is the smallest sphere, the first step on the ladder of *istijabat* (acceptance), which is the first of the *Mumin*. After that, the *Mumin* learns, starts to read, starts to perform *namaz* (prayer), and starts to act upon the sermon, and slowly a *Mumin* progresses through one degree, two degrees. Now, how much you progress in one lifetime (*span of life*) is up to you. And the system of *da'wat* has been laid out. Hazrat Sayyidna Dhari Sahib used to say that where the realm of choice (*ikhtiyar*) has been given to man, there the book of *shari'at* (divine law), Da'a'im al-Islam, has been placed in his hands. And where compulsion (*majburi*) arises, there the angels arrive, to support him, a *Mumin*. That if such a moment comes, if you are told to drink something forbidden (*haram*), or it is being forced upon you, this and that. So if you are a true *Mumin*, your intention (*niyyat*) is sound, but you fall into a difficult situation, if such a thing happens, then at that time the angels support you. So in difficulties and compulsions, the angels support, and in the realm of choice, you yourself must act and follow the *shari'at*. And *shari'at* is the name of the Da'a'im, and its soul (*ruh*) is the *wilayat* of Amir al-Muminin. *Namaz* is not complete, fasting is not complete, *zakat* (alms) is not complete, no part of the Da'a'im can be considered complete until there is the *wilayat* of Amir al-Muminin. And regarding this, I have explained many things, how essential the *wilayat* of Amir al-Muminin is for us. Without it, nothing is complete. The sphere of *iman* (faith) does not even begin. We will be considered Muslims. All Muslims are on a large platform, but it is not necessary that those who are... are in the sphere of *iman*. This example is just like how every person who is at a train station is not a traveler. Some are there to see people off. So similarly, understand that all the Muslims you see are there to see people off, they are on the Islamic platform. And only he will sit in the train who has a ticket. And he will take the ticket who says the name, through whose hand he gives the pledge, that is when his spiritual birth happens and he enters the sphere of *iman*. So whoever enters the sphere of *iman* will necessarily be in the sphere of Islam, which is the larger sphere. And within it is the smaller sphere of *iman*. As Mawlana Ja'far al-Sadiq showed with his finger, that this is the large sphere, and within it is this small sphere. So whoever is in the small sphere is necessarily in the large sphere also. But it is not necessary that whoever is in the large sphere is also in the small sphere. So all the Muslims we have, we cannot say that they have entered into *iman*. As has happened before, several discussions have taken place regarding the *shahadat* (testimony of faith). In other contexts, it is happening daily. That just saying *La ilaha illa Allah* (There is no god but God), it does not have sincerity (*ikhlas*), its sincerity is not there. For sincerity, one needs *ma'rifat al-hudud* (knowledge of the ranks) and *ada-i huquq* (fulfillment of rights). The explanation of *La ilaha illa Allah* is a world in itself, and until that is complete, your sincerity is not complete. And in the Prophet's time, it was asked, "Brother, by just saying *La ilaha illa Allah*, people are entering paradise. *Qulu La ilaha illa Allah tuflihu*" (Say 'There is no god but God' and you will be successful). It has come that "*man qala La ilaha illa Allah dakhala al-jannah*" (whoever says 'There is no god but God' will enter paradise). So, O Messenger of Allah, you are letting people in... hordes upon hordes are entering. And whoever is saying it... At that time, he said, "*Qulu La ilaha illa Allah mukhlisan tadkhulu al-jannah*" (Say 'There is no god but God' with sincerity, and you will enter Paradise). Whoever said it with sincerity. Now, what sincerity (*ikhlas*) is, that has a long explanation, and you will hear it in other contexts.

Our topic is that of the *manzumat* (system). So I was clarifying the ranks (*maratib*), that *Mustajib* is the first rank. After that, a *Mumin* has ten ranks. Every *Mumin* is not on an equal basis. Who is what, is known only to God and the Imam of the Time. We may be on the same traveler's bench, but with God and the Imam of the Time, his rank is determined. And where they, who are *ma'mur min Allah* (appointed by God), who are appointed by God, wherever they place someone's rank, he will be made a *Mukasir* or he will enter the sphere of the *hudud* (ranks). So from the sphere of the *Mumin*, the sphere of the *hudud* is higher. In the *hudud*, the last rank is the *hadd* (rank/limit) of the *Mukasir*. *Mukasir* means one who breaks, it comes from *kasr* (breaking). In Arabic, nuts, like walnuts and other dry fruits, are called *mukassarat*, things that are broken. We eat plain fruit, but this has to be broken before we can eat it. So this *kasr* is what needs to be done. So that *kasr* is a rank that can break—can defeat an *'alim* (scholar) or a *maulvi* (cleric) with his *hujjat* (argument), to prove his *madhhab* (school of thought) when a debate occurs, that he can break him. Only then does he attain the rank of *Mukasir*. In our tradition, this rank is hidden (*makhfi*). It is placed in the *mafasil* (sections/joints). It is below the *Ma'dhun*. No one is told, although the Bohras have a specific *Mukasir Sahib*. He is below the *Ma'dhun*. *Ma'dhun* is the rank above that, and *Ma'dhun* means one who is permitted (*ijazat yaftah*), who has been given permission to do as he wishes in the two *jazirahs* (islands/regions). And in that too, there is a *Ma'dhun-i Mahdud* (limited *Ma'dhun*), who is confined to one village, one town, one country. And the *Ma'dhun-i Mutlaq* (absolute *Ma'dhun*) is the master of the two *jazirahs*, and he is our *Mansub*. And the one who is the master of three *jazirahs* is the *Da'i-i Mutlaq* (Absolute Missionary). And the master of three *jazirahs*, you should remember, are Yemen, Hind (India), and Sindh. The *Da'i* of Yemen is the only one who has the authority to appoint *Mansubs*. No *Mansub* can appoint another *Mansub*. This has been a rule for hundreds of years, that no *Mansub* can appoint his successor until an order comes from the *Da'i*. And the *Da'i* is the one who appoints the *Mansub*. And the *Mansub* appoints the *Mufasi'*. Each rank, the higher one appoints the lower one. This is our *tasbih* (rosary/praise), that when we do *tasbih*... *sabbaha* means *tasbih*. In one sense it means to praise, in another sense it means to do *tasbih*. The *tasbih* we do, we move the bead from the bottom up. In the whole universe, this *tasbih* is happening. Every higher one is lifting the lower one up, the *adna* (lower) is being lifted by the *a'la* (higher) and being raised. And this is the *tasbih* that is going on. So here, our *Da'i* is a *Da'i-i Mutlaq*, and above that is the rank of *Da'i al-Balagh*. As I mentioned yesterday, I am reviewing everything, that *Da'i al-Balagh*, when he crosses the ten ranks of *Mutlaq*, only then does he attain the rank of *Da'i al-Balagh*. When the ten ranks of *Da'i al-Balagh* are crossed, then a person enters the ranks of *Hujjat*, and he is called a *Hujjat*. And who is that *Hujjat*? Our *Da'i* today. The current *Da'i* are in the ranks of *Hujjat*. Look, compared to the *du'at* of the past era, our *marasim* (venerable figures) have advanced thirty ranks. Because the End of Times (*Qiyamat*) is near. As great personalities are about to appear, so their commanders (*sipah salar*), their people, they also have to be of a high rank. And in this regard, this *Hujjat*, now within the *Hujjat* also, *Hujjat-i 'Uzma* (greater *Hujjat*) is separate, *Hujjat-i Layli* (of the night) is separate, and *Hujjat-i Nahari* (of the day) is separate. The *du'at* of the day and the *du'at* of the night. There are two types of *du'at*. You see, the *du'at* work in twelve *jazirahs*. Now at this time, we don't have twelve *jazirahs*, but three *jazirahs* are established. If there are other *jazirahs*, then the Imam would know, or the *Da'i* would know. What we are told now, or what we know, are Yemen, Hind, and Sindh. And in this, one *Da'i* is working, and the other two *jazirahs* are with the other. So every *jazirah* has one head. Now what is happening here? Every *jazirah* should have one head, but our *Mansub* of Hindustan has, for a very long time, been controlling two *jazirahs*. So this means he is *Sahib al-Jaziratayn* (Master of the Two Regions). That is why we write for them *al-Hadir al-Haramayn al-Sharifayn* (The Present in the Two Holy Sanctuaries). What we write is for this reason, that they are the masters of both Hind and Sindh. And *Salasat al-Zuhayr*, who is above that, the *Mutlaq*, is called *Salasat al-Zuhayr*. In the ranks of *Hujjat*. And in this, *Hujjat-i 'Uzma* is even higher. And *Hujjat-i Layli* and *Hujjat-i Nahari*. So the twelve *hudud* who are in the *hadarat* (presence/higher realms), they are higher than the *hudud* here. In terms of rank, the *hudud* of the *hadarat* are greater, and they are in the *Layli hudud*. This comes into play in our prayers, when *tahajjud* prayer or *salat al-layl* comes, the twelve and twelve are evidence for them, twelve here and twelve there. And after that is the *Bab al-Zahir* (Outer Gate). Now look, sometimes it happens that due to the demands of the time, two or three positions are given. You are a senior person, but the people are few and the conditions are not good, so you are given two ministries. That you manage this ministry and that one too. In the same way, in our view, God knows best, but I am saying from the perspective of my own heart, that the *Bab al-Zahir* who is the gate in the world, it is possible that he is also the *Hujjat* of the night. It's not written anywhere, God knows best. But I am saying that today there is no *Bab al-Zahir* who should be there. There was in the Fatimid era, there were *Bab al-Zahir* and *Bab al-Batin*. Now all the ranks have been made hidden (*makhfi*). And in those hidden ranks, it is possible that the rank of *Bab al-Zahir* is also with him. The *Bab al-Batin* (Inner Gate) is there, in the *hadarat*. *Bab al-Batin* is higher, he controls there. Why? Because the *Suwarat* (forms/images) reach him before... the topic of *Suwarat* has not come up yet, from tomorrow, God willing, this will begin.

...the history of love... And after *Bab al-Zahir*, the highest rank is *Bab al-Inbi'ath* (Gate of البعث, Resurrection/Mission). *Bab al-Inbi'ath* is the peak rank, which is below the Imam and above all *Muminin*. The last station of the journey. This is *Bab al-Inbi'ath*, and it also has ten ranks. And in terms of *ma'rifat* (gnosis), this rank belonged to Janab Salman al-Farsi. Mawlana Salman al-Farsi crossed all ten ranks of *Bab al-Inbi'ath*. He was the one unique individual in the era of Muhammad. Others came later. And he was the first. He is Janab Salman al-Farsi. You see... [coughs]... One unique individual, Mawlana Salman al-Farsi, about whom it has been said, you see, you should remember this hadith. We go to Shia gatherings, we listen to many things, and Baba... there's a lot of mourning, a lot of slogans, all that happens. But we don't understand anything. It is this, that *As-Salmanu minna Ahl al-Bayt* (Salman is from us, the People of the House). Salman is from our *Ahl al-Bayt*. In Shia gatherings, this is the sermon. And Salman... "Wow! What a thing has been said!" And along with that, slogans of Haidari and all this, this is the practice in India, that it goes on. Salutations, salutations, it keeps going. But what does it actually mean, *As-Salmanu minna Ahl al-Bayt*? What is it? The great Prophet said this. "*As-Salmanu minna Ahl al-Bayt*." "We are from the *Ahl al-Bayt*." What a status for Salman! They are describing Salman's status, but why does he have this status? They don't tell you that. What does it mean? Salman al-Farsi's? At the time when he was crossing the ten ranks of *Bab al-Inbi'ath* and moving forward, the stage of *babiyyat* (gatehood) was ending. After this, Imamate was to begin. At that moment, these words came from the Prophet's mouth: "*As-Salmanu minna Ahl al-Bayt*." "Salman is from us, the *Ahl al-Bayt*." So what does this mean? In his future manifestations, his *babiyyat* has ended, and he has entered Imamate. And regarding this, in the Haqaiq... many things have been written. Anyway, the point is that *Bab al-Inbi'ath* is the final rank. After that is the Imam. And up to the Imam, from bottom to top, there are seven *rutbahs* (ranks), just as there are seven heavens. Or there are ten *darjat* (degrees), just as there are ten intellects. They have been made smaller or bigger. Meaning, within the *Ma'dhun*, there is *Ma'dhun-i Mahdud* and *Ma'dhun-i Mutlaq*. The *Da'i* is *Mutlaq*, *Balagh*, or in the rank of *Hujjat*. And similarly, the *Da'i* is *Ihram*, *Halal*, *Ghayr Munfarid*, *Munfarid*, *Sayf*, *Qalam*—there are various kinds of *du'at*. *Da'i-i Qalam* is the one who does exposition (*tashrih*). There have been *du'at* who have filled books. And some are such that they have waged jihad in this era, two or three hundred years ago. And they have passed away after waging jihad. So they were *Sahib-i Sayf* (Master of the Sword). And some were *Sahib-i Qalam* (Master of the Pen). So each has their own expertise. Some are such that they are *Da'i* upon whom *nass* (appointment) was made. *Ghayr Munfarid*. Sayyidna Khattab bin Hasan's *nass* was made. But he passed away during the lifetime of the previous one. So they are called *ghayr munfarid* (non-solitary). Like Hazrat Sayyidna Izzat Ali Sahib, whose name you read. Mawlana Izzat Ali Sahib, whose grave is in Hyderabad. Before Sayyidna Ghulam Husayn Sahib, the *nass* was on him. But he passed away during the lifetime of the previous one. So he is not considered *munfarid*, he comes under *ghayr munfarid*. So this is a separate jurisprudence (*fiqh*), of *munfarid* and *ghayr munfarid*. And then after that *Da'i-i Sayf*, *Da'i-i Qalam*. And after that... of the *du'at*, there are *Mutlaq*, *Balagh*, and *Hujjat*. These are all the ranks. And in this, the highest rank is that of *Da'i-i Shumuq*. *Shumuq*. *Shamikh* is said to be a mountain, a high peak. Such *du'at* who are of great height. *Shamikhah*. *Shumuq*. So, of the *Du'at-i Shumuq*, four have been mentioned in our tradition. Who have achieved this rank. Among them are Sayyidna Ja'far, Sayyidna Isma'il bin Muhammad, Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hibah, and our Ali bin Hibatullah, who passed away not even a hundred years ago. He was a special master of miracles (*mu'jizat*), Ali bin Hibah. Before Sayyidna Ghulam Husayn Sahib, before Sayyidna Ali bin Mohsin Shiwam, there was Ali bin Hibah. He was a very strange *Da'i*. That he would be sitting here and seeing things behind him. He would see behind him. So there are various types of miracles, and he is called *Du'at-i Shumuq*. Four such personalities. Now, regarding the *du'at*, I want to say a few things, that the *du'at* who have passed among us, so that we know our own context, the Sulaymani community and the Sulaymani *du'at*, how they were, some of their conditions and dialogues, so that our sermon... today's subject cannot be finished. It will take the whole night. The name is long. So many things about the *du'at*, as I mentioned yesterday about the *maratib*, and other things you have heard. So there are many such things that occurred to them at the right time. And this is an inspiration (*juzbi*) they receive, the *marasim*, *du'at*, the great *hudud*. So that it is useful to them at the right time. In this context of *juzbi*, I will narrate one incident to you. Listen to two things. Mawlana Farakh Ali Sahib, I mentioned him yesterday, he was a very high personality. He was so venerable that he never spoke harshly to anyone, and he was considerate of the people and the *hudud*. The feeling was that he might be born in the *hadarat* (presence/higher realms) or come here. Because he had no conflict (*tasadum*) with anyone. Here we have a situation where if you hit me with a slap, I'll hit you with another. This is a common practice. But there, there was nothing. Conflict was a distant thing. So the feeling is that he will be in the *hadarat*. One time he went to a *majlis* (gathering) in Hyderabad. To a Shia *majlis*. In those days, venerable people used to go, taking their retinue, to show that "we too are of the Shia creed." All the *hudud* went with him. When the *majlis* ended, the *matam* (lamentation) started. Now at that time, people get up for their *matam*. They all got up. He also got up and came... Now, from where he was sitting on the stage, to come outside... how could he show that "we are also your companions, we are leaving for some work, so..." with a *takiyah* (cushion). And the *takiyah* was such that he held his hand like this and started doing *matam*. He was doing *matam*. So when the Hazrat is doing it, and you people are doing it... you don't do it, but I do it... so he was doing a light *matam*. So Sayyidna... Hazrat... Mawlana Farakh Ali Sahib was doing it. At that time, they were quiet, but on the way back, the *hudud* said, "Hazrat, you were also doing *matam*?" You were indicating something? For us, *matam* is not permissible, but you were doing it. So he said at that very moment, "Brother, where was I doing *matam*? *Matam* is what those people were doing. I was indicating. Those people were saying, 'Hay Husayn!' (Alas, Husayn!). I was saying, 'Yahan Husayn, yahan hai Husayn' (Husayn is here, Husayn is here)." So at that moment, this... he did such a... ...created the world. He is not the *Mubdi'-i Jawanoo* (Originator of Generosity). The *Mubdi'-i Jawanoo* is the one who brought existence from nothingness for us in the spiritual world. But there were some flaws, for which we were brought into this dense world.

For those who are listening for the first time, some things might be ambiguous, but later you can ask me or... make it up. That there was a spiritual world where all beings existed. All those who come into this world of creation—good, bad, polytheists (*mushrik*), disbelievers (*kafir*), all of them—all those beings were there. But when it was time to acknowledge the oneness (*tawhid*) of the Lord of the Worlds, God, it was not acknowledged, which is why they were sent down from there. And in that, there are implications, that some acknowledged God but not the First Intellect; a third did not acknowledge... Even here, all the sects we see, different people—Parsees, Muslims, Christians, Jews, whom we call *ahl al-taslis* (Trinitarians), they also acknowledge God. All this depends on intentions (*niyyat*). As were the intentions there, so they have been brought here. And there was a world of intentions. I told you about the *'alam al-hayat* (world of life), which is above the *'alam al-jurmani* (physical world) and below the *'alam al-'aql* (world of intellect). The intentions were made there, and based on that, our place here was determined. As we intended there, so we have come here. And along with this, now the situation is that our perspective or our sect, which we call Sulaymani, is from the *ahl al-haqq* (people of truth). And what is this? It is one of the fifteen sects of the *Nadimun* (the repentant). One section. Whereas the *Nadimun* are fifteen, and a very large part of them has been kept in the heavens. Kept in the heavens. On earth, if 25% came, 75% are up. And similarly, among the *Shaqawiyyun* (the wretched), a large portion is in the heavens, and the rest are in the planets. And a lot has come down here. This is the *Musirrun wa Mustakbirun* (the insistent and the arrogant). Where are they? In what we call *patal* (the underworld), which is below this earth, that is the specific abode of the *Musirrun wa Mustakbirun*. And very few of them have been brought here. Meaning, the polytheists and disbelievers we see today, a much larger number of them are down below, undergoing punishment. So these are different kinds of percentages. For those who understand, because the sermon has gone quite far. Anyway, the point is that we are one of those fifteen sects. And after that, there are twenty sects of the *Mutahharin* (the purified), twenty sections. And in those twenty, they are of different kinds. Some are less, some are more. Each one's level is not the same. Christians have one, Jews have another. As they acknowledged there and intended there, so they have come here. And along with this, now the situation is that this game has been ground down. Millions of years have passed for us in this world. And because of that, the *Nadimun* are here and there. The *Shaqawiyyun* are above and below. And the *Musirrun wa Mustakbirun* have been amalgamated. In trees and plants, in mountains, in rivers, down in the underworld, and here too. Everything is mixed up. Now, only the eye that sees, that runs the system of the *shari'at*, the *Natiq-i Waqt* (Speaker of the Time), who organizes the *shari'at*, his eye sees which thing is descending and which is in ascending order, which in descending order. Remember that which is in ascending order, that which is climbing, upon that it has been ruled that this is a *halal* (permissible) thing. And that which is descending, its name has been given as *haram* (forbidden). In every era, the *Natiq*... in the time of Musa alayhissalam, in the time of Isa alayhissalam, every era as it passed... you know that in the time of Musa alayhissalam, *charbi* (fat) was forbidden. Today we eat fat. For us, wine (*sharab*) is forbidden. The substances in wine are all forbidden. A lot of explanation will come about wine. A whole lesson will be on it. That what is evil? What is wrong with it? Is it inherently bad, or does it corrupt the one who drinks it, or what is it? That explanation will come. So I was saying that this world that is running, according to our own system, we will first take the *ma'ad* (return) of the *Mumin*. And for this, I had gone one step ahead before, about what the *madarij* (stages/ranks) are. If a *Mumin* can progress, how far can the progress go? For one's spiritual form (*surat-i ruhani*) to enter the structure (*haykal*) of an Imam, and for its effects (*ri'ayat*) to go from the Imam's *nasut* (physical form), and when its waste products (*fuzlat*) come back out, for one part to be subtracted from it, that is his paradise (*jannat*). The details of that will come. I'm just giving an outline. So, look, I'll review the *madarij* once more. When a *Mumin* takes the pledge (*'ahd*)... Taking the pledge is a condition for us. From that, his *nafs-i natiqah* (speaking soul), which is placed there... We have this belief that the *nafs-i natiqah* of every *Mumin* who is a Sulaymani is deposited there in the *'alam al-azal* (world of pre-eternity), in the spiritual world. When we were falling, we were acknowledging, accepting, "A mistake has been made by us, we accept." This acknowledging, being regretful (*nadamat*), repenting, that was placed there, deposited. And whenever we come into the world and reach the age of 12 or 14, and the pledge becomes obligatory upon us, after becoming an adult, as per the conditions written in the *sahifah* (booklet)... after that, when we take the pledge, for the first time, whoever's hand we take the pledge on and say the name, at that very moment, the connection is made from there. And it's not a visible thing, like an electric wire where you get a shock. It's not like that, but it is a spiritual connection that passes through the Imam, through all your *hadd-i 'ali*, and reaches you, who is your pledge-giver. And from that, you receive congratulations that you have taken the pledge, you are now a *Mumin*. Until then, all our children who are 12, 13, 14, and are deprived of taking the pledge and pass away or have an accident, whatever... the Muslims who are... we can only pray for them that may God return this one to my house. And the prayer that is prescribed in the *sahifah*, when we go to bury such children who couldn't take the pledge... they reached the age of 12 or 14 and passed away... at that time, when we have the funeral prayer, the meaning of that prayer is this: O Allah, return this one to me as a replacement. Make him my child again and bring him here. And all this is a system with God, of how one returns, when one returns, for how long one stays where... all these arrangements, I will explain them slowly, *in sha Allah*. I am laying out a framework so that you understand what our lesson will be for today and the next two or three days.
