So the *Mumin* who accepts, when he takes the pledge, his rank is called *Mustajib* (one who responds). It's an acceptance. "Yes, what you are saying, the one in front of me, I believe it." He hasn't started *namaz* yet, hasn't fasted, hasn't done anything, but he accepts Sulaymaniyyat, "I believe in the religion of truth." Now, whether this *din-i haqq* (religion of truth) is the religion of truth or not, you can ask as many questions as you like when you get the chance. How do you prove that our *madhhab* (school of thought) is the correct one? Many are such that they think... [unclear]

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-2002 lecture 7 -->

...to do. Prepare, prepare. What are they doing? "Well, I have to get a degree, I have to get a job, I have to go abroad." But for the eternal life that comes after, the *abadi zindagi* (eternal life), for that, what have we prepared? We have acquired the provision, we have gathered the certificates, we have done all the practical things. What else is there to do? This is for this short life, for which we have so many assets. But for the future life that is to come, for that, what have we done? What provision do we have there? What is there? The provision of the hereafter, that is our spiritual provision. So, we who do good deeds, what has been told to us is good deeds, the one who does good deeds, what happens is that the *nafs-e natiqah* (rational soul) pulls the *nafs-e hissiyyah* (sensory soul). It will come sometime. Now, another example. The development of the soul, which we have, is in this one way. And the second thing is that of our... What is it? No, it can be called *nafs-e namiyyah*. The *namiyyah* (vegetative soul) and *hissiyyah* (sensory soul). The development of the *natiqah* (rational soul). So what is the *natiqah*? Just as there is a pilot who has come from the spiritual realm, so that he may guide us, that is, it is a great blessing from Allah, it is a great grace from Allah that he is there to guide us. And he comes here as a pilot, and then he sees what the body is doing and what it is not, and by giving development to this *hissiyyah*, he takes it from here. What is the meaning of taking? That it is taken to the hereafter. If there is a shortcoming, it is sent back down. This is the process. And by giving the *hissiyyah* its development... But what happens? Because our times are such, this world is so corrupt, that the *hissiyyah* cannot be fully developed. Some part of it is left behind. A lot of parts are left behind. Some part goes. And this is all according to one's individual deeds. Now, what is it? Which is the second power? The development of the soul has been described. The body's, I have told you. Now, the second development that is there, that is of the *namiyyah*. The *namiyyah*, the power of the *namiyyah*. When it comes, it came along with... that is, it has finished. And the power of the *namiyyah* is finished. Now, the *namiyyah* emerges from the senses, it does not originate. Just as our body grows from the senses, in the same way, the *nafs-e namiyyah*... what is the *nafs*? The *nafs-e namiyyah* is that which... from where does it emerge? Some say that it is something that is born with the body, from many actions, from many such things, that the *nafs-e namiyyah* of many people, very little emerges. For some, a lot emerges. The entire thing can also emerge. According to one's capacity. But however much emerges... now we understand that if 70% emerged, then 30% is left. This 30% that is left, when does it come back? At the time of death. Now look, this is... see, that the *nafs-e hissiyyah* is there, and this is the *namiyyah*. And this one... this is the *natiqah*. The *natiqah* is that which... it wants the *hissiyyah* to become like me. Due to its company, its influence. What happens is that the *hissiyyah* is under the command of the *natiqah*. Under the command of the *natiqah* means, being regular in *namaz*, giving *zakat*, doing this and that. All these things... whatever our principles of *shari'ah* are, they are all its influence. The *natiqah*'s influence is on the *hissiyyah*. It makes our nature. And our *hissiyyah* also has an influence on the *natiqah*. So just as this... the one which is our *hissiyyah*... in the *hissiyyah*, the *natiqah* has a power. In the same way, the *namiyyah* also has a power over the *hissiyyah*. This is the whole matter. It keeps developing. For example, as a child is growing up, there are two powers within him. So one power gives, and the other has to obey. So the *nafs-e namiyyah* is also in this way. Now see, the *nafs-e namiyyah* itself is also a power. And we have the *nafs-e hissiyyah* and the *namiyyah*. These two *namiyyahs* are of a different kind. I am talking about the era of *kashf*. Don't take it as the era of *kashf*. The two *namiyyahs* are of a different kind. And he, who was *Nashr-e Safi*, when he passed away, it was said about him that he is "ten out of ten." "Ten out of ten." For many scholars, it is known. That is, what happens to many people when they pass away? That they leave some things behind. "Ten out of ten." Neither nine out of ten, nor ten out of ten. That is, "ten out of ten." So what happened is that his... and I have heard about such people that their names are known. So this one who was "ten out of ten," what was said about him? Put him in the dust of horses' hooves. Let him be, he is inanimate. He was not buried. And he was a very great personality. So a story is available about this. An *'amil* (a religious functionary). He was 33 or 34 years old. One day... a funeral came and went. People came from far away. They were silent. Now, a few months passed, and in this one, what happened was that his brother passed away. The brother came... they came, and... they were there. So when they arrived, here the burial and all was done, and the coffin was there. Now, people were saying, "Brother, what should we do? Should we recite this?" He said, "I don't know." He was the brother. Now, brotherly love was aroused. He called out his name. After calling his name, he started preparing to decorate the grave. A voice came from the grave. He heard it. "What are you doing? This is for those in the graves of horses. This is 'ten out of ten'." All the people of the Bombay *jama'at* (congregation) who were there... so the man of the time said, he gave an order and it was understood that, ah, a human is a righteous human, a *mu'min* (believer), so what is his value? After death, he is not considered like this, to be thrown away. So what happened was that the *nashr-e safi*... now look at this. In a corpse, we understand that there is only the body. That is not the case. Rather, his *nafs-e namiyyah* and *hissiyyah*, which could not progress, are present there. The *hissiyyah* leaves, the *hissiyyah* is pulled, but some part of it is left behind. The *hissiyyah* should not remain, it should leave, but our era is so corrupt that one only reads the Friday-to-Friday prayer. What is prayer? This is like the Hindus. What is the situation with the Hindus? Now look, what do the Hindus have? "Oh, you Muslims, what do you have? You read the Friday-to-Friday prayer, the Friday-to-Friday..." And a Hindu comes when? A week, Sunday, Tuesday... for them, Friday is just Friday. They say Diwali will come, Diwali will come, Diwali will come. It comes once a year. Our situation has become such that they think that this progress has not happened. There, the *nafs-e namiyyah* remains in the body. In it, there is a slight effect of the *hiss*. We who talk, it hears it. It understands. "Well, I am going to a good place." And my relatives are all crying. What is this grief they are expressing? Or is it enmity they are showing? Something is happening. Whatever is happening, it understands it all. So this is the state of our *anjam* (end), of the *mu'minin*, that after that... that is why they say, "Bury them quickly." Now, when they bury, so look, what we are saying is, leave the *hissiyyah*. The *namiyyah* of everyone remains to some extent. Our state that is at this time... the *namiyyah* is being buried along with us. Not just the body. Now, the *namiyyah* is a *nafs*. You, for a moment, close your nose and mouth for five minutes and see. You feel suffocated, don't you? Just as we feel suffocated, there, if we are buried with the *namiyyah*, and the soil is all being poured on it, and after that, what will happen to it? So this is the torment (*'adhab*) that is called *'adhab al-qabr* (torment of the grave). And this is why there is the *wahshat al-qubur* (terror of the grave) prayer. Now, for its salvation, for its remedy, what is there? That we should recite *Yasin* for them. One thing. The second is the *ziyarat ka khana* (food for the visitation). This is a must. The *ziyarat ka khana* must be done for a *mu'min*. The third thing is *fikah*. *Fikah* is that as soon as someone senior comes, he will ask the children if the father's *fikah* was given. If not, give it quickly. So, the *fikah* is that there are three levels in our community. 400, 800, and 1600. The total is 2800. If 2800 is given, then these three, which are the *nufus* (souls), *nafs-e namiyyah*, *hissiyyah*, *natiqah*, for each of the three *nufus*, the three *fikah* have been kept. *Fikah* is from *fakk*, and *fakk* means to open. The knot (*bandish*) that is on our *namiyyah*, on our *ruh*, the lock that is there, that is... Allah slowly opens it when we give this. Now why money? They say it is a worldly thing, so... They do this and that. In money, currency is the ultimate... our, of any thing, we... will see, we will give in money. And what is it? This is... let me also tell you about this... that I have to go to different places. There is the collection of *zakat*, there is this, and that. So every question must have an answer. So, it happened in different places... "Well, brother, *qaza namaz* (missed prayers)." Now see, this will also be mentioned, this week it will be mentioned, that *qaza namaz*, *qaza roza* (missed fasts). For *qaza roza*, we have a category (*madd*), that however many fasts have been missed, we, in a year, see and... how many they are. For that, we give money. So how much do they give? As the city is, according to that. So that is... to feed a poor person. Now, in the old days, a poor person could eat for five rupees. Today, he can't eat for ten rupees. So it is different in different places. You, according to your own understanding, whatever you think is appropriate... Now this is... that is, seven... your... thirty fasts were missed. For thirty, a lump sum amount has also been made, which we collect here. Now this *qaza sawm* (atonement for missed fasts) is that... it came up somewhere. There, for *qaza sawm*... the date was announced. And 35 people's *zakat* was being collected from them. That is, their family was so big. 35 individuals. And one *zakat*. They said, "How is this?" The one who is the elder brother, everyone is his children. They are his... they are giving. So, they made a joke about this. Then what happened is that it was split. If it was 10,000 or 20,000, it was split among his children... something was reduced and given. This matter was settled. "Okay, we accept it." After that, *qaza sawm*. So, I was sitting with them, I had just newly gone, two years had passed. They said, "Brother, you explain *qaza sawm* to them because *qaza sawm* is not in their custom." So they said, "What is *qaza sawm*?" "What is it?" Good, there are two daughters... The *qaza sawm* is that the fasts that you have missed in your life, for that, money has been appointed. That is, you have missed them. You missed them intentionally. If you are sick, then for that... you are exempt. But if you are on a journey, you are not. But where you have missed them intentionally, for every fast, there is this much amount. Now 35 people... 35 people said, "How many fasts have been there?" So this one said this, that one said that. Thinking, two men were ready. That they had kept all their fasts. The rest, one said my... 20 fasts. One said I... so, it came to 3,000, 3,500 rupees for *qaza sawm*. So, now, they had to accept it. "Alright, we will give it." He gave it. This is a side story I am telling, explaining the context. He gave it. Now what happened after that is that the next day when they came, they said, "Brother, there is one thing about this. We have given it. But what about the person who doesn't have money? And if we have given the money, is our fast complete? When we did not keep them... now we are doing it." What is its method? So I told them that there is no need to count all of them. We will take it for the year and finish the matter. An easy, easy method was found. So, I am saying that the *da'wat* is not like that. It is that you are given a chance to make up for it. If you have missed 10 fasts, you have from this whole year until the next year, you can keep them. Women especially miss them. They can keep them and finish them. So we insist that you keep the fasts, rather than giving money. But now you are coming on the day after *Shab-e-Qadr*. Ramadan has started. Now the matter is finished. This is a matter of the past two Ramadans. Always, *qaza sawm* is done on the last Friday of Sha'ban. So that... now, you have only to give *qaza*. So now, you will have to give because you have been delayed. So he said, "I asked him. If you give it, the matter is finished." So I said, "Look, I am telling you one thing. Now you will understand." I had to explain it to each person according to their own capacity. I said, "You, why did you miss the fasts?" "Well, there was work at the shops. There were offices, and there are factories, this and that." What kind of work? Overtime. Overtime. And you were not keeping the fasts. And what kind of work were you doing? Overtime. So what happens with overtime? Extra money comes. So you, by shedding your sweat, by shedding your blood, you earned that overtime, and there you took the money. Now, the thing that you have earned, Allah says, *lan tanalu 'l-birra hatta tunfiqu mimma tuhibbun* (You will not attain righteousness until you spend from that which you love). Now, in today's world, everyone loves money. They don't love their lives. So the thing that you love, give that thing. Otherwise, there is no meaning in why there are rupees for *zakat*, why there are rupees for *fikah*, and why there are rupees for *qaza sawm*. All of them are calculations. In the time of the Prophet, it was dates for *fitra*. But today, all things are in this way, that the final thing we consider is that we do all our transactions with it. And besides that, this is a *ta'wil* (interpretation) according to the rulings of *shari'ah*. Apart from that, let's go higher. We who give the amount for *fikah*, and listen to this carefully, the amount for *fikah*, so the price of *fikah*, you are a slave. What thing is being freed? It is related to you, related to the grave, related to the *namiyyah*. It has come to a great person's hand so that your atonement is complete, the matter is finished. So all things are connected. This, which has come in the form of gold, has come as a pilot, has come in the form of a soul, whatever form it has come in, all of them are... just in this way, they have done great work. And to pay for that, these have been appointed. So no thing is such that there is no reality behind it. And in between, *ta'wil* comes. I can go into *ta'wil* and many things can get confused. So anyway, this... our time is... oh, time is up. So this *nafs-e namiyyah* is there. When the *namiyyah* is taken away from our body, it is taken away, but it does not leave completely. A little bit of it remains. When it remains, then what is it? That is the torment of the grave. The torment of three days. This is called *'adhab al-qabr*. The *wahshat al-qubur* prayer is for this reason. And in one way, it is a restriction on the *mu'min*. *Fakk* is to open, it is a knot, on which we, by giving three *fikah*, open it. That is, when we are buried, and after that, three days have passed, the *ziyarat ka khana* is done, all these things, or *Yasin* is recited, or a whole Quran is recited, or *sadaqa* and *khayrat* are given, from that, many things are forgiven. And after that, from there, after three days, some, after a short time... everyone according to their own status. He is that... the thing that emerges from the grave, now see, it is the *namiyyah* that was buried, that was imprisoned inside, was captive, that is, by Allah's command, it emerges from the grave. And how does it emerge? In the form of wind, in the form of air. It is called *nafs-e rihiyyah*. And the *nafs-e namiyyah* was when it was in our body. Now it has become *nafs-e rihiyyah*. Its name is *rihiyyah*. One is a *rihiyyah*. If there are ten, they are called *rihiyyat*. And those *rihiyyat* are all... however many people are being buried, all those *rihiyyat* rise up and go up. They are in the form of vapors. So they will go to the moon. The surface of the moon is a station. From the moon, it goes to Mercury, from Mercury to Venus, from Venus to Jupiter. From Jupiter, it goes to the sun, and then, turning back, it comes and stops near the moon. I will mention this tomorrow, I will recap it. Here, on the surface of the moon, it comes and stops. And after that, its purification and all that is done. It stays there until the Imam's will is not such that "Now my successor should be prepared." When the Imam's will, his order came, that "Now my son should be born, who is fit to become an Imam." Now, however many there are, now, for example, Imam Husayn has 'Ali Akbar, 'Ali Asghar, and Imam Zayn al-'Abidin. So Maulana 'Ali Akbar, Maulana 'Ali Asghar, they were sons of an Imam, but they were not Imams. We say out of reverence, "Imam 'Ali Akbar, Imam 'Ali Asghar." But the Imam is Maulana Zayn al-'Abidin, *salawatullah 'alayh*. So his formation, his creation, is completely different from others. Just like ours happens, it is not like that. Rather, the Imam's *Nasut-e Sharifa* is being formed. And how is the *Nasut-e Sharifa* formed? Our spirits (*arwah*), their bodies are formed from them. Now see, this is a degree. That our spirits, which we cannot see, from them the *rihiyyat*... and in the *rihiyyat*, because of the impurities, he becomes visible. If the impurities were not there, he would not be visible. So, there is a leveling in it, when to appear, when not to appear, and which part is coming forward. Anyway, this... from the surface of the moon, that substance, in the form of dew, or in the form of water, or else, in the fruits of paradise, pomegranate and apple, in the apple, it penetrates, that substance. Which one? All these *nafs-e rihiyyah* that have gathered, in a condensed form, an essence is formed. The essence is formed and it... the esteemed ones, where the Imam is present today, it goes there. Wherever the Imam may be. It goes there, and that Imam himself eats it, and then his wife eats it. Both must eat it. And then, after meeting, the child that is born, that, in the true sense, is the *Nasut-e Sharifa* of the second, the coming Imam. *Nasut*, the word is for the body of the Imam. Now this body of the Imam, which is formed from our very *rihiyyat*... Imam... now see, I... in one... a good four or five days ago, when there was a lesson, there I explained the sect of the *Qalamun*, that I will explain later. That in the *Qalamun*, their mistake was a blink of an eye. And they remained there. But their mistake was a blink of an eye, the effect of which was that Allah... the order came for the *'Aql-e 'Ashir*, that "Now you go to the world and give them guidance." "You are celestial beings." So they cannot come, and they have to come. So how will it be? They have to come, but they are a creation from there. So how will they come here? So the essence remains there, its reflection comes here. And that reflection is the *hijabat* (veils). Here, now the issue of *hijabat* is being cleared up. That there, the essence of the *Muhtajib* remains, and just as the switch is flipped there, the current is there, but the bulb is lit here. Here, in the world, Maulana Zayn al-'Abidin came. And he... the body of the Imam was prepared, in which... the Imam who is the father, he wishes that "Now my son should be born." After his order, all these things happen, a movement happens wherever it is, and from there, the movement happens and it... from the surface of the moon, it comes down and... this birth took place. After birth, again, the foods that are there, the remaining ones from there, from above, they come. That is the best food that our mothers take in, and from it... the best, the best... dry fruits and that food and all... from that, the child is nourished. Now this child who is born, he is the body of the Imam. The Imam's body is not separate. The Imam is from there. But our very body, our very *rihiyyat*, they make their body. Understand? They have taken it. Our... our *nafs* is there, and their body is there. This is one level. One level. So just as we cannot see our *nafs*, in the same way, we cannot see the Imam. But what is it? That in the *Nasut* also, there are levels. The one which is the lowest of the low, which is so dense that our eyes can see it, at that time, the Imam appears. Where the level is higher, the Imam disappears. So that... I will explain tomorrow, that the *Nasut*... how many kinds are there, what is the highest *Nasut*, what is the lowest, and then there are many... a whole explanation will come on this. And according to this *rihiyyah*, the veil of the Imam, the *Nasut* of the Imam is prepared. Now after this, our real progress is that we have progressed from the body. The *namiyyah*'s progress has happened. Now the *hissiyyah*'s progress. The one which is saying "I," its progress... and that... we who are saying "I," its progress is the *haykal-e zuhuriyat*, which I will tell you. Now going to the Imam, we are in the form of *Nasut*. Now, the *Nasut*... see, this is a bit... The Imam is... see... a human being. On whom... in our own way, it is said that it is the *hissiyyah*. In the same way, the Imam has a *dhat* (essence). Now, below it are things, and above it are things. So what is below? Below the *dhat* of the Imam... now, it is the *nafs-e natiqah*, the *nafs-e hissiyyah*, the *nafs-e namiyyah*, and after that, the Imam's *nasut*. *Nasut-e tabi'i*, *nasut-e khass*, *nasut-e zilli*. *Nasut-e khass* is the highest. Then *nasut-e zilli*, then *nasut-e tabi'i*. Below the *nasut-e tabi'i* is the *nasut-e tanasuli*. Below the *nasut-e tanasuli*, going down, he gives *hijabat* (veils). Because the *nasut-e tanasuli* of Maulana 'Ali *salawatullah 'alayh* was so high that it was not visible to people. So, he took *hijab*. And he did such high work in *hijab* that he had to put a *hijab* over the *hijab*. He was veiled within another veil. So, *hijabun 'ala hijabin 'ala 'l-mu'minin* (a veil upon a veil for the believers) came into play. After that, above the *hijab* is the *nasut-e tanasuli*, *nasut-e tabi'i*, from *tabi'i* up is *zilli*, from *zilli* is *khass*, then the *dhat-e Imam*. After the *dhat-e Imam*, again the degrees are upwards. Their *nafs-e hissiyyah*... *namiyyah*, *hissiyyah*, *natiqah*. Above the *natiqah* is the *khamir-e nadamat*. *Dhat-e Imam*. Then after the *dhat-e Imam*, *khamir-e nadamat*, their way is there. Then *nazar-e Imam*, their gaze, where it fell. Above the *nazar-e Imam* are the *sawaqit*. The final station is *sawaqit*. What is *sawaqit*? If there were no *sawaqit*, Maulana Hasan and Husayn would not have come. So the *sawaqit* are... one degree that... the first Imam came, the second came. So the former are coming, and below them are the latter. So these are different... I will give more details on this, maybe I will keep it for tomorrow... It will be the topic for tomorrow. The lesson for tomorrow is over. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. [Arabic du'a follows]. Al-Fatiha. [Sound of people leaving, chatter]. Lesson 8. Believing men and women. The discussion we have had, in that, I want to recap a little, because many new individuals have also come, and there are many who are hearing it for the first time, it will be better for them. We also have your saying that for the people who have understood, if they understand a little less, it's okay, because they have already heard it. It's not for them, but for the new ones, such an arrangement should be made that they can always listen and understand. So I will make a brief sketch so that many people who are not coming from the beginning of the lessons, for them too, it will not be a problem. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. This is the *da'wat* (mission) of our *Imam-e Zaman*, and the tradition and custom of the *Ahl-e Haqa'iq* (People of Realities) is that the way we live our lives is on a special path, on a special order it has been kept, and this order, the Prophet, *salawatullah 'alayh*, has organized it through his *Shari'at-e Gharra* (Radiant Law). That is, the meaning is that the shortcomings from us in the *'alam-e arwah*... The shortcomings that occurred from us... in a special system, this our *Shari'at-e Gharra* has been made. And I have explained this before, that how the Prophet, *salawatullah 'alayh*, who is our *natiq* and he is our perfect guide, he, for the shortcomings that occurred from us in the *'alam-e arwah*, as a remedy, as a medicine, as a cure, has presented it here in the form of the book *Da'a'im al-Islam*, that is, our seven pillars. You know that this world that we are seeing, it is not the world whose creation Allah, the *Mabda' Subhanuhu* (The Originator, Glorified is He), did. Now, the explanation of *Mabda' Subhanuhu*, you have heard it before, and for the new people, I am saying that *Mabda'* is he who creates existence from nothingness. There was nothing, at that time, he who created, he is the perfect one. If there is some thing and we make something from it, that is not perfection. We are creating, we are reshaping something. But he who creates from nothing, he is our *Mabda' Subhanuhu*, for whom we pray, for whom we prostrate, for whom there is worship, for whom there are prayers, he is the *Mabda' Subhanuhu*, who is in the *'alam-e 'adam* (world of non-existence), who is in the *'alam-e ruhani* (spiritual world), in the *'alam-e nurani* (luminous world), he does his work. And for him, there is sanctification, for him, there is glorification. We, alhamdulillah, the glorification we do is for him, the *Mabda' Subhanuhu*, who is the final being, about whom we can know nothing, we only have the order that we must affirm it. About his gnosis (*ma'rifat*), all the saints (*awliya*), from Janab 'Ali and Janab Prophet, it is said that "Do not think about your creator, think about the creation." There is no need to think about the creator. That is, about the *Mabda' Subhanuhu*... we cannot understand our own *nafs*, what the soul is, so how will we understand the creator and the *Mabda' Subhanuhu*? So, the *Mabda' Subhanuhu* is that being who brought from non-existence into existence, there was nothing, from that he created us, and countless beings he created there in the *'alam-e arwah*. Imagine that there is an *'alam-e arwah*, a spiritual world, which is all a matter for later. This world, this universe that has been made, this whole universe, this is a reflection, it is a punishment, it is a manifestation, it is an image of that spiritual world. In that spiritual world, he created all beings in a uniform state. Otherwise, Allah's justice would not be maintained. Here, someone is rich, someone is poor, someone is black, someone is yellow, someone lives in Africa, someone lives in America, for everyone, there are different fates, different situations. All these fates being different is for here, because we have brought it upon ourselves. If we are rich, it is our own deed, that we have done something, because of which wealth has come. Those who have become poor, it is all their own doing. This will all come later. But Allah is just. If we say that, then that is the being of the *Mabda' Subhanuhu*, who created the universe, where there is the spiritual world, he created all beings at one time. And after that... it should have been that when everyone was created at one time, all the reasoning is the same, everything is there, so the result should have been one. But the *a'imma* say that that is not what happened there. Because, look, there is one thing, that we fire a gun. When we fire a gun, the pellets you put inside, and they are filled in the barrel, so when you press the trigger, the pellets that come out, as long as they are in the barrel, confined, until then, one will come out after the other, they will come out in a constrained manner. But as soon as they are outside the barrel, one goes forward, one goes backward, one goes here, one goes there. In the realm of freedom, everyone has the choice to go wherever they want. Just like our situation is today. We had a freedom much greater than that. We had complete freedom. Three things, *'ilm*, *hayat*, and *qudrah* (knowledge, life, and power), Allah created us with. And these three great abilities, all the abilities of the whole world, all the capacities and abilities, if you condense them into one collection, they will be included in these three: *'ilm*, *hayat*, and *qudrah*. And the explanation of all these things has been done, that *'ilm* is the knowledge of what has not happened and what has happened. *Qudrat-e taammah*, that is, complete free will was given. It wasn't that we can lift a small thing but not a big thing. It wasn't that kind of will, but complete free will was given. And with it, a life was given that was not like our life today, that today we are sitting, and after half an hour, a headache starts, then after two hours, we get diarrhea. This is not life. Life is called the eternal state, and that life, the eternal state, was given to all of us. There, everyone was created on an equal basis. And at that time, he who... and his intention was this, Allah's, that they should sanctify him, that they should worship him, that they should bow down before the object of worship. And what happened? That one single individual, you have all heard this, I am recapping it quickly, that one single individual, in the whole world, in the whole universe, where today, see, the population has reached five billion, there was a time when there would have been several thousand in Adam's time. It has increased to this state. The population that was there, ours, was countless. There was no count of it. There, in such a state, one single individual thought that "I have been created by myself, and these are my kind who are in front of me, they have been created by themselves, but the one who created us all is someone else." And this thing, today, seems very easy to us, that "Brother, we could have thought this too," but we did not think. This is after taking these slaps that it is being understood. At that time, only one single individual thought, and he became the *'aql-e dahdah* (tenth intellect), and he became the *'aql-e 'ashir* (tenth intellect)... *'aql-e awwal* (first intellect). Such a being was he, who achieved precedence. The entire universe, that is, just as someone comes first in a class, he feels a strange joy. In the same way, he was the first of the universe. No one could surpass him. Neither could they surpass him before, nor today, if the whole universe shrinks and goes into the spiritual world, no one can reach the *'aql-e awwal*. He is *al-awwalu fa 'l-awwal* (the first is the first). He became the first, it's over. The pen has dried up. That is, that pen has dried up. One order has been given, that he is the first. He is the *mawjud-e awwal* (first existent), the *mabda'-e awwal* (first origin), the *'aql-e awwal*. That being is the greatest of all. When this happened, then seeing him, now it should have been that however many there were, ten, fifteen, twenty, would have advanced, but that did not happen. Rather, two individuals arose from there. Now see, two, two individuals have arisen. Their name is *Munba'ithayn*. The individual's name is not there, but it is *Munba'ithayn*, meaning those who have arisen. *Munba'ith* means to arise. Two *Munba'ith* arose from there, and among them, one reasoned about them, looked at their inner reality (*batin*), "What is the reason that they have this light?" Because in the first *'aql*, when he achieved recognition, when he achieved the oneness of the Lord of Glory, then veils of joy began to cover him. Just as someone comes first in the university, how much joy he feels. A blushing happens on the face. In the same way, the state of the *'aql-e awwal* happened. So many veils of joy covered him, and today, it is said in the *du'a* that "Just as, just as it is happening there in the *'alam-e arwah*, so what is happening?" These veils of joy are being lifted. There, their glorification, their *namaz*, their worship is only this, that the veils of joy... Anyway, this... that the first being, he did not see anyone, he did not know anyone. Without a teacher, without a guide, he understood the oneness of the Lord of Glory. And after that, however many came after, they advanced by seeing him. And they were two, the *Munba'ithayn*. This is why, see, in any language of the world, there will be singular and plural. There will be one, and two... from two, the form of the plural begins. But in the Arabic language, which is the *'Arabi Mubin* (clear Arabic), the language of the Quran, the language of Allah, in it, after the singular comes the dual. This is proof that there, the *Munba'ithayn*, two beings arose. After one, two. After one, two who are... the dual form is taken. Those who know Arabic language. And in them, now see, if two men arise together, it should be that both of them... but there, the difference of gaze occurred. And the biggest, the biggest issue that happened there was this, that one looked at his outward form, the outward form of the *'aql-e awwal*, and one looked at "Why is there such a change in them? Why is there such light in them?" The one who looked at the inner reality, he became the *Munba'ith-e Awwal* (First Emitted One). And the one who looked at the outward form, he just kept looking. He just stood there. Constant. Frozen, petrified. He just stood there. And immediately what happened? That after that, it was a field of freedom. No one was... everyone had a choice, there was no restriction on anyone. There, seven beings arose, like seven Imams. Leaders. With each one, there was a large circle. With each one, there were millions and crores of people. The *du'a* says that no one can know the count of Allah's army, how big it was. Seven beings arose and advanced in the spiritual world. And they also recognized the oneness of the Lord of Glory. And after that, they knew the *'aql-e awwal* and the *munba'ith-e awwal*. But except for the one who was the second one, he just kept looking. And he was constant. That he did not understand what to do. Then it was... what happened? That a fault occurred from them. I am being brief. Their fault was this, that they accepted the *'aql-e awwal*, they accepted the *Mabda' Subhanuhu*, but the one who was their peer, who had advanced, who had seen the inner reality, it was incumbent upon the one of the outward reality to follow him. He did not follow his peer. That he is superior to me. And this was the cause, this is the whole universe that was formed. The false notion of equality. The false notion of equality that arose, that "We are on an equal basis. You and I have arisen together, we are both equal." But it was not so. The one who had advanced had advanced. It was necessary for him to follow him. When they stopped, and seven beings advanced, then he realized, and there, he requested that "Why have I stopped?" He woke up from his sleep. He woke up and said, "Brother, from whom should I ask?" So the ninth intellect, that is, two intellects had already gone up, the *munba'ith-e awwal* and the *'aql-e awwal*, and after that, seven had advanced. Seven and two are nine. He approached the last individual of the ninth intellect and asked, "Brother, what is my fault?" And his fault was pointed out, that "You considered your peer to be your equal." This fault in the world was very big. The fault that happened there at that time, that is the cause of this whole world and universe. There, he immediately accepted it, that "Yes, a fault has occurred from me. I have repented and turned back." He repented at that very moment and turned back. Now, it should have been that with his repentance, his large circle... and the *du'a* says that his was the largest circle. That circle should have advanced with him and immediately accepted the repentance. But that did not happen. And what happened there? *La nasma'u wa la nuti'* (we will not listen and we will not obey). This slogan was echoing in the sky, that "We will not believe, and we will not listen to your words." "We will not listen to your words." The slogan of *la nasma'u wa la nuti'* was echoing. In that, what happened was that a few, say about 15%, a few of his peers who were there, they advanced. It happens that if there is a leader, then some, right away, are with him. In the same way, they were with him. And they repented first. They are called *tabu bi 'l-tawbah* (those who repented with repentance). After that, he... a *da'wat* (call) was given. This *'aql-e 'ashir* that was formed, the one who was to be the tenth, when he accepted his fault, his number became the tenth. He is called *'ashir-e mudabbir* (the tenth, the administrator). He is our creator. He is the one who has administered the whole world. He is the one who has made it. So what happened was that he immediately started giving *da'wat*. This is called *da'wat-e 'ashiri* (the call of the tenth). And that is the *da'wat* on which, here, wherever the people of truth are, there will be an institution of the *da'wat sharifa*. In this institution of the *da'wat sharifa*, this is what happens, that people are called towards Allah. There, they were given the *da'wat* that "A mistake has occurred from me, you people also accept it." You also accept it. But a few people from among them accepted it, who are called *labbaw 'n-nida'* (they answered the call). On his call, a few people, say 10%, 20% more, repented and advanced. Then, a third time, it so happened that always, where there is a group, there will be a committee, there will be a conference. A few people will agree, a few people will remain silent, a few people will be stubborn. Here too, it happened that some people, in a blink of an eye, the blink of an eye, they made a mistake. And they are the *qalamun* (pen-like beings). *Tabu bi 'l-tawbah*, *labbaw 'n-nida'*, and *qalamun*. This *qalamun* sect, they are our *mutaqaddimin* (predecessor) Imams. From Maulana 'Ali onwards, all the Imams who are coming, *wahidan ba'da wahid, waladan min walad* (one after another, a son from a son), all these are the *mustakharin* (successor) Imams, they are from the *qalamun* sect. And I have explained this on the occasion of *ta'mur* that they are from the *qalamun* sect. A small mistake had occurred from them there. And in its recompense, what happened? That they had to come here. Because they were designated as obligated, that *man kasa azman, fa 'alayhi 'l-jabruhu* (he who breaks a bone, upon him is its mending). The *'ashir* had entrusted it to the one below him. The *'ashir*... going down, down, down, this was entrusted to the *qalamun* sect, that "Because of you, these people are wandering. Now you go into the world and give them guidance." But they are a creation from there. From there. How can they come here? So he did what... and this is today's lesson... he took a garb from us. Neither Maulana Zayn al-'Abidin came, nor Maulana Husayn came, nor Janab 'Ali came. Their *muhtajib* (veiled reality) is there. The essence is there. Only its reflection has come here, an image has come, a veil has come. And what is that veil? That is... from our very *rihiyyat* (spiritual emanations), their formation has been made, their *nasut-e sharifa* (holy human form) has been made. Otherwise, they would not come. Now, why did they have to come? Because how will we get guidance? And it is incumbent upon them to give us guidance. So this is an example like this, just as there is a jail. This whole world is a jail. And these are the jailers. So just as a jailer comes and what does he do? He puts a chair there and sits in one place. And he comes in a special attire. The jailer has a special attire. Here, the human... they have also come in human attire. They also have to give that much duty, eight hours, ten hours, however much one has to sit. But the jailer, you, although he will also sit for that long as the prisoners, but the prisoner is a prisoner, and the jailer is a jailer. On this example, see that our *Imam-e Zaman* and all the *mustakharin* Imams who are coming, it is his reflection that comes. The essence, their *muhtajib*, is present there in the *ta'mur-e ruhani*. This is a sketch. After that, you all know how the world was created, that the *'ashir*... what happened was that this huge group that was left behind, it was a very big group, how should they be dealt with? And they do not need to stay there, because now all the people were raising the slogan of *la nasma'u wa la nuti'*, "We do not believe." "Because a mistake was made by you, now how can we believe that you are speaking the truth?" So, they kept on being stubborn. When execution was carried out on them, and along with the order, the implementation was also carried out, the matter became implemented, that these people should be sent down to *'aybat*. And with *'aybat*, one group went out. One. But the execution had started. And what is that? It is the group of the *nadimin* (the repentant). I have explained in detail, the group of the *nadimin* is there, after that there is the group of the *tahirin* (the pure), and a third one is the *musirrin* (the stubborn). The *musirrin* were those who were stubborn, and even today they are stubborn. Whether they are Hindus or atheists, they who do not believe in God, all of them belong to that sect. The *musirrin*. They are insisting that "There is no God or anything." They are atheists. There, in the world of non-existence, their seed is there. Whenever they come here, they will keep doing the same thing. Look. And there is one such group that immediately... *nadim*, the meaning of *nadim* is to ask for forgiveness for one's sins. At that very moment, they said, "A mistake has occurred from us." If they had accepted it at that very moment, they would have remained up there. But that world was gone, the spiritual world. Now, in the one below it, which is the world of life... I have drawn the whole map, that after the spiritual world, a layer comes, the world of life. In that world of life, there is the *ta'mur-e ruhani*. Just as here there is the Ka'ba, there, up there, in the spiritual world, there is a Ka'ba, in an abstract form. And next to it, there is a pillar, a pillar of light, where our *nafs-e natiqah*, that is, the *mu'minin*, who are the *nadimin*, their *nafs-e natiqah* has been kept. And this is Allah's grace. If our *nafs-e natiqah* were not there, we would not be sitting here. It is because of the *nafs-e natiqah*. Anyway, this... that there, the *qalamun* sect did its work, and after that, the *nadimin* became, the *musirrin* became, and the *shakk-e muta'ayyin*. What is *shakk-e muta'ayyin*? That two ends, one who does not believe and one who believes, they remained in between. And they said, "Brother, we don't understand if these people are true or those people are true." So they remained in between, they were hesitant, and they were doubtful. *Shakk-o-muta'ayyin*. They were in doubt and were hesitant. And a group of them was formed. And after coming here, when Allah's order came that "They should be thrown out," so how can they be thrown out? They were in a luminous form. There, all the beings were in a luminous form. So now, to bring density in them, first, darkness was brought. Darkness is in the inner reality, and after that, the physical... so when darkness came, a dark quality came, they started to get scared. They were filled with terror. When they felt terror, they ran. From that, their length came, their height came. And after that, they felt terror again, they ran in another direction. So from that, their width came. And after that, their depth came. And I have explained this in great detail, how, how first the inner depth came, the inner all things came, and then the physical density came. Because even after this much, they were not believing. So their outward and inward forms were made. And with that, you have the map of last week, from that you will see, you will know. And from that, our future discussion, which is about halal and haram, will be known. Anyway, this... in three groups, and I have told you about each group, that the *nadimin* became fifteen groups, and the *musirrin* became thirty-five, and the *shakk-e muta'ayyin* became twenty. This makes seventy groups, in which the world was divided. And they... the administrators, who were there, there was *Adam-e Bid'ah*. The details become too much. That there was *Adam-e Bid'ah*, and with him were twelve administrators. These administrators... that "How should the world be run here?" So first, Saturn was given kingship, and after that, Jupiter got it, then Mars got it, then the Sun got it, and after that, Venus, then Mercury, and then the Moon. Each one took 7,7000 years, in partnership and individually. First, Jupiter worked alone, then it took another one with it. When it took another one, good and evil mixed, and here in the world, things started to appear. The greenery was formed at that time, trees, mountains, all these things are there, which I have explained in detail. All these things happened, and after that, when 49,000 years passed, that is, the era of the Moon came, and it was the final era, then... at that time, these seven, that is, with the Moon, when the Sun and Jupiter and as their combinations happened, according to that, here, the leavens of humans began to be formed. The leavens, that is, just as a thing is prepared first, and from its leaven, just as we keep a little yogurt, and the next day we put it in milk, so the next day the milk also becomes yogurt. In the same way, the leavens were deposited all over the world. And then Allah's power willed that how... here, that masterpiece, for which the world is waiting, this whole world was adorned, the house was built, then the resident came, and he was the human being, the noblest of creatures, who, in this upright stature, because this upright form of ours, we are not bent like animals, we are not rooted like plants, but the human being, who walks with his head held high, so this, in this upright stature, the thing that was there, that was the seed of the pre-eternal world. There too, all of them were in an upright stature, that is, in the form of an 'alif', in an upright form they were, in a luminous form they were, just as you light a candle, the light is in an upright form. In that form, they all were. And this was the most beloved form, that is why Allah created us here in the form of humans. Now what happened? That the first time when he created, so all these things had to be set, that where should the *nadimin* be, and where should the *musirrin* be, and where should the *shakk-e muta'ayyin* be? So the portion of the *nadimin*, as I said, the whole sun and moon, and whatever there is, the world of stars, is filled with *nadimin*. And after that, it also happened that some of their parts came down. 25% came down, 75% are up there. The *shakk-e muta'ayyin*, if 90% are there, then... that is, in the spheres, so here in the upright stature, only 10% have come. The percentage of each is fixed, how many, how many people have come. Here, what we are seeing, the people of the Book, who are Christians, who are others, who believe in Allah, their number is different, his number is different. It is different according to every era. So this matter, which has a lot of detail, that how Allah's system ran, and there... these planets, the influence of Saturn was different, the influence of Jupiter was different, and according to that, here, the matter of the creation of humans was formed. And after that, here, the firmament was set in motion. With the motion of the firmament, all the spheres started to rotate. And all these planets that are visible to us, the Sun, Venus, and these, they were also imperceptible. They were so subtle, the portions of the *nadimin*, that they were not visible. So the light from there came down here. And it came and struck. And after striking, what did it do? That then, again, it rebounded. And when it went up again, then there, because it had come for this reason, that here, its... the things that were scattered, inside the earth, it took them. That is, the things of density. They were the portions of the *nadimin*. Some were portions of density. Allah's plan knows that. So it took those things of density, and the light that turned back, there it went and their halos were formed. And this is called mother of pearl, this is called crest, this is called shell. That which is visible, Venus. Venus is an imperceptible thing. This which is visible, its halo which is on the outside, its outer surface, was formed at that time. Again, a second time, the light came, but it was weak, and it could not turn back. Going, it got stuck inside the spheres. And then the spheres were formed. You know that there is a sphere of fire, a sphere of air, a sphere of water, and a sphere of earth. In these four spheres, all the *mutahhirin* have been placed. Who are the *mutahhirin*? Those who were in doubt. There, they had spent time in a state of doubt. That is, in the world of life, those who were... they were in a state of doubt. That "Is this correct or is that correct?" So here too, as long as they remain in the spheres, the same thing will be inherent in them. You see the fire, it is always flickering, and its flames are rising. And when we are there, the waves of the water are such that the water is in turmoil. It is this that shows that there is doubt and hesitation in it. Or there is air, the cyclones that move, in them there is doubt and hesitation. And in the same way, there is the sphere of earth. These four spheres have appeared in this way. And after that, how the human being was created, I have told you, that here, the first creation that happened, it was not like being born from a mother and father's womb. We were born from nature. This happened at the beginning of the *qawr-e 'azam*. You have all heard this in detail. I am just drawing a sketch, that *khudad*, that is, pits. Pits in the world, wherever Allah's will was, that "Now the house is built, a resident is needed, a masterpiece is needed," so there, the biggest and the best and the most magnificent pits, they were formed in the Ka'ba, in the *Baytullah al-Haram*. After that, they were in Ghadir Khumm, they were in the Jabal al-Rahmah, and in the Masjid al-Nabawi, in the Masjid al-Aqsa, in the Jannat al-Baqi', wherever it is, in the Masjid Quba, these... wherever the blessed places are, there these *khudad* were formed, pits were formed, just like a woman's womb. And in it, then these vapors, which had already been prepared, the animals, their... the praiseworthy animals that had been prepared. What happened with the praiseworthy animals? That there was no one to hunt them, no one to slaughter them, no one to kill them. They died a natural, normal death. After dying a natural death, their vapors that arose, they were the purest, the highest. And from that, the formation of the human being was to happen. Those vapors went up into the world, and they came down in the form of rain, on the pits. And after coming on the pits, these... just like the pits are. This was their nature. Just like a mother's womb. They fell here. Here, the heat of the earth was applied, just as we boil water in a pot. And from the heat, this went up. It struck its surface. And then it came down. The surface above was cold. Striking the surface, it came down, then it went up, then it came down. In the same way, that water, those vapors that had become water, that became thick, it took an oily form. And it took a form like that of a man's semen. And after that, then the formation of the human being happened. There, in every pit, in every pit, one human was born, who was of the male kind. All the pits... 28 pits were formed. Its details are many, in the future, in the madaris and in it, I will explain. That the number 28 is very auspicious for us. In those 28 pits, every place there were 28, 28 pits. And what we are saying is that what happened in the Ka'ba was the most auspicious. And the first human who was born in the world, his name was Qaymurth-e 'Azam. The person of excellence, who... the first one whose sitting took place. That is, everyone was born, but the real human, who is worthy of being called an Imam, he was Qaymurth-e 'Azam. With him, 27 were born. And you know that just as with every Imam, there is a cabinet, that twelve *du'at* are of the day, twelve are of the night, who are of the islands. One is a *mukasir*, one is a *ma'dhun*, and one is a *bab al-abwab*. So this count of 27, and the Imam himself is 28. This is necessary in the time of every Imam. And this is a reflection of that time when, at the beginning of the *qawr*, this birth took place. So this birth took place, and after that, the world moved on. And how did the world move on? It started with *kashf* (unveiling). *Kashf* and *sitr* (veiling). *Kashf* and *sitr* kept on going, just as our world is running, by the rotation of day and night. First, the day comes, then the night comes, then the day comes, then the night comes. And I have explained this to you, that the day is when the sun is out. The night is when the sun has set. And what is the manifestation of the sun? Its manifestation is the person of the Imam of the time. When the Imam appears, then it will be *kashf*. Where the Imam is veiled, we will call it *sitr*. This is in terms of the *din* (religion). So today, we are sitting in the era of night. The era of *kashf*, 50,000 years, has passed. How old is the world? I have told you this before. We will move forward, and we will get more clarification on this. Now this is the thing, that one *kashf* has passed. Many *kashf* have passed, but the final *kashf* that ended, that happened at the time of Janab Adam. And after that, the era of *sitr* began. The Imam of the time saw that "Now the era has become corrupt. Now it is time for the Imam to be veiled." And his representatives, his servants, the *du'at* and the prophets will do the work. So one after another, they were sent. And Janab Adam came, Nuh came, Ibrahim came, Musa came, 'Isa came, and after that, the *Shari'at-e Muhammadi*, which is the *Shari'at-e Gharra*, which came with complete perfection, that came of the Prophet. And I have told you that from Adam until here, five *ulu 'l-'azm* (possessors of steadfastness) prophets have come, on whom our five prayers are based. And six *nutaqa'* (speakers) have passed. And the seventh is to come. So this is... the structure of the *shari'ah* was being formed. In the time of Adam *'alayhi 's-salam*, the *shari'ah* that was small, now it expanded. And various principles were made for it. In the time of Nuh, *'ulum-e dini* (religious sciences) began. Then Ibrahim came, then Janab Musa came, 'Isa came, and in a complete state, it came in the time of the Janab Prophet. That is why you are the *Sayyid al-bashar* (master of humanity) and *Sayyid al-mursalin* (master of the messengers). In your time, now what happens? That *al-yawma akmaltu lakum dinakum* (Today I have perfected your religion for you). Allah says, "O Prophet, today I have perfected your religion. *wa atmamtu 'alaykum ni'mati* (and I have completed My favor upon you)." So what is this completion? So how do the madaris come? Just as our medical science tells us that first a thing is formed, a child is formed. First, there is a *sulalah* (extract), then a *nutfah* (sperm-drop), then a *mudghah* (lump of flesh), then an *'alaqah* (clot), and after that, *'izam* (bones). After *'izam*, *lahm* (flesh). The stage of *lahm* is the sixth. These are six stages. And in many places in the Quran, this has been mentioned, how the creation happens. *Sulalah* is what is in the father's loins. And from there, when it is transferred and comes into the mother's womb, it becomes a *nutfah*. And after that, then there is a *mudghah*, then an *'alaqah*. After the *'alaqah*, then there are *'izam*. *'Izam* is the fifth stage. And the fifth is what is manifested in Janab 'Isa. And the sixth is *fa kasawna 'l-'izama lahma* (and We clothed the bones with flesh). And we have clothed the bones, on the bones, this flesh and skin have been put on. That is, the structure has been perfected. Now this is a complete religion. Whose era is this? It is the era of the Prophet. So in the manifestation of *lahm*, remember, wherever *lahm* is mentioned, the Prophet will be its manifestation. Wherever Hajj is mentioned, the Prophet will be its manifestation. Wherever the sixth thing is, the Prophet will be its manifestation. Anyway, this happened. Now, our lesson for the past two or three days is that when the world was made, and after that, the system of religion came, so how did the religion spread? In every era, a *natiq* came and spread his *shari'ah*. So in every era, people came according to them, and the *shari'ah* came. Where there was a flaw in it, where it was seen that "Now this is not working with small prophets," then a great prophet comes and, by bringing his *shari'ah*, abrogates the first *shari'ah*. And in the same way, the final abrogation that happened, that happened in the time of the Janab Prophet. Because after this, this *Shari'at-e Muhammadi*, it will remain until the Day of Resurrection. He is the final prophet of Allah. And it is the faith of all of us that he, until the Day of Resurrection, in this, no change can come. In the *shari'ah*, all those who want to bring change, the great, great scholars who are sitting, that "The *shari'ah* should be like this, it should be like this," no one has the power to bring change in the *Shari'at-e Muhammadi*. Not even a little bit of change can they bring. What happened in the time of the Prophet, *salawatullah 'alayh*, has happened. And what is that? The wise one knows that. The doctor knows how to treat. We who are the patients, the Prophet knew what mistakes have occurred from us in the *'alam-e arwah* and which medicine, whether it is bitter, whether there is difficulty in praying, or they are called difficult deeds. Whether there is difficulty in fasting. But this is the very thing because of which there will be recovery. And from here, we will again turn back and be able to go to that *'alam-e arwah*. Anyway, this... all this happened, and after that, the system of the *din* was established. How the prophethood spread. And under them, Imams kept coming. Imams kept coming. And after that, *du'at* came, *hujjats* came, *mukasir*, *ma'dhun*, *mu'minin*, and the formation happened in such a way that a child that is born, as soon as the *nutfah* settles in the mother's womb, he gets things from the moon. The *namiyat al-kubra* (great vegetative power) has been placed in the moon. All these are... of the world, that is, according to our *din*, two big factories. The moon and the sun are such big factories that we cannot know how many works of Allah they are doing. So when the *nutfah* settles, he gets the *namiyat al-kubra* from the moon, when the decision is made about the child, that the child is going to be born. And after that, when he comes out of the mother's womb, he gets the *quwwat-e hissiyyah* (sensory power), he gets the senses. And from that, his life happens. Then, growing, after one year, two years, four years, when he becomes thirteen or fourteen years old, then the time of puberty, when he reaches outward youth, then the *shari'ah* is presented to him. On the sane, on the mature, the *shari'ah* will be, not on the insane. So on the sane, when the *shari'ah* is presented, what is it like? That you should believe in it. And we, at that time, when we accept it, by saying "Yes," we do its *tasdiq*, that "Brother, we believe in it." As soon as we believe, understand that we get the third power, the *quwwat-e natiqah* (rational power). Until now, what did we get? The *namiyyah* we got, the *hissiyyah* we got, and the *natiqah* we are getting now. The *natiqah* does not enter into us. Rather, its reflection... because that *natiqah* is where? It is that very thing which we have left behind as the *khamir-e nadamat* (leaven of repentance) in the pre-eternal world. As I said, it has been deposited. When we were repenting and falling from there, some of our tears of repentance, understand, tears, they were kept there. And whenever we come into the world, we reach the age of puberty, the *shari'ah* will be presented, we will say yes, then we will get its ray. And with getting it, we will be able to be called *mu'min*. So the stage of *mustajib* (respondent) is the first. After that, there are ten degrees. After ten degrees, we will progress. We will become a *mu'min*. After the *mu'min*, *mukasir*, after *mukasir*, *ma'dhun*, *ma'dhun-e mahdud*, *ma'dhun-e mutlaq*, then *da'i-e mutlaq*, *da'i-e balagh*, *hujjat*, *hujjat-e 'uzma*, and then the stages of *babiyat* will begin: *bab-e zahir*, *bab-e batin*, *bab-e silsal*, and *bab-e anbiyas*. And after that is the Imamate. And the Imam who becomes, this is the final stage of humanity. *Babiyat* is the final stage. After that, humanity is not there, but he enters into Imamate. So today, its... its discussion is there. That it has been discussed that we, as humans, have come here, so we have a body, we have a soul. As it is famous among the common people, that we have a soul, a spirit. We don't have one spirit, but three souls. And the one who has it, also has four, five, and six. We don't have that, so leave its discussion. The great saints, if you read their books, you will know that there are other souls as well, which are given as special support. So what do we have? We carry three souls: *namiyyah*, *hissiyyah*, and *natiqah*. And from this, *Nuh* is formed. *Nun*, *ha*, and *nun*. One *nun* is of the *namiyyah*, the other *ha* is of the *hissiyyah*, and the third, the two *nuns*, are of the *natiqah*. Sayyidna Ghulam Husayn has said this. So for these three souls, we also have a body. The body, for the whole life, works under the command of the soul. It also fasts, it also gives *zakat*, all things are under its command. So for it too, there should be paradise, and for the soul too, there should be. The paradise of the body, I have told you, that this body that we have brought, is not this much. But we have eaten thousands of maunds of wheat. Until now, we have eaten many things. So all that has been digested by our channel has been accepted, and all that has been excreted, all that was our share. And we have accepted it. But despite that, many parts and parcels of ours are still present in the world. When this body of ours, this upright stature ends, then those will start to come from below. That is Allah's plan, how he joins it. I have also mentioned this in yesterday's lesson. But this is that, this body's paradise, what is its meaning? What reward will the body get? What progress will the body have? That progress of the body is this, that however much body we have brought, after every thousand years, we have to return to the world. After every thousand years, we have to come here. Remember this. And in that, if, after a thousand years, we bring the same body, the progress has no meaning. So what will happen? If this is 99%, 100%, if there are 100 parts of our body, we will come with 99. One part will go to the higher world, whose progress has happened. Now these 99 that are there, in them, there will be recruitment from below, and they will go there. Just as there is oil, a container, you understand that it is... a lamp is lit, in which oil is filled, and a wick is placed. So the flame is visible to you from here, from below, the oil is being added, and the wick is burning. In the same way, from below, things are coming to us. And which ones are coming? I will give the details after a few days, there are many subtleties in it. Anyway, this... those remains, which we call the body, that is when our death occurs. After death, when they are put in the grave, they are not called the body, they are called remains. And those remains go to different places. First, they go to the *qalb-e jazira* (heart of the island). From the *qalb-e jazira*, they go to *qubiqat*, from *qubiqat* to *bata'ih*, from *bata'ih* then... the water spout of mercy, which is the drainpipe, after going there, it becomes so soft, it becomes so condensed, that from there, it goes straight to the higher world, where there is the world of the constellations, which is called the zodiac. There are twelve constellations there. In every constellation, it takes 60, 60 years, and in this way, it spends 720 years there. Then from there, that substance, having been purified, comes down. It comes into minerals, from minerals to plants, from plants to animals, and from animals to humans. It will come into that very human who is your father, and there, coming into his loins, it will come into the mother's womb, and then you will be born. This takes a thousand years. 280 years below, and 720 years above. This is the paradise of what? Of the remains. Or understand, of our body. And you know that one part being less, how it happened, the details of that were given yesterday. The second progress is this, that our souls should also progress. So the souls are the *namiyyah* and the *hissiyyah*. The *natiqah* is the thing from there. It has already progressed. And it is guiding. All the religious consciousness that is in us at this time, it is because of the *natiqah*. And the *natiqah* is guiding us, that "Brother, read the Quran, in this, this fast has to be kept, this and this has to be done, that has to be done." All the good deeds to be done, all are the effect of the *natiqah*. The one which we have come from, leaving the *khamir-e nadamat* there. Anyway, this... that the *rihiyyat*, the progress of the *rihiyyat*, of the *namiyyah*, how does it happen? That when we are buried, not just the body is buried, but the human is so at a loss, in today's era and especially in the final era of *sitr*, that in him, not just the parts of the body, but also the parts and particles of the *namiyyah* remain. A little bit of the *namiyyah* is left. With that power of growth, we are buried. And the burial is so severe... that in it, the state of confinement that is there, that is called *'adhab al-qabr*. If you put a body that is inanimate, then it is a different matter, but in it, there is the *namiyyah*, the power of growth is there, and in it, we are being put. That is why it is a torment. And that is the torment of three days. It is Allah's greatest mercy that it is only for three days, and it is until the *ziyarat*. Until the *ziyarat*, all things are finished for a righteous *mu'min*. And from there, that *nafs-e namiyyah* which was imprisoned for three days, from there it emerges. And after emerging from the grave, its name becomes *nafs-e rihiyyah*. It becomes *rih*. It becomes like a fragrance, or understand, like air. And it goes up. It goes to the moon. From the moon to Mercury, from Mercury to Venus, from Venus to the sun... to Jupiter, and from Jupiter it turns back and comes. And it stays in the moon until our Imam does not think this, that "Now my son should be born, who will become an Imam." Now see, from here the formation has begun. That the Imam's son, our birth has already happened. Now how do Imams become? So the Imam is that... for every Imam's son, this is the formation, that the one on whom the *nass* (designation) is made... not 'Ali Akbar, not 'Ali Asghar. Not Imam 'Urwah, not... but Janab Zayn al-'Abidin is the true Imam. And on him, the *nass* that was made, so how was it made? His formation, his structure, all of it is... Maulana Zayn al-'Abidin is there. He is the *muhtajib*. His reflection is here. His reflection bent down, and here the bulb lit up. So at that time, Maulana Zayn al-'Abidin came here. So how did they come? This... their body was not there. I have told you that the *qalamun* sect, whose mistake was a blink of an eye, it was made incumbent upon them that "You go and be our guides, our leaders." So from there, they came. So how did they come? Taking our garb. "Brother, you give me the garb, I will wear it." Because I have to do the work. So how did they come? From our *rihiyyat*, their *nasut-e sharifa* was formed. Our *rihiyyat* are an abstract form. And their body is being formed. So see, the level is that. That their body is so subtle, and even today if we think about it, it will be understood that why is their body better than ours? Because our souls, the *nafs-e namiyyah*, from them, their *nasut-e sharifa*, their pure body is being formed. But why are they visible? The *nafs-e namiyyah* was not visible. Because our *rihiyyat* are so full of faults, are so base, that they are visible. If they were even a little... *hijab-e zilli*, then they would disappear from our eyes. And such incidents you have read about the Imamate. Anyway, this... that this Imam, he... all of them are... in the moon, every person's *rihiyyat* gathers, and it stays there until the Imam desires that "Now my son should be born." As soon as the movement happened, at that time, from the moon, all the twelve islands' *mu'minin*, whose *rihiyyat* have gathered, they come down from above. How? In the form of dew, or in the form of water. And no hand can touch it. The *du'a* says that when they come from there, they only come for the esteemed ones. The esteemed ones, they come, in the form of an apple, in the form of a grape, or whatever the fruits of paradise are called, or in the form of water and dew, they come, and the Imam eats it, and his wife eats it. And from that, the child that is born, he is our Imam, the coming Imam. And the Imams who are born, so when that child is born, then after four years, six years, whatever their age may be, after that, the *nass* is made on them. As soon as the *nass* is made on the Imam, the first Imam has to depart from here. His death happens within a minute and a half. Because two swords cannot be in one sheath. Two Imams cannot be in one place, the residing ones. The father's rank becomes higher, because he has established his successor. The duty that he had, he has fulfilled, and he has departed from here. So in this way, the world moved on. And *wahidan ba'da wahid, waladan min walad* (one after another, a son from a son), from Maulana Hasan to Imam al-Zaman, and today, our... which one are they? The 48th Imam is today's present Imam. He is the 48th Imam. Now, in the future lessons, in these great nights, after that, at that time, I will tell you what is the value of the 48th Imam. Until we know our own Imam... as I have told you, our designated *da'i*, our Imam of the time, the great ones who are there, if we do not have their gnosis, then what will happen? *Man mata wa lam ya'rif imama zamanihi, mata mitatan jahiliyyah* (He who dies without knowing the Imam of his time, dies the death of ignorance). He who dies and does not know the Imam of his time, his death is the death of ignorance. He is finished. He did not know his Imam. On which number they are, where their seat is, what is their relation to Janab 'Ali, what is their status with the Janab Qa'im, all these things are mentioned in our books. And as much as I am allowed to say, that much I will try to say, as long as our lesson continues. These are the final stages. Now we are in the praiseworthy afterlife. After this, there will be the blameworthy afterlife. And one, one small thing has just come to my mind, later I may not get a chance, or may not remember, that on Friday, the lesson will be closed. The 5 o'clock one also, and this night's lesson. So that you also get a little break, and I also get a break. And insha'Allah, on the remaining days, and except for these great nights, in them, we will see how much time there is in the nights, otherwise, it will be closed in them too. These three nights are very virtuous. In them, there are prayers to be read. So three nights. The rest, insha'Allah, before the night of *namo*, our lesson will be finished. In that, the discussion of Hajj will also come, and many manifestations, many things are still remaining, but insha'Allah, it will be done. Sir, the afternoon lesson? The afternoon lesson will be there. The one that is there... it is attached to our... so that is why our... the afternoon is there and other people also come, but it is attached to the *namaz*, before and after, so that will be there. But this night's lesson is on Friday, and it is a holiday, so. Anyway, this... see that two, our... it has also been discussed in yesterday's lesson, that the remains, that is, in the body of the *mu'min*, how the recompense happens, and after that, of the *rihiyyat*, how its growth happens and the *nasut-e Imam*... Now, the *nasut* that... 1400 years ago, the Prophet, Janab 'Ali, Janab Hasan, Husayn, who were visible, they were in the form of *nasut*. This is the blessed body of the Imam, it is not the Imamate. This is the Imam, who has taken the form of *nasut*. The Imamate is higher than that. That is... for example, a minister is nothing until he gets a ministry. If he gets a portfolio, then he becomes a minister, otherwise, he is an MLA. So in the same way, until the Imamate is not received, he cannot become an Imam. So how will it be? The discussion of receiving the Imamate will be today's. See, I have discussed that we are *mu'minin*. All those who are Sulaymanis, are from the people of truth, are on the true religion, and believe in their elders. A correct belief is necessary. If there is a disagreement with this, because *Ta'atullahi ta'atu 'llah, ta'atu rasulihi, fa ta'atu 'l-imam-e 'ashir* (Obedience to Allah is obedience to Allah, obedience to His Messenger, then obedience to the Tenth Imam), or you can say *uli 'l-amr* (those in authority). So the formula of *uli 'l-amr* goes from top to bottom, until the order is given that "Obey them." So if all these chains are correct, then our beliefs will be correct, otherwise, there will be a flaw in the belief. Now, how is there a flaw? That the degrees of faith, if you read the *Da'a'im al-Islam*, that faith is not of one kind. There is perfect faith, there is deficient faith, there is wavering faith, there are various stages of it. You see that. So anyway, this... that we who are true *mu'min*, *mustaqim al-siyar* (straight-walking) *mu'min*, we are following a standard. That how a *mu'min* progresses. So how? Whether it is slow, it doesn't matter, but it should be complete. And Hazrat Ziyali Sahab Muqaddas used to say that the *Da'a'im al-Islam*, the seven pillars, in them, you should not fall behind in any one pillar. But every thing should go at an average pace. If you are praying, then you should pray the five daily prayers, and with it, give *zakat*, and fast. The things that have been made obligatory. It is not this, that in *namaz*, you are... the whole, the whole night you are praying *tahajjud*, but you cannot fast. This is not a complete deed. The whole month has gone to waste. Or you have fasted and done this and that, but you cannot give *zakat*. You have given *zakat*, but about Hajj, you say, "I don't understand, I can't go to Hajj." Every one of these pillars is a medicine for you, which has been applied by the Prophet. The whole things are average. Each one has a special thing. You see that Hajj is only once in a lifetime. The obligatory Hajj is only once. And that too, in a year, once, when you go, it is for six days. And after this Hajj, the second thing is jihad. Jihad, in our whole lifetime, has not been obligatory for us yet. The Imam is not present, so it is not there. In the time of the *du'at*, some orders for jihad were given, but today, alhamdulillah, several generations have passed, jihad is not obligatory for us. Now after that is the fast. And *zakat*. Purity. Purity is that, you also see that cleanliness, the cleanliness is considered a good quality by all the sciences and all the guides of the world. Because without purity, without ablution, no ablution is complete. Without ablution, no prayer is complete. And if there is no prayer, then one's deeds will not be seen. So purity has a very special place here for us. And after that is the fast. The fast is what? It is the purification of the body. The purification of the body. And why has this been placed only in the month of Allah, that is, in your Ramadan? That at one go, the order for thirty fasts has been given. If it was divided, ten fasts in Sha'ban, in Rajab, and in this... then it would be easier for the people. But it was not so. That from the very first of the month of Allah, it should begin. At the time of *sehri*, you eat, and you break the fast. Then there are realities to this, there is an interpretation to this. I, when the occasion arises, will explain. So the reality of the fast is there. So the fast also comes when? It has been said to fast for the whole year, only the month of Allah. The obligatory fasts. The supererogatory and voluntary ones are separate. And after that, what is there? *Zakat*. *Zakat* is only once in a year. And that too, at the rate of two and a half rupees. If you have 40 rupees, it is one rupee. If it is 100 rupees, it is two and a half rupees. If it is a thousand, it is 25 rupees. If it is 10,000, it is 250. It is according to this rate that you have to give. And how is that? That for twelve months, you should have that amount, then *zakat* is obligatory on you. On investment, on this, on that... if you give something out of your own goodwill for the sake of Allah, that is a different matter. Otherwise, what is obligatory is on the savings, *zakat* is on that. This is about *zakat*. And this... I have told you that this is a very important pillar. That is why *wa aqimu 's-salata wa atu 'z-zakata* (and establish prayer and give *zakat*). As *namaz* is obligatory, in the same way, *zakat* is also obligatory. One is manifested by a prophet, and one is manifested by a *wali*. Janab 'Ali and Janab... that is why it is special. And *namaz*... *namaz* is such a thing that without *namaz*, there is no concept of religion at all. Because if there is a man who has no face, how will you recognize him? The face of the religion is *namaz*. A person's identity is from his face. Our religion's identity is from our *namaz*. He who prays correctly, who prays more, he has value. *inna akramakum 'inda 'llahi atqakum* (Indeed, the most noble of you in the sight of Allah is the most righteous of you).
