The one who fears Allah, in Allah's sight, he is the most honorable. And our prayers, that is the prayer that is ours, and today, there are all-night prayers. You go outside and see that for the whole, the whole night, they are praying. They are praying voluntary prayers, they are praying *tarawih*, all things are happening. And what is this? This is happening in the wrong way. This... this is an innovation. The thing that has been newly started, in the religion, the newness that has been started... whose is this? I won't take the name. He is at number two, that "Brother, from where has this thing come?" The *tarawih* started from there. The Prophet, *salawatullah 'alayh*, had not told them. They started a new thing. And from that, if their prayers pile up, become more, then Allah says that "Their prayers will be thrown in their faces." They will be struck. So this is... a thing like *namaz*, and their prayers are being thrown in their faces. Why? Because with them, the spirit of the pillar... neither fast, *namaz*, Hajj, *zakat*, all these things are of no use until with us... there is the *wilayat* (guardianship) of Amir al-Mu'minin, the *wilayat* of 'Ali ibn Abi Talib. All things are useless. Amir al-Mu'minin is the touchstone. The whole of Islam believes in the Prophet. The only difference is where? At Janab Amir al-Mu'minin. The identity of a hypocrite and a *mu'min* is from there. See. A hypocrite or a *mu'min*, it is evident from his face. A hypocrite or a *mu'min*, it is evident from his face. Just touch upon the matter of Haydar-e Karrar's words. Touch upon the matter of Haydar-e Karrar's words, and you will understand. Look at the Sunni, look at the Shia, how one's face lights up, and the other's face falls. A hypocrite or a *mu'min*, it is evident from his face. Just touch upon the matter of Haydar-e Karrar's words. So anyway, this is that Janab 'Ali *salawatullah 'alayh* is such a touchstone that from there, the level of faith begins. Before that, it did not. The 23-year period of the Janab Prophet passed. That was the era of Islam. And today, when the *nass* was made, at Ghadir, then understand that your work was finished. The work of *ta'wil* began. And Janab 'Ali came. So how did they come? After 30 years had passed. After 30 years had passed, Janab 'Ali came. Because Janab 'Umar's caliphate happened, Janab Abu Bakr's happened, and Janab 'Uthman's. At that time, people saw 'Ali. Until then, they had not seen. Why had 'Ali not been seen until then? Just as the moon is not visible until the third night. The mansions of the moon are with 'Ali. The moon, on the first two days, is not visible at all. In the same way, 'Ali, until the third caliphate, was not visible. People were asking. The father of Janab Abu Bakr was Abu Qahafa. Abu Qahafa was the father. He lived far away. To him, Janab 'Umar was the first to bring Islam. Janab 'Umar. He was the first to put his hand and said, "I take allegiance from you." So Janab Abu Bakr was silent. Being silent means that they are agreeable. And all the people took allegiance. At that time, the allegiance was being taken, at which time the Prophet was breathing his last. The founder of Islam, who brought the *shari'ah*, and all of us... the moment of his departure is there, and here at Saqifa, a meeting is being held, that "Who will now run this, run Islam?" So you tell me that Janab 'Ali and Janab Fatima were sitting at his head. And Janab Haydar-e Karrar had wiped his face with his final breath. So they are better, who hold the status of a father. *'ana wa 'Ali, abaway hadhihi 'l-ummah* (I and 'Ali are the two fathers of this nation). O 'Ali, "I and you are the two fathers of the people." And when the father is passing away, and there you are holding a meeting. This is the custom of our world, and our Sunni scholars... it has been so. And one after another, the caliphates... for 30 years, it went on. For 30 years, Maulana 'Ali was silent. He did not say anything. Those 30 years are based on the 30 days that we remain silent in fasting and abstain from eating, drinking, and intercourse. This is its *ta'wil*. The realities are even further, I will explain this later. Higher than this is the thing that we have left. Insha'Allah, it will be discussed. So anyway, this... all these things happened. Now, today's lesson... Five minutes. A little bit I will take, then tomorrow it will be... Now here, this is that the human's third... that is, the *nafs-e natiqah* and the *hissiyyah*, what will happen to this? The *namiyyah*'s, we have already told about its paradise. The body's paradise has happened. Now, the paradise that is greater than that, what is it? It is that the *hiss*, which is called the self, the "I, I" that the human is saying, what is that? It is the *nafs-e hissiyyah* speaking. So the *nafs-e hissiyyah* also needs to progress from here. And the one who makes it progress, what is that? It is that pilot who has come from there. The *nafs-e natiqah* has come. The *nafs-e natiqah*, staying with it, staying, staying, staying, this is so accustomed that it has kept so many fasts, it has prayed so many prayers, all these are its grace. When the moment of death comes, it is not known how much of our *nafs-e hissiyyah* has become pure. For each one, it is different. No one can claim that his *nafs-e hissiyyah* has come out completely or not. The *du'at* know, the Imam knows, or else the saints know, how much of his has been purified. Some part of the *nafs-e hissiyyah*, or a lot of it, or a little bit, however much has been done, the *nafs-e natiqah* pulls this, plus his deeds. These deeds that we are doing, we are giving charity, we are doing good deeds, we are maintaining kinship, we are praying, we are praying, and we are also reading the Quran, and however many other things are there, which are good deeds, on which the stamp is there. Any thing... today, any action that is there, is not without a reaction. Science also believes this, that every action has an equal and opposite reaction. Here, we are praying, all things are happening. With Allah, it is being deposited. And those deeds, the bundle of those deeds, that is our capital, that is our investment, that is our possession, that is our provision for the hereafter, that is everything, which the *nafs-e natiqah* takes along with the *hissiyyah*, plus our deeds. This will become one bundle. One bundle will be formed. And this is called the *surat-e ruhani* (spiritual form). The *surat-e sharifa* (noble form). This will be in the form of a form, and the *du'a* says that this is so luminous that a *surat-e sharifa* of a *mu'min*, the standard one... I am talking about the standard one... that it is so pure and so luminous that we cannot meet its gaze. Just as one cannot look at the sun for five minutes, in the same way, one cannot behold the *surat-e ruhani*. Where does this *surat-e ruhani* go? It goes to our *hadd-e 'ali* (higher limit). I will just hint at this today, its details will be tomorrow. It goes to the *hadd-e 'ali*. *'Ali hadd*. Everyone has a limit. Where are we? In the circle of the *mu'min*. The one who is greater than the *mu'min*, or the one who is greater than us... The *Imam-e Zaman*'s *da'wat* that is spread over the twelve islands, wherever it may be, Allah or the Imam knows, so in that, each one... someone will be 19, someone will be 20. Here, however we may stand, in any order of age, in the order of a traveler, whatever it may be, with the *Imam-e Zaman*, a sequence has been made. A string has been made. There, whatever our *hadd-e 'ali* is, our *surat-e ruhani* will go to him. And what will happen? It will remain in his neighborhood. His *nafs*, his *hissiyyah*, it will remain in its neighborhood. It does not merge. See, it remains a neighbor. *Mujawir* means neighbor. It remains in the neighborhood. And it does not merge until his death occurs. Because there is a possibility of his downfall too. It is a world of free will. If that man goes bad, then your *surat-e ruhani* also goes bad. So what happens is that as long as that man is fine, if his end is good, with the prayers of the elders or on this, then what happens is that both *surat-e ruhani* will go to the third *hadd-e 'ali*, and it will become one plus one. And this one plus one will keep on going. From *hadd-e 'ali* to *hadd-e 'ali* to *hadd-e 'ali* to *hadd-e 'ali*... until, just as our remains went to the *qalb-e jazira*, remember this parallel, that just as our remains went to the *qalb-e jazira*, in the same way, to the master of the island, with him our... goes. The one who is the designated of the island, if one dies in his time, then that thing goes to him. And what is that? It is your property, it is your thing, it is your capital, and where is it going? To the master who has become. So he is the master because your thing has come into his possession. So all the islands, what we have... we are talking about three islands. Our two islands, which are the big ones with us, it goes to their designated one. And from there, it goes to that station, which are the three islands of the *da'i*. Their *surat-e ruhani* goes up to the three islands. And then, however much the stages are, we do not know if there are more islands, if they have progressed or not, in the *ta'mur* there are, or not. Those who are of a higher rank than you, the esteemed ones, it will go there. From there to there, from there to there... this happens, and it reaches the stage of *babiyat*. There is a *bab-e zahir* (outer gate), a *bab-e batin* (inner gate), a *bab-e silsal* (gate of the chain), and a *bab-e anbiyas* (gate of the prophets). I will give more details on this. This happens, and it reaches the *bab-e anbiyas*, the *bab al-abwab* (gate of gates). The gate of gates, the door of doors, that is the final stage of humanity. The final stage. After this, humanity is not there, but he enters into Imamate. So to reach there is a great, noble task. That our *surat-e ruhani*, if it reaches there... that is why we pray that "O Allah, forgive them, include them in the company of the family of Muhammad, give them a place in the high paradises." So what is the high paradise? One is the paradise of the remains, one is the paradise of the *namiyyah*, the third is the paradise that is the highest of all. The highest of all paradise, that is the *haykal-e imami* (Imamic structure). That is the *haykal-e imami*, which is the high paradise. And in that, all our forms gather. So how will they go? First, the *bab al-abwab*, he is its caretaker. He is the complete controller, the *bab al-abwab*. All the *du'at*, of the twelve islands... one master of every three islands, in this way, four *du'at*, from them, the message comes, and it reaches the *bab al-abwab*. All of them... of the whole world, of all the *mu'minin* who are dying, their *surat-e ruhani* comes to him. After coming to the *bab al-abwab*, there, their... administration happens. Their training happens. That many people were deficient in *namaz*. Many were in other matters. There, a scholarly promotion happens. Until the Imam does not wish that "Now I should make the *nass* on my son..." The son is born, the *nasut* has come. But the *nass* has not been made yet. Until then, at the *bab al-abwab*, all these things happen. Their spiritual training happens. And after that, when the Imam wishes that "Now, on my son, I should make the *nass*." As soon as the *nass* is made and the guidance is given that "Brother, it has been appointed that now Maulana Zayn al-'Abidin is my successor." How did it happen? Maulana Imam Husayn *salawatullah 'alayh*, when he was going out of the tent for the final time in the battle, at that time, Janab Maulana Zayn al-'Abidin was pulling a spear. He was weak, he was sick, he had become thin. He came out pulling it, that "I will also be sacrificed for you." At that time, he said, "O 'Ali, from you, my lineage will continue until the Day of Resurrection. What are you doing? You are sick. Go, sit inside." And at that time, his weapons and all... this is in the narrations of Karbala... that he entrusted his weapons and all to you, that "You are now my successor." So what were the weapons? It was the collection of the *surat-e ruhani*. That "Now, I have made the *nass* on you." And with making the *nass*, what will happen? That the *bab al-abwab*, he will die at that very moment. The *bab al-abwab* will die at that very moment because their duty is complete. And these, who are going from *hadd-e 'ali* to *hadd-e 'ali*, so what happens? Just as there is an upright stature, the *nasut-e Imam*. Imam Husayn, for example. So Imam Husayn has a structure. And the image of the structure is also an upright stature, a human stature. So the human stature, just as it consists of eyes, nose, teeth, all these things, in the same way, in the structure too, it will take the same position. If it is A or B, then the one who is at the position of the teeth, or at the position of the nose... in the *rihiyyat*, in the *nasut-e Imam*, in the body of the Imam, whatever the position is, the same will be in the structure. And in the same way, all the limbs that will be there, the liver, our lungs, this and that, all of them, in a spiritual way, will be fitted there. Except that the position of the heart will be empty. The position of the heart remains empty, because all the forms have come where? They have come to the presence of the *bab al-abwab*. And the *bab al-abwab* is the one who is the head of all. He is the chief of all. And the chief in the body is what? It is the position of the heart. As soon as this structure is ready, the *bab al-abwab* will die. His own *surat-e ruhani* will take the position of the heart in the noble structure, and that whole bundle, that whole capital, that whole bag, Janab Imam, who is the residing one, who is the present one at this time, he gives it to his son as a gift. And the caliphate... the *nass* is made, and with it, the *haykal-e imami* is given. As soon as it is given here, within a minute and a half, his own death occurs. He departs from this world. His death does not happen. He himself wants it, because according to Allah's will, he can do anything. So that is why, when death comes, he departs from this world. Now a new caliphate, a new Imamate has started, of his son. When Imam Husayn died, the Imamate of Janab Zayn al-'Abidin began. In this way, it keeps on going. Five minutes are left. This is the matter of the *hadd-e 'ali* that is there... it has to be understood. That the *hadd-e 'ali* is everything for us. He is much greater than us. This *hadd-e 'ali* is the one for whom we, as soon as we come, pray two *rak'at* of *namaz* in the mosque. We pray two *rak'at* of *namaz*, the *tahiyyat al-masjid*. So what is the *tahiyyat al-masjid*? It is for the *hadd-e 'ali* and his *hadd*. The two. Father and grandfather. Today, the identity happens... if you just write the father's name, the identity is not complete, but the grandfather's name also has to be written. In the same way, for the *hadd-e 'ali* and their *hadd-e 'ali*, two *rak'at* are prayed. The second manifestation of it is the *da'i* of the time. The *da'i* of the island or the *da'i-e mutlaq*. And the one who is the builder of the mosque. If someone has given a donation or something. Otherwise, what usually happens is that it is built by the *da'wat sharifa*. So the *da'i* is its manifestation. This... that's all for today.
